### [1] [Multistability by Design in Complex Triangular Mechanical Metamaterials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36317)
*Chaviva Sirote-Katz,Yair Shokef*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该论文提出混合两种梁厚度的受挫三角网络（frustrated triangular networks），通过将二阶屈曲（second-order buckling）与角变形的能量代价拆分为四种竞争性贡献，构建了基于无量纲几何参数的相图（phase diagram）。特定梁排布呈现局部双稳态（local bistability），且机械稳定构型数随系统尺寸指数增长；硅胶超材料（silicone metamaterials）实验验证了预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在三角机械超材料（triangular mechanical metamaterials）中引入受挫（frustration）并同时实现架构与变形状态的多重性，是值得探索的设计方向；本文旨在通过梁厚度图案化（beam-thickness patterning）来实现受挫、多稳态（multistability）与广泛简并（extensive degeneracy）。

Method: 采用标度论证（scaling arguments）并将系统映射到有效伊辛描述（effective Ising description），以无量纲几何参数为变量构建相图；同时制备硅胶超材料进行实验验证，并指出允许的梁排布可映射为菱形镶嵌（rhombus tilings）。

Result: 两种厚度将二阶屈曲与角变形能量代价分离为四种竞争性贡献；部分梁排布具局部双稳态——六边形图案（hexagonal motifs）可在相反扭转态间独立切换，线性图案（linear motifs）支持可独立切换的梁状态。两种情形下机械稳定构型数均随系统尺寸指数增长；实验证实了预测的双稳态与局部切换行为。

Conclusion: 该系统在架构与稳定变形状态两方面兼具多重性，确立了梁厚度图案化作为在三角机械超材料中编程受挫、多稳态与广泛简并的途径。

Abstract: We introduce frustrated triangular networks that mix two different beam thicknesses. The two thicknesses separate the energetic costs of second-order buckling and angular deformation into four competing contributions. Using scaling arguments and mapping to an effective Ising description, we construct the system's phase diagram in terms of its dimensionless geometric parameters. Selected beam arrangements exhibit local bistability: hexagonal motifs switch independently between opposite twisting states, while linear motifs support independently switchable beam states. In both cases, the number of mechanically stable configurations grows exponentially with system size. Experiments on fabricated silicone metamaterials confirm the predicted bistability and local switching. More generally, the allowed beam arrangements map onto rhombus tilings, producing a large combinatorial space of architectures. The system thus combines multiplicity in both architecture and stable deformation states, establishing beam-thickness patterning as a route for programming frustration, multistability, and extensive degeneracy in triangular mechanical metamaterials.

</details>


### [2] [Mechanical Signature of a Chemically Driven Bath](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36591)
*Tian Huang,Jintae Park,Hyuk Kyu Pak,Steve Granick*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 利用光镊（optical tweezers）测量CuAAC点击反应（click reaction）过程中被捕获胶体位置涨落的变化，发现反应使涨落增加至少20%，且该额外噪声为高斯型、具有平坦功率谱，但其幅度与宏观反应速率解耦，无法用独立分子力偶极子之和解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究化学反应释放的自由能如何以机械方式耦合到微观探针，检验反应浴（reacting bath）的机械作用能否被还原为独立分子事件的叠加。

Method: 使用光镊捕获直径2微米（micrometer）的胶体，在CuAAC点击反应进行时测量其位置涨落，并分析噪声的幅度、统计分布（高斯性）、功率谱（power spectrum）以及随试剂消耗的衰减行为；同时将观测方差与分子力偶极子（force dipole）估算值及宏观反应速率进行对比。

Result: 位置涨落增加至少20%，而陷阱拐点频率（corner frequency）不变；额外方差随试剂耗尽回归热基线（thermal baseline）；噪声呈高斯分布，平坦功率谱延伸至拐点频率以上达7 kHz；噪声幅度与宏观反应速率解耦；独立分子力偶极子比观测方差小若干数量级；局部释放的化学自由能比微球吸收的能量高五个数量级，但能量耦合效率低下。

Conclusion: 反应浴的机械作用不能被表示为独立分子事件之和，表明存在超出单分子描述的集体或非加性耦合机制，且化学自由能向微球机械自由度的传递效率极低。

Abstract: Using optical tweezers, we demonstrate that the CuAAC click reaction increases the positional fluctuations of a trapped 2 micrometer colloid by at least 20% without altering the trap's corner frequency. This excess variance relaxes to the thermal baseline as reagents are depleted. The additional noise is Gaussian, with a flat power spectrum extending past the corner frequency up to 7 kHz. Notably, the noise amplitude decouples from the macroscale reaction rate, and independent molecular force dipoles are orders of magnitude too small to generate the observed variance. Although the chemical free energy released locally exceeds the energy absorbed by the bead by five orders of magnitude, this energy couples inefficiently. The mechanical action of the reacting bath cannot be represented as a sum of independent molecular events.

</details>


### [3] [Polar Phase of an Egg-Yolk Particle in a Rotating Magnetic Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37008)
*Roberts Treize,Janis Cimurs*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 该研究通过宏观“蛋黃型”（egg-yolk）粒子实验与引入内摩擦的模型，揭示了在旋转磁场中超出现有瞬时 Stoner–Wohlfarth 极限的新“极性相”（polar phase），并表明极性相仅存在于中等内/外摩擦比 κ。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统描述磁性粒子在旋转磁场中的轨道时采用瞬时 Stoner–Wohlfarth 极限（instantaneous Stoner–Wohlfarth limit），认为磁矩始终处于各向异性能量极小值，因而在强场（B0/Ba<1/√2 以上）仅允许平面同步旋转或摆动，无法解释实验中出现的非平面行为。

Method: 设计并光学追踪由补偿旋转磁场驱动的宏观蛋黄型粒子（外壳内含两个可自由旋转永磁体于充液腔）；在模型中引入磁体与外壳之间的有限粘性摩擦（viscous friction），得到单一无量纲参数 κ（内摩擦与外摩擦之比），当 κ→0 时退化为瞬时理论，并给出数值相图。

Result: 除同步旋转、进动（precession）与摆动（libration）外，粒子在高频且超出瞬时界限时出现极性相（polar phase），壳轴离开磁场平面并锁定于绕旋转轴的窄锥面。数值相图显示增大 κ 使双稳带在高频下向更高场倾斜、将稳定进动扩展至 B0/Ba=1/√2 以上，而大 κ 时高频下平面摆动回归；经校准各向异性场、弛豫率与约为 1 量级的 κ 后，模型可复现全部实测记录的终态及其倾角（含极性锥）。

Conclusion: 模型表明极性相由磁体相对外壳的稳态旋转维持——由场矩与各向异性矩驱动、由内摩擦耗散——因此仅在中等的 κ 条件下存在。

Abstract: The orbit selected by a magnetic particle in a viscous liquid under a rotating field is commonly described in the instantaneous Stoner--Wohlfarth limit, in which the magnetic moment always occupies a minimum of the anisotropy energy. In this limit, precession out of the field plane exists only for reduced fields $B_0/B_a<1/\sqrt2$, and stronger fields admit only planar synchronous rotation or libration. We study a macroscopic egg-yolk particle, a printed shell containing two freely rotating permanent magnets in fluid-filled cavities, driven by a compensated rotating field and tracked optically. In addition to synchronous rotation, precession, and libration, the particle exhibits a polar phase: above the instantaneous bound and at high frequency, the shell axis leaves the field plane and locks onto a narrow cone about the rotation axis. We extend the model by a finite viscous friction between the magnets and the shell. The extension introduces a single dimensionless parameter, the ratio $κ$ of inner to outer rotational friction, and reduces to the instantaneous theory as $κ\to0$. Numerical phase diagrams show that increasing $κ$ tilts the bistable band to higher fields at higher frequencies and extends stable precession above $B_0/B_a=1/\sqrt2$, whereas for large $κ$ planar libration returns at high frequency. With a calibrated anisotropy field, relaxation rate, and $κ$ of order unity, the model reproduces the terminal state of all measured records and their tilt angles, including the polar cone. In the model the polar state is maintained by a steady rotation of the magnets relative to the shell, driven by the field and anisotropy torques and dissipated by the inner friction; it therefore exists only for intermediate $κ$.

</details>


### [4] [Stiffness-dependent Dielectric Relaxation in Thermoreversible Microgels: Effects of Temperature and Strain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37428)
*Sayantan Chanda,Sonali Vasant Kawale,Ranjini Bandyopadhyay*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 本研究发现，PNIPAM 微凝胶（microgel）的颗粒刚度（particle stiffness）支配着浓悬浮液在不同长度尺度上的弛豫动力学（relaxation dynamics）：刚硬颗粒表现更慢的介电弛豫（dielectric relaxation）并出现应变诱导减速，而柔软网络颗粒的介电动力学几乎与应变无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于厘清颗粒刚度与内部结构如何调控浓悬浮液在微观与宏观尺度上的弛豫路径，并服务于自适应软超材料（soft metamaterials）、应力耗散涂层与自修复机器人材料的设计需求。

Method: 方法上，将介电谱（dielectric spectroscopy）与流变测量（rheometric measurements）结合，对温度变化和大幅振荡剪切（large amplitude oscillatory shear）下的热响应 PNIPAM 微凝胶悬浮液进行测试，从而关联微观极化涨落动力学与宏观力学弛豫。

Result: 结果显示：高交联、较硬的微凝胶在宽温区内具有更长的介电弛豫时间并呈现应变诱导减慢，而较软的开网络微凝胶介电动力学几乎不依赖应变；在体积相变温度（volume phase transition temperature, VPTT）以下，最软颗粒悬浮液的宏观体相应力弛豫最快，而较硬微凝胶体系出现解耦——体相弛豫随刚度增加而加快、介电弛豫却变慢；VPTT 以上，较软颗粒体积塌缩更大，导致体相弛豫非常迅速，同时较硬体系除应力衰减更慢外，还在形变后段发生剪切诱导的结构再生。

Conclusion: 结论指出，颗粒刚度与内部构筑决定了跨越微观与宏观长度尺度的弛豫路径，为设计自适应软材料、应力耗散涂层与自修复机器人材料提供了框架。

Abstract: We investigate how particle stiffness governs length-scale-dependent relaxation dynamics in dense suspensions of thermoresponsive PNIPAM microgels subjected to temperature variations and large amplitude oscillatory shear. By combining dielectric spectroscopy with rheometric measurements, we directly correlate microscopic polarization fluctuation dynamics with macroscopic mechanical relaxation. Highly crosslinked, stiffer microgels exhibit longer dielectric relaxation times across a broad temperature range and strain-induced slowing down, whereas softer, open-network microgels exhibit nearly strain-independent dielectric dynamics. Below the volume phase transition temperature (VPTT), macroscopic bulk stress relaxation experiments reveal that suspensions of the softest particles relax most rapidly. For the stiffer microgel suspensions, however, the dynamics decouple: bulk stress relaxation speeds up with increasing stiffness even as dielectric relaxation slows down. Above the VPTT, softer particles undergo greater volume collapse, leading to very rapid bulk stress relaxation. Besides exhibiting slower stress decay, stiffer microgel suspensions also undergo shear-induced structural regeneration during the later stages of deformation. These findings demonstrate that particle stiffness and internal architecture govern relaxation pathways across microscopic and macroscopic length scales, thereby providing a framework for designing adaptive soft metamaterials, stress-dissipative coatings, and self-healing robotic materials.

</details>


### [5] [A Bioinspired Magnetothermally Triggered Capsule for Rapid Microrobot Release](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37598)
*Mengfan Zhang,Zike Chen,Zhihao Lv,Zheng Jia,Rui Xiao,Guoyong Mao*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 受凤仙花（Impatiens balsamina）种子传播的锁存弹簧机制启发，该研究提出一种磁热触发胶囊（magnetothermally triggered capsule），结合相变水凝胶（phase-change hydrogel）锁存、单稳态（monostable）弹性盖与载荷底座，在交变磁场下 50 ms 内开盖并以约 1.0 m/s 弹射微型机器人（microrobot）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 胶囊（capsule）作为微型机器人递送平台兼具结构简单与受保护递送的优势，但如何同时实现保护性递送与快速释放仍具挑战。研究借鉴凤仙花种子传播的锁存弹簧（latched spring）机制，以解决受保护递送与按需快速释放之间的矛盾。

Method: 该胶囊将相变水凝胶锁存（phase-change hydrogel latch）、作为弹性弹簧的单稳态盖（monostable cover）与载荷底座集成一体；通过力学分析（mechanical analysis）指导盖设计，以避开装配后的双稳态（bistability）并防止触发前水凝胶锁存提前破裂。运输过程中水凝胶约束倒置的盖；在交变磁场（alternating magnetic field）下，Fe3O4 介导加热使水凝胶软化并发生内聚破坏（cohesive failure），释放储能驱动开盖。

Result: 水凝胶软化后胶囊在 50 ms 内开启，并以估计约 1.0 m/s 的初速度弹射微型机器人。在三维平台与离体猪胃（ex vivo porcine stomach）上的演示验证了受保护递送、靶点开盖及释放后驱动（post-release actuation）。

Conclusion: 该磁热触发胶囊验证了仿生锁存弹簧机制在微型机器人递送中的可行性，兼具结构简单、受保护运输、靶点快速释放与释放后自主驱动的功能，适用于三维平台与离体胃环境等场景。

Abstract: Capsules are attractive platforms for microrobot delivery, combining structural simplicity with protected delivery and rapid release remains challenging. Inspired by the latched spring mechanism of Impatiens balsamina seed dispersal, a magnetothermally triggered capsule integrates a phase-change hydrogel latch, a monostable cover that serves as an elastic spring, and a payload-carrying base. Mechanical analysis guides the cover design to avoid bistability after assembly and premature hydrogel latch rupture before triggering. During transport, the hydrogel constrains the inverted cover. Under an alternating magnetic field, Fe3O4-mediated heating softens the hydrogel and induces cohesive failure, releasing stored energy to drive capsule opening and microrobot ejection. The capsule opens within 50 ms after hydrogel softening and ejects microrobots at an estimated initial velocity of 1.0 m/s. Demonstrations on a three-dimensional platform and ex vivo porcine stomach establish protected delivery, target-site opening, and post-release actuation.

</details>


### [6] [Defect Organization in Coexisting Hexagonal and Square Lattices on Ellipsoids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37668)
*Wenyu Liu,Han Xie,Baohui Li,Jeff Z. Y. Chen,Yao Li*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 采用模拟退火朗之万动力学（simulated-annealing Langevin dynamics）研究Hertzian粒子在长椭球与扁椭球上共存的六角（Hex）与正方（Sq）晶格，揭示曲率与拓扑如何共同组织缺陷形貌。结果显示曲率各向异性通过选择拓扑荷的空间分布与有限缺陷图案的连接方式，可工程化丰富的缺陷模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 二维晶体中曲率与拓扑共同组织缺陷，但当竞争性晶格对称性与空间变化的曲率共存时，二者的联合作用尚不明确。论文旨在解答这一未解问题。

Method: 使用模拟退火朗之万动力学，研究在长椭球（prolate）与扁椭球（oblate）表面形成六角（Hex）与正方（Sq）共存晶格的Hertzian粒子体系；通过绘制约化密度与长径比相图，并进行按纬度分辨（latitude-resolved）的比较分析。

Result: 约化密度与长径比映射揭示在Hex主导与Sq主导背景下存在广泛的scar与畴基（domain-based）形貌序列。弱形变时高斯曲率（Gaussian curvature）将缺陷偏向曲率极大处；强长椭率下高曲率被限制于小极冠，缺陷向低曲率纬度扩散以缓解拥挤与弹性排斥。Hex主导区形成顶点接触畴并中和角接触，补偿性正缺陷远离极点；Sq主导区出现富Hex三角形畴、桥接态及线性或开放scar。扁椭球上赤道高曲率带允许缺陷沿方位角分离但仍保持曲率局域化。

Conclusion: 非均匀曲率可通过选择拓扑荷的空间分布与有限缺陷图案的连接性来工程化丰富的缺陷模式。

Abstract: Curvature and topology jointly organize defects in two-dimensional crystals, but their combined role remains unresolved when competing lattice symmetries coexist with spatially varying curvature. We use simulated-annealing Langevin dynamics to study Hertzian particles forming coexisting hexagonal (Hex) and square (Sq) lattices on prolate and oblate ellipsoids. Mapping reduced density and aspect ratio reveals a broad sequence of scar and domain-based morphologies in both Hex-dominant and Sq-dominant backgrounds. Latitude-resolved comparisons show that Gaussian curvature biases defects toward its maxima under weak deformation. Strong prolateness, however, confines high curvature to small polar caps that cannot independently accommodate all defect motifs. Defects then spread toward lower-curvature latitudes to relieve defect crowding and elastic repulsion. In the Hex-dominant regime, this competition drives vertex-contacted domains with neutralized corner contacts, and compensating positive defects locate away from the poles. The Sq-dominant regime features Hex-rich triangular domains, bridged states, and linear or open scars similarly reorganized by curvature anisotropy. On oblate ellipsoids, the extended equatorial high-curvature belt allows defects to separate azimuthally while remaining curvature-localized. This work elucidates that nonuniform curvature can engineer rich defect patterns by selecting the spatial distribution of topological charge and the connectivity of finite defect motifs.

</details>


### [7] [Emergent activity powers a path to chaos for pairs of acoustically trapped spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37946)
*Matthew K. Gronert,Ben Cao,Ella M. King,David G. Grier*

Main category: cond-mat.soft

TL;DR: 无自驱动与外部能源的声学俘获双球对，可通过非互易波散射产生间歇自旋等涌现活动，并呈现从被动到混沌的完整动力学路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究关注在无自驱动、无明显能源、无对称破缺机制条件下，成对球体为何在声学陷阱中仍会间歇性自旋。其动机在于解释这种反常的涌现活动性（emergent activity）来源及其动力学机制。

Method: 以实验现象为出发点，提出非互易波散射（nonreciprocal wave scattering）驱动该活动，并提出阻力（drag）通过耦合旋转与平动塑造其动力学。结合实验与模拟，追踪无噪声条件下从被动到混沌的路径。

Result: 不匹配的球对可经超临界Hopf分岔（supercritical Hopf bifurcation）进入准周期环面（quasiperiodic torus），其轨道规律性反转；远离平衡时进入确定性混沌（deterministic chaos）。实验呈现相同定性特征，模拟揭示完整无噪声路径。

Conclusion: 非互易波散射可驱动无自驱动系统的涌现活动，阻力耦合旋转与平动塑造其复杂动力学；该机制暗示类似动力学可出现在其他活性物质（active-matter）系统中。

Abstract: Pairs of spheres sharing an acoustic trap sometimes spin intermittently despite having no self-propulsion, no obvious power source, and no symmetry-breaking mechanism, switching between rocking and spinning within a single trajectory. </details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 将Mg/Si比从3.5调至1.8时，颗粒组成由MgO与镁硅酸盐的混合物转变为以镁硅酸盐为主；UHV退火同样使组成向镁硅酸盐偏移，但始终存在非化学计量结构基元，形成具有潜在催化活性的位点分布。电子显微图像显示形貌依赖组成，且在不同热退火条件下发生不同重构。

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 为评估中文版 Starterkit 课程的效果，项目加入了分析（analytics）功能，结果表明访问 Starterkit 网站的总访问量中约 22% 会访问至少一个中文页面。

Conclusion: 新版 Starterkit 通过持续集成测试与多语言支持提升了可维护性与可及性；中文翻译及其配套活动已落地，量化数据显示中文内容具有可观的访问占比。

Abstract: The Starterkit Lessons online and Starterkit Workshops held in Geneva each year have been the main method of onboarding newcomers to the LHCb experiment since its founding in 2015. This new version has made several improvements over the old one, with increased maintainability through testing of examples in CI pipelines, and has also allowed for translations of the Starterkit Lessons. In late 2025 the Run 3 Starterkit lessons were translated into Mandarin Chinese, to allow for a sibling Starterkit event to take place in China, with a dedicated liaison role set up to ensure synchronization between the two translations. To measure the success of the Chinese Starterkit Lessons, analytics have been added revealing that roughly 22% of all visits to the Starterkit website will access at least one Chinese-language page.

</details>


### [11] [CMS Open Data Visualization with FireworksWeb](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35843)
*Yuxiao Wang,Alja Mrak-Tadel,Matevž Tadel,Avi Yagil,Mario Masciovecchio,Dmytro Kovalskyi,Sergey Linev*

Main category: physics.ed-ph

TL;DR: FireworksWeb 是一套基于浏览器的粒子物理事件显示器（event display），采用 C++ ROOT-Eve 后端与 SAPUI5 前端，可在无需本地安装软件的前提下实现交互式三维事件可视化，目前已用于 CMS 控制室的实时事件监测，并扩展至 CMS 开放数据（Open Data）的可视化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CMS 实验通过 CERN Open Data 门户公开了大量对撞数据集，这些数据是教育、科普与外展（outreach）及研究的宝贵资源；但传统事件显示工具通常需要本地软件安装，构成使用门槛。该工作旨在降低这一门槛，使多样化受众可便捷访问粒子物理数据。

Method: 在 ROOT-Eve 与 RenderCore 基础上构建基于网页的事件显示系统，后端为 C++ ROOT-Eve、前端为 SAPUI5。原型功能包括独立相机（standalone cameras）、事件筛选器（event filters）、可配置的集合设置（configurable collection settings）以及针对圆柱几何的鱼眼畸变（fish-eye distortion）投影控制；网络架构通过 CGI 通信，支持数据集查询与基于物理判据的过滤事件选择。

Result: FireworksWeb 已在 CMS 控制室部署用于实时事件监测，验证了其在生产环境中进行实时数据可视化的可靠性；原型已通过 CMS 开放数据事件进行演示，并给出了技术架构说明。

Conclusion: FireworksWeb 在保持专业级事件显示能力的同时消除了本地软件安装需求，并可用于教育、公众外展与探索性物理分析等应用场景。

Abstract: FireworksWeb is a web-based event display utilizing a C++ ROOT-Eve backend with SAPUI5 frontend for interactive 3D visualization of particle physics events directly in the browser. Building upon ROOT-Eve and RenderCore, it eliminates local software installation while maintaining professional-grade event display capabilities. FireworksWeb is currently deployed for live event monitoring in the CMS control room, demonstrating its reliability for real-time data visualization in production environments.
  The CMS experiment has made a large amount of collision datasets publicly available through the CERN Open Data portal, which are the valuable resources for education, outreach, and research. We have extended FireworksWeb to enable visualization of these open datasets, making particle physics data accessible to diverse audiences without requiring local software installation.
  Our prototype implementation includes standalone cameras, event filters, configurable collection settings, and projection controls with fish-eye distortion for cylindrical geometries. The web architecture communicating via CGI enables dataset queries and filtered event selection based on physics criteria. We demonstrate the prototype with CMS Open Data events, discuss the technical architecture, and outline applications for education, public outreach, and exploratory physics analysis.

</details>

### [12] [Spin-exchange interactions between circular Rydberg atoms over long times](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37766)
*Andrés Durán-Hernández,Gautier Creutzer,Aurore Alice Young,Abderrahmane Kassid,Yohann Machu,Jean-Michel Raimond,Michel Brune,Clément Sayrin*

Main category: physics.atom-ph

TL;DR: 该工作利用激光捕获的圆形里德堡原子（circular Rydberg atoms）实现超过 60 μs、40 个自旋振荡周期的自旋交换相互作用，将现有技术水平提升一个数量级，并首次在该体系中观测到自旋-运动耦合（spin-motion coupling）引起的振荡对比度坍缩与复苏，同时提出抑制该效应的动力学解耦（dynamical decoupling）方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中性原子里德堡阵列是量子计算与量子模拟（quantum simulation）的重要平台，原子数已大幅增加，但相互作用时间（interaction time）仅限数微秒，限制了每原子门数及量子多体系统长时动力学的模拟。因此需要延长相互作用时间。

Method: 在激光捕获的圆形里德堡原子中编码两个自旋 1/2（spin 1/2），观测其自旋交换相互作用（spin-exchange interaction）；在弱原子捕获条件下记录自旋-运动耦合引起的自旋交换振荡对比度（spin-exchange oscillation contrast）的坍缩与复苏；并提出一种新型动力学解耦方法以抑制该效应，该方法基于近期发展的混合平台（hybrid platform），可用辅助里德堡原子对长寿命圆形里德堡原子进行测量与光学操控。

Result: 自旋交换相互作用持续时间超过 60 μs、跨越 40 个自旋振荡周期，将现有技术水平提升一个数量级；在弱原子捕获下观测到自旋交换振荡对比度的坍缩与复苏，为激光捕获里德堡原子中首次观测到该耦合；所提出的动力学解耦方法可防止自旋振荡的坍缩。

Conclusion: 超长相互作用时间与动力学解耦方法为强相互作用量子多体系统的长时量子模拟提供了直接路径，推动该平台在超越经典器件方面的发展。

Abstract: Arrays of neutral atoms excited to Rydberg levels have emerged as one of the most promising platforms for quantum computation and simulation. With the hope to outperform classical devices, the number of atoms has been increased by orders of magnitude. However, the interaction time, i.e., the maximum accumulated time during which an atom interacts with its neighbours, has been limited to a few microseconds only. This restrains the number of gates per atom or prevents the simulation of long-time dynamics of quantum many-body systems. Here, we observe the spin-exchange interaction between two spin 1/2s encoded in laser-trapped circular Rydberg atoms over more than $60μs$ and 40 spin-oscillation periods, improving the state of the art by an order of magnitude. These unprecedented timescales allow us to record for weak atomic trapping a collapse and revival of the spin-exchange oscillation contrast induced by spin-motion coupling. Our results constitute the first observation of this coupling with laser-trapped Rydberg atoms. To counteract its detrimental effect for quantum simulation, we demonstrate a novel dynamical decoupling method that prevents the collapse of the spin oscillations. This method exploits the advantages of our recently developed hybrid platform that enables the measurement and the optical manipulation of long-lived circular Rydberg atoms with auxiliary Rydberg atoms. This work opens a direct route to long-duration quantum simulation of strongly-interacting many-body systems.

</details>

### [13] [From $3$-spin to Negative $r$-spin: Limits of Cohomological Field Theories and Tautological Relations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36509)
*Deniz Genlik,Felix Janda*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 该论文证明 Chidambaram–Garcia-Failde–Giacchetto 的负 $r$-spin tautological relations（tautological relations）可由 Pixton 的 $3$-spin relations 推出，对任意 $r\ge2$ 成立，并由此得到其在 $\overline{\mathcal{M}}_{g,n}$ 的 tautological Chow ring（tautological Chow ring）中成立；$r=2$ 的情形蕴含 Kazarian–Norbury $K$-relations（$K$-relations）在 Chow 中成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在建立不同来源的 tautological relations（tautological relations）之间的推导关系，特别是将负 $r$-spin relations 归约到 Pixton 的 $3$-spin relations，从而统一理解并确认其在 Chow 环中的有效性。

Method: 证明分两部分：其一，通过对 Givental–Teleman graph contributions（Givental–Teleman graph contributions）极限行为的精细研究，证明由 Chiodo's class（Chiodo's class）产生的 tautological relations 蕴含负 $r$-spin relations；其二，应用对 Janda 关于 CohFTs（CohFTs）的 tautological relations 工作的推广，证明 Chiodo relations 是 Pixton 的 $3$-spin relations 的推论。

Result: 对每个 $r\ge2$，负 $r$-spin tautological relations 是 Pixton 的 $3$-spin relations 的推论，并在 $\overline{\mathcal{M}}_{g,n}$ 的 tautological Chow ring 中成立；当 $r=2$ 时，Kazarian–Norbury $K$-relations 在 Chow 中成立。

Conclusion: 负 $r$-spin relations 可由 Pixton 的 $3$-spin relations 推出并在 tautological Chow ring 中成立，且 $r=2$ 情形给出 Kazarian–Norbury $K$-relations 在 Chow 中成立的结果。

Abstract: We prove that the negative $r$-spin tautological relations of Chidambaram--Garcia-Failde--Giacchetto are a consequence of Pixton's $3$-spin relations, for every $r\ge2$, and deduce that they hold in the tautological Chow ring of $\overline{\mathcal{M}}_{g,n}$. In particular, our result for $r=2$ implies that Kazarian--Norbury $K$-relations hold in Chow.
  The proof factors through two essential parts. The first part shows that the tautological relations arising from Chiodo's class imply the negative $r$-spin relations. This requires an intricate study of limiting behavior of the Givental--Teleman graph contributions. In the second part, we show that the Chiodo relations are a consequence of Pixton's $3$-spin relations as an application of a generalization of Janda's work on tautological relations from CohFTs.

</details>


### [14] [Global Sections and Birational Geometry of Calabi-Yau Type Varieties](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36034)
*Andrei Constantin,Andre Lukas,Elijah Sheridan*

Main category: math.AG

TL;DR: 本文研究除子（divisor）的整体截面与双有理几何（birational geometry）之间的关系：对 klt Calabi-Yau 型簇，整体截面由支配 $D$-极小模型纲领（$D$-minimal model program, $D$-MMP）的同一双有理数据控制，并据此给出大锥上分片拟多项式公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典基于正性（positivity）的方法在可动锥（movable cone）之外失效，难以系统处理任意大除子的整体截面。作者希望借用 $D$-MMP 的双有理数据，将截面计算推广到更大范围。

Method: 在 klt Calabi-Yau 型簇上，通过直接减除或双有理收缩去除不贡献整体截面的固定除子分量（fixed divisorial components），并借助消没定理（vanishing theorems）将任意大除子的整体截面归结为合适双有理模型 $Y$ 上的欧拉示性数 $\\chi(Y,\\mathcal O_Y(D))$。

Result: 得到大锥（big cone）上整体截面的分片拟多项式（piecewise quasipolynomial）公式，其定义域为 $D$-MMP 的森氏腔（Mori chambers）；限制到 Fano 型簇时，公式可延伸至整个有效锥（effective cone）。这些公式仅由 $X$ 的小双有理类构造，却编码 $X$ 所有双有理收缩的性质。

Conclusion: 整体截面与双有理几何存在双向关系：一方面可由双有理几何计算整体截面，另一方面 $h^0(X,\\mathcal O_X(D))$ 的有限次计算可反推腔分解及相应双有理模型的信息。

Abstract: We investigate aspects of the relationship between global sections of divisors and birational geometry. We show that for varieties $X$ of klt Calabi-Yau type, global sections are governed by the same birational data that control the $D$-minimal model program ($D$-MMP). As a consequence, global sections of arbitrary big divisors can be systematically reduced to Euler characteristics $χ(Y,\mathcal O_Y(D))$ on suitable birational models $Y$ using vanishing theorems, extending classical positivity-based methods beyond the movable cone. The reduction proceeds by removing fixed divisorial components, which do not contribute to the space of global sections, either directly by subtraction or birationally by contraction to a suitable $D$-minimal model. We obtain piecewise quasipolynomial formulae for global sections on the big cone, whose domains are the Mori chambers of the $D$-MMP. Restricting to the subclass of Fano type varieties extends such formulae to the entire effective cone. These formulae can be constructed from just the small birational class of $X$, yet encode properties of all birational contractions of $X$. Alongside the computation of global sections from birational geometry, we study the inverse process, wherein finite computations of $h^0(X,\mathcal O_X(D))$ determine aspects of the chamber decomposition and the associated birational models.

</details>

### [15] [It Just Takes Two: Scaling Amortized Inference to Large Sets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.07972)
*Antoine Wehenkel,Michael Kagan,Lukas Heinrich,Chris Pollard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种将表示学习（representation learning）与后验建模（posterior modeling）解耦的策略，用于集合型观测条件下的摊销推断（amortized inference）。方法仅在规模不超过二的集合上训练均值池化深度集合（mean-pool Deep Set）编码器，再对推断头（inference head）在预聚合嵌入上微调，使训练成本基本不随部署集合规模 N 变化，并在多种基准上以更低计算量达到或超越标准基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经后验估计（neural posterior estimation）在科学应用中常面对条件变量为观测集合的情形，集合元素既依赖目标又依赖集合内共享的未知因素。最优推断需联合处理集合，因而须在部署集合规模上训练估计器，但该规模下内存与计算开销迅速变得难以承受。

Method: 方法将表示学习与后验建模解耦：训练一个均值池化深度集合（mean-pool Deep Set）编码器，训练所用集合规模至多为二，使编码器可泛化至任意集合规模；随后在预聚合嵌入（pre-aggregated embeddings）上对推断头（inference head）进行微调。

Result: 在标量（scalar）、图像、多视图三维（multi-view 3D）、分子以及高维条件生成（conditional generation）等基准上，当 N 达到数千时，该方法以远低于基线的计算量达到或优于标准基线，其训练成本基本独立于部署集合规模 N。

Conclusion: 通过解耦表示学习与后验建模、并以小规模集合训练可泛化的集合编码器，可在不牺牲推断性能的前提下显著降低大规模集合条件下的训练计算开销，为高维、大集合的摊销推断提供了简单且有理论依据的方案。

Abstract: Neural posterior estimation has emerged as a powerful tool for amortized inference, with growing adoption across scientific and applied domains. In many of these applications, the conditioning variable is a set of observations whose elements depend not only on the target but also on unknown factors shared across the set. Optimal inference therefore requires treating the set jointly, which in turn requires training the estimator at the deployment set size -- a regime where memory and compute quickly become prohibitive. We introduce a simple, theoretically grounded strategy that decouples representation learning from posterior modeling. Our method trains a mean-pool Deep Set on sets of size at most two, producing an encoder that generalizes to arbitrary set sizes. The inference head is then finetuned on pre-aggregated embeddings, making training cost essentially independent of the deployment set size N. Across scalar, image, multi-view 3D, molecular, and high-dimensional conditional generation benchmarks with N in the thousands, our approach matches or outperforms standard baselines at a fraction of the compute.

</details>


### [16] [Fast and Precise Learned Charged-Particle Trajectory Regression at the Large Hadron Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.32479)
*Jonathan Renusch,Benjamin Huth,Daniel Murnane,Eleni Xochelli,Doğa Elitez,Paul Gessinger-Befurt,Andreas Stefl,Jeremy Couthures,Andreas Salzburger,Lukas Heinrich,Michael Kagan,Markus Elsing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究将高能物理探测器数据上的带电粒子轨迹参数回归视为序列建模任务，采用双向门控线性循环编码器（bidirectional gated linear recurrent encoder）复现经典轨迹拟合（track fitting）的完整精度，并通过自定义内核（custom kernel）在GPU推理中实现显著吞吐量提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典方法（卡尔曼滤波（Kalman filter）与线性化最小二乘拟合（linearized least-squares fit））虽为稀疏采样线性高斯数据的最优估计器，但其达到10^-5精度需依赖探测器几何、材料、探测效应的细致建模及非均匀磁场中运动方程的精确数值积分，计算成本高。作者旨在探索能否用学习方法在保持同等精度的同时降低大型强子对撞机（Large Hadron Collider, LHC）上模式识别（pattern recognition）的算力开销。

Method: 将带电粒子轨迹参数回归建模为序列建模任务，使用双向门控线性循环编码器进行端到端学习；并借助自定义内核优化GPU推理。

Result: 所提方法复现了经典轨迹拟合技术的完整精度；相比运行在同等价位多核CPU服务器上的经典拟合软件，GPU推理吞吐量显著更高。

Conclusion: 据作者所知，这是首个在达到完整精度的同时提供降低计算成本机会的端到端学习式轨迹拟合（end-to-end learned track fit），其加速可为LHC模式识别带来可观的成本节约。

Abstract: We propose a training recipe that treats charged-particle trajectory parameter regression on high-energy physics detector data as a sequence-modeling task. Kalman filters and linearized least-squares fits have been the classical standard approach for this task: they are optimal estimators for sparsely sampled linear-Gaussian data and are commonly used for trajectory parameter regression (fitting). The classical fitting techniques implemented for this domain reach a final precision of one part in $10^5$ through detailed modeling of detector geometry, material, detection effects and precise numerical integration of the equations of motion through the detector's inhomogeneous magnetic field. With this study, we demonstrate that using a bidirectional gated linear recurrent encoder, one is able to reproduce the full precision of classical track fitting techniques. Using a custom kernel, we also achieve significantly higher throughput during GPU inference, compared to classical fitting software running on similarly priced multi-core CPU servers representing typically employed hardware. Such a speedup would lead to considerable cost savings for the pattern recognition at the Large Hadron Collider. To our knowledge, this is the first end-to-end learned track fit to reach the full precision and, at the same time, offer the opportunity to reduce the computing costs.

</details>


### [17] [Neural Constitutive Learning for Generalized Reaction-Diffusion Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37113)
*Shang-Ke Chen,Yu-Peng Wang,Shih-Hsuan Hung,Wei-Fang Sun,Chao-Shun Zhan,Simon See,Min-Jhe Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出 NCL-MCT 求解器，将广义反应-扩散（reaction-diffusion）PDE 的学习目标定位为 PDE 特异性本构响应（constitutive responses），并保留共享的 MCT 时间积分器；在七个系统上实现 10^{-4} 至 10^{-2} 的相对 rollout L^2 误差，并支持无轨迹的本构学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义反应-扩散系统涵盖多样的输运机制与耦合反应动力学，而神经 PDE 求解器（neural PDE solver）的核心问题是：应当学习什么，才能用统一接口同时容纳相场（phase-field）与退化输运（degenerate transport）、局域反应及多组分耦合。作者据此提出以本构响应为学习目标。

Method: 提出 NCL-MCT 求解器：学习 PDE 特异的本构响应，而时间演化交由共享的 MCT 积分器承担；输运以迁移率（mobility）与热力学驱动力（thermodynamic driving force）表示，反应以相对反应速率（relative reaction rates）表示。本构响应依赖当前密度而非显式依赖初始条件或已流逝时间，因此可跨初始条件与时间尺度复用。同一接口支持速度数据监督与已知定律监督，二者在训练中均无需时间积分；当本构定律已知时，监督可在独立采样的密度场上评估，实现不生成解轨迹的无轨迹本构学习（trajectory-free constitutive learning）。

Result: 在七个系统上，分别训练的本构模块共用相同接口与 MCT 积分器，取得 10^{-4} 至 10^{-2} 的相对 rollout L^2 误差。使用未见过的初始条件族（unseen initial-condition families）与延长时间尺度的测试评估了超出训练条件下的复用性，另有独立实验演示了无轨迹本构学习。

Conclusion: 结果支持将本构响应作为共享神经 PDE 框架的有效学习目标。

Abstract: Generalized reaction-diffusion systems encompass diverse transport mechanisms and coupled reaction kinetics. A central question for neural PDE solvers is what should be learned so that a common interface can accommodate phase-field and degenerate transport, local reactions, and multispecies coupling. We propose the Neural Constitutive Laws--Mass-Compression-Transport (NCL-MCT) Solver, which learns PDE-specific constitutive responses while retaining temporal evolution in a shared MCT integrator. Transport is represented through mobility and thermodynamic driving force, and reaction through relative reaction rates. These constitutive responses depend on the current density rather than explicitly on the initial condition or elapsed time, motivating their reuse across different initial conditions and time horizons. The same interface supports velocity-data supervision and known-law supervision, neither of which requires time integration during training. When constitutive laws are known, supervision can be evaluated on independently sampled density fields, enabling trajectory-free constitutive learning without generating solution trajectories. Across seven systems, separately trained constitutive modules share the same interface and MCT integrator and achieve relative rollout $L^2$ errors of $10^{-4}$ to $10^{-2}$. Tests with unseen initial-condition families and an extended time horizon assess reuse beyond training conditions, while separate experiments demonstrate trajectory-free constitutive learning. These results support constitutive responses as an effective learning target for a shared neural PDE framework.

</details>


### [18] [A Digital Simulation Toolkit for Physics-Based Generation of Realistic Experimental Scanning Tunneling Microscopy Images](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36639)
*Huanhuan Zhao,Laxmi Bhurtel,Connor Vernachio,Fahmy Paiziah,Wonhee Ko,Arpan Biswas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种低成本、物理驱动的数字工具包，通过模拟干净STM图像并叠加物理先验噪声与伪影，生成大量逼真且可用于AI训练的数据，在Cu(111)与Pb(111)图像去噪及电子波模式学习上优于无监督基准方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扫描隧道显微镜（Scanning Tunneling Microscopy, STM）具有极高空间分辨率，但极易受噪声干扰。现有无监督去噪方法训练成本低，却难以恢复关键物理信息；有监督方法性能更优，但受限于难以获得大量成对的干净-含噪实验图像。

Method: 开发物理驱动的数字工具包：先针对选定材料体系模拟干净图像；再依据STM实验中伪影与噪声的物理特征，构造高斯电子噪声（Gaussian electronic noise）、1/f闪烁噪声（1/f flicker noise）、扫描线噪声、背景倾斜与机械漂移等伪影-噪声函数；将其叠加至模拟干净图像以生成逼真STM图像。

Result: 该工具包可为铜（Cu(111)）与铅（Pb(111)）表面去噪生成AI可用（AI-ready）数据，并保留原子、缺陷与电子波等特征；在Cu(111)图像中量子干涉所诱导电子波模式的学习任务上，基于数字生成数据训练的有监督模型在去噪与模式学习方面优于基准无监督方法。

Conclusion: 该物理驱动数字工具包能够低成本生成大规模逼真STM图像数据，支持有监督去噪并有效学习电子波模式，从而促进相关科学发现。

Abstract: Scanning Tunneling Microscopy (STM) is a widely used tool for characterizing surfaces of materials at the atomic scale, playing a crucial role in discoveries across condensed matter physics and materials science. Despite its extreme spatial resolution, STM is one of the most sensitive microscopy techniques and is highly prone to noise. While existing unsupervised denoising methods are very cheap to train, these are primarily focused on removing the noise with minimal recovery of key physical information. While supervised methods can offer superior performance, the major bottleneck is that a large amount of paired clean-noisy experimental images is required which are impractical to obtain. Thus, we developed a low-cost physics-driven digital toolkit to rapidly generate large volume of realistic STM images. Firstly, we simulate clean images from a chosen material system. Then, with prior knowledge of the physical characteristics of the artifacts and noise present in STM experiments, we formulate several artifact-noise functions such as Gaussian electronic noise, 1/f flicker noise, scan-line noise, background tilt and mechanical drift. These physically informed noise components are then added to the simulated clean images to generate realistic STM images. We demonstrated the capability of the proposed digital toolkit to generate AI-ready data for denoising images of the (111) surfaces of copper and lead, while preserving atoms, defects, and electron waves. We also validated the quality of the downstream image analysis of learning electron wave patterns induced by quantum interference from Cu(111) images. Results show that the supervised models trained on digitally generated AI-ready data can more effectively denoise and learn electron wave patterns on Cu(111) images than benchmarked unsupervised approaches, indicating that the proposed toolkit facilitate scientific discovery.

</details>


### [19] [GLASS: Global Latent Aggregation with Slot-based Set Decoding for Scalable All-Atom Crystal Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37158)
*Hendrik Kraß,Seyed Mohamad Moosavi,Mathias Niepert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GLASS 通过将对应关系分配（correspondence assignment）从生成输运（generative transport）中分离，在置换不变（permutation-invariant）的全局潜空间中建模晶体结构分布，实现了对含 150 原子/晶胞的 MOF 的高有效性生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全原子生成（all-atom generation）向金属有机框架（metal-organic frameworks, MOFs）等大体系扩展存在困难，论文将此困难归结为粒子空间生成（particle-space generation）中的对应问题（correspondence problem）：即使目标集合固定，无索引的置换等变粒子流（index-free permutation-equivariant particle flows）在集合规模与密度增大时也需显著更多训练才能可靠生成。

Method: 提出 GLASS（Global Latent Aggregation with Slot-based Set Decoding），在置换不变的全局潜空间（global latent space）中编码结构，并以流匹配（flow matching）学习其分布；由可学习的槽解码器（learned-slot decoder）并行构建全部原子，从而将逐原子对应关系从生成输运中移除。

Result: 在 MP20 上 GLASS 与粒子空间模型相当，且流训练在每种结构尺寸下均可达到训练数据的有效性；在 QMOF 子集上，无需以构建单元、拓扑或组成为条件即可生成每晶胞最多 150 原子的 MOF，结构有效性接近训练数据。两个数据集上均显示有效性—新颖性权衡（validity–novelty tradeoff），MOF 新颖性受自编码器（autoencoder）在可用数据上的泛化能力限制。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将对应关系分配与生成输运分离，为更大原子体系的高有效性生成提供了一条简洁路径。

Abstract: Generative models for crystals enable the discovery of novel structures, but scaling all-atom generation to larger systems such as metal--organic frameworks remains challenging. We connect this difficulty to the correspondence problem of particle-space generation. Even on a single fixed target set, index-free permutation-equivariant particle flows require substantially more training for reliable generation as set size and density increase, under both independent and optimal-transport couplings. To resolve this challenge, we introduce GLASS---Global Latent Aggregation with Slot-based Set Decoding, which encodes structures in a permutation-invariant global latent space and learns their distribution via flow matching. A learned-slot decoder constructs all atoms in parallel, removing atom-wise correspondence from generative transport. On MP20, GLASS is competitive with particle-space models, and flow training can reach the validity of the training data at every structure size. On a QMOF subset, GLASS generates MOFs with up to 150 atoms per unit cell without conditioning on building blocks, topology, or composition, and approaches the structural validity of the training data. On both datasets, flow training exposes a validity--novelty tradeoff, and MOF novelty remains limited by autoencoder generalization on the available data. These results show that separating correspondence assignment from generative transport provides a simple route toward high-validity generation of larger atomistic systems.

</details>


### [20] [Breakdown of Local Denoising as Semantic Speciation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38176)
*Guangkuo Liu,Mert Okyay,Yifan F. Zhang,Fangjun Hu,Rahul Nandkishore,Xun Gao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文从语义信息的空间分布入手，研究生成模型（generative models）中"物种形成窗口（speciation window）"与"非局域窗口（nonlocality window）"两个时间窗口的关系，在"共同原因（common cause）"假设下证明后者必包含于前者，并给出二者随系统规模增大收缩为单一极限时刻（相变，phase transition）的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿模型（frontier models）中观察到这两个时间窗口近乎同时出现，但其理论关联尚不明确，需要解释这种共现（concurrence）现象的成因。

Method: 以语义信息的空间分布为分析视角，提出"共同原因"假设：语义标签（semantic labels）可解释远距离词元（distant tokens）之间相关性的一部分，并在此假设下进行理论证明；同时在高斯混合（Gaussian mixtures）中解析验证所得行为。

Result: 证明在"共同原因"假设下非局域窗口必然落在物种形成窗口之内；并给出两个窗口随系统规模（system size）增大而收缩为单一极限时刻（即相变）的条件，并在高斯混合情形下解析验证。

Conclusion: 结果刻画了语义信息能够解释物种形成与非局域共现的条件，将生成建模（generative modeling）中语义结构涌现的两个互补视角联系起来。

Abstract: The dynamics of generative models exhibit two apparently distinct temporal windows: a speciation window, in which a sample commits to a semantic class, and a nonlocality window, in which local context windows become insufficient for generation. Motivated by evidence of their near-concurrence in a variety of frontier models, we investigate their relationship through the spatial distribution of semantic information. Under a "common cause" hypothesis, we prove that the nonlocality window must lie in the speciation window. This hypothesis postulates that semantic labels explain a fraction of the correlations between distant tokens, a condition that is natural for many real datasets. We further give conditions under which both windows shrink to a single limiting time as system size grows, defining a "phase transition", and verify this behavior analytically in Gaussian mixtures. Together, these results identify conditions under which semantic information explains the concurrence of speciation and nonlocality, connecting two complementary perspectives on the emergence of semantic structure in generative modeling.

</details>

### [21] [Optically-Induced Modulation and Programming of Diamond Photonic Cavities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37163)
*Yuchun Zhu,Amirali Arabmoheghi,Nicolas Le Thomas,Niels Quack,Valentin Goblot,Christophe Galland*

Main category: physics.optics

TL;DR: 该工作利用532 nm绿光照射含氮-空位色心（nitrogen-vacancy centres）的悬浮金刚石法布里-珀罗纳米梁腔（Fabry–Perot nanobeam cavities），实现了红外波段的全光调制与准永久性调谐，为单片金刚石纳米光子平台提供了动态重构手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单晶金刚石（single-crystal diamond）兼具优异光学、热学与力学性质，并能承载可光学寻址与自旋相干色心，是集成量子与非线性光子学的理想平台。然而，器件制备后对单片金刚石光子电路进行动态控制与调谐的实用机制仍然有限，构成本文的研究动机。

Method: 实验采用含氮-空位色心的悬浮金刚石法布里-珀罗纳米梁腔，在532 nm光照下测量其红外（1000–1100 nm）腔响应，并区分两种竞争机制：热光红移（thermo-optic red-shift）与归因于自由载流子等离子体色散效应（free-carrier plasma dispersion effect）的电荷介导蓝移。使用20 kHz调制、功率10 mW的绿光进行全光调制，并考察绿光照射引起的准永久光折变（photo-refractive）共振蓝移。

Result: 在10 mW、20 kHz调制的绿光下，实现约为可用反射对比度45%的红外调制深度。绿光照射还产生准永久性光折变共振蓝移，最大观测调谐量为3.15 nm（0.87 THz），且腔线宽与对比度未见可测退化。若在铌酸锂普克尔斯调制器（lithium-niobate Pockels modulator）中实现同等腔移，需在5 μm电极间隙上施加200–500 V电压。

Conclusion: 结果确立了一种单片金刚石纳米光子平台，同时具备敏捷可逆调制与长寿命光学重构两种关键能力，有望服务于光子技术应用。

Abstract: Single-crystal diamond combines exceptional optical, thermal, and mechanical properties while hosting optically addressable and spin-coherent colour centres, making it a promising platform for integrated quantum and nonlinear photonics. However, practical post-fabrication mechanisms for dynamically controlling and tuning monolithic diamond photonic circuits remain limited. Here, we demonstrate all-optical modulation and long-lived tuning of suspended diamond Fabry--Perot nanobeam cavities containing nitrogen-vacancy centres. Under 532~nm illumination, the infrared (1000 to 1100~nm) cavity response is governed by two competing contributions: a well-understood thermo-optic red-shift, and a novel charge-mediated blue-shift that we attribute to the free-carrier plasma dispersion effect. With 10~mW of 20~kHz-modulated green light, we achieve an infrared modulation depth of approximately 45\% of the available reflection contrast. Green illumination also produces quasi-permanent photo-refractive resonance blue-shifts, with a maximum observed tuning of 3.15~nm (0.87~THz), without measurable degradation of the cavity linewidth or contrast. Achieving the same cavity shift in a lithium-niobate Pockels modulator would require 200 to 500~V across a \(5~μ\mathrm{m}\) electrode gap. These results establish a monolithic diamond nanophotonic platform combining agile and reversible modulation with long-lived optical reconfiguration, two key ingredients in photonic technologies.

</details>

# quant-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [22] [One-dimensional quantum Gibbs states in constant circuit depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35973)
*Saúl Pilatowsky-Cameo,Georgios Styliaris,Ainesh Bakshi,Daniel Malz*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该论文证明任意自旋链（spin chain）的 Gibbs 态在任意恒定温度下都可精确分解为有界长度连续量子比特块上的乘积态（product states）的混合，并据此给出热纠缠结构限制、局域制备方案及量子热化推论，同时将结果推广至一维费米子系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于刻画有限温度下量子多体系统热态的空间纠缠结构，并厘清 Gibbs 态的可制备复杂度及其与量子热化（quantum thermalization）之间的关系。摘要未明确说明更具体的问题背景或已有工作的不足。

Method: 核心方法是构造 Gibbs 态的精确分解：将其表示为定义在连续量子比特块（contiguous blocks）上、长度有界的乘积态的凸混合（mixture）。对费米子系统（fermionic systems），则采用作用于高斯态（Gaussian states）上的常数深度局域费米子线路的混合分解。

Result: 主要结果包括：任意此类 Gibbs 态可由常数深度局域幺正线路（local unitary circuits）配合高效经典预处理制备；热纠缠的纠缠深度（entanglement depth）与纠缠宽度（entanglement width）均被常数界限制，可局域化纠缠（localizable entanglement）在超过某常数阈值距离后精确消失；对于具非简并谱隙（nondegenerate spectral gaps）的平移不变自旋链哈密顿量，来自最大熵系综的典型低复杂度纯态在幺正演化后与 Gibbs 态局域不可区分。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Riccati 方程在控制与量子化学等领域广泛应用，但其解矩阵的高效量子制备尚缺乏统一处理框架。该工作旨在为多类 Riccati 问题提供统一的量子算法，并刻画其查询复杂度的最优性与量子计算难度。

Abstract: We develop quantum algorithms that construct block-encodings of solution matrices for continuous- and discrete-time algebraic Riccati equations (CAREs and DAREs), differential Riccati equations (DREs), and finite Riccati recursions. The Quantum Weighted Riesz Method, unifying four kinds of Riccati problems, constructs projectors onto the solution graphs using weighted Riesz branching operators and recovers the solution matrices. Under the stated access and normalization assumptions, our algorithms for CAREs and DAREs have near-optimal query complexity $Θ(\mathcal Rα)$ up to logarithmic factors, with the generalized singularity factor $\mathcal R$ that reflects spectral separation, and the output normalization $α$. For DREs and finite-horizon problems, the query complexity has an additional initialization conditioning factor $κ_{\rm init}$. We establish product query lower bounds for specified input-oracle families, demonstrating necessary joint dependence on spectral separation and initialization or output scale. We also prove that selected-value promise problems are BQP-complete on explicit circuit-generated families of single-control discrete-time algebraic and zero-terminal differential Riccati equations.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在一致历史（consistent histories）表述中，有限模型里干涉项通常不严格为零，其“小”本身并不提供历史空间上的渐近几何。因而需要在记录不断累积的情形下，构造能够刻画历史间渐近分离的几何结构。

Conclusion: 该度量的取值称为“一致性速率（consistency rate）”，具有三种等价解释：扇区分离度量、归一化干涉负对数的多项式增长指数，以及由 Helstrom 公式给出的区分累积记录的最小错误概率负对数指数。经典分离剖面决定该速率：长时间平均缺陷为正给出最大值 $\delta=1$，而边界剖面 $d_m^2\asymp m^{-1}$ 在零多项式速率下仍产生不同的超选择扇区，即 $\delta=0$。

Abstract: The consistent histories formulation of quantum mechanics assigns classical probabilities to sequences of coarse-grained events when the corresponding interference terms vanish. In finite models these terms generically need not vanish exactly, and their smallness alone supplies no asymptotic geometry on the space of histories. We construct such a geometry for histories that accumulate quantum records. The convergence exponent of the Bures defect defines a pseudo-ultrametric on histories. After quotienting its zero distance degeneracy, it becomes a complete ultrametric, requiring neither compactness of the outcome space nor continuity of the record family, and coincides with the gauge invariant sector metric $\tilde d$ introduced in \cite{LesTP}. Classical separation profiles determine the rate: positive long time mean defect gives the maximal value $δ=1$, while the borderline profile $d_m^2\asymp m^{-1}$ produces distinct superselection sectors at zero polynomial rate, $δ=0$.

Abstract: The out-of-time-order correlator (OTOC) has emerged as an important diagnostic for quantum information scrambling and a key signature of quantum chaos. In this work, we discuss out-of-time-order correlators, analytically and numerically, for the so-called Volcano potentials. This family of potentials parametrically interpolates between bounded wells and confining walls, and includes Pöschl-Teller as a specific case. Through a combination of numerical diagonalization and semiclassical analysis, we investigate various regimes of the potential parameter regimes containing regions of both positive and negative curvatures. Moreover, we demonstrate in this case that short-time exponential growth of the OTOC does not necessarily signal global quantum chaos; instead, it correlates with the sampling of negative curvature regions by excited states.

### [29] [Dynamical resource theory of time-reversal symmetry breaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36408)
*Jisho Miyazaki,Kohdai Kuroiwa,Mio Murao*

Main category: quant-ph

TL;DR: 该文提出一个具有操作意义的框架，用于量化量子信道（quantum channels）的内在时间反演破缺（T-violation），并将其纳入动力学资源理论（dynamical resource theory）中形式化为一种资源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间反演对称性及其破缺在从粒子物理到涨落定理（fluctuation theorems）与互易性（reciprocity）等多个物理领域均具基础性地位。孤立系统中的时间反演对称性由哈密顿量（Hamiltonian）直接决定，但在含噪声的量子过程中，如何量化T-violation的幅度长期存在争议。

Method: 将操作定义的（operationally defined）时间反演变换整合进动力学资源理论，把T-violation刻画为一种资源；并将T-violation分解为两个分量：源于反幺正运动反转（antiunitary motion-reversal）的运动学T-violation（kinematic T-violation），以及纯由热力学驱动的非幺性（nonunitality）。随后分析运动学T-violation所对应的资源理论。

Conclusion: 研究为量子信道内在T-violation提供了可操作且具有资源理论意义的量化框架，区分了运动学与热力学来源，并揭示了运动学T-violation资源结构的独特性质及普适黄金单元的存在。

Abstract: Time-reversal symmetry and its violation (T-violation) are fundamental across diverse physics domains, from particle physics to fluctuation theorems and reciprocity. While time-reversal symmetry for isolated systems is simply determined by the Hamiltonian, quantifying the magnitude of T-violation, especially in noisy quantum processes, has been a subject of ongoing debate. Here, we propose an operationally meaningful framework to quantify the intrinsic T-violation of quantum channels. We integrate operationally defined time-reversal transformations into dynamical resource theory to formulate T-violation as a resource. T-violation decomposes into two distinct components: kinematic T-violation originating from antiunitary motion-reversal, and nonunitality driven purely by thermodynamics. This decomposition resolves the longstanding confusion between broken time-reversal symmetry and non-invertibility. Abstract: Monolithic integration of single-photon sources into silicon photonics offers a compelling solution for realizing scalable quantum telecommunication architectures. Among the candidate silicon color centers, the C-center features a zero-phonon emission in the telecom L-band, corresponding to the spectral window of minimum transmission loss in optical fibers. Despite this advantage, isolating single C-centers has remained an unrealized challenge in solid-state quantum optics. In this work, we report the isolation of a single silicon C-center. We enhance the spontaneous emission rate of the zero-phonon line by coupling the single emitter to a nanophotonic crystal cavity, observing a fivefold reduction in the excited-state lifetime compared to bulk conditions, which corresponds to a Purcell factor lower bound of 33. Second-order autocorrelation measurements confirm single-photon emission from the investigated defect, obtaining an optical purity of $g^{(2)}(0) = 0.23$ without background correction. Our results establish a native telecom L-band quantum light source in silicon, laying the foundation for ultra-low-loss and long-distance quantum applications.

Abstract: We introduce a local and distributed framework for decoding quantum LDPC codes. We establish the framework at two complementary levels. First, we show that any code exhibiting a threshold under iterative BP decoding can be promoted to an autonomous local measurement-and-feedback dynamics while preserving that threshold. We then turn to codes for which naive BP does not itself exhibit threshold behavior. Our key observation is that BP can nevertheless provide sufficiently accurate \emph{local} marginal information: active syndrome defects use these local beliefs to \emph{move}, pair, and annihilate through asynchronous feedback. We demonstrate numerically that this construction exhibits threshold behavior in the point-like sectors of the two- and three-dimensional toric codes, where conventional BP decoding fails. Exploiting the graph-generality of BP, we then study the inference dynamics on the membrane-like sector of the three-dimensional toric code and in a family of bivariate-bicycle quantum LDPC codes.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究通常认为离散标度不变性源自共振少体动力学（resonant few-body dynamics）或奇异标度不变动力学（singular scale-invariant dynamics），缺乏在有序多体系统中由内部运动自发产生的微观机制。本文旨在证明该现象可在有序量子磁体的复合激发内部运动中出现，从而拓展其物理起源。

Method: 研究对象为边际 $1/r^4$ 横场 Ising 链，其中长程键（long-range bonds）在缀饰畴壁（dressed domain walls）之间产生渐近平方反比吸引（asymptotic inverse-square attraction），其强度由自发磁化强度固定；缀饰双扭结阈值曲率（dressed two-kink threshold curvature）设定相对动力学标度。验证手段包括全自旋精确对角化（full-spin exact diagonalization）与四阶弱场哈密顿量（fourth-order weak-field Hamiltonian）。

Result: 两个可独立获取的量（吸引强度与阈值曲率）共同决定标度反常指数，进而决定介子层级。在超临界区，同一指数同时支配几何束缚能比与尺寸比、对数能级累积（logarithmic level accumulation）以及对数周期阈值散射（log-periodic threshold scattering）。精确对角化与四阶弱场哈密顿量结果支持在核效应与环效应之间的有限窗口内存在耦合的能量—尺寸标度（energy–size scaling）。

Conclusion: 本文提供了量子极限环物理（quantum limit-cycle physics）的微观多体实现，其普适标度（universal scaling）由磁序（magnetic order）与扭结迁移率（kink mobility）共同固定。

Abstract: Discrete scale invariance often arises from resonant few-body or singular scale-invariant dynamics. We show that it can instead emerge in the internal motion of a composite excitation in an ordered quantum magnet. In the marginal $1/r^4$ transverse-field Ising chain, long-range bonds generate an asymptotic inverse-square attraction between dressed domain walls, with strength fixed by the spontaneous magnetization. The dressed two-kink threshold curvature sets the relative kinetic scale. Together, these independently accessible quantities determine the scale-anomaly exponent and hence the meson hierarchy. In the supercritical regime, the same exponent governs geometric binding-energy and size ratios, logarithmic level accumulation, and log-periodic threshold scattering. Full-spin exact diagonalization and a fourth-order weak-field Hamiltonian support the coupled energy--size scaling in the finite window between core and ring effects. This provides a microscopic many-body realization of quantum limit-cycle physics, with its universal scaling fixed by magnetic order and kink mobility.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Conclusion: 共享无序景观的耦合副本可在正温度下发生全局锁定，该锁定由一阶相变主导且不经过连续增长的关联长度；锁定目标取决于模型（REM 为最低能量态，GREM 为最低能量态或最低自由能谷）。

Abstract: When a system of many interacting constituents is extended across space, every region carries the same disordered landscape, and it is unclear whether distant regions settle into the same state. We study copies of the Random Energy Model (REM), sharing one disorder realization, coupled along a chain. A first-order transition separates a phase with short-range correlations, from a locked phase in which the entire chain occupies the lowest-energy state with probability one, even at positive temperature. No intermediate ordering lengths occur. In the Generalized REM the chain locks either to the lowest-energy state or to the lowest free-energy valley.

</details>

### [42] [Vertical and reentrant ferromagnetic boundaries below the Nishimori point](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37479)
*Yan Ru Pei*

Main category: math.PR

TL;DR: 该工作证明在多重临界（multicritical，即 Nishimori）点以下，偏置伊辛自旋玻璃（biased Ising spin glass）的铁磁边界既可为垂直，也可为再入型（reentrant），即固定无序下降温会破坏有序。垂直情形见于带 Curie-Weiss 偏置的 SK 模型；再入情形见于全连通 p-spin 玻璃的三相点附近及一种修饰平面点阵。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Nishimori（1986）曾论证偏置自旋玻璃的铁磁边界在 Nishimori 点以下应严格垂直，本文旨在检验该论断是否普遍成立。结果指出边界除垂直外还可呈再入型，从而修正了原有预期。

Method: 对 SK 模型（Curie-Weiss 偏置 J0）分析磁化随偏置与温度的行为；对全连通 p-spin 玻璃（p≥3）在三相点偏置附近求磁化；对装饰性平面 Z^2 周期点阵（每条键替换为固定串并联小装置，最大度数四，iid ±J 耦合）考察 Gibbs 态唯一性窗口。并证明零场磁化率等于零场 Parisi PDE 解在原点的曲率，给出 Parisi 测度支撑集在原点累积定理的简短替代证明；p-spin 与点阵结果及替代证明均借助计算机辅助完成。

Result: SK 模型中对于任意 T&lt;J，磁化在 J0≤J 时消失（在 J0=J 处连续趋于零），在 J0&gt;J 时为宏观量；边界垂直由 Dey 与 Kang 观察到的零场磁化率加上规范不等式（gauge inequality）给出的自由能增益包络界决定。p-spin 模型中，对每个整数 p≥3，当偏置略大于三相点偏置时，Nishimori 温度处磁化为宏观量，并在某个显式较低温度处消失（即 Nishimori 近期复本预测的两温形式）。点阵模型中，高温下 Gibbs 态几乎必然唯一，在包含 Nishimori 温度的窗口内非唯一，低温下再次唯一。

Conclusion: Nishimori 点以下铁磁边界不必垂直，可为垂直亦可再入，故固定无序下降温可在一定参数区破坏有序。上述模型仅作为 Z^2 上最近邻 ±J 模型的代理，不对 Z^d 作任何断言。

Abstract: We prove that below the multicritical (Nishimori) point the ferromagnetic boundary of a biased Ising spin glass, argued by Nishimori (1986) to be exactly vertical, can be vertical and can also be reentrant, so that cooling at fixed disorder destroys order. It is vertical in the Sherrington-Kirkpatrick (SK) model with a Curie-Weiss bias $J_0$, and reentrant near the triple point of fully connected $p$-spin glasses and, at one disorder strength, on a decorated planar $\mathbb{Z}^2$-periodic lattice. For every $T<J$ the SK magnetization vanishes for $J_0\le J$, continuously at $J_0=J$, and is macroscopic for $J_0>J$. For every integer $p\ge3$, at each bias slightly larger than the triple-point bias, the $p$-spin magnetization is macroscopic at the Nishimori temperature and vanishes at an explicit lower temperature; this is Nishimori's recent replica prediction in two-temperature form. On the lattice ($\mathbb{Z}^2$ with each bond replaced by a fixed series-parallel gadget; maximum degree four), with iid $\pm J$ couplings at $\mathbb{P}(J_e=-1)=9/10000$, the Gibbs state is almost surely unique at high temperature, non-unique in a window containing the Nishimori temperature, and unique again at low temperature. As Dey and Kang observed, the SK boundary is set by the zero-field susceptibility together with an envelope bound on the free-energy gain in a field, which follows from the gauge inequality. We prove that this susceptibility is the curvature at the origin of the zero-field Parisi PDE solution, which Lopatto evaluated for every $β>1$ via his theorem that the support of the Parisi measure accumulates at the origin; we give a short alternative proof of that theorem. The $p$-spin and lattice results and the alternative proof are computer-assisted. The models are proxies for the nearest-neighbour $\pm J$ model on $\mathbb{Z}^2$ and say nothing about $\mathbb{Z}^d$.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称排他过程这类相互作用粒子系统的流体动力学极限（hydrodynamic limit）与涨落行为是统计力学（statistical mechanics）与概率论的核心问题。本文旨在刻画在非齐次情形下、由非线性抛物型方程支配的经验测度（empirical measure）其涨落场的极限规律。

Method: 在离散环面 $T^d_n$（$d\le 3$）上考察梯度、变速、对称排他过程；要求在扩散尺度下经验测度依非线性抛物型方程演化。证明手法基于相对熵方法，假设初始状态相对初始剖面（profile）对应的非齐次乘积测度（inhomogeneous product measure）的相对熵为 $o(n^{d/2})$，且密度涨落场已收敛。

Result: 在上述假设下，密度涨落场在空间 $L^1(0,T; H_{-r})$ 中收敛，其极限为带时变系数（time-dependent coefficients）、由守恒型时空白噪声（conservative space-time white noise）驱动的线性随机偏微分方程之解。

Conclusion: 结果表明，非齐次初态下的涨落场遵循线性随机偏微分方程（SPDE）而非非线性方程，体现了涨落层级（fluctuation level）上正交性/线性化的普遍特征；该结论以相对熵与小维数（$d\le 3$）条件为前提，为相关流体动力学涨落理论提供了推广。

Abstract: We consider a gradient, speed-change, symmetric exclusion process on the discrete torus $T^d_n$, $d\le 3$, whose empirical measure evolves, on the diffusive scale, according to a non-linear parabolic equation. We prove that, starting from a sequence of initial states whose relative entropy with respect to the inhomogeneous product measure associated with the initial profile is $o(n^{d/2})$, and whose density fluctuation field converges, the density fluctuation field converges, in $L^1(0,T; H_{- r})$, to the solution of a linear stochastic partial differential equation with time-dependent coefficients driven by a conservative space-time white noise.

</details>

### [44] [Infrared Subtraction with Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36007)
*Wenjie He,Xiaohui Liu,Yandong Liu,Zhan Wang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种由人工智能（AI）辅助发展的局域红外减除（local infrared subtraction）框架，基于投影到 Born 运动学与有效场论（EFT）匹配，将辐射项分解为可积部分与 Born 接触项（Born contact），并在电子-正电子湮灭的 3、4 喷注 NLO 及双喷注 NNLO 尝试中验证了其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高阶微扰计算依赖红外减除方法以处理软/共线发散，而传统切片方法依赖切片参数且计算复杂，因此需要发展无需切片参数的局域减除框架。该研究动机在于利用 AI（特别是大语言模型）在人类物理指导下构建并实现此类减除方案。

Method: 框架将辐射项分离为可积辐射项与 Born 运动学处的有限接触项，接触项由分辨观测量（如 N-jettiness τ_N）中的 EFT 奇异分布确定。在人类物理指导下由 LLM 发展出两套实现：其一用神经网络进行相空间投影并拟合接触项；其二采用解析构造，保持 Born 动量固定并对辐射积分，结合 EFT δ(τ_N) 系数与有限四维辐射积分直接计算接触项。

Result: 在电子-正电子湮灭中重建了无质量 3、4 喷注产生的完整 NLO 修正；通过递归使用 NLO 的 P2B 构造并借助 LLM 设计机器学习控制以降低接触积分方差，进行了 NNLO 双喷注产生的尝试。测试预测与 EERAD3 吻合良好。计算与投影网络训练在 2020 款 Apple M1 MacBook 上完成，无 GPU 加速，表明在有限算力下亦可实现。

Conclusion: 结果表明 AI 可通过构造红外减除并改进其数值积分来协助高阶计算。附录进一步将局域减除推广至 3 喷注 NNLO，给出显式辐射映射与接触公式提案，并展示在保持 Born 动量固定下对任意数量无质量末态喷注的 NNLO 辐射积分方法。

Abstract: We present AI-developed local infrared subtraction, building on projection to Born and EFT matching. The framework separates an integrable radiation term from a finite contribution at Born kinematics, referred to as the Born contact. The contact is determined using the EFT singular distribution in a resolution observable such as N-jettiness $τ_N$. Under human physics guidance, an LLM develops two implementations. One uses a neural network for phase space projection and fits the contact by matching to EFT cumulants. The other uses an analytic construction that keeps the Born momenta fixed while integrating over radiation. It combines the EFT $δ(τ_N)$ coefficient with finite 4-dimensional radiation integrals to calculate the contact term directly. This gives a local subtraction formula without a slicing parameter, while reusing existing lower-order radiation calculations and EFT singular predictions. As a demonstration, we reconstruct the full NLO correction for massless 3- and 4-jet production in electron-positron annihilation. The attempt to the NNLO dijet production is also made by recursively using the NLO P2B construction with the LLM designing machine-learning controls to reduce the variance of the contact integral. The tested predictions are in good agreement with EERAD3. The numerical calculation and projection-network training use a 2020 Apple M1 MacBook, without GPU acceleration, illustrating the feasibility of the construction with modest computing resources. The appendices develop an extension of the local subtraction to 3-jet NNLO, giving explicit radiation maps and a proposed contact formula. We also show how to integrate over NNLO radiation while keeping the Born momenta fixed, for any number of massless final-state jets. Our results demonstrate how AI can help higher-order calculations by constructing infrared subtraction and improving its numerical integration.

</details>

*Subhajit Kala,Soumitra Nandi,Sonakshi Saha*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 在标准模型有效场论（SMEFT）框架下，结合轻子与强子电偶极矩（EDM）测量，系统约束CP破坏的顶夸克相互作用，并指出未来质子EDM搜索的互补性潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电偶极矩（electric dipole moments, EDM）是探测超出标准模型（beyond the Standard Model）CP破坏相互作用最灵敏的探针之一。研究旨在利用现有及预期EDM测量，约束SMEFT中CP破坏的顶夸克相互作用。

Method: 在标准模型有效场论（Standard Model Effective Field Theory, SMEFT）框架内，纳入完整效应链：单圈与双圈匹配（one- and two-loop matching）、Barr-Zee贡献、Weinberg算符与重夸克阈值修正，以及重整化群（renormalization-group）诱导的算符混合。

Result: 构建了满足正则微分方程的基积分，并给出处理嵌套平方根情形的策略；在纳入具有交叉无质量腿（crossed massless legs）的积分族后，将 $\varepsilon$ 展开后的积分表示为代数独立函数基，并通过微分方程数值积分完成评估。

Conclusion: 所提策略可统一处理平面与非平面两圈积分族，包括含嵌套平方根的情形，为希格斯玻色子产生中非因子化QCD修正的积分求解提供了可行的解析与数值框架。

Abstract: We consider a planar and a non-planar two-loop Feynman integral family arising in non-factorisable quantum chromodynamics corrections to Higgs-boson production via vector boson fusion. We construct basis integrals which fulfil canonical differential equations with respect to seven variables for the external kinematics and an internal mass. One of the integral families involves nested square roots. We present strategies for deriving canonical master integrals, $\mathrm{d}\log$ forms and analytic continuation for such cases. Including also families with crossed massless legs, we express the $\varepsilon$-expanded integrals in terms of an algebraically independent function basis. For their evaluation, we employ numerical integration of the differential equations.

*Pedro J. Llanos*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本研究提出 ECLIPSE-X 架构，将单一的相对论参数 gamma 与逐帧导航状态联合估计，用于评估近太阳天体测量中该联合解及其形式协方差在真实模型误差下是否保持统计有效性。结果显示，未增广估计量对板比例失配极为敏感，须引入板比例参数才能恢复协方差一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近太阳精密恒星天体测量可通过引力光线偏折约束参数化后牛顿（parametrized post-Newtonian, PPN）参数 gamma。本研究的动机并非竞争性的 gamma 测定，而是检验此类联合解及其形式协方差（formal covariance）在现实天体测量模型误差下是否仍统计有效。

Method: 构建 ECLIPSE-X 局部多帧估计架构，利用空间特征差异与 gamma 跨帧的持续性，将单一持久相对论参数与逐帧航天器视线、滚转状态分离。采用 250 颗星（1.22-8 太阳视半径）、40 帧、5 秒采样，通过加权最小二乘（weighted least squares）估计，并用 Schur 补边缘化（Schur-complement marginalization）给出导航耦合后的 gamma 形式不确定度。

Result: 名义解恢复 gamma = 1.0001198167，sigma_gamma = 3.075104e-4，1000 次实现集合确认标定。对估计量未知的扰动显示板比例失配为主导失效机制：仅 300-ppm 比例误差即令经验/形式不确定度比升至 7126.6 且 1-sigma、2-sigma 覆盖为零，而星表坐标失配为 2.18、帧相干径向扰动为 1.12；所有实现均数值收敛，说明收敛不保证统计有效性。增广板比例参数可恢复一致性，gamma 精度固定代价为 21.2%。

Conclusion: 标定扫描表明，未增广估计量仅在 sigma_p = 2.7884e-8 以下保持协方差一致，故对近太阳相对论天体测量估计量而言，增广状态是必要条件而非改进项。

Abstract: Precision stellar astrometry near the Sun can constrain the parametrized post-Newtonian (PPN) parameter gamma through gravitational light deflection. This study develops ECLIPSE-X, a localized multi-frame estimation architecture in which one persistent relativistic parameter is estimated simultaneously with frame-dependent spacecraft line-of-sight and roll states, separated by their differing spatial signatures and by the persistence of gamma across frames. The aim is not a competitive determination of gamma, but an assessment of whether such a joint solution and its formal covariance stay statistically valid under realistic astrometric model error. A synthetic field of 250 stars spanning 1.22-8 apparent solar radii is observed over 40 frames at 5-s cadence; weighted least squares estimates gamma with three navigation states per frame, and Schur-complement marginalization gives the formal uncertainty in gamma after navigation coupling. The nominal solution recovers gamma = 1.0001198167 with sigma_gamma = 3.075104e-4, and a 1000-realization ensemble confirms calibration. Perturbations unknown to the estimator identify plate-scale mismatch as the dominant failure mechanism: a 300-ppm scale error alone inflates the empirical-to-formal uncertainty ratio to 7126.6 with zero 1-sigma and 2-sigma coverage, against 2.18 for catalog-coordinate mismatch and 1.12 for a frame-coherent radial perturbation, while every realization converges numerically: convergence does not guarantee statistical validity. Augmenting the state with a plate-scale parameter restores consistency, at a fixed 21.2% cost in the precision of gamma set by the correlation between the two persistent parameters. A calibration sweep shows the unaugmented estimator is covariance-consistent only below sigma_p = 2.7884e-8, so the augmented state is a requirement, not a refinement, for near-Sun relativistic astrometric estimators.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 研究对象为延迟一维 swarmalator 模型（delayed one-dimensional swarmalator model），设定所有单元共享同一固有频率 ω；通过分析模型在延迟作用下的序参量（order parameter）与稳定性分支来刻画动力学行为。

Conclusion: 该模型构成延迟 Kuramoto 模型的两序参量类似物；延迟通过选择性稳定窗口导致多分支共存，并可在特定参数（K=-J 附近）实现三种规范态同时稳定，而在无稳定分支区域序参量表现为持续振荡。

Abstract: We study the delayed one-dimensional swarmalator model when all units share a common intrinsic frequency ω. Unlike the zero-frequency case, ω cannot be removed by a rotating-frame transformation because delayed interactions retain the phase accumulated over the lag. The result is a two-order-parameter analogue of the delayed Kuramoto model: the asynchronous state acquires incoherence lobes, the synchronized state becomes a rotating branch stable even when both the spatial and phase couplings are repulsive, and the phase wave develops narrow resonant stability bands. These delay-selected windows overlap to produce broad branch coexistence, including a narrow window near K=-J where all three canonical states-asynchronous, phase wave, and synchronized-are simultaneously stable. In regions where no canonical branch is stable, the order parameters oscillate persistently.

</details>

### [57] [Solver Agent: an Agentic AI Framework for Theoretical Physics Computations Applied to F-theory Uplifts of O3-planes and S-folds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35958)
*Eliott Morgensztern,Cesar Fierro Cota,Alessandro Mininno*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 论文提出基于大语言模型（large language models, LLMs）的 Solver Agent 框架，通过持久化账本（persistent ledger）与专用子代理（sub-agents）、独立验证代理协作，提升数学与理论物理中计算与证明的可追溯性、可复现性与可验证性；并以此研究 Type IIB orientifold 的全局 F-theory 提升及其 S-fold 推广，给出 Weierstrass 模型在射影三维簇上产生特定奇点的 Calabi-Yau 四维流形的充分条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算机辅助计算与证明在数学和理论物理中日益重要，但其步骤追踪、可复现性与独立验证仍存在不足。论文旨在通过 AI 框架改善这一现状，并将其应用于弦理论（string theory）中 F-theory 提升与离散规范对称性等复杂几何问题。

Method: 构建 Solver Agent：以持久化账本记录假设、推导与计算；由中心代理（central agent）向专用子代理分派任务，并由独立代理验证中间步骤与最终结果。在物理侧，研究 Type IIB orientifold 的全局 F-theory 提升及 S-fold 推广，分析带终末 ℤ_k 商奇点（terminal ℤ_k quotient singularities, k∈{2,3,4,6}）的射影三维簇上的 Weierstrass 模型，并结合弦论不变量（stringy invariants）与环面超曲面（toric hypersurface）构造。

Result: 建立了使 Weierstrass 模型给出具有孤立 Gorenstein 终末商奇点的 ℚ-阶乘射影椭圆纤维化 Calabi-Yau 四维流形的充分条件；这些几何实现 O3-平面（O3-planes）与 S-folds，其局部 D3-膜探针给出四维 𝒩=3 超共形场论（superconformal field theories）。由弦论不变量导出 Hodge 数据与 Euler 示性数的固定点贡献，并表明这些 Euler 修正决定 tadpole cancellation 所需的局域 D3-膜电荷；在环面构造中，单一三维多胞体同时决定 Type IIB Calabi-Yau 三维流形与 F-theory 底空间，orientifold 双覆盖构成具离散 ℤ_2 规范对称性的另一 genus-one 纤维化提升的双截面（bisection）。

Conclusion: Solver Agent 通过账本化流程与多层验证机制提升了计算机辅助计算的可追溯性、可复现性与可验证性，并在 F-theory 提升、S-fold 与离散规范对称性等几何问题中展示了有效性。论文进一步提供了四维 𝒩=1 紧致化（compactifications）中含非阿贝尔规范扇区（non-abelian gauge sectors）的环面计算与四形式通量（four-form flux）分析方法。

Abstract: We introduce Solver Agent, an AI framework based on large language models for calculations and proofs in mathematics and theoretical physics. The solution process is tracked through a persistent ledger that records assumptions, derivations, and computations. A central agent delegates tasks to specialized sub-agents, while independent agents verify both intermediate steps and the final result. This setup improves the traceability, reproducibility, and verification of computer-assisted calculations. Applying Solver Agent, we study global F-theory uplifts of Type IIB orientifolds and their S-fold generalizations. We establish sufficient conditions for Weierstrass models over projective threefolds with terminal $\mathbb{Z}_k$ quotient singularities ($k\in\{2,3,4,6\}$) to give $\mathbb{Q}$-factorial projective elliptically fibered Calabi-Yau fourfolds with isolated Gorenstein terminal quotient singularities. These geometries realize O3-planes and S-folds, where local D3-brane probes of the latter yield four-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=3$ superconformal field theories. Using stringy invariants, we derive fixed-point contributions to Hodge data and Euler characteristics, and show that these Euler corrections determine the localized D3-brane charges required for tadpole cancellation. We illustrate these results using toric hypersurface constructions, where a single three-dimensional polytope determines both the Type IIB Calabi-Yau threefold and the F-theory base; here, the orientifold double cover naturally forms a bisection of an alternative genus-one-fibered uplift with discrete $\mathbb{Z}_2$ gauge symmetry. Finally, we provide methods for toric computations and four-form flux analysis in four-dimensional $\mathcal{N}=1$ compactifications with non-abelian gauge sectors.

</details>

Motivation: 研究动机在于判定所构造的复化黑洞解是否构成引力路径积分（gravitational path integral）的可容许超对称鞍点（admissible supersymmetric saddles），并厘清引力侧约束与对偶指标收敛条件之间的关系。

Method: 方法为构造具实电荷（real charges）与多个复视界（multiple complex horizons）的超对称非极端复化解，并对其施加 Kontsevich-Segal-Witten (KSW) 判据，进而将所得引力约束与对偶超共形指标（dual superconformal index）的收敛条件进行对比。

Conclusion: 渐近 KSW 条件与指标收敛部分吻合，但整体体条件更具限制性，说明引力路径积分可容许性与对偶指标收敛并非完全等价，二者在化学势参数空间中的覆盖范围存在差异。

Abstract: We present a new supersymmetric, non-extremal complexification of asymptotically $\mathrm{AdS}$ black holes, characterized by real charges and multiple complex horizons. We investigate whether these solutions define admissible supersymmetric saddles of the gravitational path integral by applying the Kontsevich-Segal-Witten (KSW) criterion, and compare the resulting gravitational constraints with the convergence conditions of the dual superconformal index. For most of the cases studied, the KSW criterion in the asymptotic region agrees with the index convergence, while the full bulk condition is more stringent and excludes saddles whose chemical potentials lie within the index-convergence domain.

</details>

*Eric D'Hoker,Michael Gutperle,Christoph F. Uhlemann*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该工作为不变于复李超代数 $F(4)$ 的复化 IIB 型超引力局域解补充通量势与场强构造，并在 IIB$^\star$ 实形式下给出 $dS_{1,5}\times S^2\times\Sigma$ 解及其解析延拓条件，进而构造显式整体解并与极化 IKKT 模型的全息描述提案对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作（arXiv:2508.14959）已导出并分类不变于复李超代数 $F(4)$ 的复化 IIB 型超引力（complexified Type IIB supergravity）一般局域解，但缺少相应的通量势（flux potentials）与场强（field strengths）构造，且其物理实现（实形式、整体解与全息诠释）尚未明确。

Method: 首先构造上述局域解的通量势与场强；随后聚焦复化 IIB 型超引力的 IIB$^\star$ 实形式（real form），获得时空度规为翘曲乘积（warped product）形式 $dS_{1,5}\times S^2\times\Sigma$ 的解；系统推导边界与正则性条件（boundary and regularity conditions），使该类解可解析延拓（analytically continued）为欧几里得 IIB 型超引力中正则的 $S^6\times S^2\times\Sigma$ 解；据此构造显式整体解（global solutions）。

Result: 得到 IIB$^\star$ 实形式下 $dS_{1,5}\times S^2\times\Sigma$ 形式的解及相应的通量势与场强；给出可实现向欧氏正则 $S^6\times S^2\times\Sigma$ 解解析延拓的边界与正则性条件；构造出显式整体解，其中包含对极化 IKKT 模型（polarized IKKT model）全息描述近期提案的显式实现，并厘清了这些解所携带的荷（charges）；此外在同一正则类中获得具有不同膜荷（brane charges）的新解。

Conclusion: 该研究把 $F(4)$ 不变复化 IIB 型超引力的局域解推进为含通量势/场强的完整表述，并通过 IIB$^\star$ 实形式与解析延拓构造出正则整体解，从而为极化 IKKT 模型的全息描述提供显式几何实现，同时揭示同一正则类中荷不同的新解。

Abstract: The general local solutions to complexified Type IIB supergravity which are invariant under the complex Lie superalgebra $F(4)$, and which were derived and classified in arXiv:2508.14959, are supplemented here by the construction of their flux potentials and field strengths. We then focus on the Type IIB$^\star$ real form of complex Type IIB supergravity, where we obtain solutions with spacetime metric of the warped product form $dS_{1,5}\times S^2\timesΣ$. We systematically derive boundary and regularity conditions under which the $dS_{1,5}\times S^2\timesΣ$ solutions can be analytically continued to regular $S^6\times S^2\timesΣ$ solutions in Euclidean Type IIB supergravity. Using these results, we construct explicit global solutions. These solutions include explicit realizations of recent proposals for a holographic description of the polarized IKKT model, and we clarify the charges carried by these solutions. We also obtain novel solutions in the same regularity class with different brane charges.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非极端（nonextremal）高自旋波函数在大 $z$ 下出现的额外增长，威胁到高自旋振幅的可构造性（constructibility），需要澄清其真正控制参数是总自旋还是螺旋度。

Method: 将四维大质量动量及其质量嵌入一个辅助零五维矢量（auxiliary null five-vector），用其第五流分量刻画无质量 Ward 恒等式（massless Ward identities）的破缺与恢复，并施加统一 Ward 恒等式，使其独立作用于每个矢量指标。

Result: 统一 Ward 恒等式消除了非极端高自旋波函数的表观额外 $z$ 增长，确定了固定螺旋度（fixed-helicity）的大 $z$ 标度行为，并给出任意自旋下有效的可构造性判据。

Conclusion: 在剥离后的运动学张量（stripped kinematic tensor）于无质量极限下保持有限的前提下，增大总自旋本身不会恶化可构造性；对于五点和更高点（five points and beyond），非负耦合量纲（nonnegative coupling dimension）的振幅在所有螺旋度组态下均可构造。

Abstract: We show that the large-$z$ behavior of massive higher-spin particles under all-line transverse shifts is controlled by the helicity magnitude rather than the total spin. The apparent additional $z$ growth of nonextremal higher-spin wavefunctions is removed by unified Ward identities acting independently on each vector index. To expose this structure, we embed a four-dimensional massive momentum and its mass into an auxiliary null five-vector, whose fifth-current components characterize the breaking and restoration of the massless Ward identities. These identities determine the fixed-helicity large-$z$ scaling and yield a helicity-resolved constructibility criterion valid for arbitrary spin. Provided the stripped kinematic tensor remains finite in the massless limit, increasing the total spin does not by itself worsen constructibility, and amplitudes with nonnegative coupling dimension are constructible for all helicity configurations at five points and beyond.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Motivation: 量子引力中不存在精确整体对称性是被广泛接受的观点，而全息等场景中出现的系综平均可能使整体对称性重新涌现，这一张力需要系统澄清。作者希望以可精确计算的具体模型为案例，考察整体对称性如何在系综平均后涌现，并厘清其与 Swampland 纲领、混合态对称破缺的关系。

Result: 系综平均后，体（bulk）任意子（anyons）的整体对称性可以涌现，其来源是系综内不同理论的 T-对偶被折叠为单一理论的对称性。文中还讨论了与 Swampland 纲领的关系，包括整体对称性在无限距离处的涌现；并将混合态的强到弱自发对称破缺（SWSSB）重新表述为系综平均中的对称性涌现，其全息实现由连接热场双（thermofield-double）构造两侧的虫洞（wormhole）提供。

Conclusion: 整体对称性可在系综平均后涌现，这一机制统一了全息中的对偶折纸、Swampland 纲领中的无限距离极限，以及混合态 SWSSB 的虫洞实现；后者进一步启发了一个新的 Swampland 猜想（Swampland conjecture）。

Abstract: It is widely believed that there are no exact global symmetries in quantum gravity. Global symmetries of the bulk anyons can emerge after the average, as T-dualities relating different theories in the ensemble are "folded" into symmetries of a single theory (duality origami). We discuss the relation to the Swampland program, including the emergence of global symmetries at infinite distance. Finally, we recast strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking (SWSSB) of mixed states as an emergence of symmetries in ensemble averages; in holography, wormholes connecting the two sides of a thermofield-double construction provide its bulk realization. This motivates a new Swampland conjecture.

Result: 荷代数由 Cartan 代表（Cartan representative）失去等变性所产生；所诱导的上同调类（cohomology class）不依赖于下降代表的 Bardeen 型改变。经典异常与 BRST 异常是同一 Weil 超限类（Weil transgression class）的两种实现，同时仍分属不同理论。作为显式例子，五维流入超限（five dimensional inflow transgression）表明经典荷余圈（charge cocycle）与 BRST 下降元是同一 Weil 超限类的两种实现。

Abstract: We formulate a precise sense in which a BRST anomaly may possess a classical phase space shadow. It is the non-equivariance class of a Hamiltonian symmetry action on covariant phase space. We then compare this purely classical class with local BRST descent. For a Hamiltonian admissible mixed transgression, the ghost-number-two descendant $a^{2}_{d-1}$ defines a deghostification map to the charge algebra, which is generated by the failure of the Cartan representative to be equivariant. Thus the classical anomaly is a shadow of the BRST anomaly in the precise sense that both are realizations of the same Weil transgression class, while remaining objects of different theories. </details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: Only a handful of elementary color building blocks are known to participate in the double copy, most notably the structure constants of gauge theory. Abstract: We derive the AdS deformation of the flat-space soft-graviton algebra with Dirichlet boundary conditions. Assuming linear closure and normalizing the global modes to reproduce the geometric AdS isometries, covariance and the Jacobi identities uniquely determine the soft-mode bracket. The result is the wedge of $\mathcal L_Λw_{1+\infty}$, where $Λ=-\ell^{-2}$. This provides a bulk derivation of the cosmological-constant deformation that incorporates the AdS boundary condition, without relying on collinear splitting functions or a self-dual truncation.

Abstract: Neural network conformal theories were first introduced in \cite{Halverson:2024axc} and exact two-, three- and four-point correlators were computed from an ensemble of infinite-width neural networks.
