  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Coleman--De Luccia 隧穿中无限振荡负模塔使路径积分（path integral）的半经典评估复杂化，构成系统性计算预因子（prefactor）的主要概念障碍。因此需要厘清该负模塔的物理来源，判断其是否代表无穷多个真实的失稳模式。

Method: 作者指出该扇区以两种方式暴露：气泡壁附近的大标量梯度（large scalar gradient）与紧致欧几里得几何（compact Euclidean geometry）的转向点（turning point）。并在同一良定义规范（gauge）与共同正则化器（regulator）下，将 bounce 与假真空（false vacuum）的涨落作对比分析。

Result: 相应共形扇区在假真空涨落中已经存在；在相同规范与共同正则化器下，bounce 与假真空的高频负谱（high-frequency negative spectra）相互趋近。因此该紫外负模塔（ultraviolet tower）在相对负模计数中相消，仅剩一个 bounce 特有的负模。

Conclusion: 该负模塔不代表无穷多个 bounce 特有失稳，而是与单一引力共形扇区相联系。这一相消结果消除了系统性计算 Coleman--De Luccia 预因子的主要概念障碍。

Abstract: Coleman--De Luccia tunneling can exhibit an infinite tower of rapidly oscillating negative modes, complicating the semiclassical evaluation of the path integral. We show that this tower does not represent infinitely many bounce-specific instabilities, but instead is tied to a single underlying gravitational conformal sector. This sector is exposed in two different ways: by a large scalar gradient near the bubble wall and by the turning point of a compact Euclidean geometry. The corresponding conformal sector is already present in fluctuations about the false vacuum. Treating the bounce and the false vacuum in the same well-defined gauge and with a common regulator, we find that their high-frequency negative spectra approach one another. The ultraviolet tower therefore cancels from the relative negative-mode count, leaving a single bounce-specific negative mode. This clears a major conceptual obstacle toward a systematic calculation of the Coleman--De Luccia prefactor.

</details>

Conclusion: 结果表明，定位于单一边界上的精细调节探针（fine-tuned probe）可以检验所提出的三边界壳态，并有可能将其与两边界对应物区分开来。

Abstract: Partially entangled thermal states (PETS) provide semiclassical families of black-hole microstates prepared through heavy shell insertions. We extend this shell-state framework and propose three-boundary partially entangled thermal states (PETS$_3$) by replacing the two-index insertion data with a trivalent junction tensor. The corresponding bulk three-boundary booklet geometry is constrained semiclassically by the Israel junction condition, allowing us to identify candidate symmetric and mixed-orientation branches. We verify the self-consistency of this construction by counting states in the symmetric all-black-hole sector. Using a heavy-operator probe, we further estimate the detection-to-universal ratio to be $Z_D/Z_U \simeq 3$, twice the two-boundary value of $3/2$.   <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两流体模型为超流体提供了有力的唯象框架，其中组分通过涡旋诱导的互摩擦相互作用，而量子湍流（quantum turbulence）已成为低温物理的核心主题。但离散涡线（vortex-line）描述与连续介质描述之间的根本不相容，历史上迫使研究依赖唯象参数，缺乏第一性原理基础。

Result: 在法向流体（normal-fluid）速度统计中发现显著的非高斯尾部（non-Gaussian tails）与空间各向异性（spatial anisotropy），其直接来源于动力学量化涡旋背反应。升高温度会抑制法向流体速度涨落，尽管涡旋密度增加；该抑制被明确归因于超流凝聚体（superfluid condensate）的热耗竭（thermal depletion）。

Conclusion: 结果确立全息对偶（holographic duality）作为量子湍流的严格第一性原理框架，揭示法向流体在远离平衡（far from equilibrium）时的主动作用，并为正在进行的冷原子（cold-atom）与超流实验提供预测。

Abstract: The two-fluid model provides a powerful phenomenological framework for superfluids, where components interact through vortex-induced mutual friction. Quantum turbulence in such coupled two-fluid systems has become a central theme in low-temperature physics. However, the fundamental incompatibility between discrete vortex-line and continuum descriptions has historically forced reliance on phenomenological parameters. Here, we present an ab initio holographic simulation of this active two-fluid dynamics. Using a fully backreacted gravitational model, we intrinsically incorporate reciprocal momentum exchange and finite-temperature dissipation without empirical inputs. Contrasting continuously driven and freely decaying turbulence, we uncover pronounced non-Gaussian tails and spatial anisotropy in the normal-fluid velocity statistics, arising directly from the dynamical quantized vortex backreaction. Raising the temperature suppresses normal-fluid velocity fluctuations despite increasing the vortex density. This suppression is unambiguously traced to the thermal depletion of the superfluid condensate. Our results establish holographic duality as a rigorous first-principles framework for quantum turbulence, revealing the active role of the normal fluid far from equilibrium and yielding predictions for ongoing cold-atom and superfluid experiments.

Result: 在所分析的构型类中，未发现所需参数区：全局最小的 $\mathtt q=4$ 网络在整个所考虑的范围内严格 $τ$-无关。该结论并非先验显然，而是源于多个可容许构型之间的竞争——随着几何变化，这些构型的相对次序多次改变。该 $τ$-无关性一直持续到双体 RT 熵本身开始对扭转敏感的临界点。

Conclusion: 结果提供了明确示例，说明 $\mathtt{q}=4$ 真多熵（genuine multi-entropy）可探测对所有双分纠缠熵不可见的体几何（bulk geometric）信息。

Our result provides an explicit example in which the $\mathtt q=4$ genuine multi-entropy probes bulk geometric information invisible to all bipartite entanglement entropies.

### [80] [Quantum states in an AdS-invariant theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37401)

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文献中 AdS 对称性（AdS symmetry）被广泛用于各类量子应用，如 AdS/QCD，但这些框架通常未考虑 AdS 代数 IRs 的反常（非常规）性质，本文正是针对这一被忽视的问题展开研究。

Result: 发现 AdS 量子理论对基本粒子的描述与标准方法存在根本性（radically）差异，并指出 AdS 代数 IRs 具有非常规性质。

Conclusion: 现有 AdS/QCD 等框架普遍未纳入本文所探讨的 AdS 代数 IRs 非常规性质，提示相关量子应用需重新审视其对称性假设。

Abstract: We note that the description of elementary particles in anti-de Sitter (AdS) quantum theory differs radically from standard approaches. Since the Cartan subalgebra of the AdS algebra has rank 2, it is necessary to consider only those elements that explicitly depend on the eigenvalues of operators in the irreducible representations (IRs) of the algebra. In the literature, AdS symmetry is widely invoked in various quantum applications, such as AdS/QCD. However, such frameworks typically do not take into account the unconventional properties of the AdS algebra IRs investigated in the present paper.

</details>


### [81] [The Stieltjes Bootstrap for Feynman Integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37536)
*Hadrien Brochet,Maximilian Haensch,Johannes Henn,Benjamin Hollering,Prashanth Raman,Qinglin Yang,Simone Zoia*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该论文证明在满足特定指数条件时，标量费曼积分（scalar Feynman integral）对每个运动学变量与粒子质量均为Stieltjes函数，并利用该性质结合微分方程与半定优化（semidefinite optimization）计算主积分（master integral）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 费曼积分的解析性质（如单调性、正性）可用于约束其泰勒系数并改进数值计算。既有工作仅给出完全单调性（complete monotonicity）界，作者希望推广至Stieltjes性质，从而获得更强约束，并处理物理应用中常见的运动学变量间存在关系的情形。

Method: 证明基于第二Symanzik多项式（second Symanzik polynomial）的一个规范分解，其系数以生成双森林（spanning two-forests）为指标且显式非负。论文分析运动学变量满足关系的情形，并论证通过分部积分（integration-by-parts）关系可选取使每个元素均为Stieltjes函数的主积分基。

Result: Stieltjes性质保证Padé逼近（Padé approximants）收敛，并给出主积分泰勒系数的行列式（Hankel）约束，强于已知的完全单调性界。所发展的数值策略将Stieltjes约束与微分方程结合，通过半定优化在欧几里得区域（Euclidean region）求解主积分；在六维大质量箱图（six-dimensional massive box integral）上演示后，用于计算三环双质量积分（three-loop two-mass integrals），该积分为此前三环电弱修正（electroweak corrections）中Z玻色子与Higgs玻色子自能（self-energy）所需。

Conclusion: 标量费曼积分的Stieltjes性质在任意圈数、外腿数与内质量下（满足指数条件时）成立，且可与分部积分及微分方程结合形成有效数值方法，并已成功应用于三环电弱自能所需积分的计算。

Abstract: We prove that scalar Feynman integrals are Stieltjes functions in each kinematic variable $-p_i\cdot p_j$, as well as in the particle masses, for arbitrary loop order, arbitrary numbers of external legs, and arbitrary internal masses, whenever a certain exponent condition is satisfied. The proof rests on a canonical decomposition of the second Symanzik polynomial with manifestly non-negative coefficients indexed by spanning two-forests. We argue that integration-by-parts relations allow the selection of master-integral bases in which every element is a Stieltjes function. The Stieltjes property guarantees the convergence of Padé approximants and provides determinantal (Hankel) constraints on the Taylor coefficients of master integrals that go beyond previously known complete-monotonicity bounds. We develop a numerical strategy that combines these Stieltjes constraints with differential equations to determine the master integrals in the Euclidean region via semidefinite optimization. We demonstrate this approach on the pedagogical example of the six-dimensional massive box integral, and then apply it to the calculation of previously unknown three-loop two-mass integrals needed for three-loop electroweak corrections to the $Z$- and Higgs-boson self-energies.

</details>


### [82] [Reconciling Inflation with the Festina Lente Bound through Affleck-Dine Baryogenesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37674)
*Kazuto Nakamura,Masaki Yamada*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文澄清 Festina-Lente（FL）界限的隐含假设，指出在暴胀（inflation）期间令规范玻色子（gauge boson）质量高于哈勃尺度（Hubble scale）并完全破缺连续规范对称性，可自然规避该约束，并将此机制与 Affleck-Dine 重子生成（baryogenesis）联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FL 界限作为由 dS 时空带电黑洞行为激发的 swampland 猜想（swampland conjecture），对带电粒子质量施加约束；该约束在当今宇宙较弱，但对暴胀可形成严重限制，因此需澄清其成立假设并寻找规避途径。

Method: 澄清 FL 界限的基本假设，论证暴胀期间完全破缺所有规范对称性且规范玻色子质量高于哈勃尺度可规避约束；系统编目最小超对称标准模型（MSSM）中可完全破缺连续规范群的单平坦方向（flat directions），以及仅在两个方向同时激发并满足 F-平坦与 D-平坦条件时才能完全破缺的方向对。

Result: 借助适当的抬升算符（lifting operators），此类凝聚体可通过 Affleck-Dine 机制产生重子不对称；在分类中，所有允许完全破缺的单方向单项式（monomials）均携带非零 B−L，可作为 Affleck-Dine 重子生成的候选者；与观测到的重子不对称及等曲率（isocurvature）约束相容的大凝聚体可轻易满足 FL 界限。

Conclusion: 该分析揭示 FL 界限与 Affleck-Dine 重子生成之间存在自然联系，表明通过暴胀期间规范对称性完全破缺可同时规避 FL 约束并实现重子不对称生成。

Abstract: The Festina-Lente bound is a swampland conjecture motivated by the behavior of charged BHs in dS spacetime, which imposes a constraint on the masses of charged particles. Although relatively weak in the present-day Universe, it can severely constrain inflation. We clarify its underlying assumptions and show that breaking all gauge symmetries during inflation, with gauge boson masses above the Hubble scale, provides a natural way to evade these constraints. We catalogue single flat directions in the Minimal Supersymmetric Standard Model that completely break its continuous gauge group, together with pairs that achieve complete breaking only when both directions are excited and simultaneously satisfy F- and D-flatness. With suitable lifting operators, such condensates can also generate the baryon asymmetry through the Affleck-Dine mechanism. Within this classification, all single-direction monomials that admit complete breaking carry nonzero $B-L$, providing candidates for Affleck-Dine baryogenesis. We further show that, in this setting, a large condensate compatible with the observed baryon asymmetry and isocurvature constraints readily satisfies the Festina-Lente bound, establishing a natural connection between the FL bound and Affleck-Dine baryogenesis.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究维度6非最小洛伦兹破缺（Lorentz-violating, LV）算符是否会在辐射层面诱导出CFJ类项，并判断其单圈修正是否带来发散问题。

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

### [85] [Effective Field Theory for Holographic Compact Objects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37806)

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于刻画全息对偶下紧致对象（compact object）的有限尺寸效应，尤其是潮汐勒夫数（tidal Love numbers）等响应系数如何在边界共形场论数据中体现。该问题连接引力波物理中的有限尺寸修正与 AdS/CFT 对应，需要一种在长波极限下系统处理重态内部结构的方法。

Method: 方法上采用长波区域（long-wavelength regime）的世界线有效场论（worldline effective field theory）描述重态内部结构，其局域算符编码潮汐勒夫数等有限尺寸响应系数。针对弱自引力对象，证明由局域重-轻相互作用生成的 Witten 图重组为轻体场有效波动方程的玻恩级数（Born series），并通过求解含局域相互作用的该波动方程复现同一关联函数。

Result: 结果显示，有效波动方程的求解可重现原本由 Witten 图给出的重-重-轻-轻关联函数，并明确世界线威尔逊系数（worldline Wilson coefficients）如何编码于 CFT 数据中。由此建立了 AdS 中紧致对象有限尺寸响应与对偶 CFT 中重-轻及轻-轻复合算符的标度维度与 OPE 系数之间的直接关系。

Conclusion: 结论指出，紧致对象的有限尺寸响应系数与对偶 CFT 中复合算符的维度及 OPE 系数之间存在直接对应，为从共形场论数据提取引力侧有限尺寸物理提供了系统框架。

Abstract: We study heavy-heavy-light-light correlators in holographic CFTs when the heavy operator is dual to a compact object localized on scales much smaller than the AdS radius. In the long-wavelength regime, the internal structure of the heavy state is described by a worldline effective field theory, whose local operators encode finite-size response coefficients such as tidal Love numbers. Focusing on weakly self-gravitating objects, we show that the Witten diagrams generated by local heavy-light interactions reorganize into the Born series of an effective wave equation for the light bulk field. We reproduce the same correlator by solving this wave equation with localized interactions and identify how the worldline Wilson coefficients are encoded in the CFT data. Our results provide a direct relation between the finite-size response of compact objects in AdS and the dimensions and OPE coefficients of heavy-light and light-light composite operators in the dual CFT.

</details>


### [86] [Diagrammatic construction of GKZ systems for polygonal functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38030)
*K. B. Alkalaev,Semyon Mandrygin,Y. M. Zalishchansky*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文研究新近引入的多边形超几何函数（polygonal hypergeometric functions），给出构造其 GKZ 超几何系统（GKZ hypergeometric system）的图解流程，并识别出由 n(n-3)/2 个环面方程（toric equations）组成的有限子系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多边形超几何函数被猜想可计算任意维度的多点多参数单圈共形积分（multipoint parametric one-loop conformal integrals），其系统化构造依赖平面图形的图解算法（diagrammatic algorithm）。为刻画其解析结构，需要建立相应的 GKZ 系统并赋予图论解释。

Method: 针对给定的 n 点多边形幂级数（polygonal power series），构造对应的 GKZ 超几何系统，并给出显式图解解释。以传递矩阵（transianic matrix）编码全部图解数据，进而确定环面矩阵（toric matrix）与关系格（lattice of relations）的一组基，由此选出有限的环面子系统。

Result: 该有限子系统由二阶与三阶方程构成，而完整环面族还包含更高阶方程。文中以若干典型多边形函数为例说明一般流程，包括第四类 Appell 函数（fourth Appell function）与 Srivastava–Daoust 函数。

Conclusion: 多边形超几何函数可纳入 GKZ 框架，其图解数据经由传递矩阵转化为环面矩阵与关系格基，从而约化为有限个低阶环面方程；完整族中则存在更高阶方程。该框架通过 Appell 与 Srivastava–Daoust 等实例得到验证。

Abstract: We investigate the recently introduced polygonal hypergeometric functions [arXiv:2502.12127, arXiv:2507.01904], which are conjectured to evaluate multipoint parametric one-loop conformal integrals in arbitrary dimensions. These functions can be systematically constructed using a diagrammatic algorithm that operates in terms of simple planar shapes. Given an $n$-point polygonal power series, we formulate the corresponding GKZ hypergeometric system, which admits an explicit diagrammatic interpretation. In particular, the transianic matrix, which encodes all the relevant diagrammatic data, determines the corresponding toric matrix along with a basis of the lattice of relations, thereby singling out a finite subsystem of $n(n-3)/2$ toric equations. This finite subsystem consists of second- and third-order equations, whereas the full toric family also includes higher-order ones. We illustrate the general procedure with several prominent examples of polygonal functions, including the fourth Appell and the Srivastava--Daoust functions.

</details>


### [87] [Logarithmic gravity from a very slow roll limit of inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38052)
*Jordan Cotler,Victor Ivo,Juan Maldacena*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该文研究线性滚动极限下的无边界暴胀波函数，指出暴胀子量子涨落（inflaton quantum fluctuations）在引力子涨落被抑制时仍保持有限，并转化为重加热度规共形因子（conformal factor）的涨落，其概率测度化为高斯形式，导出称为对数引力（Logarithmic Gravity）的log关联理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在无边界（no-boundary）框架下研究暴胀波函数时，体内引力子涨落与暴胀子涨落的作用关系需要厘清。作者引入线性滚动极限（linear-roll limit，即取 $G_N \to 0$ 而保持哈勃尺度 $H$ 与暴胀子势斜率固定），以分离出暴胀子量子涨落这一主导自由度。

Method: 在 $G_N \to 0$、$H$ 与暴胀子势斜率固定的极限下分析无边界波函数；利用重加热发生在固定暴胀子值曲面这一条件，将暴胀子涨落映射为重加热度规共形因子的涨落，并将所得超视界尺度（superhorizon scales）上的log关联理论识别为不含指数势的Liouville引力 $d$ 维类比。进一步借助共形协变散射数据确定二次项与线性项，在偶数维情形将其与临界GJMS算子（critical GJMS operator）和Branson $Q$-曲率（Branson $Q$-curvature）联系，并考察单圈效应及规范固定问题。

Result: 涨落概率测度被大幅简化并呈高斯形式，即使对大涨落亦成立；所导出的超视界log关联理论称为对数引力，其二次项与线性项由共形协变散射数据确定，偶数维下与临界GJMS算子及Branson $Q$-曲率相关联。由体幺正性（bulk unitarity）论证单圈效应不会在物理概率测度中产生额外的共形因子依赖，并在 $2+1$ 维显式验证了该抵消。对于带滚动暴胀子的无边界范数，证明非紧致残余共形对称性可用有限Faddeev-Popov行列式规范固定，因而不会迫使球面贡献消失。此外，以不存在气泡成核真空衰变为条件，会在概率测度中生成Liouville指数势。

Conclusion: 在线性滚动极限下，无边界暴胀波函数的概率测度由暴胀子量子涨落主导，并化为定义在超视界尺度上的对数引力理论；该理论的共形结构与临界GJMS算子、Branson $Q$-曲率相关，单圈修正不引入额外共形因子依赖，残余共形对称性可有限地规范固定，而真空衰变条件则可还原Liouville指数势。

Abstract: We study the inflationary no-boundary wavefunction in a ``linear-roll limit,'' in which $G_N \to 0$ while the Hubble scale $H$ and the slope of the inflaton potential are held fixed. Although bulk graviton fluctuations are suppressed in this limit, quantum inflaton fluctuations remain finite. Because reheating occurs on a surface of fixed inflaton value, these fluctuations become fluctuations of the conformal factor of the reheating metric. The probability measure for these fluctuations simplifies drastically and becomes Gaussian, even for large fluctuations. The resulting log-correlated theory at superhorizon scales is a $d$-dimensional analog of Liouville gravity without the exponential potential, which we call Logarithmic Gravity. The quadratic and linear terms are determined by conformally covariant scattering data; in even dimensions we relate them to the critical GJMS operator and Branson $Q$-curvature. From bulk unitarity, we argue that one-loop effects do not generate additional conformal-factor dependence in the physical probability measure, and we verify the cancellation explicitly in $2 + 1$ dimensions. For the no-boundary norm with a rolling inflaton, we show that the noncompact residual conformal symmetries can be gauge fixed with a finite Faddeev-Popov determinant, so they do not force the sphere contribution to vanish. In addition, conditioning on the absence of vacuum decay by bubble nucleation generates the Liouville exponential potential in the probability measure.

</details>


### [88] [On Grey Galaxies and D3-branes on the Conifold](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38064)
*Antonio Amariti,Riccardo Salto,Chiara Toldo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在 Sasaki-Einstein 五维流形（Sasaki-Einstein 5-manifolds）$Y^{p,q}$（特别关注 conifold 基底 $T^{1,1}$）的 IIB 型超引力一致截断中，研究环绕 AdS$_5$ 黑洞的探针 D-膜有效势，指出超辐射（superradiance）的出现与探针有效势中稳定极小值的形成相对应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于刻画 AdS$_5$ 黑洞周围探针 D-膜的有效势行为，并厘清超辐射现象与探针稳定性之间的联系，同时扩展 Dual Dressed Black Holes 与 Grey Galaxies 的相关构造。

Method: 采用探针 D-膜方法：探针为支撑于 AdS 时空维度上的对偶巨引力子（dual Giant Gravitons），或包裹内流形非收缩圈（non-contractible cycles）从而在 AdS 时空中呈“点状”的 D3 探针膜；内流形 $T^{1,1}$ 与 $Y^{p,q}$ 的非收缩圈从拓扑上稳定膜于有限尺度。

Result: 在 Gutowski-Reall 黑洞上发现稳定的超对称包裹 D3 膜，从而在 AdS$_5$ 中有效构造出由黑洞与其周围重子凝聚（baryonic condensate）组成的稳定 BPS 复合系统；并表明黑洞背景中超辐射的出现与探针有效势稳定极小值的出现相匹配。

Conclusion: 结果扩展了 Dual Dressed Black Holes 与 Grey Galaxies 的构造，并对近期关于经典上同调问题（classical cohomology problem）及对偶 Klebanov-Witten 理论中相关偶然态（fortuitous states）的研究具有参考意义。

Abstract: We study the effective potentials for various probe D-branes surrounding AdS$_5$ black holes in consistent truncations of type IIB supergravity on Sasaki-Einstein 5-manifolds $Y^{p,q}$, focusing in particular on the conifold base $T^{1,1}$. The probes are either dual Giant Gravitons having support on AdS spacetime dimensions or D3 probe branes wrapped on non-contractible cycles of the internal manifold, which then appear "point-like" in the AdS spacetime. The non-contractible cycles of $T^{1,1}$ and $Y^{p,q}$ topologically stabilize the branes at a finite size, and we find stable supersymmetric wrapped D3 branes on Gutowski-Reall black holes, effectively providing a stable BPS composite system in AdS$_5$ made by a black hole surrounded by a baryonic condensate. We show that the onset of superradiance in the black hole backgrounds matches the emergence of stable minima in the probe effective potentials, extending the constructions of Dual Dressed Black Holes and the Grey Galaxies. We comment on the relevance of this result for recent studies concerning the classical cohomology problem and the associated fortuitous states in the dual Klebanov-Witten theory.

</details>


### [89] [Boundaries and defects in conformal field theories at finite temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38130)
*Daniele Artico,Adam Chalabi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在有限温度、无限体积的欧几里得共形场论（CFT）中研究共形边界与缺陷，区分环绕热圈（thermal circle）的缺陷与定位于热圈上一点的缺陷，并对任意维数与余维数下标量、矢量与自旋-2原初算符的体单点函数进行分类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限温度下的共形边界与缺陷（conformal boundaries and defects）缺乏系统的分类与数据刻画，本文旨在建立该情形的单点函数分类框架，并联系零温缺陷CFT数据与新的有限温度数据。

Method: 对环绕热圈与局部化（localised）两类缺陷，分类体单点函数；将体标量单点函数的低温展开与零温缺陷CFT数据及缺陷原初算符的新有限温度数据相关联；对局部化缺陷利用周期性（periodicity）与缺陷算符展开（defect operator expansion）推导单点自举求和规则（one-point bootstrap sum rules）；并在自由体CFT的若干示例中确定热缺陷CFT数据。

Result: 在自由体CFT示例（幺正与非幺正边界CFT、环绕单值（monodromy）缺陷、局部化磁力线）中确定了热缺陷CFT数据；对环绕热圈的缺陷计算了热力学观测量；对局部化缺陷观察到单点函数的Hurwitz zeta函数分解，暗示存在底层色散关系（dispersion relation）。

Conclusion: 该工作为有限温度共形边界与缺陷提供了分类与数据框架，并揭示局部化缺陷单点函数中Hurwitz zeta函数分解所暗示的色散关系结构。

Abstract: We consider conformal boundaries and defects in Euclidean conformal field theory (CFT) at finite temperature and infinite volume. We distinguish defects that wrap the thermal circle and defects that are localised at a point on it. In both set-ups, and for any dimension and co-dimension, we classify bulk one-point functions of scalar, vector and spin-2 primaries. We relate the low-temperature expansion of bulk scalar one-point functions to zero-temperature defect CFT data and new finite-temperature data of defect primaries. For localised defects, we derive one-point bootstrap sum rules using periodicity and the defect operator expansion. We consider several examples in free bulk CFTs: unitary and non-unitary boundary CFTs, a wrapping monodromy defect, and a localised magnetic line. We determine thermal defect CFT data in all examples. For defects wrapping the thermal circle, we compute thermodynamic observables. For localised defects, we observe a Hurwitz zeta function decomposition of one-point functions suggestive of an underlying dispersion relation.

</details>


### [90] [CMB Injection Bounds on Moduli Fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38151)
*Naman Agarwal,Andrew R. Frey,Ratul Mahanta,Anshuman Maharana,Fernando Quevedo,Gonzalo Villa*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文系统推导了质量在 $10^3\ {\rm eV}$ 至 $10^8\ {\rm eV}$ 之间的光模量（light moduli）通过缓慢衰变向标准模型等离子体注入能量所留下的宇宙微波背景（cosmic microwave background, CMB）限制，覆盖真空失配、重标量/暴胀子衰变与热发射等多个产生通道，并指出这些注入限制与标准宇宙学模量问题及第五力限制互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具有引力强度相互作用的轻标量场（模量）在弦理论、超引力与额外维度模型中普遍存在。若其在早期宇宙中产生且寿命超过复合时期（recombination），缓慢衰变会将高能粒子注入标准模型等离子体，从而在 CMB 上留下可观测印记，因此需要系统量化相应的限制。

Method: 系统推导光模量的 CMB 注入限制，质量范围为 $10^3\ {\rm eV}\leq m_{\rm mod}\leq 10^8\ {\rm eV}$，并覆盖主要宇宙学产生通道：真空失配（vacuum misalignment）、重标量/暴胀子衰变（heavy scalar/inflaton decay），以及来自原初等离子体的热发射（thermal emission），其中包括热弦 Hagedorn 相的产生贡献。

Result: 所得到的注入限制对光模量参数空间施加了严格约束，这些约束与标准宇宙学模量问题以及第五力（fifth force）限制形成互补。

Conclusion: CMB 注入限制为光模量提供了独立且严格的探测途径，其约束能力与既有宇宙学与第五力边界互补，可用于检验弦理论、超引力及额外维度模型中的模量场。

Abstract: Light scalar fields with gravitational strength interactions (moduli) are ubiquitous in string, supergravity, and extra-dimensional models. If produced in the early universe with lifetimes exceeding the epoch of recombination, slowly decaying moduli inject energetic particles into the Standard Model plasma, leaving distinct imprints on the cosmic microwave background (CMB). In this paper we systematically derive CMB injection bounds on light moduli ($10^3\ {\rm eV}\leq m_{\rm{mod}}\leq 10^8\ {\rm eV}$) across the primary cosmological production channels: vacuum misalignment, heavy scalar/inflaton decay, thermal emission from the primordial plasma including production from a Hagedorn phase of hot strings. We show that these injection bounds impose stringent constraints that are complementary to both the standard cosmological moduli problem and fifth force bounds.

</details>


### [91] [Exact bispectra in strongly mixed multifield inflation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38167)
*Lucas Pinol*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 该工作基于含大质量等曲率标量（isocurvature scalar）的暴胀有效场论（effective field theory），在单位声速下将所有曲率—等曲率二次混合（mixing）重整求和至无量纲混合强度 λ=ρ/H 的所有阶，系统计算了 unitary gauge 中所有四次及以下立方相互作用产生的原初双谱（bispectrum）。强混合区给出与标准模板无关联的精确多场形状，弱混合区复现已知微扰结果并首次给出三交换（triple-exchange）首阶系数闭式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多场暴胀中曲率与等曲率模式的混合通常按微扰处理，其强混合区的非微扰效应缺乏统一、精确的描述框架。该文旨在建立能够覆盖任意混合强度与质量参数的双谱计算框架，并检验这些新形状是否被 Planck 数据偏好。

Method: 采用扩充了大质量等曲率标量的暴胀涨落有效场论，在单位声速、unitary gauge 下枚举维度不超过四的全部立方相互作用。将二次混合置于自由哈密顿量中并求和至所有阶，使每个尺度不变树级双谱化约为 Schwinger 参数积分；微扰论中的单、双、三交换对应重整理论中的接触图（contact diagram）。

Result: 强混合下发现此前未见的精确多场双谱形状，与数据分析常用标准形状无相关性；挤压极限（squeezed limit）在各交换阶均携带由 dressed 质量参数 μ_eff 决定普适频率的宇宙对撞机（cosmological collider）信号。弱混合下 clock 系数以闭式给出并精确退化为已知单、双交换结果，三交换首阶系数首次以超几何函数（hypergeometric function）形式在所有质量下闭式给出；强混合下对撞机振幅在整个 (λ, μ_eff) 平面上按 e^{π(λ-2μ_eff)/2} 标度。六个精确形状与 Planck PR4 分箱双谱的首次对照显示偏好强混合，此时对撞机振荡侵入轻度挤压构型，且全部相互作用形状随生成代码一并公开。

Conclusion: 将曲率—等曲率混合非微扰求和，可统一描述从弱到强混合的原初非高斯性，涵盖此前所有研究情形为特例，并给出可快速调用的通用模板。首个数据对照提示强混合区具有观测相关性，为多场暴胀的宇宙对撞机搜寻提供了新的理论形状与检验途径。

Abstract: Using the effective field theory of inflationary fluctuations extended by a massive isocurvature scalar, at unit sound speed, we compute the primordial bispectra generated by every cubic interaction of dimension four or less in the unitary gauge, with the curvature--isocurvature quadratic mixing resummed to all orders in the dimensionless mixing strength $λ=ρ/H$. Placing the mixing in the free Hamiltonian [...], every scale-invariant tree-level bispectrum reduces to a single Schwinger-parameter integral over independent leg kernels, and what perturbation theory calls single, double and triple exchange are contact diagrams of the resummed theory. At strong mixing we unveil genuinely new, exact multifield bispectrum shapes with no correlation to the standard shapes used in data analyses. The squeezed limit carries a cosmological collider signal with a universal frequency set by the dressed mass parameter $μ_{\rm eff}$ at every exchange order. At weak mixing the clock coefficients are obtained in closed form and reduce [...] to the known perturbative single- and double-exchange results, exactly; the triple-exchange leading coefficient is obtained in closed form at every mass, in terms of hypergeometric functions, for the first time. At strong mixing the collider amplitude scales as $e^{π(λ-2μ_{\rm eff})/2}$ over the whole $(λ,μ_{\rm eff})$ plane. This encompasses all previously studied regimes as particular cases [...]. A first confrontation of the six exact shapes with the Planck PR4 binned bispectrum shows a preference for strong mixing, where the cosmological collider oscillations invade mildly squeezed configurations [...]. The shapes of all interactions over the whole plane [...] are released with the code that produced them, so that a template at any mixing and mass can be read off in milliseconds.

</details>

### [92] [A Hopf Algebraic Theory of the Quantum Magnusian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.33587)
*Li Guo,Joon-Hwi Kim,Jung-Wook Kim,Sungsoo Kim,Sangmin Lee,Jian-Rong Li*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 本文建立量子马格努斯展开（quantum Magnus expansion）中图系数的霍普夫代数（Hopf algebra）理论，引入混合箭图（mixed quiver）的收缩霍普夫代数，给出连通函数 ω_c 的普适闭式公式及其局部刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典图展开由有向树支配，而量子对应物涉及环图、多重边及不同边数据，缺乏统一代数框架；本文旨在用霍普夫代数刻画这些物理图系数。

Method: 构造包含物理图且对收缩封闭的混合箭图收缩霍普夫代数；在箭图张成的霍普夫子代数上，由归一化线性扩张数据与蝌蚪规则（tadpole prescription）定义特征 e，以其卷积逆 ω 为核心，导出连通函数 ω_c 的公式。

Result: 得到任意有限箭图上 ω_c 的普适闭式公式——顶点集有序集合划分上的有限和，无需无环或单边假设；并给出由两顶点收缩恒等式、蝌蚪分解、非连通图零值与边界数据构成的唯一性局部刻画；对无环箭图化为仅依赖下降数的置换公式。

Conclusion: 该公式亦可独立地由量子马格努斯展开经算子乘积与 Wick 收缩导出，表明物理图系数受收缩霍普夫代数卷积结构支配；并由此得到精细化的量子 Murua 公式及与 Tutte、色多项式相关的定向和恒等式。

Abstract: We develop a Hopf-algebraic theory of the graph coefficients arising in the quantum Magnus expansion. At the classical level, the graph expansion is governed by directed trees, whereas its quantum counterpart involves loop graphs, multiple edges, and different types of edge data. We introduce a contraction Hopf algebra of mixed quivers containing these physical diagrams and closed under contraction.
  On the Hopf subalgebra spanned by quivers, we define a character $e$ from normalized linear-extension data and a tadpole prescription, and let $ω$ be its convolution inverse. Our main result is a universal closed formula for the connected function $ω_c$ on every finite quiver. The formula is a finite sum over ordered set partitions of the vertex set and is valid without acyclicity or simplicity assumptions. We also give a local characterization: a two-vertex contraction identity, together with tadpole factorization, vanishing on disconnected graphs, and boundary data, determines $ω_c$ uniquely.
  For acyclic quivers, the general formula reduces to a permutation formula whose coefficients depend only on descent numbers. We derive the same formula independently from the quantum Magnus expansion via operator products and Wick contractions, showing that its physical graph coefficients are governed by the convolution structure of the contraction Hopf algebra. As further consequences, we obtain a refined quantum Murua formula and orientation-sum identities related to Tutte and chromatic polynomials.

</details>


### [93] [Elementary Askey-Wilson Functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37390)
*J. F. van Diejen,A. N. Kirillov*

Main category: math.CO

TL;DR: 该文给出非常良平衡（very-well-poised）${}_8\Phi_7$ 基本超几何（basic hypergeometric）Askey-Wilson 函数的行列式求值公式，将其表示为初等函数；公式在四类 Askey-Wilson 参数各出现一次的离散取值下成立，证明依赖一个 Cauchy 型行列式的乘积公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于为非常良平衡 ${}_8\Phi_7$ 的 Askey-Wilson 函数寻求初等函数形式的显式求值，此前此类求值公式尚缺乏。关键在于限定于特定离散参数取值，使求值可行。

Method: 方法核心是构造并证明一个相关的 Cauchy 型行列式（Cauchy-type determinant）的乘积公式，其本身具有独立意义；证明采用初等手段，依托 Krattenthaler 的“因子识别”（identification of factors）行列式求值技术。所限参数为置换对称（permutation-symmetric）的四个 Askey-Wilson 参数，取值（至符号）为 $q^k$（$k$ 为正整数或半整数），且正值/负值、整数/半整数幂四种类型各出现一次。

Result: 得到 ${}_8\Phi_7$ Askey-Wilson 函数的行列式求值公式，其结果为初等函数的显式表达，适用范围为上述离散参数四元组。同时获得一个相伴的 Cauchy 型行列式乘积公式。

Conclusion: 该行列式求值公式在上述参数离散取值条件下成立；其证明归结为 Cauchy 型行列式乘积公式，该乘积公式具有独立价值，并可用初等方法结合 Krattenthaler 的因子识别法建立。

Abstract: We present a determinantal evaluation formula for the very-well-poised ${}_8Φ_7$ basic hypergeometric Askey-Wilson function in terms of elementary functions. The formula in question is valid for discrete values of the four permutation-symmetric Askey-Wilson parameters; specifically, these consist of all quadruples of parameter values that are given, up to a sign, by powers $q^k$ with $k$ a positive integer or half-integer, in such a way that each of the four types (positive/negative sign, integer/half-integer power) occurs exactly once. The proof hinges on a product formula for an associated Cauchy-type determinant, which is of independent interest and is established here by elementary means using Krattenthaler's `identification of factors' method for determinant evaluations.

</details>

### [94] [On minima of theta and Epstein zeta functions in dimension four](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37615)
*Senping Luo,Juncheng Wei*

Main category: math.NT

TL;DR: 该论文证明在四维、协体积为 1 的格（lattice）中，根格 $\mathcal D_4$ 在所有 $\alpha>0$ 下最小化 theta 函数，从而对 $s>2$ 最小化 Epstein zeta 函数，解决了 Sarnak–Strömbergsson 猜想及四维 Rankin–Sobolev 问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sarnak-Strömbergsson（2006）已建立 $\mathcal D_4$ 的局部极小性并猜其全局最优，而四维 Rankin-Sobolev 问题可追溯至 Éndibaev（1978）与 Shushbaev（1978），本文旨在给出该全局最小性证明。

Method: 研究对象为 $\mathbb R^4$ 中协体积（covolume）为 1 的满秩格（full-rank lattice），考虑 theta 函数 $\Theta(\alpha,L)$ 与 Epstein zeta 函数 $E(L,s)$；通过证明对任意 $\alpha>0$ 有 $\arg\min\Theta(\alpha,L)=\mathcal D_4$（在正交变换意义下）来完成论证。

Result: 证明了 $\arg\min_{covol(L)=1}\Theta(\alpha,L)=\mathcal D_4$（$\alpha>0$），并由此推出 $\arg\min_{covol(L)=1}E(L,s)=\mathcal D_4$（$s>2$），即根格在四维所有格中同时最小化这两个函数。

Conclusion: 该结果证实了 Sarnak-Strömbergsson 的猜想，并由 theta 函数的最小性推出 Epstein zeta 函数的最小性，从而解决四维的 Rankin-Sobolev 问题。

Abstract: Let the theta and Epstein zeta functions be $Θ(α,L)=\sum_{v\in L}e^{-πα|v|^2}$ for $α>0$ and $E(L,s)=\sum_{v\in L\setminus\{0\}}{|v|^{-2s}}$ for $s>2$, respectively. We consider full-rank lattices $L\subset\mathbb R^4$. Let the covolume of $L$ be one, and let $\mathcal D_4=2^{-1/4}D_4$ be the root lattice $D_4$ rescaled to covolume one. We prove that, up to orthogonal transformations, \begin{equation}\nonumber arg\,min_{covol(L)=1}Θ(α,L)=\mathcal D_4 \qquad\text{if}\quad α>0, \end{equation} and this implies that \begin{equation}\nonumber arg\,min_{covol(L)=1}E(L,s)=\mathcal D_4 \qquad\text{if}\quad s>2. \end{equation} This proves that the root lattice minimizes the theta and Epstein zeta functions among all lattices in dimension four, as conjectured by Sarnak-Strömbergsson (\cite{SS}, 2006), who established the local minimality of $\mathcal D_4$. Thereby, this resolves the Rankin-Sobolev problem in dimension four dating back to Éndibaev (\cite{End1978}, 1978) and Shushbaev (\cite{Shu1978}, 1978).

</details>

### [95] [Rectification of plasma-powered active matter into macroscopic work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37257)
*Ting-yu Yao,Shuo Wang,Shao-peng Li,Ming-hang Zhao,Bao-quan Ai,Ya-feng He*

Main category: physics.plasm-ph

TL;DR: 该研究将等离子体（plasma）中自驱动微马达与棘轮整流（ratchet rectification）耦合，构建出等离子体驱动的主动引擎（active engine），实现宏观定向转动，并在功率与效率上较液相主动引擎提升数个量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 等离子体具有强能量与动量通量，但通常被视为耗散损失通道而非可用资源，将其转化为宏观功仍具挑战。研究旨在探索整流非平衡等离子体活动以获取宏观功的通用路径。

Method: 在等离子体鞘层（plasma sheath）中，介电微球（dielectric microspheres）自发形成不对称表面充电并发生Quincke旋转不稳定性，成为从等离子体直接提取能量的快速微马达。其随机碰撞经锯齿形转子（sawtooth rotor）整流为定向角动量通量，外部的非对称齿轮（asymmetric gear）将活性浴组织为相干环流以放大扭矩传递。

Result: 引擎工作在惯性相关（inertia-relevant）区间，其功率输出与端到端效率相较液相主动引擎提升数个量级，并可维持运转至单微马达极限。

Conclusion: 结果确立了整流非平衡等离子体活动以产生宏观功的通用途径。

Abstract: Plasmas sustain strong energy and momentum flux, yet this activity is typically treated as a dissipative loss channel rather than a usable resource, and its conversion into macroscopic work remains challenging. Here we demonstrate a plasma-powered active engine by coupling self-propelled micromotors to ratchet rectification. Dielectric microspheres in the plasma sheath spontaneously develop asymmetric surface charging and undergo a Quincke rotational instability, forming fast micromotors that extract energy directly from the plasma. Their stochastic impacts are rectified by a sawtooth rotor into a directed angular-momentum flux that drives steady rotation, while an outer asymmetric gear organizes the active bath into coherent circulation to amplify torque transfer. Operating in an inertia-relevant regime, the engine achieves orders-of-magnitude enhancements in both power output and end-to-end efficiency compared with liquid-phase active engines, and remains functional down to the single-micromotor limit. More broadly, our results establish a general route to rectify nonequilibrium plasma activity for macroscopic work.

</details>

### [96] [Slip Wall Boundary Conditions in Curved Domains for Finite Element ALE Hydrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36053)
*Nabil Atallah,Kevin Sweet,Paul Moujaes,Jan Nikl,Ketan Mittal,Vladimir Z. Tomov*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出一种用于解析曲面域上带滑移壁边界条件（slip-wall boundary conditions）流体力学的任意拉格朗日-欧拉（arbitrary Lagrangian-Eulerian, ALE）高阶有限元方法，通过弱边界条件施加与TMOP自适应，实现边界节点切向运动并保持几何保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 曲面域上滑移壁边界的高阶数值模拟需同时保持曲线边界几何特征并允许边界节点切向移动，传统方法易冻结切向运动或损失边界保真度，故需一种兼顾两者的ALE框架。

Method: 方法包含三个独立阶段：拉格朗日（Lagrangian）阶段以弱边界条件（weak boundary condition enforcement）施加滑移壁条件且不冻结切向运动；网格优化阶段将目标矩阵优化范式（target-matrix optimization paradigm, TMOP）适配到解析壁面，用曲线或曲面参数替换选定的边界坐标自由度，Newton求解使边界节点沿切向运动并携带拉格朗日阶段产生的漂移；重映射（remap）阶段通过基于保守对流（conservative advection）的伪时间重映射传递解。

Result: 二维与三维数值结果展示了该方法在高阶曲面网格（high-order curved meshes）上的效果，表明其保持边界保真度（boundary fidelity）并产生预期的流体力学解特征。

Conclusion: 该高阶ALE方法可在解析曲面域上有效处理滑移壁边界，兼顾边界几何保真与切向节点运动，适用于二维与三维高阶曲面网格的流体力学模拟。

Abstract: We present a high-order finite element arbitrary Lagrangian-Eulerian (ALE) method for hydrodynamics with slip-wall boundary conditions on analytically curved domains. The method combines distinct Lagrangian, remesh, and remap phases while preserving the geometric features of curved boundaries and allowing tangential motion of boundary nodes. In the Lagrangian phase, we use weak boundary condition enforcement to impose the slip-wall condition without freezing tangential motion. In the mesh optimization phase, we adapt TMOP (target-matrix optimization paradigm) to analytic walls by replacing selected boundary coordinate degrees of freedom with curve or surface parameters. The Newton solve then moves boundary nodes tangentially with respect to the prescribed geometry while carrying the drift introduced by the Lagrangian phase. In the remap phase, we transfer the solution from the old mesh to the optimized mesh by a conservative advection-based remap over pseudo-time. We present numerical results in two and three dimensions demonstrating the method on high-order curved meshes and showing that it preserves boundary fidelity while producing the expected hydrodynamic solution features.

</details>

### [97] [Wave-Particle Decomposition for Kinetic Equations II: Full Boltzmann equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35894)
*Chang Liu,Kun Xu*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 本文将动理学松弛方程的波粒分解（wave-particle decomposition）推广至完整玻尔兹曼方程（Boltzmann equation），构造一个由波-粒多尺度方程组构成的封闭系统，并给出耦合隐式迭代求解方法；该系统可连续覆盖从玻尔兹曼方程到纳维-斯托克斯（Navier-Stokes）流体力学极限，数值测试显示其效率远高于传统迭代方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有波粒分解仅适用于动理学松弛方程，尚未覆盖完整玻尔兹曼碰撞项，而玻尔兹曼方程的数值求解在近连续区收敛缓慢、迭代代价高昂。因此需要一个既保持玻尔兹曼解精确性、又能高效跨尺度求解的框架。

Method: 通过恒等变换将碰撞项写为向局部麦克斯韦分布（local Maxwellian）的松弛加一个守恒余项；沿特征线（characteristics）对局部动理学视界（kinetic horizon）积分定义“波”，其精确补量“粒子”承载余项。波与粒子方程的矩（moments）构成扩展纳维-斯托克斯方程，与总守恒律及粒子方程共同组成封闭的波-粒多尺度方程组，由视界-松弛比参数化。求解采用耦合隐式波-粒迭代：固定粒子更新守恒变量，再以预测波与宏观预测的端点牵引力更新粒子。

Result: 在离散相容性假设下，该迭代以离散玻尔兹曼解为不动点，其近连续区收敛因子被粒子输运份额（particle transport share）所界定，且该份额随视界-松弛比指数衰减。正激波、库埃特流（Couette flow）、空腔流以及高超声速圆柱与阿波罗（Apollo）流动的测试表明：在稀薄与过渡区结果与传统迭代方案一致，近连续极限与纳维-斯托克斯解一致；此时波-粒迭代所需迭代次数比该方案（或在其不收敛时的 Shakhov 版本）少约三个到四个数量级以上，且玻尔兹曼解的计算耗时仅为同一网格上纳维-斯托克斯求解器的一到四倍。

Conclusion: 波粒分解可严格推广至完整玻尔兹曼方程，所得波-粒多尺度方程组对任意视界均精确，并连续连接玻尔兹曼方程与纳维-斯托克斯流体力学。配套的耦合隐式迭代在保持玻尔兹曼不动点的同时显著提升近连续区求解效率，具备跨稀薄、过渡与连续区的统一求解能力。

Abstract: The wave-particle decomposition of kinetic relaxation equations is extended to the full Boltzmann equation. An identity transformation writes the collision term as a relaxation toward the local Maxwellian plus a conservative remainder. Along characteristics, the equilibrium integral over a local kinetic horizon defines the wave, and its exact complement, the particle, carries the remainder. The moments of the wave and particle equations are extended Navier-Stokes equations, and the total conservation law and the particle equation form the wave-particle multiscale equations, a closed system, exact for every horizon, that spans a continuous spectrum from the Boltzmann equation to Navier-Stokes hydrodynamics, parametrized by the horizon-to-relaxation ratio. A coupled implicit wave-particle iteration solves this system, updating the conservative variables with the particle fixed and the particle with the predicted wave and an endpoint traction from the macroscopic prediction. Under discrete compatibility assumptions the iteration preserves the discrete Boltzmann solution as its fixed point, and its near-continuum convergence factor is bounded by a particle transport share decaying exponentially with this ratio. Tests on a normal shock, Couette and cavity flows, and hypersonic cylinder and Apollo flows agree with the conventional iterative scheme in rarefied and transition regimes and with Navier-Stokes solutions near the continuum limit. There the wave-particle iteration needs about three to more than four orders of magnitude fewer iterations than this scheme or, where it did not converge, its Shakhov version, and the Boltzmann solution costs one to four times the wall time of a Navier-Stokes solver on the same mesh.

</details>


### [98] [A conservative bulk-surface closure for soluble-surfactant falling films: an analytical and numerical mass-balance audit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37869)
*Sanghasri Mukhopadhyay,Asim Mukhopadhyay*

Main category: physics.flu-dyn

TL;DR: 该论文审查含可溶性表面活性剂（soluble surfactant）降膜（falling film）的加权残差（weighted-residual）模型中的质量守恒问题。研究发现，除已知的非线性表面 Marangoni 通量修正外，深度积分（depth-integrated）体相扩散项仍存在不一致；补上斜界面法向扩散通量中的流向浓度梯度贡献后，耦合体相-表面输运系统的局部守恒得以恢复，而流体力学方程、最低阶吸附-脱附闭合及线性稳定性均不受影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在封闭周期域中，吸附与脱附只在液膜与其自由表面之间重新分配可溶表面活性剂，不能改变总存量。作者据此把质量守恒作为审计标准，检验已有可溶表面活性剂降膜模型是否真正满足这一物理要求。

Method: 推导深度积分体相扩散项导致的质量失衡，并构造显式的正周期浓度场以展示该失衡；指出缺失项来自斜界面法向扩散通量中的流向浓度梯度，将其纳入后恢复局部守恒。另以早期行波（travelling-wave）研究的参数做独立无滑移（no-slip）数值计算，对比参考模型与修正模型。

Result: 参考模型在 t=4500 时损失了初始表面活性剂质量的 0.0228832%，而修正模型绝对相对质量变化仅为 1.2×10⁻¹⁵；在 t≈4500 时，主峰（main hump）与毛细波纹（capillary ripples）仍保持相近。这表明与行波计算吻合本身并不能保证约化模型遵守底层输运问题的守恒律。

Conclusion: 修正后的方程对任意光滑周期解均守恒约化总存量，虽本例漂移很小，但守恒性质是严格的。作者进一步将这一精确守恒性质与全曲面界面边界条件的高阶相容性（higher-order compatibility）区分开来。

Abstract: Adsorption and desorption redistribute soluble surfactant between a liquid film and its free surface, but cannot change the total inventory in a closed periodic domain. We audit this requirement in nonlinear weighted-residual models for soluble-surfactant-laden falling films. Although a previous correction restores conservation of the nonlinear surface Marangoni flux, a separate inconsistency remains in the depth-integrated bulk diffusion. We derive the resulting mass imbalance and construct explicit positive periodic concentration fields that exhibit it. The missing contribution arises from the streamwise concentration gradient appearing in the normal diffusive flux at a sloping interface. Including this contribution restores local conservation of the coupled bulk-surface transport system while leaving the hydrodynamic equations, the leading-order adsorption-desorption closure, and the linear stability properties of the uniform equilibrium unchanged. In an independent no-slip calculation using the parameters of an earlier travelling-wave study, the reference model loses $0.0228832\%$ of its initial surfactant mass by $t=4500$, whereas the corrected run has an absolute relative mass change of $1.2\times10^{-15}$. The main hump and capillary ripples remain closely comparable at $t\approx4500$. This benchmark therefore shows that agreement with travelling-wave calculations does not, by itself, guarantee that a reduced model respects the conservation laws inherited from the underlying transport problem. Although the drift is small in this case, the corrected equations conserve the reduced total inventory for every smooth periodic solution. We also distinguish this exact conservation property from higher-order compatibility with the full curved-interface boundary conditions.

</details>

# astro-ph.HE [[Back]](#toc)

### [99] [ALMA - CTAO synergies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37797)
*Paola Andreani,Maria Diaz-Trigo*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文旨在向ESO天文界宣传即将建成的切伦科夫望远镜阵列（Cherenkov Telescope Array, CTAO）所提供的科学机遇，重点阐述将阿塔卡马大型毫米波/亚毫米波阵列（Atacama Large Millimeter/submillimeter Array, ALMA）的毫米波/亚毫米波能力与CTAO的甚高能（very high energy, VHE）太电子伏特（TeV）观测相结合所能开辟的新发现途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多天体物理源与天体环境在毫米波/亚毫米波段和TeV波段均表现出可探测的辐射过程，两套设施的协同效应不仅限于共享辐射机制，还涉及更广泛的物理过程与观测关联，因此有必要提升ESO天文界对CTAO科学价值的认知。

Method: 本文为综述/倡议性质的文章，通过梳理同时涉及毫米波、亚毫米波与TeV波段的天体物理源与环境案例，论证ALMA与CTAO联合观测的协同价值，而非提出具体观测方法或数据分析流程。

Result: 摘要指出，ALMA与CTAO的协同可开辟新的发现途径，其协同性体现在共享的辐射机制之外，还延伸至更广泛的物理过程以及连接毫米波、亚毫米波与TeV能量的观测关联。

Conclusion: 在CTAO即将投入运行的背景下，推动ESO天文界认识ALMA与CTAO多波段协同观测的科学潜力，有助于拓展甚高能天体物理的研究空间。

Abstract: This article aims to raise awareness within the ESO astronomical community of the scientific opportunities offered by the upcoming Cherenkov Telescope Array, CTAO, highlighting cases where combining millimetre/submillimetre capabilities of the Atacama Large Millimeter / submillimeter Array, ALMA with the CTAO's very high energy, TeV, observations enables new avenues for discovery. Many astrophysical sources and environments exhibit emission processes detectable in both regimes; however, the synergies extend beyond shared emission mechanisms and include broader physical processes and observational connections linking millimetre, submillimetre and TeV energies.

</details>


### [100] [Bayesian Evidence for Inspiraling Hotspot Motion in the Galactic Center](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35995)
*Pablo Ruales,Maciek Wielgus,Delilah E. A. Gates,Alejandro Cardenas-Avendano*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该工作构建贝叶斯框架（Bayesian framework），从偏振光变曲线中推断银心黑洞人马座A*（Sagittarius A*, Sgr A*）耀发期间热斑（hotspot）的时变运动学，结果显示数据显著偏好非测地线内旋轨迹（non-geodesic inspiraling trajectory）而非严格开普勒轨道（Keplerian orbit）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耀发期间Sgr A*的偏振观测在Stokes Q-U平面呈现演化环状轨迹（Q-U loops），通常以瞬时被激发的轨道热斑的开普勒模型（Keplerian models）解释，但该模型假设是否成立尚需检验。本文旨在利用偏振光变曲线对热斑运动学进行更一般的推断。

Method: 采用贝叶斯框架，以通用运动描述将辐射区轨迹建模为K个连续分段运动学段（piecewise kinematic segments），每段由径向与方位角速度参数刻画；取K=1、2、3进行比较，并与2017年4月11日ALMA毫米波观测（含两次相继Q-U loops）对照，同时考察局部磁场几何（magnetic field geometry）、谱指数（spectral index）与热斑轨迹运动学剖面的影响。

Result: 发现统计上显著的证据表明辐射区沿非测地线内旋轨迹运动；当建模数据段更长时，这一相对于严格开普勒轨道的偏好在统计上明显增强。基于贝叶斯对数证据（Bayesian log-evidence）比较与约化卡方统计量（reduced chi-square statistic），内旋非开普勒模型均优于纯开普勒模型。

Conclusion: 结果提示银心耀发事件及其伴生的间歇性热斑可能具有非开普勒、内旋的运动性质，为理解Sgr A*耀发提供新视角，并扩展了对热斑运动学的观测约束。

Abstract: During flaring episodes, polarimetric observations of the Galactic Center black hole Sagittarius A* (Sgr A*) reveal evolving, loop-like trajectories in the Stokes Q-U plane, the so-called Q-$U loops. Keplerian models of transiently energized orbiting features, hotspots, have been employed to reproduce these polarimetric signatures. In this work, we develop a Bayesian framework to infer the time-dependent kinematics of the hotspot from polarized light curves, using a general prescription for its motion. We model the emitting region's trajectory as a continuous, piecewise sequence of K kinematic segments, each described by radial and azimuthal velocity parameters. We perform analyses with K=1,2,3 and compare these models to millimeter-wavelength ALMA observations of Sgr A* from 2017 April 11 that include two subsequent Q-U loops, extending the observational constraints on the kinematics. We examine the effects of the local magnetic field geometry, spectral index, and kinematic profile of the hotspot's trajectory on the polarized signal. We find statistically significant evidence that the emitting region follows a non-geodesic inspiraling trajectory, and this preference over a strictly Keplerian orbit strengthens sharply when a longer data segment is modeled. The inspiraling non-Keplerian models are preferred to a purely Keplerian model based on both the Bayesian log-evidence comparison and the reduced chi-square statistic. Our results offer new insight into the nature of flaring events in the Galactic Center and the episodically produced hotspots associated with them.

</details>


### [101] [Joint Mass-Radius and Magnetic-Geometry Inference of PSR J0740+6620](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37928)
*Chun Huang,Tuomo Salmi,Alexander Y. Chen*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究将X射线热斑（hotspot）直接关联到脉冲星磁层结构（magnetospheric structure），对毫秒脉冲星PSR J0740+6620联合推断质量、半径与磁场几何。相比中心轴对称偶极—四极模型，偏置偶极（shifted dipole）模型以Δln Z=5.65（贝叶斯因子约285）显著占优，给出M=2.059 M☉、R=13.28 km，且要求远小于标称无力极化冠的X射线发射足迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: X射线脉冲轮廓建模（pulse-profile modeling）虽可测量中子星半径，但其物理解释受限于对表面热斑近乎唯象的描述。该工作旨在把热斑与脉冲星磁层结构直接联系起来，以提升质量—半径推断的物理基础，并将热斑形态转为磁层结构与发射物理的探针。

Method: 采用GPU加速实现，利用NICER与XMM-Newton观测数据，比较两种可产生非对跖（non-antipodal）发射的低维磁场构型：中心轴对称偶极—四极（dipole–quadrupole）模型与偏置偶极（shifted dipole）模型，并在给定先验下进行贝叶斯模型比较与参数推断。

Result: 偏置偶极模型在所用先验下被决定性偏好，Δln Z=5.65（贝叶斯因子约285），推断出M=2.059^{+0.068}_{-0.069} M☉、R=13.28^{+1.47}_{-1.07} km，与以往唯象分析大致一致，同时要求两极化冠近乎对称加热。边缘化后的偏置偶极后验与符合计时（timing-compatible）的代表性解均要求有效X射线发射足迹远小于按中心偶极标定的标称无力极化冠（force-free polar cap）。该紧凑足迹无法由探索性光子转换（photon-conversion）估计再现，暗示从磁层电流到可观测X射线发射的映射中缺失物理环节。

Conclusion: 物理动机明确的热斑模型可在保持质量—半径推断结果的同时，将热斑形态转化为探测脉冲星磁层结构与发射物理的手段；紧凑发射足迹所揭示的缺失物理表明，磁层电流与X射线发射之间的映射仍需进一步研究。

Abstract: X-ray pulse-profile modeling can measure neutron-star radii, but its physical interpretation remains limited by the largely phenomenological description of the surface hotspots. Here we connect the X-ray hotspots directly to the pulsar magnetospheric structure and jointly infer the mass, radius, and magnetic geometry of the massive millisecond pulsar PSR J0740+6620 from \emph{NICER} and \emph{XMM-Newton} observations. Using a GPU-accelerated implementation, we compare two low-dimensional magnetic configurations that can produce non-antipodal emission: a centered axisymmetric dipole--quadrupole and a shifted dipole. The shifted-dipole model is decisively favored under the adopted priors, with $Δ\ln Z=5.65$ (Bayes factor $\sim285$), and yields $M=2.059^{+0.068}_{-0.069}\,M_{\odot}$ and $R=13.28^{+1.47}_{-1.07}\,\mathrm{km}$, broadly consistent with previous phenomenological analyses, while requiring nearly symmetric heating of the two polar caps. Both the marginalized shifted-dipole posterior and the timing-compatible representative require an effective X-ray-emitting footprint much smaller than the nominal force-free polar cap calibrated for a centered dipole. This compact footprint is not reproduced by our exploratory photon-conversion estimates and instead points to missing physics in the mapping from magnetospheric currents to observable X-ray emission. Our results show that physically motivated hotspots can preserve the mass--radius inference while turning hotspot morphology into a probe of pulsar magnetospheric structure and emission physics.

</details>


<div id='physics.ins-det'></div>

# physics.ins-det [[Back]](#toc)

### [102] [Muon Bulb: A Cosmic-Ray Detector Inside a Light Bulb](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35784)
*Yuvaraj Elangovan*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 将标准商用LED灯泡改装为自持式宇宙线缪子探测器（Muon Bulb），缪子通过时灯泡实时发出可见闪光，并可无线传输计数与通量数据，用于科学传播与课堂教学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 宇宙线缪子（cosmic-ray muon）是到达地表最丰富的高能粒子之一，海平面通量约为每分钟每平方厘米一个缪子，但肉眼完全不可见，这为粒子物理（particle physics）的公众科普与参与带来持续困难。因此需要一个无需专用基础设施、可直观呈现缪子事件的普及化装置。

Method: 探测器封装于标准商用LED灯泡外壳内：塑料闪烁体（plastic scintillator）板耦合硅光电倍增管（Silicon Photomultiplier, SiPM）探测缪子电离产生的闪烁光子；SiPM信号经含放大器与前缘甄别器（leading-edge discriminator）的紧凑模拟前端（analog front-end）处理，以区分真实缪子脉冲与背景；有效触发信号送入ESP32/RP2350微控制器驱动内部LED闪光，并支持无线数据传输。

Result: 装置可在缪子通过时产生鲜明、即时的可见闪光；读出系统支持无线数据传输，实现对缪子事件率与通量的实时监测。多个Muon Bulb可同时组网运行，用于课堂协作式通量测量。

Conclusion: Muon Bulb可作为高影响力的科普（outreach）仪器，适用于科学博物馆、天文馆、K-12课堂与公众科学节等场景，无需专业基础设施。其无线组网能力支持学生开展真实数据驱动的科学探究，把日常家用物品转变为粒子物理工具，使不可见的宇宙对所有人可见。

Abstract: Cosmic-ray muons are among the most abundant high-energy particles reaching Earth's surface, with a flux of approximately one muon per square centimeter per minute at sea level, yet they remain entirely invisible to the naked eye, posing a persistent challenge for public engagement in particle physics. We present the Muon Bulb, a self-contained cosmic ray muon detector built within the enclosure of a standard commercial LED bulb, designed to produce a vivid and immediate visible light flash upon the passage of a cosmic-ray muon. The detector integrates a plastic scintillator tile coupled to a Silicon Photomultiplier (SiPM) for efficient detection of scintillation photons produced by ionizing muons. The SiPM signal is processed by a compact analog front-end comprising an amplifier and a leading-edge discriminator, which isolates genuine muon-induced pulses from background. Validated trigger signals are passed to an ESP32/RP2350 microcontroller, which drives an internal LED, causing the bulb to flash visibly in real time. The readout additionally enables wireless data transmission for real-time monitoring of muon event rates and flux measurements. The Muon Bulb is designed to serve as a high-impact outreach instrument across science museums, planetariums, K-12 classrooms and public science festivals without requiring specialized infrastructure. Its wireless capability enables networked classroom activities where multiple Muon Bulbs can be operated simultaneously, allowing students to collaboratively measure muon flux and engage in genuine data-driven scientific inquiry. The Muon Bulb transforms a commercial house hold item into a powerful tool for particle physics, making the invisible universe visible to everyone.

</details>


### [103] [Performance of the MPD experiment in dielectron measurements at NICA](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35829)
*Sudhir Pandurang Rode,Itzhak Tserruya,Victor Riabov,Yonghong Wang,Chi Yang*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本研究评估了 NICA 上多用途探测器（Multi-Purpose Detector, MPD）在 $\sqrt{s_{NN}}$ = 9.2 GeV 的 $^{209}$Bi+$^{209}$Bi 模拟碰撞中开展双电子（dielectron）测量的性能：以多层感知机（multilayer-perceptron）分类器优化电子鉴别，并通过对分析策略在电子对层面标记光子转换（photon conversion）与 $\pi^0$ Dalitz 衰变，将信噪比提升约 3.5 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MPD 实验旨在通过重离子测量研究高净重子密度下的强相互作用物质，双电子因其贯穿碰撞全时空演化而成为关键探针。然而其测量受到大量组合本底（combinatorial background）的严重挑战，该本底主要来自重建不完整的光子转换与轻中性介子的 Dalitz 衰变。

Method: 基于模拟的最小偏差（minimum-bias）$^{209}$Bi+$^{209}$Bi 碰撞数据，结合 TPC、TOF 探测器与 ECal 的信息进行电子鉴别，并采用多层感知机分类器优化鉴别效果。针对主要组合本底来源，开发了利用部分重建电子径迹的对分析策略（pair-analysis strategy），在电子对层面综合使用对张角、TPC dE/dx 信号以及电子对不变质量的近似重建来标记本底径迹。

Result: 相对于顺序的一维选择（sequential one-dimensional selections），多层感知机分类器在宽动量范围内显著提升电子探测效率，同时保持接近 1 的电子样本纯度。在 0.2 &lt; $m_{ee}$ &lt; 0.7 GeV/$c^2$ 的质量区间内，所提出的对分析方法在现有径迹重建算法下将信噪比提升约 3.5 倍。

Conclusion: 该研究表明 MPD 实验在 NICA 能量下开展双电子测量具有很强潜力，同时凸显了进一步改进低横动量（low-$p_{\rm T}$）径迹重建的重要性。

Abstract: The Multi-Purpose Detector (MPD) experiment at NICA is designed to investigate strongly interacting matter at high net-baryon density through a broad program of heavy-ion measurements. Dielectrons constitute one of the key probes in this program, with contributions emitted throughout the entire space-time evolution of the collision. Their experimental measurement is, however, challenged by a large combinatorial background arising primarily from incompletely reconstructed photon conversions and Dalitz decays of light neutral mesons. In this work, we study the performance and capabilities of the MPD experiment for dielectron measurements using simulated minimum-bias $^{209}$Bi+$^{209}$Bi collisions at $\sqrt{s_{NN}}$ = 9.2 GeV. Electron identification is performed using the combined information from the TPC, the TOF detector and the ECal. A multilayer-perceptron classifier is used to optimize electron identification, leading to a substantial increase in electron detection efficiency relative to sequential one-dimensional selections while preserving an electron-sample purity close to unity over a broad momentum range. To address the dominant sources of combinatorial background, a pair-analysis strategy is developed that exploits partially reconstructed electron tracks. The method combines the pair opening angle, the TPC dE/dx signal, and an approximate reconstruction of the pair invariant mass to tag tracks from photon conversions and $π^0$ Dalitz decays at the pair level. In the mass interval 0.2 $< m_{ee}<0.7 $ GeV/$c^2$, the method improves the signal-to-background ratio by a factor of about 3.5 with the current track reconstruction algorithm. The study demonstrates the strong potential of the MPD experiment for dielectron measurements at NICA energies and highlights the importance of further improvements in low-$p_{\rm T}$ track reconstruction.

</details>


### [104] [WLCG Mini-Capability Challenge: Host Tuning to Im- prove WAN Data Transfers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35859)
*Andrew Malone Melo,Carlos Fernando Gamboa,Diego Devila Foyo,Eduardo Bach,Eli Dart,Shawn McKee,Garhan Attebury,Philippe Laurens,Wendy Wu,Syed Asif Shaw,Hiro Ito*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 该研究通过 WLCG 小型能力挑战（mini-capability challenge），在 ATLAS 与 CMS 多个站点的主机层开展 TCP、队列、卸载与环形缓冲区调优，结果表明调优收益取决于本地瓶颈所在位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WLCG 计算模型要求高通量数据移动，但端到端性能常受主机层配置（host-level configuration）而非网络骨干限制。研究动机在于评估主机优化对实际传输吞吐量的影响。

Method: 方法结合 ESnet Fasterdata 指南与 OSG 托管的 fasterdata-tuning.sh 框架，对采用 EL8/9、25 Gbps 及以上网卡的主机实施 TCP、队列、卸载与环形缓冲区调优，并支持保存与恢复以实现安全的基线对比；NET2 参与了后续跨大西洋测试。

Result: 在 DTN 与存储均具备能力的 UNL，FTS 吞吐量由 60 Gbps 升至 90 Gbps；在存储成为约束的 UCSD，iperf3 吞吐量由 20 Gbps 提升至 90 Gbps，而 FTS 无变化；在网卡成为约束的 Vanderbilt（25 G 网卡），该调优配置反而降低了吞吐量，提示需按站点评估适用性。挑战还发现并解决了 AGLT2 的 dCache 代理配置错误。

Conclusion: 调优收益关键取决于本地瓶颈，因而需按站点逐一评估其适用性；小型能力挑战除性能测量外，还具有诊断功能。

Abstract: High-throughput data movement is a defining requirement of the WLCG computing model, yet end-to-end performance is often constrained less by the network backbone than by host-level configuration. We present the results of a WLCG mini-capability challenge focused on host optimization at ATLAS and CMS sites (FNAL, UCSD, UNL, BNL, AGLT2, MWT2, and Vanderbilt), using EL8/9 hosts with 25\,Gbps or higher NICs. NET2 participated in a subsequent trans-Atlantic test. The methodology combines ESnet Fasterdata guidance with the OSG-hosted \texttt{fasterdata-tuning.sh} framework, applying TCP, queue, offload, and ring-buffer tuning with save and restore support for safe baseline comparisons. Results show that tuning benefit depends critically on the local bottleneck. Where DTNs and storage were both capable (UNL), FTS throughput rose from 60 to 90\,Gbps. Where storage was the binding constraint (UCSD), iperf3 throughput improved from 20 to 90\,Gbps while FTS was unchanged. Where the NIC was the binding constraint (Vanderbilt, 25\,G NIC), the tuning profile actively reduced throughput, motivating a review of per-site applicability. The challenge also surfaced and resolved a dCache proxying misconfiguration at AGLT2, demonstrating that mini-challenges serve a diagnostic function beyond raw performance measurement.

</details>


### [105] [Development of LGAD Sensor Technology on 12 inch Wafers for 3D Integration with advanced CMOS processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35861)
*Ron Lipton,Julie Segal,Angela Kok,Troy England,Artur Apresyan,Davide Braga,Farah Fahim,Shuoxing Wu,Christopher Kenney,Bojan Markovic,Ariel Gustavo Schwartzman,Lorenzo Rota,Arthur Carpenter*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本工作设计、仿真并初步测试了基于 65 nm CMOS 工艺、12 英寸晶圆制造的低增益雪崩二极管（Low Gain Avalanche Diode, LGAD）传感器，目标是验证传感器与 28 nm 读出集成电路（Readout Integrated Circuit, ROIC）的整片晶圆对晶圆混合键合（wafer to wafer hybrid bonding），并提交了多种 LGAD 结构及测试结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该器件研发属于一项演示传感器与 28 nm 读出集成电路（ROIC）整片晶圆对晶圆混合键合（wafer to wafer hybrid bonding）的项目，以推动高集成度传感器—电子学互连方案。

Method: 在 12 英寸晶圆上采用 65 nm CMOS 工艺制造 LGAD 传感器，并开展设计、仿真与初步测试；提交与测试的器件涵盖标准（reach-through）LGAD、交流耦合（AC coupled）LGAD、深结（Deep Junction）LGAD 以及多种测试结构。

Result: 摘要仅说明已完成该批传感器的设计、仿真与初步测试，并涵盖上述多种 LGAD 类型与测试结构，未给出具体测量数值或性能指标。

Conclusion: 65 nm CMOS 工艺、12 英寸晶圆路线可用于制备多类型 LGAD 传感器，为后续与 28 nm ROIC 的整片混合键合提供器件基础。

Abstract: We report on the design, simulation, and initial testing of Low Gain Avalanche Diode (LGAD) sensors fabricated in 65 nm CMOS technology on 12 inch wafers. These sensors were developed as part of a project to demonstrate full wafer to wafer hybrid bonding of sensors and 28 nm Readout Integrated Circuits (ROICs). The sensor submission and test included standard (reach-through) LGADs, AC coupled LGADs, and Deep Junction LGADs as well as various test structures.

</details>


### [106] [Modeling the Effects of Temperature and Electric Field on the Dark Count Rate of Photomultiplier Tubes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35871)
*Yuto Maekawa,Masamitsu Fukazawa,Yasuhiro Nishimura*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 该研究以 Richardson-Dushman 方程（Richardson-Dushman equation）为基础并引入肖特基效应（Schottky effect），建立光电倍增管（PMT）暗计数率随温度与外电场变化的模型，并通过 Hamamatsu R14374 型 PMT 实验验证，进而加入误差函数（error function）以预测实际工况下的暗计数率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管光电倍增管（photomultiplier tube, PMT）在单光子计数应用中灵敏度足够，但光阴极（photocathode）的热电子发射（thermionic emission）是主要背景来源之一。研究动机在于刻画并预测温度与外电场对暗计数率（dark count rate）的影响。

Method: 基于 Richardson-Dushman 方程（Richardson-Dushman equation）并纳入肖特基效应（Schottky effect），将给定 PMT 的暗计数率建模为温度与外电场的函数；使用配备双碱光阴极（bialkali photocathode）的八厘米 Hamamatsu R14374 PMT 进行测量，温度范围为 10 至 40 摄氏度，并通过将光阴极与周围介质间电位差在 0 至 1100 V 间变化，在光学窗口外表面施加外电场。

Result: 结果显示，在 200 V 电位差范围内，外部电位差的影响相对较小，暗计数率的增加在所有测量温度下均低于 50%。

Conclusion: 通过在所提模型中引入误差函数（error function），可在温度与外电场变化的工作条件下预测暗计数率。

Abstract: Although photomultiplier tubes (PMTs) are sufficiently sensitive for applications based on counting single photons, the thermionic emission of electrons from the photocathode is one of the dominant background sources. In this study, we modeled the dark count rate of a given PMT as a function of the temperature and external electric field based on the Richardson-Dushman equation and incorporated the Schottky effect. To validate the model, measurements were performed using an eight-centimeter Hamamatsu R14374 PMT equipped with a bialkali photocathode. The dark count rate was measured over a range of temperatures from 10 to 40 degree Celsius. An external electric field was applied to the outside surface of the optical window with the photocathode by varying the potential difference between the photocathode and the surrounding medium from 0 to 1100 V. The result confirmed that the influence of the external potential difference remained relatively small within a difference of 200 V, with the increase in the dark count rate remaining below 50 % across all measured temperatures. This behavior was incorporated into the proposed model by including an error function to predict the dark count rate under operating conditions with varying temperatures and external electric fields.

</details>


### [107] [A portable neutron scatter camera with isotropic sensitivity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36342)
*S. G. Yoon,L. Larizgoitia,C. M. Lewis,F. Monrabal,J. I. Collar*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种专门为中子场（neutron fields）研究设计的中子散射相机（neutron scatter camera），通过硬件与逻辑触发的组合实现各向同性响应，并可对散裂设施中多源中子场进行成像与重建。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在散裂设施（spallation facilities）的实验区域，多个中子源可能同时贡献中子通量，需要全面理解中子场（通量、能量、方向）。此类信息可用于选址及降低影响敏感仪器（如相干弹性中微子-核散射（CEνNS）探测器）的中子本底（neutron backgrounds）。

Method: 采用硬件与逻辑触发（logic triggers）相结合的方式，使中子散射相机（neutron scatter camera）具备该应用所需的各向同性响应（isotropic response）；并借助内置的360°全景相机（panoramic camera）在图像上准确重建中子源位置。

Result: 器件可对各向同性的中子场进行表征，并准确重建中子源位置；所获中子场信息可用于站点选择与中子本底削减。

Conclusion: 该中子散射相机能为散裂设施中多源中子场的理解提供手段，并服务于拟建于欧洲散裂源（European Spallation Source）的CEνNS探测器等敏感仪器的中子本底评估与抑制。

Abstract: We describe the design and characterization of a neutron scatter camera specifically developed for the study of neutron fields (flux, energy, direction) in experimental areas of spallation facilities, where multiple neutron sources can simultaneously contribute. A combination of hardware and logic triggers provides the isotropic response required for this application. Neutron source locations are accurately reconstructed on images from a built-in 360$^\circ$ panoramic camera. The full understanding of neutron fields that the device provides can be used for site selection and abatement of neutron backgrounds affecting sensitive instruments such as coherent elastic neutrino-nucleus scattering (CE$ν$NS) detectors to be located at the upcoming European Spallation Source.

</details>


### [108] [Quantitative Interleaved-Pulse Acquisition Extends the Range of Isotope-Ratio Measurements in ToF-SIMS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37413)
*Anton V. Ievlev*

Main category: physics.ins-det

TL;DR: 该工作提出“交错脉冲采集定量重构”（quantitative reconstruction interleaved-pulse acquisition）方法，通过多脉冲宽度采集与剂量因子校准，解决了飞行时间二次离子质谱（ToF-SIMS）中同位素强度相差数个量级时难以定量同位素比的问题。方法无需硬件改动，可在单个剥蚀坑（crater）内实现宽范围、深度分辨的同位素比测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同位素比（isotope ratios）是氧化物输运、电池材料与生物系统示踪研究的基础，但 ToF-SIMS 在定量强度相差数个量级的同位素对时存在固有矛盾：脉冲过长可精确计数少量同位素却使主同位素饱和，脉冲过短则主同位素保持线性但少量同位素计数不足。

Method: 采用多脉冲宽度采集，在每个剥蚀坑内测量剂量因子（dose factor）以连接不同采集阶段，且每个同位素仅取自其保持线性的阶段；脉冲宽度依据实测传递曲线（transfer curves）与峰形选取。

Result: 在热氧化硅（thermal SiO2）上，优化的（6, 35）ns 脉冲对达到与短脉冲采集相同的逐层精度，分析帧数减少 13 倍；在 18O 富集的 WOx 薄膜上，低丰度端可定量范围扩展约 14 倍。在约 1:500 至 1:1 的同位素比范围内，交错重构与对应的线性参考测量一致，未观察到交错引入的可测偏差，氧、锶、钛的天然丰度比复现误差在百分之几以内。

Conclusion: 该方法无需额外硬件，可在单次剥蚀坑内实现宽范围、深度分辨的同位素比测量，为 ToF-SIMS 同位素定量提供了可行途径。

Abstract: Isotope ratios underpin tracer studies of transport in oxides, battery materials, and biological systems, yet time-of-flight secondary ion mass spectrometry (ToF-SIMS) struggles to quantify ratios whose members differ in intensity by orders of magnitude: a primary-ion pulse long enough to count the minor isotope precisely can saturate the major isotope, whereas a pulse short enough to keep the major isotope linear yields too few minor-isotope counts. We show that multi-pulse-width acquisition yields quantitative isotope ratios when a dose factor measured within each crater links the phases and each isotope is taken only from phases in which it remains linear; we call this quantitative reconstruction interleaved-pulse acquisition. Pulse widths are selected from measured transfer curves and peak shapes. On thermal SiO2, a tuned (6, 35) ns pair achieves the same per-layer precision as short-pulse acquisition with a 13-fold reduction in the number of analysis frames. On 18O-enriched WOx films, interleaving extends the quantifiable range at the low-fraction end by about 14-fold. Across isotope ratios spanning approximately 1:500 to 1:1, the interleaved reconstruction agrees with the corresponding linear reference measurements, showing no measurable bias introduced by interleaving, and natural-abundance ratios of oxygen, strontium and titanium are reproduced within several percent. The method requires no hardware additions and enables depth-resolved isotope-ratio measurements over a wide range within a single crater.

</details>


<div id='gr-qc'></div>

# gr-qc [[Back]](#toc)

### [109] [Five-dimensional thick branes in the scalar representation of $f(Q,B)$ gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35862)
*F. C. E. Lima,F. M. Belchior,C. A. S. Almeida*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在 $f(Q,B)$ 引力（$Q$ 为非度规标量，$B$ 为满足 $R=Q-B$ 的边界项）的标量表示下研究余维一厚膜（thick braneworld），通过辅助标量场与 Legendre 变换导出膜方程，并分析张量扰动与无质量引力子的局域化及稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目的是在 $f(Q,B)$ 引力框架下构建并考察光滑、渐近 AdS（asymptotically AdS）的厚膜模型，并检验其张量扰动行为，从而扩展厚膜（thick brane）在非度规引力（nonmetricity gravity）中的实现方式。

Method: 将引力部分用两个辅助标量场 $\varphi=f_Q$、$\psi=f_B$ 及由 $f(Q,B)$ 函数经 Legendre 变换（Legendre transformation）得到的相互作用势 $U(\varphi,\psi)$ 表示；在重合规范（coincident gauge）下，假定具有四维 Poincaré 对称性的五维翘曲几何（warped geometry），推导由正则体标量场（canonical bulk scalar field）作为源的完整膜方程。

Result: 给出了两个具有局域化标量剖面（localized scalar profiles）的光滑渐近 AdS 厚膜模型；并对张量扰动（tensor perturbations）进行分析，表明无质量引力子（massless graviton）可实现局域化且具有稳定性。

Conclusion: 该工作表明 $f(Q,B)$ 引力的标量表示可支持光滑渐近 AdS 厚膜解，且其张量扇区中无质量引力子局域化并保持稳定。

Abstract: One examines a codimension-one thick braneworld in the scalar representation of $f(Q,B)$ gravity, where $Q$ is the nonmetricity scalar, and $B$ is the boundary term satisfying $R=Q-B$. We write the gravitational sector in terms of two auxiliary scalar fields, $\varphi=f_Q$ and $ψ=f_B$, and an interaction potential $U(\varphi,ψ)$ obtained by a Legendre transformation from the $f(Q,B)$ function. Working in the coincident gauge and assuming a five-dimensional warped geometry with four-dimensional Poincaré symmetry, we derive the complete set of brane equations sourced by a canonical bulk scalar field. To accomplish our purpose, we address two models of smooth asymptotically AdS thick brane with localized scalar profiles. Furthermore, we also analyze tensor perturbations and demonstrate the localization and stability of the massless graviton.

</details>


### [110] [Operational Meaning of the Cosmological Evolution of Dimensional Quantities: A Generalized Cosmological Time Framework for Cross-Epoch Comparisons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35903)
*Seokcheon Lee*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在 Robertson–Walker 时空中引入广义宇宙时（Generalized Cosmological Time），区分宇宙时参数 T_G 与固有时 tau，并以此重新表述 meVSL（modified varying speed of light）中时间速率因子 N_G 的作用，指出宇宙时间膨胀观测对"常数随时间变化"的检验依赖于时间基准的定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有量纲常数（dimensional constant）的数值依赖于所选单位制，因此其孤立的时间变化本身并非单位不变量（unit-invariant observable），需要在一个具有时间重参数化自由的几何框架中重新审视该问题。

Method: 在 Robertson–Walker（RW）时空的均匀各向同性几何中利用沿基本同余（fundamental congruence）的时间重参数化自由，引入广义宇宙时，定义时间速率因子 N_G = d tau/d T_G，将时间速率关系嵌入标准超曲面正交 RW 同余结构，并保持坐标 lapse 自由度、保留的时间参数与现象学 meVSL 赋值之间的区分。

Result: 在 meVSL 中 N_G(a) = a^(b/4)，同一 b 控制各量纲量在不同宇宙时期（cross-epoch）的关联标度。宇宙时间膨胀给出 Delta tau_o = (1+z) Delta tau_e，而同一零测地线关联区间在 T_G 下满足 Delta T_G,o/Delta T_G,e = (1+z) N_G,e/N_G,o。若跨红移比较源所用的内禀时标定义于 T_G，取当前归一化 N_G,o = 1 得 (1+z)^(1-b/4)；若定义于固有时，则恢复通常的 (1+z) 定律。

Conclusion: meVSL 关联标度的观测检验需要完整的模型预言，而非仅对有量纲量施加孤立约束；观测结果对时间基准（T_G 或固有时）的选择敏感。

Abstract: The numerical value of a dimensional constant depends on the adopted units, so its isolated temporal variation is not by itself a unit-invariant observable. We reconsider this issue in Robertson--Walker spacetime, whose homogeneous and isotropic geometry admits temporal reparametrization freedom along the fundamental congruence. Generalized Cosmological Time retains a common cosmological time parameter T_G and defines the temporal-rate factor N_G = d tau/d T_G, with the proper-time representation as the unit-rate member. In this formulation, the temporal-rate relation is embedded in the standard hypersurface-orthogonal RW congruence structure, while coordinate lapse freedom is kept distinct from the retained temporal parameter and from the phenomenological meVSL assignment. In meVSL, N_G(a) = a^(b/4), with the same b governing correlated cross-epoch scalings of dimensional quantities. Cosmological time dilation provides the explicit timing application: Delta tau_o = (1+z) Delta tau_e, while the same null-linked intervals in T_G satisfy Delta T_G,o/Delta T_G,e = (1+z) N_G,e/N_G,o. If the intrinsic timescale used to compare sources across redshift is defined in T_G, the present normalization N_G,o = 1 gives (1+z)^(1-b/4); if defined in proper time, the usual (1+z) law is recovered. Observational tests of the correlated meVSL scalings require complete model predictions rather than constraints on isolated dimensional quantities.

</details>


### [111] [Low Frequency Gravitational Wave Detection with Gravito-Magnetism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35962)
*Reza Ebadi,Surjeet Rajendran*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文提出利用引力波的相对论性特征，通过探测其引力磁场分量（gravito-magnetic component），将低频引力波信号从小行星带引起的牛顿引力梯度噪声（GGN）中分离出来，并建议以原子干涉仪（atom interferometer）作为纯净惯性参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在内太阳系利用检验质量探测低频段（10 nHz 至 1 微赫兹）引力波时，来自小行星带的牛顿引力梯度噪声（gravitational gradient noise, GGN）构成严重限制。信号与噪声背景之间的简并难以通过传统手段破除，因此需要新的探测思路。

Method: 核心思路是利用引力波的相对论性质破除信号与噪声的简并：小行星的非相对论运动主要产生引力电场（gravito-electric field），其引力磁场（gravito-magnetic）分量受速度压低；而相对论性引力波产生的引力电场与引力磁场分量量级相等。据此提出通过检验质量的洛伦兹加速度（Lorentz acceleration）探测低频引力波的引力磁场分量，并采用三卫星星座构型进行测量，同时建议使用原子干涉仪（atom interferometer）作为隔离非引力噪声的惯性参考。

Result: 引力磁场分量的测量在计量学上是可行的，因为预期低频引力波的天体物理源具有的特征应变（characteristic strain）显著高于较高频段通常预期的应变水平。在三卫星星座中，该测量能够明确地从引力梯度噪声中提取出引力波信号。

Conclusion: 通过探测低频引力波的引力磁场分量，可在三卫星星座构型中无歧义地分离引力波信号与小行星引力梯度噪声；该方法仍需与引力梯度噪声良好隔离的检验质量，作者提出以原子干涉仪作为此类纯净惯性参考。

Abstract: The detection of gravitational waves in the low-frequency band (10 nHz to 1 micro-Hz) using inner solar system proof masses is severely limited by Newtonian gravity gradient noise (GGN) sourced by the asteroid belt. In this paper, we propose a novel detection concept that exploits the relativistic nature of gravitational waves to break the degeneracy between the signal and the asteroid GGN background. The non-relativistic motion of asteroids dominantly sources gravito-electric fields, with velocity suppressed gravito-magnetic components. Relativistic gravitational waves produce gravito-electric and gravito-magnetic components of equal magnitude. We propose detecting the gravito-magnetic component of low frequency gravitational waves using the Lorentz acceleration of test bodies. The measurement of this Lorentz acceleration is metrologically possible due to the fact that expected astrophysical sources of low frequency gravitational waves have considerably higher characteristic strains than the strains typically expected at higher frequencies. We show that this measurement allows for unambiguous extraction of the gravitational wave signal from the gravity gradient noise in a three satellite constellation. The gravito-magnetic measurement still requires a proof mass that is well isolated from non-gravitational noise. We propose the use of atom interferometers to serve as such a pristine inertial reference.

</details>


### [112] [Black Hole Quasinormal Mode Resonances and Reconnections in Coupled Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35971)
*Leah Jenks,Hayato Motohashi,Kazufumi Takahashi,Takuya Takahashi,Emanuele Berti*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在扰动动力学陈-西蒙斯（dynamical Chern-Simons, dCS）理论的具体模型及一个最小玩具模型中，研究黑洞准正规模（quasinormal modes, QNMs）轨迹的双螺旋（double spiral）现象学，揭示其避免交叉与模式重连的普遍性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 黑洞准正规模是探测黑洞结构及其所处环境的窗口；在广义相对论之外的耦合系统中，QNM具有更丰富的动力学，如避免交叉、共振激发与模式重连。近期工作表明重连的QNM轨迹可在复频率平面上勾勒出独特的双螺旋结构，本文即针对该现象展开研究。

Method: 在扰动dCS理论的具体模型中，针对若干扰动参数值刻画异常点（exceptional points）、避免交叉（avoided crossings）与重连，并计算激发因子（excitation factors）；同时构建一个含平方势垒的耦合自由度玩具模型（toy model），以检验上述现象是否依赖于dCS的特定性质。

Result: 在dCS模型中，双螺旋端点的极限频率与扰动强度无关；激发因子呈现出标量模与引力模之间共振所特有的标度行为。玩具模型同样可复现避免交叉与重连现象。

Conclusion: 这些现象可在耦合系统中更普遍地出现，且对系统参数高度敏感；其存在并不依赖于dCS的具体特征。

Abstract: Black hole quasinormal modes (QNMs) provide a window into the underlying structure of black holes and the environments in which they arise. QNMs have particularly rich dynamics in coupled systems beyond general relativity, in which they are known to undergo avoided crossings, resonant excitation, and mode reconnections. Recent work has shown that reconnecting QNM trajectories can trace out a distinctive double spiral structure in the complex frequency plane. In this work, we study this phenomenology in a concrete model of perturbed dynamical Chern-Simons (dCS) theory, as well as in a minimal toy model. In the former, we characterize the exceptional points, avoided crossings and reconnections for several values of the perturbation parameters, and find that the limiting frequency of the double spiral endpoints is independent of the perturbation strength. We compute the excitation factors and find that they follow the scaling characteristic of a resonance between the scalar and gravitational modes. To determine whether these phenomena depend on the specific features of dCS, we construct a toy model of coupled degrees of freedom with square potential barriers, and find that we can reproduce the avoided crossings and reconnections, indicating that these phenomena can occur more generally in coupled systems and are highly sensitive to the system parameters.

</details>


### [113] [Spectral instabilities in the time domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35993)
*Taillte May,Adrien Kuntz,Nicola Franchini,Valentin Boyanov,Vitor Cardoso*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在一维双 Pöschl-Teller 模型（double Pöschl-Teller model）中研究谱不稳定性（spectral instability）对时域波形（time-domain waveform）的影响，解析追踪主势垒与弱空间分离扰动之间的多次散射，并识别出首次因果往返后出现的长期项（secular contribution）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究目的在于厘清谱不稳定性如何在时域波形中体现，特别是区分第一回声（first echo）到达观察者前后准正模（quasinormal mode）频率的行为差异。

Method: 采用一维双 Pöschl-Teller 势模型，解析地追踪主势垒与一个弱且空间分离的扰动之间的逐次散射过程，并通过数值时域演化（numerical time-domain evolutions）验证解析计算结果。

Result: 所有泛音（overtones）在第一回声之前保持未扰动频率；第一回声到达后出现随时间线性增长的长期修正。当该修正为微扰量时，可解释为准正模频率的偏移，其系数与频域（frequency-domain）结果一致；更一般地，它产生有限的时变有效频率（effective frequencies），既不等于未扰动也不等于完全扰动后的准正模谱。

Conclusion: 谱不稳定性对时域波形的影响具有明确的因果时间结构，其长期修正的系数在微扰极限下与频域分析吻合，而一般情形下对应的有效频率超出未扰动与完全扰动的准正模谱之外。

Abstract: We investigate the effect of a spectral instability on the time domain waveform using a one-dimensional double Pöschl-Teller model. By analytically following successive scatterings between the primary potential barrier and a weak, spatially separated perturbation, we identify a secular contribution that first appears after one causal round trip between the barriers. Before the first echo, all overtones remain at their unperturbed frequencies. After the first echo reaches the observer, there is a secular, linear-in-time correction. When this correction is perturbative, it can be interpreted as a shift of the quasinormal mode frequency. In that case, we show that its coefficient reproduces the frequency-domain result. More generally, it generates finite-time effective frequencies that need not coincide with either the unperturbed or fully perturbed quasi-normal mode spectrum. We confirm the results of our analytic calculation using numerical time-domain evolutions.

</details>


### [114] [Stochastic gravitational-wave background from subsolar neutron stars in collapsar disks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36005)
*Valerio De Luca,Loris Del Grosso,Emanuele Berti*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文计算了坍缩星（collapsar）中黑洞周围中微子冷却吸积盘碎裂所产生的亚太阳质量中子星（subsolar neutron star）并合所贡献的随机引力波背景（stochastic gravitational-wave background）能量密度，并利用 LIGO–Virgo–KAGRA 第四次观测运行给出坍缩星比例上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 吸积盘碎裂被提出作为产生远低于标准核坍缩下限质量中子星的天体物理通道，但其群体可观测性尚未被系统量化。本文旨在通过随机引力波背景这一群体层面探针，检验该通道并在现有数据下施加约束。

Method: 针对两类并合通道（亚太阳质量中子星与中心黑洞并合、双中子星并合）计算背景能量密度，其形成历史与观测到的长伽马射线暴（long gamma-ray bursts）速率挂钩，并与 LIGO–Virgo–KAGRA 第四次观测运行数据进行对比。

Result: 亚太阳质量中子星与中心黑洞的并合是最响亮的通道，已被当前数据约束且处于下一代观测设备探测范围内；双中子星通道最多暗两个数量级，其中最宽的双星系统仍可被下一代探测器触及。由此导出两个关于坍缩星盘产生此类并合比例的上限。

Conclusion: 该背景为盘生亚太阳质量中子星提供了群体层面的探针，下一代探测器有望将其明确纳入观测视野；坍缩星盘自身的非轴对称形变引力波可作为独立辅助特征，支持与未来分赫兹（deci-Hz）探测器的互相关研究。

Abstract: The fragmentation of the neutrino-cooled accretion disks that surround the black holes formed in collapsars has recently been proposed as an astrophysical channel for the production of neutron stars with masses well below the standard core-collapse floor. These disk-born objects merge either with one another or with the central black hole, and the superposition of all such mergers across cosmic history sources a stochastic gravitational-wave background. In this work we compute the energy density of this background for both merger channels, adopting a formation history tied to the observed rate of long gamma-ray bursts. We find that the merger of a subsolar neutron star with the central black hole is the loudest channel, already constrained by current data and within reach of next-generation observatories, while the binary neutron-star channel is at most two orders of magnitude fainter, with its widest binaries remaining accessible to next-generation detectors. Confronting these predictions with the fourth observing run of the LIGO--Virgo--KAGRA Collaboration, we derive two upper bounds on the fraction of collapsars whose disks produce such mergers: a robust one from the non-detection of an isotropic background, and a tighter one that could be achieved by a dedicated search for resolvable subsolar events in current data. Gravitational waves from non-axisymmetric deformations of the collapsar disk itself would provide an additional independent signature for the collapsar scenario, enabling cross-correlation studies with future deci-Hz detectors. Overall, the background computed here provides a population-level probe of disk-born subsolar neutron stars, which next-generation detectors could bring firmly into view.

</details>


### [115] [Toward the CompactHedron: Bounds on Neutron Stars from Microscopic Principles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36025)
*Thomas Apostolidis,Alessandro Longo,Borna Salehian,Luca Santoni*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在点粒子有效场论框架下，研究因果性与正性对引力天体潮汐勒夫数（tidal Love numbers）的约束。要求潮汐响应具有推迟性、高频衰减并满足被动性（passivity），即可导出勒夫数的非平凡界限，该界限对任意局域天体成立。应用于中子星时，所得约束可转化为对恒星第一振荡模的限制，且独立于文献中常用的稳定性与热力学条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 潮汐勒夫数刻画天体在外部潮汐场下的形变响应，但其取值是否受基本物理原理（因果性、正性）限制尚缺乏系统框架。作者希望从第一性原理出发，为紧致天体的勒夫数建立普适界限，而非依赖特定物态方程或附加假设。

Method: 采用点粒子有效场论（point-particle effective field theory）方法，将勒夫数对应为曲率张量及其导数所构成算符的威尔逊系数（Wilson coefficients）。聚焦四极引力-电扇区（quadrupolar gravito-electric sector），以推迟性、高频衰减及被动性（passivity）为条件推导勒夫数界限；并通过对多组多方及表格化中子星物态方程计算至频率展开四阶的动力学勒夫数。

Result: 上述条件限定了有效理论参数空间中的一个允许区域，适用于任意局域天体。对中子星，在低致密度（low-compactness）区域，界限转化为对恒星第一振荡模的约束；该约束在概念上独立于文献中常用的稳定性与热力学条件。文中亦讨论了耗散（dissipation）存在时界限的修正方式。
