Conclusion: 研究结果为从基本原理系统推导紧致天体约束奠定了框架基础，表明因果性与正性可作为限制潮汐响应的独立物理依据。

Abstract: We investigate the implications of causality and positivity for the tidal Love numbers of gravitating objects. We work within the framework of point-particle effective field theory, where the Love numbers correspond to the Wilson coefficients of operators constructed from the curvature tensor and its derivatives. For simplicity, we focus on the quadrupolar gravito-electric sector, which provides the leading contribution to tidal deformability. We show that requiring the tidal response to be retarded and to decay at high frequencies, together with the assumption of passivity, leads to nontrivial bounds on the Love numbers. These bounds delineate an allowed region in the parameter space of the effective theory and apply to any localized object. In particular, we study their application to neutron stars. We compute the dynamical Love numbers through fourth order in the frequency expansion for a representative set of polytropic and tabulated neutron-star equations of state and show that, in the low-compactness regime, the bounds translate into constraints on the first oscillation mode of the star. Importantly, these constraints are conceptually independent of the stability and thermodynamic conditions commonly imposed in the literature. While our focus is primarily on conservative systems, we also discuss how the bounds are modified in the presence of dissipation. Our results lay the ground for a systematic framework to derive bounds on compact objects from fundamental principles.

</details>


### [116] [On the quasiblack-hole limit of rotating charged fluids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36102)
*Marcos L. W. Basso,Vilson T. Zanchin*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究稳态、轴对称、带电理想流体（charged perfect fluid）在刚性或差动转动下、含压或不含压时的极端类黑洞（quasiblack-hole, QBH）极限，重点考察 Weyl 型构型，并给出该极限下电磁正则性、匹配条件与质量公式成立所需的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有工作多关注静态 Weyl 型系统的极端 QBH 极限，旋转情形尚不充分。本文旨在将静态分析拓展至转动带电流体，并识别更一般旋转带电流体与该极限兼容的条件。

Method: 在极端 QBH 极限下，红移因子（redshift factor）在流体内部处处为零、边界成为准视界（quasihorizon）；要求物质与电磁场正则并对接外部光滑解。分别处理刚性转动与差动转动，考察带电压尘埃、含压流体（Kloster-Das、Guilfoyle、Islam 等关系）及线性 Weyl 子类，并引入收敛性与可积性条件。

Result: 在适当收敛假设下，电磁正则性与趋于均匀转动共同蕴含连通流体内部广义电磁势（generalized electromagnetic potential）为常数，且不依赖于 Weyl 假设；附加可积性条件后质量公式退化为极端 Kerr-Newman 的 Smarr 关系。刚性转动中，线性 Kloster-Das 子类于极限下变为无压，而一般 Guilfoyle 情形允许非零压强；Islam 假设在其耦合参数、极限势与极限电荷密度均非零时阻碍正则极限。差动转动中，分析了洛伦兹力项恒为零的构型及需控制角速度梯度的线性 Weyl 子类。

Conclusion: 旋转 Weyl 型系统可像其静态对应物一样容纳极端 QBH 极限，这拓展了旋转尘埃分布的分析，并给出更一般旋转带电流体与该极限兼容的条件。

Abstract: We investigate the extremal quasiblack-hole (QBH) limit of stationary, axisymmetric charged perfect fluids in rigid or differential rotation, with and without pressure. We emphasize Weyl-type configurations, whose redshift factor is functionally related to the generalized electromagnetic potential. In this limit, the redshift factor vanishes throughout the fluid interior and the boundary becomes a quasihorizon. We require regular matter and electromagnetic fields and smooth matching to the exterior. Under suitable convergence assumptions, electromagnetic regularity and the approach to uniform rotation imply a constant generalized electromagnetic potential throughout the connected fluid interior, independently of the Weyl ansatz. With additional integrability conditions, the mass formula reduces to the extremal Kerr-Newman Smarr relation. For rigid rotation, we examine charged dust obeying a linear Weyl relation and fluids with pressure obeying the Kloster-Das or Guilfoyle relations. The linear Kloster-Das subclass becomes pressureless in the limit, whereas the general Guilfoyle case allows nonzero pressure. The Islam ansatz obstructs a regular limit when its coupling parameter, limiting potential, and limiting charge density are nonzero. For differential rotation, we analyze configurations with an identically vanishing Lorentz-force term and a linear Weyl subclass whose regularity requires control of angular-velocity gradients. Our results show that rotating Weyl-type systems admit extremal QBH limits much like their static counterparts, extending analyses of rotating dust distributions and identifying conditions for more general rotating charged fluids to be compatible with this limit.

</details>


### [117] [Zero expansion is not free: the sharp minimum of Eulerian negative energy in Natário warp drives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36211)
*Nelson Bolívar,Ivaylo Vasilev,Gabriel Abellán*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文精确计算了 Natário 曲速引擎（warp drive）在 Lobo-Visser 体积积分定义下的欧拉负能量（Eulerian negative energy）需求，证明其薄壁极限下最小能量为 $\mathcal{E}_{\min}\sim v^2R^4/(4\Delta^3)$，比先前的估计高出 $(R/\Delta)^2$ 量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Natário 曲速引擎以无散度位移（divergence-free shift）替代 Alcubierre 气泡的收缩与膨胀，但其负能量代价此前未被精确确定。已有估计 $v^2R^2/\Delta$ 同时适用于 Natário 与 Alcubierre 气泡，可能低估了真实需求。本文旨在为 Natário 构造给出严格的能量下界与精确最小值。

Method: 在标准平坦切片（flat slice）、单位 lapse、半径 $R$ 的平坦内部及在 $R+\Delta$ 处与速度 $v$ 的均匀外流精确匹配的设定下，对 Lobo-Visser 体积积分 $\mathcal{E}_{\rm LV}=-\int_{\Sigma_t}\rho_{\rm E}\,d^3x$ 建立所有可行位移的下界。将完整变分问题等价为同心球间的经典 Stokes 流（Stokes flow），并辅以精确通量恒等式与独立求积验证。

Result: 对任意纵横比（aspect ratio）给出闭式精确最小值。薄壁极限下 $\mathcal{E}_{\min}\sim v^2R^4/(4\Delta^3)$，尖锐常数为 $1/4$，比旧估计 $v^2R^2/\Delta$ 高出两个 $R/\Delta$ 幂次。不可压缩性（incompressibility）迫使气泡排开的通量经壁返回，该返回流的切向剪切（tangential shear）主导能量；唯一极小化元在薄壁极限趋于最小曲率三次函数（minimal-curvature cubic）。

Conclusion: Natário 曲速引擎的负能量需求显著高于此前估计，其根本机制是返回流的切向剪切。所界定量依赖切片与观测者，且与 ADM 质量（ADM mass）不同，不应混淆。

Abstract: Natário's warp drive replaces the contraction and expansion of the Alcubierre bubble by a divergence-free shift. We determine exactly how much Eulerian negative energy this construction requires. For the Lobo-Visser volume integral $\mathcal{E}_{\rm LV}=-\int_{Σ_t}ρ_{\rm E}\,d^3x$ on the standard flat slice, with unit lapse, a flat interior of radius $R$, and exact matching to a uniform exterior stream of speed $v$ at $R+Δ$, we prove a lower bound for every admissible shift and find the exact minimum in closed form at every aspect ratio. As the wall thins, $\mathcal{E}_{\min}\sim v^2R^4/(4Δ^3)$ with sharp constant $1/4$, two powers of $R/Δ$ above the earlier estimate $v^2R^2/Δ$ applied to both Natário and Alcubierre bubbles. Incompressibility forces the flux displaced by the bubble to return through the wall, and the tangential shear of this return current dominates the energy. The full variational problem is equivalent to classical Stokes flow between concentric spheres. Its unique minimizer tends to a minimal-curvature cubic in the thin-wall limit. An exact flux identity and independent quadrature support the analytic result. The bounded quantity is slice and observer dependent and is distinct from the ADM mass.

</details>


### [118] [What Fraction of Extreme Mass Ratio Inspirals That Merge After the LISA Mission Ends Are Detectable?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36298)
*Daniel J. Oliver,Aaron D. Johnson,Jonathan E. Thompson,Curt J. Cutler*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文量化了 LISA 任务结束后才坠入大质量黑洞的极端质量比旋近（EMRIs）在可探测样本中的占比与参数分布，指出任务后事件占相当大的比例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 恒星质量致密天体在毫赫兹（millihertz）频段可辐射数年乃至数十年，因此 LISA 探测到的部分 EMRIs 在任务结束时仍处于旋近阶段，需要评估这类"任务后坠入"事件的规模及其对探测流程的影响。

Method: 采用具有天体物理动机的族群（population），对完全相对论性的 Kerr FastEMRIWaveforms 轨迹进行反向积分（backward-integrate）至给定坠入时间，并在四年名义观测窗口与十年扩展观测窗口上计算完整的 LISA 响应，进而将可探测比例表示为 SNR 阈值 ρ=10、20、30 下坠入时间的显式函数。

Result: 在 ρ>20 时，任务后 EMRIs 约占四年任务可探测族群的四分之一，十年扩展任务中约占六分之一。窗口内可探测比例随大质量黑洞自旋与次天体质量变化近六倍，但任务后份额在四年任务中稳定于 25%–33%，十年任务中为 13%–20%。

Conclusion: 任务结束后一年以上才坠入的 EMRIs 可能需要与任务内事件不同的搜索算法；由于任务后探测占全部探测的相当比例，发展适用于此类信号的探测流程（detection pipeline）具有一定紧迫性。

Abstract: Stellar-mass compact objects spiraling into massive black holes in galactic nuclei (referred to as "extreme-mass-ratio inspirals", or EMRIs) will be an important class of sources for the Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA). A stellar-mass compact object can radiate in the millihertz band for years to decades before it plunges into its massive black hole, so a substantial fraction of the EMRIs that LISA can detect will still be inspiraling when the mission ends. We calculate what fraction of detectable EMRIs will plunge after the mission ends, and for those we calculate how they are distributed as a function of various parameters, such as their post-mission lifetime. To do this, we draw from astrophysically motivated populations, backward-integrate fully relativistic Kerr FastEMRIWaveforms trajectories for a given time to plunge, and compute the full LISA response over both the four-year nominal and ten-year extended observing windows. We calculate the detectable fraction as an explicit function of time to plunge at signal-to-noise ratio (SNR) thresholds of $ρ=10$, 20, and 30. We find that post-mission EMRIs are roughly one in four of the LISA-detectable population at $ρ>20$ over the four-year mission, and roughly one in six over the ten-year extended mission. The in-window detectable fraction varies by nearly a factor of six at $ρ>20$ across the massive black hole spins and secondary masses we ran, while the share stays between 25% and 33% for the four-year mission and between 13% and 20% for the ten-year one across all of them. The search for EMRIs that plunge more than $\sim 1$ year after the mission ends may require a different algorithm than the one used for EMRIs that plunge during the mission, and the fact that post-mission detections are a substantial fraction of all detections gives some urgency to developing a detection pipeline suitable for them.

</details>


### [119] [Computing gravitational radiation from highly eccentric bound binaries in the small-mass-ratio limit](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36327)
*Diego Andrés Rivera Orona,Scott A. Hughes*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文针对小质量比（small-mass-ratio）双星系统在高偏心率（e→1）下引力辐射难以精确计算的问题，改进了频域（frequency-domain）方法，通过处理频域 Teukolsky 方程中的源积分，将可行计算范围推进至 e=0.99（极端情况下可达 e=0.999）。研究还发现经典的 Peters-Mathews 公式存在系统性误差，对经由高偏心率演化的致密双星形成模型具有影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确描述束缚致密双星（bound compact binary）演化的高强度引力模型，对理解其引力辐射及辐射反作用（backreaction）至关重要。高偏心率极限 e→1 难以建模：频域技术需要大量谐波（harmonics），而时域（time-domain）技术因系统时间尺度长而收敛缓慢；同时近期天体物理分析表明许多系统可能在演化至可探测源的过程中经历 e≃1，并可能以显著偏心率进入探测器敏感频段。

Method: 将用于小质量比极限下计算引力辐射的频域方法加以改造，以有效处理高偏心率。分析核心是频域 Teukolsky 方程在源上的一个积分；考察该积分在 e→1 时难以求值的特征，并研究规避这些困难的方法，最终给出一个简单的修正方案。

Result: 所提修正方案可便捷计算引力波振幅（gravitational-wave amplitudes）以及相关的能量与角动量通量（fluxes of energy and angular momentum），在偏心率达到 e=0.99 时仍可轻松完成；在付出更多耐心与努力后推进到 e=0.999，但数值精度在如此极端情形下似乎有所下降。此外发现经典且常用的 Peters 与 Mathews 公式存在系统性误差，且在相当宽的分离尺度上依然存在。

Conclusion: 所提简单修正使频域方法能够有效处理高偏心率引力辐射计算，扩展了可用偏心率范围。Peters-Mathews 公式的系统性误差对经由 e≃1 演化的致密双星形成模型具有启示意义。

Abstract: Precise strong-gravity models describing the evolution of bound compact binary systems provide important information about the gravitational radiation such binaries generate and how they evolve due to the radiation's backreaction. The limit of very high eccentricity, $e \to 1$, is challenging to model: frequency-domain techniques require many harmonics to accurately describe radiation in this limit, and time-domain techniques can be slow to converge due to the systems' long timescales. Many recent astrophysical analyses have nonetheless emphasized the importance of understanding gravitational-wave emission in this regime, highlighting the fact that many systems may evolve through $e \simeq 1$ en route to becoming detectable sources, and may enter the sensitive band of detectors with substantial eccentricity. In this paper, we show how to adapt frequency-domain methods for computing gravitational radiation in the small-mass-ratio limit to effectively handle high eccentricity. The key piece of this analysis is an integral over the source of the frequency-domain Teukolsky equation. We examine features of this integral that make it difficult to evaluate as $e \to 1$, and study methods to circumvent these difficulties. We find a simple fix which allows us to compute gravitational-wave amplitudes and the associated fluxes of energy and angular momentum with ease up to eccentricities $e = 0.99$; with patience (and stamina), we have pushed to $e = 0.999$, though numerical accuracy appears to degrade for such extreme cases. Interestingly, we find that the well-known and often used classic formulas of Peters and Mathews are subject to a systematic error which persists at quite wide separation. This has implications for models of compact binary formation which evolve through $e \simeq 1$.

</details>


### [120] [Threshold Tails of Black hole Differential Observables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36650)
*Vedant Subhash*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该研究考察一阶微分可观测量（first-order differential observables）如何影响黑洞波动方程的零频行为，指出准正规模（quasinormal modes）与晚期尾项（late-time tails）并不总以相同方式改变，并给出消除格林函数（Green function）主导阈值项的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准正规模与晚期尾项均源于黑洞波动方程，但二者对微分型可观测量的响应机制是否一致尚不明确，需厘清零频极限下的结构差异。

Method: 通过分析一阶微分可观测量对格林函数零频行为的作用，给出主导阈值项被移除的简单判据；并在 Schwarzschild 的 Regge-Wheeler 方程情形中，用正则静态解（regular static solution）确定相关算子，进而研究该变换在非零频与零频处的简并性（degeneracy）。

Result: 该变换在非零频率处非简并，但在零频率处全局简并。因此非零频率的准正规模问题保持不变，而固定半径处的领先晚期尾项额外获得一个时间的负幂次（inverse power of time）。

Conclusion: 一阶微分可观测量可选择性影响零频行为：在不改变准正规模谱的同时改变晚期尾项的衰减幂次，表明两类观测量对微分算子的敏感度存在本质区别。

Abstract: We study how first-order differential observables affect the zero-frequency behavior of black-hole wave equations. Quasinormal modes and late-time tails do not always change in the same way. We give a simple condition for when an observable removes the leading threshold term of a Green function. For Schwarzschild Regge-Wheeler modes, the relevant operator is determined by the regular static solution. The transformation is nondegenerate at nonzero frequency but becomes globally degenerate at zero frequency. As a result, the nonzero quasinormal-mode problem is unchanged, while the leading fixed-radius late-time tail gains one extra inverse power of time.

</details>


### [121] [Correspondence between multileaf topology of closed geodesics and spatiotemporal autocorrelations of hotspot images in Schwarzschild spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36767)
*Fengting Xie,Qing-Hua Zhu,Xin Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文通过射线追踪（ray tracing）构建沿史瓦西黑洞束缚闭合测地线运动点状热点的图像自相关（autocorrelation），建立拓扑整数 $(z,w,v)$ 与相关带数 $N_{\rm band}$、复现点数 $N_{\rm rec}$ 的定量对应关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相对论闭合轨道（relativistic closed orbits）的多叶结构分类为强场动力学研究提供了框架，但从天文图像中识别这些结构仍具挑战性，故需建立可从可观测量反推轨道拓扑的定量方法。

Method: 采用射线追踪（ray tracing）方法，计算沿史瓦西黑洞（Schwarzschild black hole）束缚闭合测地线（closed geodesics）运动的点状热点（pointlike hotspot）主图像（primary image）的时空自相关（spatiotemporal autocorrelations）；轨道以三个整数 $(z,w,v)$ 分类。

Result: 对所考察的轨道族，数值结果显示拓扑整数与相关带数 $N_{\rm band}$、复现点数 $N_{\rm rec}$ 之间存在对应关系，并可反解为 $z=N_{\rm rec}-1$、$w=\lfloor (N_{\rm band}+1)/(N_{\rm rec}-1)\rfloor-1$、$v=(N_{\rm band}+1)\bmod(N_{\rm rec}-1)$。

Conclusion: 上述关系为从热点图像自相关中恢复闭合轨道拓扑提供了一种定量方法。

Abstract: The classification of relativistic closed orbits by their multileaf structures provides a framework for studying strong-field dynamics. Identifying these structures in astronomical images remains challenging. Using ray tracing, we construct the spatiotemporal autocorrelations of the primary image of a pointlike hotspot moving along bound closed geodesics around a Schwarzschild black hole. These orbits are classified by three integers $(z,w,v)$, where $z$ counts the leaves, $w$ counts the additional whirls during each radial period, and $v$ specifies the order in which the orbital leaves are traced. For the orbit families examined, our numerical results establish a correspondence between the topological integers $(z,w,v)$ and the numbers of correlation bands $N_{\rm band}$ and recurrence points $N_{\rm rec}$. The integers are recovered as $z=N_{\rm rec}-1$, $w=\left\lfloor (N_{\rm band}+1)/(N_{\rm rec}-1)\right\rfloor-1$, and $v=(N_{\rm band}+1)\bmod(N_{\rm rec}-1)$. Here, $\lfloor x\rfloor$ denotes the greatest integer not exceeding $x$, and $\bmod$ denotes the remainder operation. These relations provide a quantitative method for recovering closed-orbit topology from hotspot image autocorrelations.

</details>


### [122] [Holographic dark energy in Einstein-Cartan spin cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36853)
*Yongjun Yun,Jungjai Lee*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在爱因斯坦-嘉当理论（Einstein-Cartan theory）中，挠率标量（torsion scalar）修正了以哈勃半径为红外截断的无相互作用全息暗能量（holographic dark energy）的类尘埃状态方程，从而在低红移有效范围内实现晚期宇宙加速，这在广义相对论（general relativity）对应模型中无法实现；同时导出了宇宙距离对偶关系（cosmic distance duality relation）的挠率诱导修正。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在考察挠率（torsion）能否使原本无法驱动加速的全息暗能量模型在晚期宇宙中产生加速膨胀，并探讨挠率对宇宙学距离关系的影响。

Method: 在爱因斯坦-嘉当理论框架下，采用以哈勃半径为红外截断（infrared cutoff）的无相互作用全息暗能量背景，分析挠率标量对状态方程的修正，并在此基础上推导宇宙距离对偶关系的修正形式。

Result: 挠率标量修改了类尘埃状态方程，使得该模型在低红移有效范围内可实现晚期宇宙加速，而广义相对论中的对应模型不具备这一特征。推导出的距离对偶关系含偏离参数 η，形式为 d_L=d_A(1+z)^2(1+η)，在晚期宇宙中 |η| 较小但不为零。

Conclusion: 挠率是使该全息暗能量模型实现晚期加速的关键因素；偏离参数的非零小值反映了挠率沿光传播路径的累积效应，可作为观测检验的潜在信号。

Abstract: We show that, in Einstein-Cartan theory, the torsion scalar modifies the dust-like equation of state of non-interacting holographic dark energy with the Hubble radius as the infrared cutoff, enabling late-time cosmic acceleration within the low-redshift domain of validity, which is not realized in the corresponding model in general relativity. Using the same holographic dark energy background, we further derive the torsion-induced modification of the cosmic distance duality relation, $d_L=d_A(1+z)^2(1+η)$. In the late-time universe, the magnitude of the deviation parameter, $|η|$, is small but non-vanishing, reflecting the cumulative effect of torsion along the light-propagation path.

</details>


### [123] [Higher-curvature gravity in Weyl geometry: No-ghost and no-tachyon conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36880)
*Tomoya Tachinami*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在Weyl几何中构造引力理论，其拉格朗日量为标量曲率的任意函数，并推导出在极大对称真空附近无鬼（ghost）与无快子（tachyon）的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Weyl几何允许长度标度在时空每一点独立选取，为通过Weyl不变性（Weyl invariance，即局部标度对称性）推广广义相对论提供了自然框架。

Method: 对任意函数形式的拉格朗日量，Weyl不变性要求除度规与Weyl向量外还需引入补偿标量场；作者借助辅助场（auxiliary field）线性化作用量并进行分析。

Result: Weyl向量仅通过标量场的单一组合与之耦合；经该组合的对数梯度平移后，Weyl向量无需规范固定即成为Weyl不变的有质量矢量场。在Einstein框架下，在引力子无鬼的前提下，Weyl向量的无快子条件与标量子（scalaron）的无鬼条件归结为补偿标量场动能系数为正。该系数为零时标量子不传播，仅剩引力子与有质量矢量。

Conclusion: 以含常数、线性与二次曲率项的拉格朗日量为例，在整个稳定区域内必有质量矢量与有质量标量子；而取消线性项后标量子变为无质量。

Abstract: Weyl geometry, in which the length scale can be chosen independently at each point of spacetime, provides a natural framework for extending general relativity through Weyl invariance, that is, local scale symmetry. We construct a gravitational theory whose Lagrangian is an arbitrary function of the scalar curvature in Weyl geometry and establish the conditions under which the theory is free of ghosts and tachyons around maximally symmetric vacua. For an arbitrary function, Weyl invariance requires a compensating scalar field in addition to the metric and the Weyl vector. By linearizing the action with an auxiliary field, we show that the Weyl vector couples to the scalar sector only through a single combination of the scalar fields. Consequently, the Weyl vector, shifted by the logarithmic gradient of this combination, becomes a Weyl-invariant massive vector field without gauge fixing. In the Einstein frame, given that the graviton is ghost-free, the no-tachyon condition for the Weyl vector, together with the no-ghost condition for the scalaron, the scalar mode associated with the higher-curvature terms, reduces to the positivity of the kinetic coefficient of the compensating scalar. When this coefficient vanishes, the scalaron does not propagate, leaving only the graviton and the massive vector. As an example, we show that for a Lagrangian containing constant, linear, and quadratic curvature terms, both a massive vector and a massive scalaron are necessarily present throughout the stability region, whereas without the linear term the scalaron becomes massless.

</details>


### [124] [Cosmological extra dimensions can mimic dark matter II. Growth of cosmological structures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36943)
*Mattia Villani*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文（系列第二篇）论证在额外维度（extra dimensions）紧致化为半径 R 的超球面（hypersphere）并处于收缩状态时，无暗物质（dark matter）的宇宙同样可以形成宇宙学结构（cosmological structures）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于摆脱暗物质假设，以带额外维度的时空作为替代解释框架。该文为系列第二篇，延续“无需暗物质”这一主张。

Method: 理论建模方法：采用含额外维度的时空，额外维度紧致化为半径 R 的超球面。

Result: 在额外维度收缩（contracting）的条件下，即使宇宙中没有暗物质，宇宙学结构依然能够增长（grow）。

Conclusion: 结论支持以收缩的额外维度替代暗物质来解释宇宙学结构的形成，构成该系列论证的第二部分。

Abstract: This is the second paper of this series in which we prove we can do without dark matter using spacetime with extra dimensions compactified to a hypersphere with radius R. Here we prove cosmological structures can grow even in a Universe without dark matter, provided that extra dimensions are contracting.

</details>


### [125] [Post-Newtonian secular dynamics of hierarchical triples. I. Eccentricity-, inclination- and node-dependence of the multiple-scale formulation, and its canonical consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36990)
*Hideyoshi Arakida*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文重新审查了Lim与Rodriguez（LR）给出的等级三体系统（hierarchical triples）领头后牛顿（post-Newtonian, 1PN）交叉项，采用双参数多重尺度展开（multiple-scale expansion）揭示其与Kuntz、Serra与Trincherini（KST）有效场论（effective-field-theory, EFT）结果的分歧根源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LR计算的1PN交叉项在$\delta\varepsilon^{3/2}$与$\delta\varepsilon^{7/2}$阶与KST的有效场论结果不一致，需澄清分歧的来源并判定哪一方更自洽。

Method: 以等级参数$\varepsilon=a_1/a_2$与1PN参数$\delta$作双参数多重尺度展开，重建LR的计算；采用单一相变量并检验正则生成函数（canonical generating function）所需的时间测度、Hamilton性与有理性（rationality）判据，并运行LR的长期演化（secular）代码进行数值验证。

Result: $\\delta\\varepsilon^{3/2}$的``天秤动（libration）''项源于平均测度（averaging measure）：四极周期解对外侧真近点角（true anomaly）为零均值，却在时间平均下被重新代入；以闭式（closed form）求值可精确重现LR实现项，但按正则生成函数所需的时间测度该项消失，与KST一致。该式仅是可移除哈密顿量流（flow of a removable Hamiltonian）的一项，缺乏伴随项，因而未通过有理性与哈密顿性检验。$\delta\varepsilon^{7/2}$项的质�量依赖仅在$m\ll m_3$时正确，其角结构与KST不符且违反Hamilton性检验。

Conclusion: 以单一相变量一致计算可给出完备且规范等价（gauge-equivalent）的长期方程；LR实现混合了真近点角四极解与偏近点角（eccentric-anomaly）1PN解。数值上，LR报告的共振ZLK调制在移除式(4.5)后仍存在，而其与八极（octupole）相关的加速并合（accelerated merger）则消失。

Abstract: We will re-examine the leading post-Newtonian (1PN) cross terms of hierarchical triples obtained by Lim and Rodriguez (LR) with a two-parameter multiple-scale expansion in the hierarchy parameter $\varepsilon=a_1/a_2$ and the 1PN parameter $δ$, which disagree with the effective-field-theory result of Kuntz, Serra, and Trincherini (KST) at the orders $δ\varepsilon^{3/2}$ and $δ\varepsilon^{7/2}$. Reconstructing the LR calculation, we show that the $δ\varepsilon^{3/2}$ ``libration'' term [LR Eq.~(4.5)] originates in the averaging measure: the quadrupole periodic solutions are made mean-free with respect to the outer true anomaly but re-substituted under the time average. The resulting term, evaluated in closed form, reproduces the implemented one exactly; with the time measure required of a canonical generating function it vanishes, in agreement with KST. Equation~(4.5) is one term of the flow of a removable Hamiltonian whose companion terms are absent, which is why it fails the rationality and Hamiltonicity tests. A calculation carried out consistently with a single phase variable yields the complete, gauge-equivalent secular equations, whereas LR's implementation combines a true-anomaly quadrupole solution with an eccentric-anomaly 1PN solution. Two structural defects of the implementation do not contribute to these terms. The $δ\varepsilon^{7/2}$ term of LR has the correct mass dependence only for $m\ll m_3$, and its angular structure disagrees with KST and violates the Hamiltonicity test. Finally, integrations with LR's secular code show that the resonant ZLK modulations reported by LR survive the removal of Eq.~(4.5), whereas their accelerated merger with the octupole disappears.

</details>


### [126] [Beyond circular and equatorial orbits: Bayesian constraint on vector charge with the adiabatic inspirals of generic Kerr geodesics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37039)
*Tieguang Zi,Genliang Li*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文推导了偏心、倾斜、非共振 Kerr 测地线的轨道平均电磁 Carter 常数通量，并构建了包含矢量辐射的一般绝热 inspirals 波形，用于通过 EMRI 对次级天体矢量电荷进行贝叶斯推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端质量比旋进（extreme-mass-ratio inspirals, EMRIs）可通过引力波相位的累积效应探测额外基本场；但一般 Kerr 轨道的矢量辐射建模需要 Carter 常数演化，而这无法仅由能量与角动量平衡确定。

Method: 推导偏心、倾斜、非共振 Kerr 测地线的轨道平均电磁 Carter 常数通量，包含无穷远处与事件视界部分；结合引力与电磁能量及角动量通量，采用四维 Chebyshev 插值方法构建一般绝热 inspirals；并将相对论轨迹纳入 FastEMRIWaveform 包。

Result: 对次级天体所带矢量电荷进行贝叶斯推断，发现电荷与源内禀参数之间存在显著相关性，且矢量电荷约束对轨道倾角呈非单调依赖。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了研究一般 EMRI 中额外矢量辐射通道及其对未来空间引力波观测检验引力理论意义的框架。

Abstract: Extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) offer a powerful probe of additional fundamental fields through their cumulative influence on gravitational-wave phases. However, modeling vector radiation from generic Kerr orbits requires the evolution of the Carter constant, which cannot be determined from energy and angular-momentum balance alone. Here we derive the orbit-averaged electromagnetic Carter-constant flux for eccentric and inclined, nonresonant Kerr geodesics, including sections at infinity and event horizon. Combining the gravitational and electromagnetic energy and angular-momentum fluxes, we construct generic adiabatic inspirals using a four-dimensional Chebyshev interpolation mathod. We incorporate the relativistic trajectories into the state of the art of FastEMRIWaveform package and perform Bayesian inference of vector charge carried by secondaries. Our analysis reveals substantial correlations between the charge and intrinsic source parameters, with vector charge constraints exhibiting a nonmonotonic dependence on orbital inclination. These results establish a framework for investigating additional vector-radiation channels in generic EMRIs and their implications for tests of gravity with future space-based gravitational wave observations.

</details>


### [127] [Self-Interacting Dark Matter-Induced Dynamical Friction and Its Imprint on EMRI Gravitational Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37079)
*Yu Wang,Meilin Liu,Haiguang Xu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构造了自相互作用暗物质（SIDM）对Chandrasekhar动力学摩擦力的领头阶有效修正，并计算其对极端质量比旋进（EMRI）轨道演化与引力波相位的影响，指出未来空间探测器可探测SIDM微观动量转移性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EMRI的长时间引力波信号为探测大质量黑洞周围暗物质环境提供了有前景的手段，而自相互作用暗物质（SIDM）的碰撞性质对EMRI动力学的影响尚待研究。

Method: 受SIDM的碰撞玻尔兹曼（collisional Boltzmann）描述启发，构造对常规Chandrasekhar动力学摩擦力的领头阶有效修正，由动量转移截面σ_T与无量纲碰撞率参数n_χσ_T r = r/λ_mfp控制；随后计算轨道演化、波形修改及累积引力波退相位（dephasing）。

Result: 在所考虑的增强阻力（enhanced-drag）区域，SIDM自相互作用强度增大导致有效动力学摩擦力增强、旋进加速，并相对无碰撞暗物质情形产生逐步增大的相位偏差；研究进一步区分了相对真空的总暗物质退相位与由暗物质自相互作用额外产生的相位修正。

Conclusion: EMRI的累积引力波相位对暗物质微观动量转移性质敏感，表明LISA等未来空间引力波探测器有望成为探测大质量黑洞附近SIDM的互补手段。

Abstract: Extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) provide a promising probe of the dark matter environment surrounding massive black holes through their long-duration gravitational-wave signals. In this work, we investigate the influence of self-interacting dark matter (SIDM) on EMRI dynamics and gravitational-wave phase evolution. Motivated by the collisional Boltzmann description of SIDM, we construct a leading-order effective correction to the conventional Chandrasekhar dynamical-friction force, controlled by the momentum-transfer cross section $σ_T$ and the dimensionless collisionality parameter $n_χσ_T r=r/λ_{\rm mfp}$. We then compute the resulting orbital evolution, waveform modification, and accumulated gravitational-wave dephasing. In the enhanced-drag regime considered here, increasing the SIDM self-interaction strength increases the effective dynamical friction, accelerates the inspiral, and produces a progressively larger phase deviation relative to the collisionless dark matter case. We further distinguish the total dark-matter-induced dephasing relative to vacuum from the additional phase correction generated specifically by dark matter self-interactions. Our results demonstrate that the cumulative gravitational-wave phase of EMRIs can be sensitive to the microscopic momentum-transfer properties of dark matter, suggesting that future space-based detectors such as LISA may provide a complementary probe of SIDM in the vicinity of massive black holes.

</details>


### [128] [Closing the Loop: from EPRL-FK spinfoams to Regge dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37179)
*Matteo Bruno,Pietro Donà,Gowrisankar Sreeram*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文指出EPRL-FK自旋泡沫模型在半经典（semiclassical）区受限于只能描述平坦几何，通过引入“几何闭合（geometric closure）”作为附加约束，可重建弯曲几何，并使其在半经典区等价于Regge演算（Regge calculus）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: EPRL-FK自旋泡沫模型在固定离散化下的半经典区存在重大局限：其主导构型仅描述平坦几何（flat geometries），无法对应广义相对论（General Relativity）的弯曲时空。研究动机在于寻找使变分原理（variational principle）复现Regge演算运动方程所需的附加条件。

Method: 将EPRL-FK模型改写为楔-和乐变量（wedge-holonomy variables），并识别出几何闭合（geometric closure）为缺失条件；把几何闭合作为与局域平坦性（local flatness）并列的附加约束加以施加，在正则、非退化、类空四面体的洛伦兹分支（Lorentzian branch with spacelike tetrahedra）上，模去规范（modulo gauge）构造约束面。

Result: 证明该约束面等价于长度-Regge几何空间（space of length-Regge geometries）；限制到该面上的楔作用量（wedge action）成为Regge作用量，其切向变分（tangent variations）给出长度-Regge方程（length-Regge equations）。由此，施加几何闭合后可重建弯曲几何。

Conclusion: 提出将几何闭合直接实现在自旋泡沫振幅（spinfoam amplitude）中的方案。加入该附加约束后，EPRL-FK模型在其半经典区等价于Regge演算。

Abstract: The EPRL-FK spinfoam model in its semiclassical regime at fixed discretization faces a big limitation: its dominant configurations describe only flat geometries. We ask what additional condition is needed for the variational principle to reproduce the equations of motion of Regge calculus, the standard discretization of General Relativity. We recast the EPRL-FK model in wedge-holonomy variables and identify geometric closure as the missing condition. We prove that imposing geometric closure as an additional constraint alongside local flatness allows us to reconstruct curved geometries. On a regular, nondegenerate Lorentzian branch with spacelike tetrahedra, modulo gauge, they define a constraint surface equivalent to the space of length-Regge geometries. The wedge action restricted to this surface becomes the Regge action, and its tangent variations yield the length-Regge equations. Finally, we propose a way to implement geometric closure directly in the spinfoam amplitude. With this additional constraint, the EPRL-FK model is therefore equivalent to Regge calculus in its semiclassical regime.

</details>


### [129] [The Irreversible Depletion of Dark Matter Spikes by Extreme-Mass-Ratio Inspirals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37234)
*Charlie Sharpe,Yonadav Barry Ginat,Thomas F. M. Spieksma,Alexander Heger,Bence Kocsis*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: EMRI 在探测致密暗物质尖峰（dark matter spike）的同时，会通过引力弹弓（gravitational slingshot）效应将其摧毁，从而大幅削弱可观测的引力波退相位（dephasing）信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有研究普遍预测大质量黑洞（massive black holes, MBHs）周围存在致密暗物质尖峰，并认为其会在极高质量比旋进（extreme-mass-ratio inspirals, EMRIs）中产生可被 LISA 观测的引力波退相位。本文质疑该探测通道的可行性，指出用于探测尖峰的 EMRI 本身也可能破坏尖峰。

Method: 将核星团（nuclear star clusters）的 Fokker-Planck 模型与 EMRI-暗物质相遇的后牛顿（post-Newtonian）三体模拟相结合，考察重复引力弹弓在十亿年（gigayear）时间尺度上对损失锥（loss cone）内暗物质粒子的抛射效应。

Result: 对于红移 $z = 3$、质量 $\lesssim 10^5\,M_\odot$ 的 MBH，即便保守的 EMRI 发生率 $\mathcal{O}(1-10)\,\mathrm{Gyr}^{-1}$ 也足以使尖峰密度降低数个量级；若采用更现实的 $\mathcal{O}(100-300)\,\mathrm{Gyr}^{-1}$，该质量上限可扩展至 $\lesssim 10^6\,M_\odot$。引力波退相位因此显著减弱。

Conclusion: 仅质量较大、高红移且 EMRI 发生率足够低的系统（如 $z \gtrsim 5$、$\lesssim 100$ EMRIs/Gyr 的 $\sim 10^6\,M_\odot$ MBH）才可能产生超过一弧度的退相位。这缩小了通过该通道探测暗物质的前景。

Abstract: Dense dark matter spikes around massive black holes (MBHs) have been widely predicted to produce gravitational-wave dephasing in extreme-mass-ratio inspirals (EMRIs) that are potentially observable by LISA. We show that EMRIs, the very objects used to probe these spikes, also destroy them. Combining Fokker-Planck models of nuclear star clusters with post-Newtonian three-body simulations of EMRI-dark matter encounters, we demonstrate that repeated gravitational slingshots irreversibly eject dark matter particles from the cluster's loss cone over gigayear time-scales. For MBHs at $z = 3$ with masses $\lesssim 10^5 \,M_\odot$, even conservative EMRI rates of $\mathcal{O}(1-10) \, \mathrm{Gyr}^{-1}$ suffice to deplete the spike density by orders of magnitude; for more realistic rates of $\mathcal{O}(100-300) \, \mathrm{Gyr}^{-1}$, the mass range extends to $\lesssim 10^6 \,M_\odot$. As a result, the gravitational-wave dephasing expected from such systems is greatly reduced, narrowing the prospects of detecting DM through this channel. Only heavy, high-redshift systems with sufficiently low EMRI rates -- for example, a $\sim 10^6 \,M_\odot$ MBH at $z \gtrsim 5$ with $\lesssim 100$ EMRIs per Gyr -- can induce dephasing that exceeds one radian.

</details>


### [130] [Gravitational-Wave Inference For Noise PSD Jumps Across Data Gaps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37248)
*Jian-Ming Yan,Zong-Kuan Guo*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该论文针对长时程引力波（gravitational-wave, GW）推断中数据缺口（data gaps）伴随噪声功率谱密度（power spectral density, PSD）突变的问题，在 Wilson–Daubechies–Meyer（WDM）时频域的贝叶斯缺口增广框架下提出两阶段补救方案。频率域先验预测预白化（prewhitening, PW）与自适应缺口扩展（adaptive gap expanding, AGE）相结合，可在不进行完全分段的情况下恢复局部对角近似的有效性。玩具模型仿真表明 WDM+PW+AGE 能得到更紧致、更准确的后验，同时保持计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时程引力波推断不可避免地遇到数据缺口，而缺口常伴随探测器噪声 PSD 的非平稳、突变变化。传统做法将缺口前后数据分开分析，会造成可避免的信息损失并可能降低参数推断质量。论文关注一种实际且重要的失效模式：伴随 PSD 大幅跳变的短缺口。

Method: 论文基于 WDM 时频域的贝叶斯缺口增广方法，指出在此情形下支撑 WDM 似然的局部对角（类 Whittle）近似在缺口边缘的时间方向上不再可靠。为此提出两阶段、有效性驱动的补救策略：第一阶段为频率域先验预测预白化（PW），引入端点信息的噪声信息以缓解导致对角行为不良的主要失配；第二阶段当时间方向平滑性判据仍不满足时，施加自适应缺口扩展（AGE），仅选择性扩大缺口边界附近恢复局部对角有效性所需的最小邻域。

Result: 在具有嵌合 PSD 跃迁（chimeric PSD transitions）的玩具模型仿真中，WDM+PW+AGE 相比单侧缺口前/缺口后分析，给出显著更紧致且更准确的后验分布，同时保持适合下一代任务的计算效率。

Conclusion: 通过 PW 与 AGE 的组合，可在避免完全分段的前提下恢复 WDM 似然局部对角近似的有效性，从而缓解短缺口伴随 PSD 大幅跳变时的推断偏差与稳健性问题，并满足未来引力波任务对计算效率的要求。

Abstract: Long-duration gravitational-wave (GW) inference inevitably encounters data gaps, which are often accompanied by abrupt, non-stationary changes in the detector noise power spectral density (PSD). Conventional practice typically analyzes the pre-gap and post-gap data separately, causing avoidable information loss and potentially degrading parameter inference. We build on Bayesian gap augmentation in the Wilson--Daubechies--Meyer (WDM) time--frequency domain and focus on a practically important failure mode: short gaps with large PSD amplitude jumps. In this regime, the local diagonal (Whittle-like) approximation that underpins the WDM likelihood can no longer be relied upon in the time direction near the gap edges, which may lead to biased and/or less robust inference unless the local diagonal validity is restored without resorting to full segmentation. We propose a two-stage, validity-driven remedy. First, frequency-domain prior-predictive prewhitening (PW) incorporates endpoint-informed noise information to mitigate the dominant mismatch responsible for poor diagonal behavior, enabling a more favorable WDM representation while retaining computational tractability. Second, when a time-direction smoothness criterion still fails, we apply adaptive gap expanding (AGE), selectively enlarging only the minimal neighborhood around the gap boundaries needed to restore local diagonal validity. Toy-model simulations with chimeric PSD transitions show that WDM+PW+AGE yields substantially tighter and more accurate posteriors than single-sided pre-gap/post-gap analyses, while maintaining computational efficiency suitable for next-generation missions.

</details>


### [131] [The alternative scalar field dark sector of the Universe: Cosmological inflation, quintessence and ekpyrotic model in the framework of scalar-tensor cosmology](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37293)
*Marcin Postolak*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本论文以标量场（scalar field）描述暗部门（dark sector）为核心，系统梳理标量-张量宇宙学、暴胀有效场论、精质（quintessence）以及火劫/循环宇宙（ekpyrotic/cyclic）四种路径，并借助DESI DR2结果论证超越严格宇宙学常数的时间依赖暗能量动机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗部门的标量场描述存在多种互竞框架，需在同一理论基础上厘清标量场作为"修改物质"或"额外引力自由度"的定位；同时DESI DR2等观测结果提示严格宇宙学常数可能不足，故需考察时间依赖的有效暗能量与暴胀替代方案。

Method: 采用理论综述与解析相结合的方式：从Brans-Dicke理论推广至一般非最小耦合标量场，辨析Jordan与Einstein共形标架（conformal frame）关系；以有效场论（effective field theory）处理暴胀，分析时间平移自发破缺、Goldstone描述、高维算符、辐射修正与Planck压低项；对精质讨论tracking、scaling、thawing、freezing行为及statefinder、(ω_φ, ω'_φ)平面、Om(z)等诊断；对火劫/循环模型分析超刚状态方程、扰动、非高斯性、张量模与反弹条件。

Result: 论文给出标量-张量理论作为统一理论基础的阐释；在暴胀有效场论中揭示紫外敏感性（ultraviolet sensitivity）与eta问题；在精质部分结合DESI DR2支持时间依赖有效暗能量；在火劫/循环部分刻画超刚标量场状态方程的平滑机制，并讨论扰动、非高斯性、张量模、反弹条件、熵产生、测地不完备性（geodesic incompleteness）与相分辨场空间距离界。

Conclusion: 标量场可统一刻画暗部门的多个候选框架，其既可视为修改物质亦可视为额外引力自由度；暴胀的有效场论描述受紫外敏感性制约，而精质与火劫/循环模型提供了值得进一步研究的替代或补充路径。

Abstract: This dissertation investigates scalar field descriptions of the dark sector (of the Universe), with emphasis on scalar-tensor cosmology, inflationary effective field theory, quintessence, and ekpyrotic/cyclic alternatives to inflation.
  The thesis first reviews scalar-tensor theories of gravity, starting from Brans-Dicke theory and extending to general non-minimally coupled scalar fields. Particular attention is paid to the interpretation of the scalar field, the relation between the Jordan and Einstein conformal frames, and the role of conformal transformations in cosmology. This provides the theoretical basis for treating scalar fields either as modified matter or as additional gravitational degrees of freedom.
  The second part discusses cosmological inflation from the viewpoint of effective field theory. The spontaneous breaking of time translations, the Goldstone description of adiabatic perturbations, higher-dimensional operators, radiative corrections and Planck-suppressed terms are analyzed in the context of ultraviolet sensitivity and the eta problem.
  The third part is devoted to quintessence as the simplest dynamical dark energy model. 'Tracking', 'scaling', 'thawing' and 'freezing' behaviors are discussed, together with observational diagnostics such as the statefinder parameters, the $(ω_φ,ω'_φ)$ plane and the $Om(z)$ diagnostic. The DESI DR2 results are used as motivation for considering time-dependent effective dark energy beyond a strict cosmological constant.
  The final part studies ekpyrotic and cyclic cosmologies. The ekpyrotic phase is analyzed as a smoothing mechanism based on an ultra-stiff scalar field equation of state. Perturbations, non-Gaussianity, tensor modes, bounce conditions, entropy production, geodesic incompleteness and phase-resolved field-space distance bounds are discussed.

</details>


### [132] [Scaling RIFT 1: Extending RIFT to third-generation gravitational-wave analyses](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37393)
*R. O'Shaughnessy,A. Jan,J. Lange,R. Mechum*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该工作针对第三代引力波探测器观测致密双星时因高信噪比与时频依赖响应而导致的参数解释难题，扩展了RIFT参数估计流程，实现对任意波形模型的通用、稳健推断。在三站点下一代探测器网络中对有限尺寸双中子星注入至信噪比1000完成端到端回收，单GPU数分钟内将外禀参数定位至0.8×10⁻³平方度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 第三代引力波探测器（third-generation gravitational-wave detectors）观测到的致密双星信号信噪比（signal-to-noise ratio, SNR）常达数百至数千，且因地球自转与探测器大尺度几何结构产生随时间与频率变化的响应，使信号解释变得复杂。已有针对LISA的研究工作提供了基础，但需进一步构建适用于此类源的通用推断能力。

Method: 在既有LISA相关工作基础上，实现RIFT参数估计流程（RIFT parameter estimation pipeline）的通用且稳健扩展，使其可直接使用任何现有波形模型（waveform model）解释这些源。该方法无需依赖中间预训练数据产品，如降阶近似（reduced-order approximations）或归一化流（normalizing flows）。

Result: 在三站点下一代探测器网络（three-site next-generation network）中，对有限尺寸双中子星（binary-neutron-star）注入信号实现端到端回收，信噪比达到1000；在单块GPU上于数分钟内将源的外禀参数（extrinsic parameters）定位至0.8×10⁻³平方度。

Conclusion: 所提出的RIFT扩展可在高信噪比与复杂时频响应条件下，无需预训练中间数据产品即完成通用参数估计，并在下一代探测器网络场景中展现出高精度与高计算效率，为第三代引力波探测器的致密双星信号解释提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Interpretation of compact binary signals observed by third-generation gravitational-wave detectors is complicated by their high signal-to-noise ratios, often reaching hundreds to thousands, and by time- and frequency-dependent detector responses arising from Earth's rotation and the detectors' large physical extent. Building on prior work for LISA, we implement generic, robust extensions of the RIFT parameter estimation pipeline that enable interpretation of these sources using any existing waveform model, without recourse to intermediate pretrained data products like reduced-order approximations or normalizing flows. We demonstrate end-to-end recovery of finite-size binary-neutron-star injections in a three-site next-generation network to $\mathrm{SNR}=1000$, localizing the source's extrinsic parameters to $0.8\times10^{-3}\,\mathrm{deg}^2$ in minutes on a single GPU.

</details>


### [133] [Asymptotics of Killing horizons: A Characterization in de Sitter Spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37427)
*Carlos Peón-Nieto*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文证明(n+1)维de Sitter时空是唯一一类其Killing视界与共形边界$\mathscr{I}$相交的$(Λ>0)$-真空时空，并通过边界值重构全部de Sitter Killing矢量场，以共形不变量$σ$刻画Killing视界类型，并给出共形不变的表面积引力公式$κ^2 = |σ|$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于刻画de Sitter时空中Killing视界的共形边界（conformal boundary）行为，并寻找区分视界类型的共形不变量，从而为de Sitter视界的几何与热力学性质提供内禀的、共形不变的描述。

Method: 方法基于对$(Λ>0)$-真空时空的Killing矢量场向其共形边界$\mathscr{I}$的延拓，考察其在$\mathscr{I}$上的孤立本质零点处与边界相交的条件；进而由边界值重构全部de Sitter Killing矢量场，并引入共形不变量$σ$对视界进行分类。

Result: 结果表明：(n+1)维de Sitter时空在局部意义上是唯一满足Killing视界与$\mathscr{I}$相交的$(Λ>0)$-真空时空；$σ<0$对应非退化分叉视界（non-degenerate bifurcate horizons），$σ=0$对应退化非分叉视界（degenerate non-bifurcate horizons）；表面积引力满足共形不变关系$κ^2 = |σ|$。

Conclusion: 结论给出de Sitter Killing视界的共形不变刻画：视界类型由$σ$的符号完全决定，且表面积引力可由$|σ|$内禀地表达，从而统一了视界的分类与其表面引力计算。

Abstract: We prove that (n + 1)-dimensional de Sitter spacetime is locally the unique $(Λ> 0)$-vacuum spacetime whose Killing horizons intersect the conformal boundary $\mathscr{I}$. The intersection occurs at the isolated essential zeros of the extension of the Killing vector field to $\mathscr{I}$. We then reconstruct all de Sitter Killing vector fields from their boundary value and obtain a characterization of those admitting a Killing horizon in terms of a conformal invariant $σ$: $σ<0$ characterizes non-degenerate bifurcate horizons, whereas $σ= 0$ yields degenerate non-bifurcate horizons. Finally, we derive an intrinsic, conformally invariant formula for the surface gravity of the horizons in de Sitter, $κ^2 = |σ|$.

</details>


### [134] [Oscillatory dark energy with phantom crossings in Einstein-Gauss-Bonnet gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37437)
*Saddam Hussain,Sandip Biswas*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文在爱因斯坦-标量-高斯-博内（Einstein-scalar-Gauss-Bonnet）引力框架下研究振荡型暗能量（oscillatory dark energy）场景，通过非最小耦合放大态方程振荡并实现多次穿越幽灵分界线（phantom divide），同时识别出无鬼（ghost）与梯度不稳定性（gradient instability）的可行参数区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DESI 的重子声学振荡（baryon acoustic oscillation, BAO）测量与超新星观测显示暗能量态方程（equation of state）可能存在演化：高红移呈幽灵型（phantom-like），z≲0.5 转向精质（quintessence）区。摘要指出，除单调演化外，振荡型暗能量可作为动力学暗能量（dynamical dark energy）的替代实现，故有必要系统研究该振荡场景及其稳定性。

Method: 采用爱因斯坦-标量-高斯-博内引力框架，标量场具有指数-二次混合势（hybrid exponential-quadratic potential），并与高斯-博内不变量（Gauss-Bonnet invariant）非最小耦合；选择局域于势能极小值附近的高斯型高斯-博内耦合函数（Gaussian GB coupling function）。晚期标量场在势能极小值附近振荡，并最终趋向稳定的德西特吸引子（de Sitter attractor）相。

Result: 在最小耦合极限下，有效态方程于低红移（z∼0）在精质区振荡但不穿越幽灵分界线；非最小耦合放大了振荡并驱动有效态方程多次穿越幽灵分界线。过强的耦合会导致标量声速（scalar sound speed）与张量声速（tensor sound speed）为负，使扰动模式不稳定。摘要同时识别出一块参数空间：模型发生多次幽灵穿越，却保持无鬼与梯度不稳定性，并在未来趋近稳定的德西特吸引子。

Conclusion: 振荡型暗能量在爱因斯坦-标量-高斯-博内引力中可自然产生多次幽灵分界线穿越，但需与非最小耦合强度相权衡以规避不稳定；存在可行参数区域兼具多重幽灵穿越与扰动稳定性，并最终趋向稳定的德西特吸引子，为动力学暗能量的振荡实现提供了一种可能方案。

Abstract: Recent baryon acoustic oscillation measurements from the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI), combined with supernova observations, show a mild preference for an evolving dark-energy equation of state, in which dark energy remains phantom-like at higher redshift while a transition toward the quintessence regime emerges around $z\lesssim0.5$. Beyond the monotonic evolution of the equation of state, an oscillatory dark-energy scenario provides a potential alternative realization of dynamical dark energy. We study such an oscillatory scenario within the Einstein-scalar-Gauss-Bonnet gravity framework, where a scalar field with a hybrid exponential-quadratic potential couples nonminimally to the Gauss-Bonnet (GB) invariant. We choose a Gaussian GB coupling function localized around the minimum of the potential. During the late-time epoch, the field thereby oscillates around the potential minimum and eventually exits toward a stable de Sitter attractor phase. In the minimally coupled limit, the effective equation of state oscillates in the quintessence regime at low redshift, $z\sim0$, without crossing the phantom divide. The nonminimal coupling amplifies the oscillations and drives the effective equation of state across the phantom divide multiple times. Although a sufficiently strong coupling can induce negative scalar and tensor sound speeds, rendering the perturbation modes unstable. We identify a region of parameter space in which the model undergoes multiple phantom crossings while remaining free of ghost and gradient instabilities and approaching a stable de Sitter attractor in the future.

</details>


### [135] [Black objects in a five-dimensional swirling spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37511)
*Adriano Viganò*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构造了旋转宇宙（swirling universe）的五维推广，并将 Myers-Perry 黑洞与旋转黑环（rotating black ring）嵌入其中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摘要未明确说明研究动机，仅指出研究的出发点是在五维框架下推广既有的旋转宇宙解并嵌入已知黑洞解。

Method: 在五维时空中构造旋转宇宙的推广解，并在此背景中嵌入 Myers-Perry 黑洞和旋转黑环；随后证明二者无锥奇点（conical singularities），分析其几何性质，计算质量与角动量。

Result: 证明了所嵌入的两种解均不存在锥奇点，给出了其几何性质的分析，并计算了质量（mass）与角动量（angular momentum）。

Conclusion: 研究讨论了这些解的唯一性（uniqueness）；摘要未给出唯一性问题的具体结论。

Abstract: We construct a five-dimensional generalization of the swirling universe and embed in it the Myers-Perry black hole and the rotating black ring. We prove that both solutions are free of conical singularities, analyze their geometrical properties, compute their mass and angular momentum, and discuss their uniqueness.

</details>


### [136] [Deformation Quantization of Schutz Quantum Cosmology: Relational Time, Constraints, and Physical States](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37585)
*Raphael Fracalossi*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在 FLRW 宇宙学（FLRW cosmology）中，对约化关系动力学（reduced relational dynamics）与扩展约束量子化（extended constrained quantization）之间建立受控对应关系，并给出映射的显式构造与一个平坦尘埃模型算例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关系动力学约化与扩展约束量子化通常被视为两种不同处理方式，二者是否存在精确、可控的对应尚不清楚。本文旨在通过 Schutz 完美流体（Schutz perfect fluid）与内部时间（internal time）机制，明确刻画二者的等价结构及其适用条件。

Method: 在 FLRW 宇宙学耦合 Schutz 完美流体的框架下，通过物质部分的规范变换（canonical transformation）得到形如 $\mathcal C=p_T+H$ 的去参数化约束（deparametrized constraint），以 $T$ 为内部时间。在扩展 Weyl--Wigner 表述中，量子约束以双边方式施加，其反对称部分给出精确的 Moyal 演化（Moyal evolution），对称部分给出独立的量子壳条件（quantum shell condition）。

Result: 对固定的 $\hat H$ 自伴实现（self-adjoint realization），负谱子空间上的每个约化密度算符（reduced density operator）都有显式的分布扩展。能量为 $E$ 与 $E'$ 的谱二元组（spectral dyad）映射到支撑在 $p_T=-(E+E')/2$ 的时钟 Wigner 分布（clock Wigner distribution），关系相位由 $E-E'$ 决定。该映射保持非对角相干性（off-diagonal coherences）、满足两个星约束（star constraints），并以约化 Wigner 态作为 $p_T$ 边缘分布；因此其在可容许正密度扇区上是单射的。平坦尘埃模型（flat dust model，$q=1/2$，Dirichlet 数据）给出显式例子：约化谱为 $(-\infty,0]$，可容许扇区包含全部可归一化约化态。

Conclusion: 该对应关系限于固定的约束代表（fixed constraint representative）与固定量子实现，为关系动力学与扩展约束量子化之间提供了精确且单射的映射机制，并以平坦尘埃模型验证其可容许扇区的完备性。

Abstract: A controlled correspondence is established between reduced relational dynamics and extended constrained quantization in an FLRW cosmology coupled to a Schutz perfect fluid. A canonical transformation in the matter sector yields a deparametrized constraint of the form $\mathcal C=p_T+H$, with $T$ serving as an internal time. In the extended Weyl--Wigner formulation, the quantum constraint is imposed bilaterally. Its antisymmetric part gives the exact Moyal evolution generated by the reduced Hamiltonian, while its symmetric part imposes an independent quantum shell condition. For a fixed self-adjoint realization of $\hat H$, every reduced density operator supported on its negative spectral subspace admits an explicit distributional extension. A spectral dyad with energies $E$ and $E'$ is mapped to a clock Wigner distribution supported at $p_T=-(E+E')/2$, with relational phase determined by $E-E'$. The map preserves off-diagonal coherences, satisfies both star constraints, and has the reduced Wigner state as its $p_T$ marginal; it is therefore injective on the admissible positive-density sector. A flat dust model with $q=1/2$ and Dirichlet data provides an explicit example: its reduced spectrum is $(-\infty,0]$, so the admissible sector contains all normalizable reduced states. The correspondence applies to a fixed constraint representative and quantum realization.

</details>


### [137] [On more general Gibbons-Hawking-York boundary terms for f(R) gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37692)
*Federico Scali,Sergio Luigi Cacciatori,Matteo Galaverni,Gabriele Gionti*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该文在 $f(R)$ 引力（$f(R)$ gravity）中引入比既有文献更一般的边界条件（boundary conditions），以保证定态作用量原理（stationary action principle）的良定性，并显式计算相应的 Gibbons-Hawking-York 边界项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有 $f(R)$ 引力文献中的边界条件形式较为受限，难以覆盖更广泛的解空间。为获得良定的作用量变分原理并扩大可容许解（admissible solutions）的范围，需要推广边界条件设定。

Method: 在 $f(R)$ 引力框架下引入更一般的边界条件，使其包含已知情形，并据此显式推导对应的 Gibbons-Hawking-York 边界项（boundary terms），该结果适用于任意解析（analytic）形式的 $f(R)$。

Result: 所提出的边界条件将已有情形作为特例纳入，从而扩大了可容许解空间；其对应的 Gibbons-Hawking-York 边界项被显式给出，并对任意解析 $f(R)$ 成立。

Conclusion: 结论部分概述了两方面的应用前景：标量-张量映射（scalar-tensor mapping）以及黑洞热力学定律（laws of black-hole thermodynamics）。

Abstract: More general boundary conditions are introduced - with respect to known literature - which produce a well-posed stationary action principle in $f(R)$ gravity. These conditions encompass the known cases, thus enlarging the space of admissible solutions. The corresponding Gibbons-Hawking-York boundary terms are explicitly computed and hold for any analytic f(R). The perspectives of application concerning the scalar-tensor mapping and the laws of black-hole thermodynamics are outlined in the conclusions.

</details>


### [138] [Quasinormal-mode analysis of a massive scalar field in a Schwarzschild background via the spectral method](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37942)
*Davide Batic,Anna Chrysostomou,Alan S. Cornell,Denys Dutykh*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文采用扩展切比雪夫谱方法（extended Chebyshev spectral method）研究 Schwarzschild 背景上大质量标量场的准正规模（quasinormal-mode）谱问题，通过均匀化（uniformisation）处理双叶质量色散关系，将径向问题转化为七次多项式本征值问题，并在标量情形下作为大质量场计算的受控基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大质量准正规模问题中，WKB 与连分式（continued-fraction）方法在低频、高泛音及质量阈值附近等区域存在应用困难。本文意在建立一个可控的标量基准（benchmark），为更复杂的大质量场准正规模计算提供参照，而非直接模拟张量型 Kerr 环降（ringdown）。

Method: 使用扩展切比雪夫谱方法（extended Chebyshev spectral method）求解大质量标量准正规模谱问题；先对具有双叶解析结构（two-sheeted analytic structure）的大质量色散关系（dispersion relation）做均匀化（uniformisation），再离散化，并在视界（horizon）与空间无穷处（spatial infinity）施加准正规模边界条件，最终转化为七次多项式本征值问题（seventh-degree polynomial eigenvalue problem）。

Result: 该方法在低多极矩（low multipoles）、高泛音（high overtones）以及名义质量阈值（nominal mass threshold）两侧的频率范围内均给出稳定谱，覆盖了 WKB 与连分式方法难以适用的区域。对复频率而言，远场行为不能仅由频率实部判定，而依赖复波数及其黎曼叶（Riemann sheet）。此外识别出一族稳定的纯虚根，其衰减率近似等间距，物理解释尚未明确。

Conclusion: 扩展切比雪夫谱方法配合色散关系均匀化可稳健求解大质量标量准正规模谱。该 Schwarzschild 标量问题定位为大质量场准正规模计算的受控基准，而非张量型 Kerr 环降的直接模型；纯虚根族的物理含义仍待进一步研究。

Abstract: We study the massive scalar quasinormal-mode spectral problem on a Schwarzschild background using an extended Chebyshev spectral method. The massive dispersion relation has a two-sheeted analytic structure, which we uniformise before discretisation. This converts the radial problem into a seventh-degree polynomial eigenvalue problem while incorporating the quasinormal-mode boundary conditions at the horizon and spatial infinity. The method yields stable spectra for low multipoles, high overtones, and frequencies on both sides of the nominal mass threshold, including regimes in which WKB and continued-fraction approaches become difficult to apply. For complex frequencies, the far-field behaviour cannot be classified from the real part of the frequency alone, but depends on the complex wavenumber and its Riemann sheet. We also identify stable families of purely imaginary roots with approximately uniform spacing in their damping rates, whose physical interpretation remains open. The Schwarzschild scalar problem is intended as a controlled benchmark for massive-field quasinormal-mode calculations rather than as a direct model of tensorial Kerr ringdown.

</details>


### [139] [Orbital stability, relativistic precession, and QPO constraints for constant-curvature black holes in $f(R)$ gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37962)
*Kourosh Nozari,Sara Saghafi,Khadijeh Salahshour,Moisés Bravo-Gaete*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 在 metric $f(R)$ 引力常曲率真空扇区中，系统分析了圆轨道稳定性、本轮共振、相对论进动与 QPO 约束；结合 GRO J1655--40 的 QPO 三重态与动力学质量测量给出的贝叶斯后验表明曲率参数与零相容，未发现偏离 Kerr 的显著证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 $f(R)$ 引力框架下检验强场时空是否偏离广义相对论（general relativity, GR），特别是通过圆轨道动力学与准周期振荡（quasi-periodic oscillation, QPO）观测对常曲率真空解施加速度学约束。摘要指出该分析同时依赖 QPO 数据与独立动力学质量测量，旨在界定曲率参数的可允许范围。

Method: 在满足代数迹方程的常曲率 $R_0$ 下，针对 Schwarzschild--(anti-)de Sitter 与 Kerr--(anti-)de Sitter 背景，推导静止坐标时（stationary-coordinate-time）的轨道频率与本轮频率，确定稳定类时圆轨道区域及 $3{:}2$、$2{:}1$、$3{:}1$ 共振分支与带符号节点进动，并评估角动量加权的刚性流进动与粘性对齐。随后采用贝叶斯马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（Markov chain Monte Carlo, MCMC）分析，将 GRO J1655--40 QPO 三重态与独立动力学质量测量联合拟合。

Result: 正曲率将稳定运动限制于最内与最外稳定圆轨道之间，二者在临界曲率处合并，并在适当频率比下允许两个共振半径；负曲率允许稳定运动延伸至任意大半径，且 $Ω_θ/Ω_r\rightarrow1/2$，区别于渐近平坦极限下的 1。同时恢复 Lense--Thirring 极限。联合拟合给出 $R_0M^2=-5.57^{+12.94}_{-16.15}\times10^{-4}$（$68\%$ 可信度），Kerr 仍被允许且非零曲率拟合改善可忽略；质量不确定度加倍使曲率区间约展宽 $2.1$ 倍，移除质量似然则出现依赖先验的扩展简并。

Conclusion: 该结果是 QPO 与动力学质量的联合约束，并非单独由 QPO 决定的曲率上限；在所用测地相对论进动处方与时间归一化假设下，无显著证据表明时空偏离 Kerr。曲率约束的强度高度依赖质量测量精度与先验设定。

Abstract: We analyze circular-orbit stability, epicyclic resonances, relativistic precession, and quasi-periodic-oscillation (QPO) constraints in the constant-curvature vacuum sector of metric $f(R)$ gravity. The Schwarzschild--(anti-)de~Sitter and Kerr--(anti-)de~Sitter backgrounds are characterized by curvature $R_0$ satisfying the algebraic trace equation. We derive stationary-coordinate-time orbital and epicyclic frequencies and determine stable timelike circular-orbit domains. Positive curvature confines stable motion between innermost and outermost stable circular orbits, which merge at a critical curvature, and permits two resonance radii for suitable frequency ratios. Negative curvature allows stable motion to arbitrarily large radii, with $Ω_θ/Ω_r\rightarrow1/2$, compared with unity in the asymptotically flat limit. We determine the $3{:}2$, $2{:}1$, and $3{:}1$ resonance branches and signed nodal precession, recovering the Lense--Thirring limit. We also evaluate angular-momentum-weighted rigid-flow precession and viscous alignment, with the outermost stable orbit bounding positive-curvature flows. A Bayesian Markov chain Monte Carlo analysis combining the simultaneous GRO J1655--40 QPO triplet with an independent dynamical-mass measurement yields $R_0M^2=-5.57^{+12.94}_{-16.15}\times10^{-4}$ at $68\%$ credibility, conditional on the geodesic relativistic-precession prescription and adopted time normalization. Kerr remains allowed, with negligible fit improvement from nonzero curvature. Doubling the mass uncertainty broadens the curvature interval by approximately $2.1$; removing the mass likelihood reveals an extended, prior-dependent degeneracy. This is therefore a joint QPO and dynamical-mass constraint, with no significant evidence for departure from Kerr.

</details>


### [140] [Fast and Flow-rious: Gravitational Wave Background Bayesian Model Comparison using Normalizing Flows and Nested Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38122)
*David C. Wright,Aaron D. Johnson,Jeffrey S. Hazboun,William G. Lamb*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种基于归一化流（normalizing flows）、嵌套采样（nested sampling）与边缘化脉冲星计时阵列（PTA）似然的模型比较方法，用于高效计算 GWB 模型的贝叶斯因子（Bayes factor）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2023 年多个 PTA 合作组报告了纳赫兹引力波背景（GWB）的证据，下一步核心目标从提升探测显著性转向刻画 GWB 并识别其来源；而 PTA 文献常用的贝叶斯因子计算方法（如乘积空间采样、热力学积分）在模型差异大或非嵌套时计算昂贵或低效。

Method: 方法结合三部分：归一化流、嵌套采样，以及对所有非 GWB 参数做边缘化（marginalized）的 PTA 似然；其中流模型由新开发的 Python 包 coppuccino 训练，该包学习数据的 copula，即在变换到均匀边缘分布（uniform marginals）后的依赖结构。

Result: 在解析目标与模拟 PTA 数据上完成验证：能够在对误差范围内复现解析证据值（analytic evidence），并在将贝叶斯因子视为注入模型的二分类器时取得 0.996 的曲线下面积（AUC）得分。

Conclusion: 该方法可在消费级硬件（consumer hardware）上于数分钟内对任意 GWB 谱模型完成贝叶斯模型比较。

Abstract: In 2023, pulsar timing array (PTA) collaborations around the world announced evidence for a nanohertz gravitational-wave background (GWB). Beyond increasing the detection significance, the next major goal is to characterize the GWB and identify its source(s). The typical model-comparison metric used in PTA analyses is the Bayes factor (BF). However, BF computation methods used in the PTA literature, such as product-space sampling and thermodynamic integration, are computationally expensive or inefficient if the models compared are dissimilar or non-nested. In this work, we introduce a PTA model-comparison method using a combination of normalizing flows, nested sampling, and a PTA likelihood that is marginalized over all non-GWB parameters. The flows are trained using a newly-developed Python package coppuccino, which learns the data's copula: the dependence structure after transforming to uniform marginals. We validate the method on both analytic targets and simulated PTA data, recovering the analytic evidence within error bars and achieving an area-under-the-curve (AUC) score of 0.996 when treating the BFs as binary classifiers of the injected models. The result is a method to perform Bayesian model comparison of arbitrary GWB spectral models in minutes on consumer hardware.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mtrl-sci'></div>

# cond-mat.mtrl-sci [[Back]](#toc)

### [141] [Linking Topological Medium Range Order and Density-Wave Coherence to Metallic Glass Ductility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36041)
*Minhazul Islam,Muchen Wang,Andrea Fantin,Geun Hee Yoo,Ji Young Kim,Hamdan Ashfaq,Eun Soo Park,Robert Maass,Yunzhi Wang,Yue Fan,Hee-Suk Chung,Sang-Chul Lee,Jinwoo Hwang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究结合同步辐射约化密度函数 G(r) 与机器学习辅助的四维扫描透射电子显微镜（4D-STEM），在五种 Zr 基金属玻璃中区分并关联了几何与拓扑两类中程有序（medium range order, MRO），并揭示其共同构成成分依赖力学响应的结构基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金属玻璃的力学性能显著依赖成分，但其结构根源尚不明确；核心难题在于如何将不同长度尺度、不同实验可观测手段所定义的中程有序（MRO）相互关联，并建立其与性能的联系。

Method: 采用同步辐射约化密度函数 G(r) 的密度波分析提取几何 MRO 描述符（原子密度关联的相干长度），并利用机器学习辅助的四维扫描透射电子显微镜（4D-STEM）表征纳米尺度拓扑与化学 MRO 基元，包括具有不同旋转对称性的类晶体局域有序。

Result: G(r) 导出的相干长度捕捉到协同韧性弛豫的重要基线趋势；成分依赖的拓扑 MRO 提供额外结构贡献。Zr 含量增加会抑制富 Cu 的类 FCC 型 MRO、增大协同相干体积，并促进与韧性增强相关的细小富 Zr 类 HCP 局域有序。

Conclusion: 几何与拓扑 MRO 的联合结构影响被确认为金属玻璃成分依赖力学响应的关键基础。

Abstract: Metallic glasses exhibit pronounced composition-dependent mechanical properties, yet identifying the structural origins of these changes remains difficult. An outstanding issue is how to correlate different forms of medium range order (MRO), defined at different length scales and accessed through different experimental observables, and to understand their connection to properties. We combine synchrotron reduced density function, G(r), with machine-learning-assisted four dimensional scanning transmission electron microscopy (4D-STEM) to examine geometric and topological MRO in five Zr-based metallic glasses. Density wave analysis of G(r) provides a geometric MRO descriptor through the coherence length of atomic density correlations, while 4D-STEM reveals nanoscale topological and chemical MRO motifs, including crystal-like local ordering with distinct rotational symmetries. We show that the G(r)-derived coherence length captures an important baseline tendency for cooperative ductile relaxation, while composition-dependent topological MRO provides an additional structural contribution that helps explain the observed mechanical responses. In particular, increasing Zr content suppresses Cu-rich FCC-like MRO, increases the cooperative coherence volume, and promotes fine Zr-rich HCP-like local order associated with enhanced ductility. These results identify the combined structural influence of geometric and topological MRO as a key basis for composition-dependent mechanical response in metallic glasses.

</details>


### [142] [Measuring trainable degrees of freedom in materials graph neural networks: a random-subspace intrinsic dimension analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36084)
*Shehroz Ahmad Shoaib,Kangming Li*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文提出“可训练度依赖性（trainable-degree dependence）”作为材料图神经网络（graph neural networks, GNNs）性能表征的补充维度，通过随机子空间内在维度分析（random-subspace intrinsic-dimension analysis）考察三种模型在六项任务上的性能恢复曲线，揭示最终精度无法体现的架构与任务差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 最终预测精度（final predictive accuracy）是材料属性预测中比较 GNN 的标准依据，但无法反映性能对可训练参数空间方向可及性的依赖强度。因此需要一种补充性表征，刻画模型恢复性能所需的可训练维度需求。

Method: 采用随机子空间内在维度分析，在随机取向的参数子空间中训练 CGCNN、ALIGNN 与 DimeNet++，覆盖六项预测任务，测量随独立可训练自由度（trainable degrees of freedom）逐步恢复时性能的回升情况；并进行数据集规模扫描（dataset-size sweeps）与宽度扫描（width sweep）。

Result: 恢复曲线（recovery curves）将端点精度与恢复所需的可训练维度需求相分离：金属分类与对数体积模量回归在较小分数子空间中即可接近参考性能；形成能与带隙预测表现出更强的架构依赖性；声子预测对维度限制最敏感。数据集增大时带隙模型需要更大的分数子空间，而形成能与体积模量响应更稳定；宽度扫描显示分数阈值可保持稳定，而绝对阈值维度随模型规模增大而上升。

Conclusion: 随机子空间分析为材料 GNN 如何利用其优化空间（optimization space）提供了一种有针对性的压力测试（stress test），可作为最终精度之外的表征补充。

Abstract: Final predictive accuracy is the standard basis for comparing graph neural networks (GNNs) in materials-property prediction, but it does not show how strongly performance depends on access to trainable parameter-space directions. Here, we introduce trainable-degree dependence as a complementary characterization of materials GNN learning. Using random-subspace intrinsic-dimension analysis, we train CGCNN, ALIGNN, and DimeNet++ in randomly oriented parameter subspaces across six prediction tasks and measure how performance recovers as independent trainable degrees of freedom are restored. The resulting recovery curves separate endpoint accuracy from the trainable-dimensional demand required to recover it. They reveal distinctions that final errors alone miss: metallic classification and log-bulk-modulus regression recover near-reference performance from small fractional subspaces, formation-energy and band-gap prediction show stronger architecture dependence, and phonon prediction is most sensitive to dimensional restriction. Dataset-size sweeps show that band-gap models require larger fractional subspaces as training data grows, whereas formation-energy and bulk-modulus responses are more stable. A width sweep shows that fractional thresholds can remain stable while absolute threshold dimensions increase with model size. Random-subspace analysis therefore provides a targeted stress test for how materials GNNs use their optimization space.

</details>


### [143] [Accelerating phase-field simulations on exascale computing systems for faster-than-real-time precipitate aging predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36100)
*Stephen DeWitt,David J. Gardner,Philip Fackler,Yonggil Song,Miroslav Stoyanov,Carol S. Woodward,Balasubramaniam Radhakrishnan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种综合性加速策略，将性能可移植GPU计算、大规模分布式内存并行与高阶隐式-显式时间积分结合于MEUMAPPS C++框架中，用于加速傅里叶伪谱相场模拟，并以19.2亿网格点、1920个gamma''析出相的Ni-Nb-Fe合金模拟验证其性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维相场模拟是材料微观组织预测的黄金标准，但其计算成本通常将应用相关计算限制在较小的域尺寸与时间尺度内，因此需要大幅提升计算效率。

Method: 在MEUMAPPS C++框架内集成性能可移植的GPU计算、大规模分布式内存并行以及高阶隐式-显式时间积分，构建整体性加速方案，并通过对Ni-Nb-Fe合金中1920个gamma''析出相生长与粗化的19.2亿网格点模拟进行验证。

Result: 模拟的七小时热处理在5.5小时内完成，快于真实时间，估计较仅用CPU的一阶基线快217-501倍；基准测试显示单节点GPU相对同等CPU资源加速最高17.5倍，120亿网格点问题在4096块GPU上接近理想强扩展，四阶时间积分在科学相关误差容限下再带来2.6-2.9倍加速。

Conclusion: 该结果建立了一种可利用领导级GPU系统的性能可移植策略，适用于相场模型之外的广泛傅里叶伪谱模拟，如流体动力学与晶体塑性模拟。该性能使数千个相互作用析出相的三维模拟变得可行，支持集体微观组织现象的定量研究、大规模模拟集合以及与实验的实时集成。

Abstract: Three-dimensional phase-field simulations are a gold-standard for microstructure prediction for materials, but their computational cost often limits application-relevant calculations to modest domain sizes and timescales. Here, we present a holistic approach for accelerating Fourier pseudospectral phase-field simulations by combining performance-portable GPU computing, large-scale distributed-memory parallelism, and high-order implicit-explicit time integration within the MEUMAPPS C++ framework. We demonstrate this approach with a 1.5-billion-grid-point simulation of the growth and coarsening of 1,920 gamma'' precipitates in a Ni-Nb-Fe alloy. The simulated seven-hour heat treatment is completed in 5.5 hours, making the calculation faster than real time and an estimated 217-501x faster than a CPU-only, first-order baseline. This performance makes three-dimensional simulations of thousands of interacting precipitates tractable, enabling quantitative studies ocollective microstructural phenomena, large simulation ensembles, and real-time integration into controlling and interpreting experiments. Benchmarking shows single-node GPU speedups of up to 17.5x relative to comparable CPU resources, near-ideal strong scaling to 4096 GPUs for a 12-billion-grid-point problem, and a further 2.6-2.9x acceleration from fourth-order time integration at scientifically relevant error tolerances. These results establish a performance-portable strategy for exploiting leadership-scale GPU systems that is applicable to a broad class of Fourier pseudospectral simulations beyond phase-field models such as fluid dynamics and crystal plasticity simulations.

</details>


### [144] [Far-field terahertz spectroscopy across the charge-density-wave transition in 2H-NbSe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36125)
*Dmitriy Yavorskiy,Adil Rehman,Wojciech Brzezicki,Jan Skolimowski,Marcin Białek,Wojciech Knap,Dawid Wutke,Natalia Olszowska,Andrzej Wiśniewski,Ashutosh S. Wadge*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 利用远场太赫兹时域光谱（THz-TDS）研究块体 2H-NbSe₂ 单晶在 CDW 转变（T_CDW≈33 K）前后的响应，观测到~1.5 THz 高频特征与亚 THz 长寿命振荡，二者在升温及跨越转变时被强烈抑制。时间依赖 Ginzburg–Landau 模拟分别将其归因于 CDW 振幅动力学与缺陷钉扎相位动力学，辅以 ARPES 揭示近费米能级谱重的动量选择性重新分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 2H-NbSe₂ 中电荷密度波（charge-density-wave, CDW）的形成会改变低能电子结构并产生与晶格耦合的集体激发，但其块体材料中集体 CDW 动力学的探测仍需更敏感的手段，本文即以此为出发点。

Method: 采用反射几何下的远场太赫兹时域光谱（far-field terahertz time-domain spectroscopy, THz-TDS）测量块体 2H-NbSe₂ 单晶在 T_CDW≈33 K 附近的太赫兹响应；并借助时间依赖 Ginzburg–Landau（time-dependent Ginzburg–Landau）模拟复现实验特征，同时开展角分辨光电子能谱（angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy, ARPES）作为补充测量。

Result: 在 T_CDW 以下，太赫兹响应呈现约 1.5 THz 的显著高频特征及寿命较长的亚太赫兹振荡；两者随升温逐渐减弱，并在跨越 CDW 转变时被强烈抑制，支持其与 CDW 态相关。模拟将高频响应主要归因于 CDW 振幅动力学、亚太赫兹响应归因于缺陷钉扎的相位动力学；~1.5 THz 特征接近拉曼 CDW 振幅模的报道频段，晶格自由度耦合也可能影响其谱位置。ARPES 进一步显示跨越转变时近费米能级谱重的动量选择性重新分布。

Conclusion: 结果表明远场太赫兹光谱可作为探测块体 2H-NbSe₂ 中集体 CDW 动力学的灵敏探针。

Abstract: Charge-density-wave (CDW) formation in 2H-NbSe$_2$ modifies the low-energy electronic structure and gives rise to collective excitations coupled to the lattice. Here, we investigate bulk 2H-NbSe$_2$ single crystals using far-field terahertz time-domain spectroscopy (THz-TDS) in reflection geometry across the CDW transition at $T_{\mathrm{CDW}} \approx 33$~K. Below $T_{\mathrm{CDW}}$, the THz response shows a pronounced high-frequency feature near 1.5~THz together with longer-lived sub-THz oscillations. Both responses progressively weaken upon warming and are strongly suppressed across the CDW transition, supporting their association with the CDW state. Using time-dependent Ginzburg--Landau simulations, we reproduce the main features of the experimental THz response, associating the high-frequency response mainly with CDW amplitude dynamics and the sub-THz response with defect-pinned phase dynamics. The $\sim1.5$~THz feature lies close to the frequency range reported for the Raman CDW amplitude mode, while coupling to lattice degrees of freedom may also influence its spectral position. We also performed complementary angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy measurements, which reveal momentum-selective redistribution of near-Fermi-level spectral weight across the transition. Together, these results show that far-field THz spectroscopy provides a sensitive probe of collective CDW dynamics in bulk 2H-NbSe$_2$.

</details>


### [145] [Altermagnetism produces pair emission and absorption from dark excitons and magnons in La$_2$O$_3$Mn$_2$Se$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36128)
*Birender Singh,Xian Xu,Sabrina R. Hatt,Suvodeep Paul,Yu-Mi Wu,Chao-Chun Wei,Violet Williams,Kyung-Mo Kim,Yihao Zhang,Mohamed Shehabeldin,Cameron Grant,April Li,Michael Geiwitz,Xiaoyin Li,Garrett E. Granroth,Feng Liu,Qiong Ma,Benedetta Flebus,Judy J. Cha,Vinod M. Menon,Benjamin A. Frandsen,Diana Y. Qiu,Huiwen Ji,Kenneth S. Burch*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究在交替磁体（altermagnet, AM）La$_2$O$_3$Mn$_2$Se$_2$ 中观测并解释了激子-磁振子（exciton-magnon）发射与吸收边带，证明其源于自旋禁戒暗激子，为交替磁体的光学识别与磁光应用提供了新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替磁体的非相对论性自旋劈裂（non-relativistic spin splitting）可催生新物态与自旋电子学、磁光器件，但其光学特征仍不明确。研究动机在于寻找并阐明交替磁体独特的光学信号。

Method: 结合光学光谱学（optical spectroscopy）与第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations），并利用非弹性中子散射（inelastic neutron scattering）独立测量磁振子能量尺度与态密度；通过光致发光（photoluminescence, PL）与吸收谱分析边带结构及其与自旋关联的联系。

Result: La$_2$O$_3$Mn$_2$Se$_2$ 被确认为交替磁绝缘体，同时具有强束缚的自旋禁戒暗激子与更高能量的亮激子。PL 与吸收谱呈现关于暗激子对称的镜像边带，发射为斯托克斯位移（Stokes-shifted）、吸收为反斯托克斯位移（anti-Stokes-shifted），其能量位移与谱形同中子散射测得的磁振子能量尺度与态密度一致；PL 强度遵循等时自旋-自旋关联函数（equal-time spin-spin correlator），并排除了其他竞争过程。

Conclusion: 结果将光致发光直接与磁性耦合，具有磁光器件应用潜力，并确立激子-磁振子光谱学（exciton-magnon spectroscopy）作为光学识别与利用交替磁体的新手段。

Abstract: Altermagnets' (AMs) non-relativistic spin splitting enables novel states, spintronic and magneto-optical devices, though their optical signatures remain elusive. Here, we report exciton-magnon emission and absorption: optical sidebands from a spin-forbidden dark exciton, a direct consequence of altermagnetic symmetry. Combined optical spectroscopy and first-principles calculations reveal that La$_2$O$_3$Mn$_2$Se$_2$ is an altermagnetic insulator, hosting a strongly bound, spin-forbidden dark exciton and a higher-energy bright exciton. Photoluminescence (PL) and absorption reveal mirror-image sidebands, Stokes-shifted in emission and anti-Stokes-shifted in absorption, symmetric about the dark exciton, whose energy shifts and spectral shapes match the magnon energy scale and density of states measured independently by inelastic neutron scattering. The PL intensity tracks the full equal-time spin-spin correlator, combining static and dynamical contributions, and rules out alternative processes. This directly couples PL to magnetism, with potential for magneto-optical devices. These results establish exciton-magnon spectroscopy as a new route for optically identifying and exploiting AMs.

</details>


### [146] [Facet- and thickness-dependent band-edge alignment at ZrSe\texorpdfstring{$_3$}{3} surfaces: hybrid-functional calculations with spin-orbit coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36150)
*Sandro G Holanda,Bruno Ipaves,Astrid Campos-Mata,Shreyasi Chattopadhyay,Pulickel M Ajayan,Douglas S Galvao,Marcelo L Pereira Junior*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作采用色散校正半局域泛函与含自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）的 HSE06 杂化泛函，计算 ZrSe₃ 四种晶面的表面能、平衡形貌（Wulff 形状）与能带边缘对齐，指出晶面活性差异由 Se–Se 二聚体（dimer）是否被保留决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 剥离的 ZrSe₃ 晶体暴露出四种晶面，电催化测量把析氢活性归因于高能 (210) 面而非基面，但既有计算仅处理体相（bulk）或自由支撑的基面单层，这些非基面表面尚未被表征。

Method: 使用色散校正的半局域泛函（dispersion-corrected semilocal functional）计算四个表面的表面能与平衡形貌，并用含自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）的 HSE06 杂化泛函（hybrid functional）计算能带边缘对齐。

Result: 表面能跨越近一个数量级，由 (001) 的 0.097 J/m² 至 (010) 的 0.869 J/m²；Wulff 形状暴露全部四个观测晶面，面积分数分别为 17.1%、4.4%、71.4% 与 7.0%。电离势（ionization potential）面间相差 0.45 eV，电子亲和能（electron affinity）相差 0.80 eV；(210) 的带隙在 10 至 23 Å 间由 1.43 eV 降至 0.94 eV，而 (010) 保持金属性。

Conclusion: 晶面可依切割是否保留或扭曲 Se–Se 二聚体（dimer）而分类，被扭曲的二聚体会富集前沿态（frontier states）；由于同一二聚体主导 MX₃ 家族的体相电子结构，该判据可推广至相关三硫属化物（trichalcogenides）。

Abstract: Transition-metal trichalcogenides MX$_3$ are quasi-one-dimensional van der Waals materials whose layers form through lateral chain binding, giving them an interlayer cleavage plane and in-plane electronic anisotropy. Among them, ZrSe$_3$ is stable under ambient conditions and semiconducting. Exfoliated crystals expose four crystallographic planes, and electrocatalytic measurements on an individual crystal assigned the hydrogen evolution activity to the high-energy (210) facet rather than to the basal plane. Those non-basal surfaces remain uncharacterized, since the available calculations treat either the bulk crystal or the free-standing basal monolayer. Here, we compute the surface energies and the equilibrium morphology of these four surfaces with a dispersion-corrected semilocal functional, and their band-edge alignment with the HSE06 hybrid functional including spin-orbit coupling. The surface energies span nearly an order of magnitude, from \SI{0.097}{\joule\per\meter\squared} for (001) to \SI{0.869}{\joule\per\meter\squared} for (010), and the Wulff shape exposes the four observed planes with area fractions of \SI{17.1}{\percent}, \SI{4.4}{\percent}, \SI{71.4}{\percent}, and \SI{7.0}{\percent}. The ionization potential varies by \SI{0.45}{\electronvolt} between facets and the electron affinity by \SI{0.80}{\electronvolt}, the gap of (210) falls from 1.43 to \SI{0.94}{\electronvolt} between 10 and \SI{23}{\angstrom}, and (010) remains metallic. The facets separate according to whether the cut preserves or distorts the Se-Se dimer, and the distorted dimers concentrate the frontier states. Because the same dimer governs the bulk electronic structure of the MX$_3$ family, the criterion transfers to the related trichalcogenides.

</details>


### [147] [Fracture of Lattice Materials from Low to High Relative Density](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36166)
*Adam P. Taylor,Sage Fulco,Kevin T. Turner*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究建立结合均匀化方法（homogenization methods）与钝裂纹断裂理论（blunt-crack fracture theory）的解析模型，预测三角形与六边形点阵（lattice）在宽相对密度范围内的断裂韧性，并揭示高相对密度下失效机制由节点应力集中主导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低密度点阵的断裂模型已较成熟，但相对密度高于30%的点阵断裂行为研究不足，需建立覆盖宽密度范围的预测框架并厘清高密度区失效机制。

Method: 将均匀化方法与钝裂纹断裂理论耦合，构建解析模型预测三角形与六边形点阵的断裂韧性；以有限元建模（finite element modeling）进行验证，并考察相对取向、点阵圆角半径等几何因素对应力重分布的影响；同时开展激光切割聚甲基丙烯酸甲酯（PMMA）点阵试件的断裂实验。

Result: 有限元结果证实了解析框架，并显示低、高相对密度区间存在失效机制转变：高密度下由节点应力集中主导，受局部裂纹尖端几何与宏观裂纹路径强烈影响；改变相对取向与圆角半径可显著重分布应力并提升断裂韧性。准脆性（quasi-brittle）PMMA点阵因局部塑性延迟失效，断裂韧性较完全脆性点阵提升约1.6倍，部分试样甚至超过基体材料韧性——此现象仅在单胞尺寸远大于基体塑性半径时可能出现。实验验证了有限元结果，同时凸显脆性点阵材料失效的随机性，高相对密度下尤为明显。

Conclusion: 所提解析模型可在宽相对密度范围内预测点阵断裂韧性，并揭示高密度区节点应力集中主导的失效机制及几何调控对韧性的增益；准脆性效应可带来超越基体的断裂韧性，但脆性点阵失效具显著随机性。

Abstract: Lattice materials have highly tunable mechanical properties that are controlled by their internal geometry and base material behavior. While fracture models for low-density lattices are well-established, the fracture of lattices at relative densities above 30% remains largely unexplored. Here, we present an analytical model that combines homogenization methods and blunt-crack fracture theory to predict the fracture toughness of triangular and hexagonal lattices over a wide range of relative densities. Finite element modeling corroborates the analytical framework and demonstrates a shift in failure mechanisms between low and high relative density regimes. At high relative densities, failure is dominated by nodal stress concentrations, which are strongly influenced by the local crack-tip geometry and the macroscopic crack path. Significant stress redistribution is observed as the relative orientation and lattice fillet radius are varied, resulting in increased fracture toughness relative to baseline geometries. Furthermore, quasi-brittle poly(methyl methacrylate) (PMMA) lattices achieve about a 1.6x enhancement in fracture toughness relative to perfectly brittle lattices as failure is delayed by localized plasticity. Notably, some quasi-brittle lattices are found to exceed the fracture toughness of their base material -- a feature only possible when the cell size is large compared to the base material's plastic radius. Experiments on lattice fracture specimens made from laser-cut PMMA validate the finite element results, but highlight the stochastic nature of failure in brittle lattice materials, especially at higher relative densities.

</details>


### [148] [Fundamental concepts of thermal phonon coherence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36270)
*Theodore Maranets,Haoran Cui,Milad Nasiri,Evan Doe,Yan Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文综述了声子（phonon）空间相干性与时间相干性的理论，旨在阐明复杂材料中晶格热传导偏离粒子式散射图像的本质，并建立统一的声子波动图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着人工周期性、构型复杂性与无序结构被广泛探索，这些结构会诱导显著的相位相关性（phase correlations），使晶格热传导显著偏离传统的类粒子散射（particle-like scattering）框架。文献中报道的各类热导率与声子性质结果缺乏统一的概念性理解。

Method: 严格梳理声子空间相干性（spatial coherence）与时间相干性（temporal coherence）的理论，并对文献中报道的多种热导率与声子性质结果进行理论解析，以厘清其间的联系。

Result: 热声子相干性（thermal phonon coherence）可分别在空间和时间上发展，从而产生不同的波动现象；本文据此建立了统一空间与时间相干性理论的完整物理图像。

Conclusion: 本综述的理论剖析为理解和工程化调控复杂材料中的热声子提供了广泛的知识基础。

Abstract: As scientists seek to transcend traditional material property relationships, novel structures with artificial periodicity, configurational complexity, and disorder are increasingly being explored. For phonons, these architectures can induce significant phase correlations, which strongly shifts lattice heat conduction away from the conventional framework of particle-like scattering. This thermal phonon coherence can develop in space and time separately, resulting in distinct wave-like phenomena. Here we rigorously outline the theories of phonon spatial and temporal coherence. Crucially, this review focuses on demystifying and building conceptual understanding of the fundamental principles linking the various thermal conductivity and phonon property results reported in the literature. Furthermore, we use this understanding to establish a comprehensive physical picture of the wave nature of thermal phonons, unifying the spatial and temporal coherence theories. Altogether, the theoretical dissections in this review provide an extensive knowledge base for understanding and engineering thermal phonons in complex materials.

</details>


### [149] [Directional strain control of magnetism in MnBi$_2$Se$_4$, MnSb$_2$Se$_4$, and MnSb$_2$Te$_4$ freestanding monolayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36343)
*Swarnava Ghosh,Tanvir Sohail,Markus Eisenbach*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文利用第一性原理计算，在完整二维应变空间（two-dimensional strain space）中系统研究了 MnBi$_2$Se$_4$、MnSb$_2$Se$_4$ 与 MnSb$_2$Te$_4$ 单层（monolayer）的应变依赖磁性，揭示局域磁矩、磁基态、交换相互作用、磁晶各向异性与有序温度的不同应变响应规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 这类层状磁性量子材料（layered magnetic quantum materials）在自旋电子学与拓扑电子学应用中前景广阔，原子尺度磁序调控至关重要，且材料对应变（strain）高度敏感。因此需要在单层极限下全面理解其应变-磁性耦合行为。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations），通过独立改变两个面内应变分量以覆盖完整二维应变空间，研究三种 Mn 基硫族化合物单层的应变依赖磁性。

Result: Mn 投影局域磁矩（Mn-projected local moments）主要取决于体积应变（volumetric strain）；而磁基态、交换相互作用、磁晶各向异性（magnetocrystalline anisotropy）与有序温度则表现出显著的方向依赖性和组分依赖性。MnBi$_2$Se$_4$ 与 MnSb$_2$Se$_4$ 单层呈现更丰富的竞争磁态，MnSb$_2$Te$_4$ 单层在更大应变范围内保持铁磁（ferromagnetic），并具有更大的面外磁晶各向异性，但其应变依赖性弱于 Se 基材料。

Conclusion: 磁有序温度映射（magnetic ordering temperature maps）凸显了二维应变对调节热磁稳定性的作用，相关结果确立了该 Mn 基硫族化合物单层家族的磁弹性（magnetoelastic）规律。

Abstract: MnBi$_2$Se$_4$, MnSb$_2$Se$_4$, and MnSb$_2$Te$4$ are layered magnetic quantum materials of interest for spintronic and topological electronic applications, where control of magnetic order at the atomic scale is essential. These materials are particularly sensitive to strain.
