We investigate their strain-dependent magnetism in the monolayer limit using first-principles calculations across the full two-dimensional strain space spanned by independent variations of the two in-plane strain components. Our results show that the Mn-projected local moments depend primarily on the volumetric strain, whereas the magnetic ground state, exchange interactions, magnetocrystalline anisotropy, and ordering temperature show pronounced directional and composition-dependent responses. We also observe strain-induced competition between the phases, with MnBi$_2$Se$_4$, and MnSb$_2$Se$_4$ monolayers exhibiting a more diverse range of competing magnetic states, while MnSb$_2$Te$_4$ monolayer remains ferromagnetic over a larger portion of strain space. MnSb$_2$Te$_4$ also exhibits a substantially larger out-of-plane magnetocrystalline anisotropy, while showing a weaker dependence on strain than the Se-based materials. The calculated magnetic ordering temperature maps further highlight the role of two-dimensional strain to tune thermal magnetic stability in these materials. These results establish magnetoelastic trends across this family of Mn-based chalcogenide monolayers.

</details>


### [150] [Orbital-engineered px,y-kagome lattice in a halogen monolayer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36450)
*Xulin Liu,Jingyi Duan,Yueqian Chen,Wenbo Liu,Peiyao Xiao,Yuxiang Liu,Pei Liu,Minjun Wang,Baojie Feng,Dongfei Wang,Xun Shi,Wei Jiang,Yugui Yao,Wende Xiao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作通过在 Ag(111) 表面沉积 Br 单层，构建了具有轨道自由度的 px,y 轨道 kagome 晶格，并证实其能带与各向异性紧束缚模型吻合，进而揭示由一阶自旋轨道耦合驱动的非平庸拓扑特性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多轨道 kagome 晶格（multi-orbital kagome lattice）在实验上鲜有探索，现有体系多依赖复杂的 d 电子多重态并近似为各向同性单轨道模型。为突破该局限，需要实现具有明确轨道自由度的 kagome 体系。

Method: 利用 Ag(111) 上 Br 单层的沉积实现轨道过滤（orbital filtering），选择性抑制 pz 通道，从而获得 px,y 轨道 kagome 晶格；结合扫描隧道显微镜（STM）、角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）与密度泛函理论（DFT）计算进行表征，并构建有效 H 钝化模型以剥离衬底背景、提取本征电子结构。

Result: 观察到大面积、高度有序的 kagome 结构，其能带色散与各向异性 px,y 紧束缚模型（tight-binding model）定量一致；有效 H 钝化模型揭示了本征电子结构，并显示由一阶自旋轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）效应驱动的 px,y kagome 多重态具有非平庸拓扑特征。

Conclusion: Br/Ag(111) 被确立为实验上可行的多轨道 kagome 物理平台，将 kagome 范式从传统的 d 轨道范畴拓展至轨道工程化的拓扑设定。

Abstract: Multi-orbital kagome lattices with explicit orbital degrees of freedom remain largely unexplored, as most experimentally realized systems rely on complex d-electron manifolds that are approximated by isotropic single-orbital models. Here, we overcome this limitation by realizing a px,y-orbital kagome lattice through deposition of a Br monolayer on Ag(111), where orbital filtering selectively suppresses the pz channel. Scanning tunneling microscopy, angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy, and density-functional-theory calculations reveal a large-area, highly ordered kagome structure whose band dispersions quantitatively match the anisotropic px,y tight-binding model. To extract the intrinsic manifold from the substrate background, we construct an effective H-passivated model, which uncover the intrinsic electronic structure and reveals nontrivial topological characteristics of the px,y kagome manifold driven by first-order spin-orbit coupling effect. Our work establishes Br/Ag(111) as an experimentally accessible platform for multi-orbital kagome physics, extending the kagome paradigm from the conventional d-orbital regime to an orbitally engineered topological setting.

</details>


### [151] [From Automated Simulation to Autonomous Discovery: A Hierarchical Framework for Agentic Computational Materials Science](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36469)
*Linggang Zhu,Jian Zhou,Zhimei Sun*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该展望（Perspective）论文提出计算材料代理自主性等级（Computational Materials Agent Autonomy Level, CMA-AL）框架，以六级分层分类法定义计算材料科学中的自主代理程度，并将新兴代理系统映射至该框架，指出迈向更高自主性的关键科学与技术挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型（large language models）、材料专用基础模型（materials-specific foundation models）与代理式人工智能（agentic AI）的融合正在重塑计算材料发现的范式。尽管高通量计算与数据驱动建模已扩大探索规模，核心科学决策回路仍主要由人类主导，而新兴自主系统的快速涌现缺乏统一定义与评估框架，因此亟需可操作的科学自主性框架。

Method: 本文属展望类研究，提出 CMA-AL 框架——一种分层分类法（hierarchical taxonomy），界定计算材料科学中自主代理的六个等级：脚本化执行者（scripted executor）、LLM 辅助操作者（LLM-assisted operator）、自适应探索者（adaptive explorer）、可实验建模者（experiment-ready modeler）、代理式数字孪生（agentic digital twin）与自我扩展智能（self-extending intelligence）。

Result: 研究将新兴的代理系统映射到 CMA-AL 框架之上，并识别了通往更高自主性所面临的关键科学与技术挑战。摘要未提供具体实验或定量结果。

Conclusion: CMA-AL 为刻画代理式计算材料发现提供了共同语言，可用于评估新兴系统的成熟度，并引导其向日益自主的材料发现方向演进。

Abstract: The convergence of large language models, materials-specific foundation models, and agentic artificial intelligence is reshaping the paradigm of computational materials discovery. While high-throughput computation, automated workflows, and data-driven modeling have greatly expanded the scale of materials exploration, the core scientific decision-making loop remains largely human-directed. Agentic AI introduces the possibility of systems that can autonomously reason about materials objectives, execute simulations, and refine strategies. However, the rapid emergence of such systems has created a critical need for a unified and operational framework to define, evaluate, and guide scientific autonomy in computational materials discovery. In this Perspective, we propose the Computational Materials Agent Autonomy Level (CMA-AL) framework, a hierarchical taxonomy defining six levels of autonomous agency in computational materials science: scripted excecutor, LLM-assisted operator, adaptive explorer, experiment-ready modeler, agentic digital twin, and self-extending intelligence. We further map emerging agentic systems onto the framework and identify key scientific and technological challenges toward higher autonomy. CMA-AL provides a common language for characterizing agentic computational materials discovery, evaluating the maturity of emerging systems, and guiding their evolution toward increasingly autonomous materials discovery.

</details>


### [152] [Enabling the Ambient Pressure Growth of ScB2 Crystals for AlGaN Power Electronics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36493)
*Satya K. Kushwaha,Ahamed Raihan,Astrid D. Kengne,Daniel Joel Harrison,R. Shipra,Han Xie,Evan N. Crites,Allana G. Iwanicki,Luke J. Meiler,Sharad Mahatara,Maxime A. Siegler,Renae N. Gannon,Steven R. Spurgeon,Rajeswari Kolagani,Amitayush Jhu Thakur,Stephan Lany,Patrick E. Hopkins,Jessica L. McChesney,Nancy Haegel,Tyrel M. McQueen,Michael G. Spencer,MVS Chandrashekhar*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文报道在常压下通过激光加热光学浮区法（laser-heated Optical Floating Zone）结合旅行溶剂法（travelling solvent method）生长超高温陶瓷ScB2单晶，并系统表征其结构、缺陷与电子性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ScB2作为超高温陶瓷（ultrahigh-temperature ceramic）的单晶生长此前缺乏常压可行路径，且其作为Al-rich AlGaN功率微电子晶格匹配衬底（lattice-matched substrate）的潜力有待验证。

Method: 以Sc-rich（55-65 at% Sc）与B-rich（80-83 at% B）自熔剂（self-flux）为原料，在常压激光加热光学浮区中采用旅行溶剂法生长单晶，生长速率0.2-2 mm/hr，并借助电子背散射衍射（electron backscattered diffraction）、摇摆曲线（rocking-curve）、表面X射线光电子能谱（XPS）与功函数测量进行表征。

Result: 晶体为AlB2型层状六方相（space group P6/mmm），Sc-rich与B-rich条件下晶格常数分别为a=3.1423(2) Å、c=3.5084(3) Å与a=3.1502(3) Å、c=3.5041(3) Å；晶体沿面内[100]方向原生生长；Sc-flux产物为含Sc夹杂的多畴晶锭但畴高度取向，B-flux晶锭在初始成核区之后为单畴，(h000)与(000l)反射摇摆曲线呈单峰，散射强度尾部不对称提示存在点缺陷；B-flux晶体电子环境优于Sc-flux晶体，B 1s与Sc 2p结合能峰分辨良好，(11-20)面功函数约为5 eV。

Conclusion: 结果证明ScB2可在常压下可行生长，确立其作为Al-rich AlGaN功率微电子晶格匹配衬底候选的地位，并表明该生长路线可实现ScB2衬底的可规模化制造。

Abstract: Here we report the growth of single crystalline ScB2, an ultrahigh-temperature ceramic, at ambient pressure in a laser-heated Optical Floating Zone via the travelling solvent method. Crystals have been grown from both Sc-rich (55-65 at% Sc) and B-rich self-flux (80-83 at% B) at growth rates in the range of 0.2-2 mm/hr. The structure of grown crystals is in good agreement with an AlB2-type layered hexagonal phase, space group P6/mmm, with lattice constants a = 3.1423(2) Å (resp. 3.1502(3) Å) and c = 3.5084(3) Å (resp. 3.5041(3) Å) for crystals grown under Sc-rich (resp. B-rich) conditions. Crystals natively grow along the in-plane [100] direction. Electron backscattered diffraction shows that Sc-flux growth results in boules with multiple domains containing Sc inclusions, with the domains highly aligned. In contrast, B-flux boules are single domain after the initial nucleation region. Rocking-curve measurements of B-flux crystals for the (h000) and (000l) reflections show single peaks, establishing that the crystals are free from grain boundaries; the asymmetry in the scattered-intensity tails suggests the presence of point defects. Surface X-ray photoemission spectroscopy shows that the electronic environment in B-flux crystals is superior to that of Sc-flux crystals and produces highly resolved binding-energy peaks for B 1s and Sc 2p. Work-function measurements for the (11-20) plane give a value of approximately 5 eV, consistent with the highly electrically conductive nature of ScB2. These results demonstrate the viable ambient-pressure growth of ScB2, establish it as a lattice-matched substrate candidate for Al-rich AlGaN power microelectronics, and show that this growth route enables scalable manufacturing of ScB2 substrates.

</details>


### [153] [Frustrated Gd3+ Double Perovskites as High-Performance Magnetocaloric Materials for Sub-100 mK Adiabatic Demagnetization Refrigeration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36538)
*Tim Treu,Marvin Klinger,Christian Heil,Hans-Albrecht Krug von Nidda,Vinicius E. S. Frehse,Mamoun Hemmida,Anton Jesche,Alexander A. Tsirlin,Philipp Gegenwart*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究提出基于 Gd3+ 的双钙钛矿（double perovskites）Ba2GdSbO6 与 Sr2GdSbO6 作为高性能磁热（magnetocaloric）材料，从 2 K、5 T 起始的绝热退磁制冷（adiabatic demagnetization refrigeration, ADR）可达 67–83 mK，创 Gd3+ 磁体（S = 7/2）在该条件下的最低 ADR 温度记录。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现低于 100 mK 的温度对推进量子技术（quantum technologies）与探索基础量子现象至关重要。传统顺磁盐（paramagnetic salts）虽支撑了 ADR，但其局限促使研究者寻找更有效的替代材料。

Method: 采用基于 Gd3+ 的双钙钛矿 Ba2GdSbO6 和 Sr2GdSbO6 作为磁热材料，并从 2 K、5 T 的初始条件开展绝热退磁制冷实验，考察其在有限磁场与零场下的最低制冷温度、磁有序温度与熵密度。

Result: 从 2 K、5 T 起始，两种化合物在小的有限磁场下分别达到 67 mK 与 68 mK，在零场下达到 83 mK 与 78 mK，为 Gd3+ 磁体（S = 7/2）在该条件下报道的最低 ADR 温度。受挫几何（frustrated geometry）以及交换与偶极相互作用（exchange and dipolar interactions）的复杂协同作用，将其反铁磁有序温度（antiferromagnetic ordering temperatures）压低至 Ba2GdSbO6 的 100 与 166 mK、Sr2GdSbO6 的 190 mK，同时保持 189 与 201 mJ K-1 cm-3 的优异熵密度（entropy density）。Ba2GdSbO6 甚至在 0.5 T 有限磁场下仍维持低于 100 mK 的制冷。

Conclusion: Ba2GdSbO6 与 Sr2GdSbO6 是高性能磁热材料，可实现极低 ADR 温度并具高熵密度；其中 Ba2GdSbO6 在 0.5 T 有限磁场下仍保持亚 100 mK 制冷，对实际 ADR 应用尤具前景。

Abstract: Achieving temperatures below 100 mK is essential for advancing quantum technologies and exploring fundamental quantum phenomena. While paramagnetic salts have traditionally enabled adiabatic demagnetization refrigeration (ADR), their limitations have driven the search for more effective alternatives. In this work, we present Gd3+-based double perovskites, Ba2GdSbO6 and Sr2GdSbO6, as high-performance magnetocaloric materials. Starting ADR from 2 K and 5 T, these compounds reach 67 mK and 68 mK in small finite magnetic fields, and 83 mK and 78 mK in zero field, respectively, which are the lowest reported ADR temperatures for Gd3+ magnets (S = 7/2) under these conditions. The frustrated geometry and the complex interplay of exchange and dipolar interactions suppress their antiferromagnetic ordering temperatures to 100 and 166 mK for Ba2GdSbO6 and 190 mK for Sr2GdSbO6, while maintaining an outstanding entropy density of 189 mJ K-1 cm-3 and 201 mJ K-1 cm-3, respectively. Notably, Ba2GdSbO6 sustains sub-100 mK cooling even at finite fields of 0.5 T, making it particularly promising for practical ADR applications.

</details>


### [154] [Averaging in thermodynamic dislocation theory: general macroscopically uniform stress and strain states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36681)
*Khanh Chau Le*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 将多晶体棒轴向对称载荷下的平均方法推广至任意宏观均匀应力/应变状态，基于晶粒取向等概率假设与各向同性取向平均的两个精确恒等式，导出平均分解剪应力正比于 von Mises 等效应力，且塑性流动方向为应力偏张量，从而得到一个关联 $J_2$ 流动理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原有平均方法仅适用于多晶棒在轴向对称拉伸或压缩情形，需推广到任意宏观均匀应力与应变状态，并厘清宏观塑性流动方向与 Johnson–Cook 指出的拉伸–扭转不一致问题。

Method: 以晶粒取向等概率假设为起点，将平均分解剪应力与平均分解弹性、塑性剪应变定义为对所有滑移系取向的均方根平均，并给出各向同性取向平均的两个精确恒等式；塑性滑移率设为正比于作用其上的分解剪应力；硬化律由热激活位错脱钉动力学及位错密度与有效无序温度演化方程给出，写作时间速率形式。

Result: 恒等式表明平均分解剪应力正比于 von Mises 等效应力，宏观塑性流动方向为应力偏张量；理论给出棒与管的扭矩–扭转关系而无需经典剪切应力–应变曲线约化。对铜，由 Hopkinson 杆拉伸、室温与高温动态压缩及多个扭转速率下的扭矩–扭转记录联合标定参数，一组参数在 142 个数据点上、室温至 1173 K、10 至 2300 s^{-1} 范围内以 6% rms 描述拉伸、压缩与扭转。

Conclusion: 单组材料参数（与先前仅基于压缩的标定一致）可统一描述拉伸、压缩与扭转行为；Johnson 与 Cook 所注意到的拉伸–扭转差异被归因于其扭转试样的初始位错状态。

Abstract: The averaging procedure, developed for polycrystalline bars under axially symmetric tension or compression, is extended to arbitrary macroscopically uniform stress and strain states. Starting from the equal probability hypothesis for grain orientations, the mean resolved shear stress and the mean resolved elastic and plastic shear strains are defined as root-mean-square averages over all slip-system orientations. Two exact identities for isotropic orientation averages show that the mean resolved shear stress is proportional to the von Mises equivalent stress, and that the direction of macroscopic plastic flow, obtained from the hypothesis that the plastic slip rate on a system is proportional to the resolved shear stress acting on it, is the stress deviator. The result is an associated $J_2$ flow theory whose hardening law is not fitted but follows from the kinetics of thermally activated dislocation depinning and the evolution equations for the dislocation density and the effective disorder temperature of thermodynamic dislocation theory, written as rates with respect to time so that arbitrary loading paths can be followed. For torsion the theory yields the torque--twist relation of bars and tubes without the classical reductions to a shear stress--strain curve. The parameters for copper are identified jointly from Hopkinson-bar tension, dynamic compression at room and elevated temperatures, and torque--twist records at several twist rates. One set of material parameters, consistent with the earlier compression-only identification, describes tension, compression and torsion from room temperature to $1173$\,K and from $10$ to $2300$\,s$^{-1}$ to $6$\,\% rms over 142 data points; the tension--torsion discrepancy noted by Johnson and Cook is traced to the initial dislocation state of their torsion specimens.

</details>


### [155] [Polar-Domain Volume as a Unified Descriptor of Transport in Ionic Liquids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36682)
*Ganesh K. Rajahmundry,Tarak K. Patra*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究通过全原子分子动力学模拟（all atom molecular dynamics simulations），建立咪唑基离子液体（imidazolium based ILs）平衡微观结构与黏度、离子电导率之间的定量构效关系，发现以平均极性域体积（mean polar-domain volume）表达时，两种输运性质可统一为幂律标度（power-law scaling）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发能够定量预测离子液体（ionic liquids, ILs）宏观输运性质的分子尺度结构描述符（molecular scale structural descriptors）仍是长期未解问题，根源在于分子相互作用、纳米尺度组织（nanoscale organization）与集体离子动力学（collective ion dynamics）之间的复杂耦合。

Method: 采用全原子分子动力学模拟，研究一系列具有化学性质不同阴离子及不同烷基链长度的咪唑基离子液体，考察其平衡微观结构与黏度（viscosity）和离子电导率（ionic conductivity）之间的关系。

Result: 黏度与离子电导率对烷基链长度和阴离子化学性质呈现不同依赖关系，但当以平均极性域体积（mean polar-domain volume）表达时，这些看似不同的趋势基本收敛为统一关联；两种输运性质均与平均极性域体积分数呈系统性幂律标度（power-law scaling）。

Conclusion: 结果确立极性域体积（polar-domain volume）为具有物理依据的分子尺度描述符，并提供将纳米尺度组织与离子液体宏观输运性质相联系的通用构效框架（structure property framework）。

Abstract: Developing molecular scale structural descriptors that can quantitatively predict the macroscopic transport properties of ionic liquids remains a longstanding question, owing to the complex interplay between molecular interactions, nanoscale organization, and collective ion dynamics. Here, we use all atom molecular dynamics simulations to establish quantitative structure property relationships between the equilibrium microstructure and two key transport properties viscosity and ionic conductivity in a series of imidazolium based ILs with chemically distinct anions and varying alkyl chain lengths. While viscosity and ionic conductivity exhibit distinct dependencies on alkyl chain length and anion chemistry, these seemingly different trends largely collapse onto unified correlations when expressed in terms of the mean polar-domain volume. In particular, both transport properties exhibit systematic power-law scaling with the mean polar domain volume fraction, revealing a common structural origin underlying the variations in ion and momentum transport across chemically distinct ILs. These results establish polar-domain volume as a physically motivated molecular-scale descriptor and provide a general structure property framework for connecting nanoscale organization to macroscopic transport properties in ILs.

</details>


### [156] [Fabrication framework for three-dimensional colloidal particles with decoupled geometry and material composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36747)
*Hamed Almohammadi,Gurminder K. Paink,Vasumathi Venkat,Jacopo Movilli,Atalaya Milan Wilborn,Haritosh Patel,Haichao Wu,David Weitz,Joanna Aizenberg*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种通用制备框架，将粒子几何形状与材料组成解耦，可在胶体尺度制备具有复杂三维构型的自由站立刺激响应粒子，并展示其可逆形变与集体光学动力学行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形状可编程（shape-programmable）粒子在微机器人系统与具有涌现功能（emergent functionalities）的层级材料中具有重要潜力，但在胶体尺度制备兼具复杂三维几何与刺激响应性的粒子仍是主要挑战，尤其难以将几何构型与材料选择解耦。

Method: 方法结合软光刻（soft lithography）、溶胀辅助萃取（swelling-assisted extraction）与牺牲黏附转移（sacrificial adhesive transfer），以实现高几何保真度的粒子制备；并建立预测性框架以界定可及的设计空间（design space），进而拓展至高纵横比柱阵列等复杂三维构型。材料方面分别采用液晶弹性体（liquid crystal elastomer）与水凝胶（hydrogel）制备粒子。

Result: 制备出高几何保真度的自由站立三维胶体粒子，并可拓展至高纵横比（high-aspect-ratio）柱阵列结构；液晶弹性体粒子在热与光刺激下呈现圆柱形与手性（chiral）形状的可逆形变。在悬浮体系中，粒子表现出集体光学动力学行为，源于粒子内程序化液晶组织变化与刺激诱导几何重构之间的耦合。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一个通用平台，可实现几何可编程胶体粒子并赋予其涌现的集体功能，为动态可编程性质的材料与流体提供了路径。

Abstract: Shape-programmable particles offer significant opportunities for microrobotic systems at the individual level and for hierarchical materials with emergent functionalities arising from collective particle behavior. However, fabricating shape-changing stimuli-responsive particles with complex three-dimensional geometries at colloidal length scales remains a major challenge. Here, we introduce a general fabrication framework that decouples particle geometry from material composition to produce free-standing three-dimensional colloidal particles with complex architectures. Our approach combines soft lithography, swelling-assisted extraction, and sacrificial adhesive transfer to fabricate particles with high geometric fidelity. We establish a predictive framework that defines the accessible design space for increasingly complex particle geometries. We further extend our framework to fabricate high-aspect-ratio pillar arrays with intricate three-dimensional architectures at colloidal length scales. To demonstrate material versatility, we fabricate particles from both liquid crystal elastomers and hydrogels. We show reversible shape-changing behavior of liquid crystal elastomer particles with cylindrical and chiral shapes under thermal and optical stimuli. In suspension, these particles display collective optical dynamic behavior arising from coupling between changes in the programmed liquid crystal organization within the particles and stimulus-induced geometric reconfiguration of the particles. Collectively, this work establishes a versatile platform for geometry-programmable colloidal particles with emergent collective functionalities, providing a route toward materials and fluids with dynamically programmable properties.

</details>


### [157] [Synthesis and modification of thin NaSICON solid electrolytes using ion beams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36758)
*Giovanni Ceccio,Jiri Vacik,Ivan Mastronardo,Clausia D Urso,Eva Stepanovska,Romana Miksova*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究制备了NASICON复合靶材与连续纳米薄膜，并利用1.1 MeV镍离子注入，通过电化学阻抗谱（EIS）研究离子注入对薄膜电学性能的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 钠基超离子导体（NaSICON）自1976年提出后因优异离子导电性受到关注，但成功制备为薄膜的钠基固态电解质（SEs）较少；薄膜可降低随厚度增加的电阻，且纳米尺度化有望显著提升离子导电性。

Method: 采用混合氧化物法与行星式球磨，经1300 °C固态法合成NASICON复合靶材；在低能离子装置中溅射制备连续NASICON纳米薄膜；使用CANAM设施（NPI Řež）的Tandetron加速器，以1.1 MeV、不同注量的Ni离子轰击薄膜。

Result: 通过电化学阻抗谱（EIS）分析了合成薄膜与注入薄膜的电学性能，给出了辐照对NASICON性能影响的结果。

Conclusion: 研究展示了离子注入对NASICON纳米薄膜电学性能的影响，相关结果可为钠基全固态电池（ASSBs）薄膜电解质的开发提供依据。

Abstract: Solid electrolytes (SEs) for sodium-based superionic conductors (NaSICON) were first introduced in 1976 and quickly recognized for their excellent ionic conductivity. While considerable effort has been made to develop thin electrolytes for all-solid-state batteries (ASSBs), only a few sodium-based SEs have been successfully fabricated as thin films. These thin films are particularly desirable for their reduced electrical resistance, which typically increases with the thickness of the SE. By reducing the thickness of the SEs to the nanometer scale, their ionic conductivity can be significantly enhanced. In this study, the NASICON composite was initially prepared in the form of pellets using the mixed oxide technique with a planetary ball mill and synthesized by the solid-state method at 1300 °C. The resulting pellets were used as sputtering targets in a low-energy ion facility to prepare continuous NASICON nanofilms. To explore the effect of ion implantation on the electrical properties of NASICON, the prepared films were bombarded with Ni ions at 1.1 MeV and varying fluences, using the Tandetron accelerator at the CANAM infrastructure (NPI Řež). The electrical properties of both the synthesized and implanted films were analyzed through electrochemical impedance spectroscopy (EIS). The results, describing the impact of irradiation on NASICON's properties, are presented here.

</details>


### [158] [Evidence for Distributed Fault Energetics and Their Impact on Deformation in a Chemically Complex Alloy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36780)
*Kaijun Yin,Jun-Ping Du,Peijun Yu,Rui Feng,Hanyu Hou,Haw-Wen Hsiao,Ke An,Peter K. Liaw,Shigenobu Ogata,Jian-Min Zuo*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究提出基于分布的框架（distribution-based framework），将实验测得的层错宽度（stacking fault width）与形变相关的表观层错能（apparent fault energy）关联，揭示 CrCoNi 中呈分布的局部层错能景观（local fault-energy landscape）及其对形变机制竞争的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 化学复杂合金（chemically complex alloys）具有本征非均匀的局部化学环境，导致局部层错能量涨落（fluctuations in local fault energetics），但实验上难以量化这种关系，使该分布式能量景观（distributed energy landscape）在形变机制中的作用尚未完全厘清。

Method: 建立基于分布的框架（distribution-based framework），将实验测量的层错宽度（stacking fault width）映射为形变相关的表观层错能（apparent fault energy）；结合原子尺度模拟（atomistic simulations）与一维动力学蒙特卡洛模拟（one-dimensional kinetic Monte Carlo simulations），并以电子显微观察（electron microscopy observations）作为支撑。

Result: 框架揭示了能量涨落（energy fluctuations）的稳定化作用，并捕捉到热处理后表观层错能由负值向零上移的转变，原子模拟将该转变归因于 L12 型化学短程有序（L12 type chemical short-range order）的出现；模拟与显微观察进一步表明，局部层错能景观的历史依赖变化会偏向层错（stacking faulting）、纳米孪生（nano-twinning）与 HCP 相变（HCP transformation）之间的竞争。

Conclusion: 研究提供一个以实验为锚定、基于分布的框架（experimentally anchored, distribution-based framework），用于理解化学复杂合金在由局部化学有序（local chemical order）塑造的分布式层错能景观中的形变演化。

Abstract: Chemically complex alloys feature intrinsically heterogeneous local chemical environments and, consequently, fluctuations in local fault energetics. However, experimentally quantifying their relationship remains challenging, leaving the role of this distributed energy landscape in deformation mechanisms incompletely resolved. Here, we develop a distribution based framework linking experimentally measured stacking fault widths to deformation relevant apparent fault energy, revealing a distributed local fault-energy landscape in CrCoNi. The framework reveals the stabilizing role of energy fluctuations and captures an upward shift in the apparent fault energetics, from negative values toward zero following heat treatment, which atomistic simulations associate with the emergence of L12 type chemical short-range order. Using one-dimensional kinetic Monte Carlo simulations, supported by electron microscopy observations, we further show that history-dependent changes in the local fault-energy landscape bias the competition among stacking faulting, nano-twinning and HCP transformation in CrCoNi. Our results provide an experimentally anchored, distribution-based framework for understanding deformation in chemically complex alloys as it evolves within a distributed fault-energy landscape shaped by local chemical order.

</details>


### [159] [Orientation engineering is a universal strategy for ferroelectric trans-switching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36871)
*Cameron Scott,Jorge Íñiguez-González*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文主张“取向工程（orientation engineering）”，即通过控制外延薄膜（epitaxial thin films）的生长方向，作为“应变工程（strain engineering）”的互补设计路径，可赋予常规铁电化合物（ferroelectric compounds）非常规功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 约二十年前兴起的“应变工程”已成为调控外延薄膜的有力策略，但作者认为取向工程这一互补设计路线尚未获得足够关注，且可能实现其他方法难以或无法获得的新奇行为。

Method: 采用唯象计算（phenomenological calculations），以代表性化合物 LiNbO$_3$ 与 BaTiO$_3$ 为对象，分析取向工程及电极电屏蔽（electric screening）调控对铁电行为的影响。

Result: 预测取向工程可促成“跨向翻转（trans-switching）”，即极化分量垂直于外加电场方向发生翻转，为新型三维器件架构（3D device architectures）提供可能；同时通过调控接触电极提供的电屏蔽，可获得铁电性（ferrielectric behavior），甚至设计出翻转过程实质上完全横向于外加电场的工作模式。

Conclusion: 取向工程被视为一种强大且通用的方法，可解锁常规铁电体的非常规功能，值得更多关注与研究。

Abstract: About two decades ago, ``strain engineering'' emerged as a powerful strategy to tune epitaxial thin films. Here we contend that ''orientation engineering'' - that is, controlling the film's growth direction - constitutes a complementary design route that opens unprecedented opportunities and deserves greater attention. Specifically, we show that orientation engineering can endow standard ferroelectric compounds with exotic and useful behaviors that may be difficult or impossible to obtain otherwise. Critically, we predict it readily enables ''trans-switching,'' where polarization components switch perpendicularly to the applied electric field, paving the way for novel 3D device architectures. We also show that, by controlling the electric screening provided by electrodes in contact with the film, ferrielectric behavior can be obtained. Notably, one can even design an operational mode where the switching is, in effect, fully transversal to the applied field. Supported by phenomenological calculations for representative compounds LiNbO$_3$ and BaTiO$_3$, we thus propose orientation engineering as a powerful and general method to unlock unprecedented functionalities in common ferroelectrics.

</details>


### [160] [Tuning the secondary electron yield and electronic conductivity of MgZnO multilayers synthesized by Atomic layer deposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36919)
*Mathieu Lafarie,Sarah Dadouch,Fréderic Miserque,Yunlin Zheng,Jocelyne Leroy,Mohamed Belhaj,Thomas Proslier*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究利用原子层沉积（atomic layer deposition, ALD）制备纳米级多元多层异质结构，实现对二次电子发射（secondary electron emission, SEE）与电导率的同步调控，获得单相材料难以实现的性能组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高能电子与材料表面相互作用产生的二次电子发射会在真空射频（radio-frequency, RF）系统中引发有害效应（尤其是一级电子倍增放电，multipacting），降低性能并威胁器件完整性；然而现有有效且被广泛采用的抑制策略仍然有限。

Method: 采用原子层沉积（ALD）制备纳米级多元多层结构，给出该异质结构的设计与制备方案，并对一系列薄膜进行系统表征，以建立发射特性、电导率与化学及结构特征之间的关联。

Result: 此类异质结构可实现单相材料无法获得的二次电子总发射产额（total electron emission yield, TEEY）与电导率的组合，包括高TEEY与相对高电导率并存，以及低TEEY与低电导率并存的区间。

Conclusion: 该结果为真空电子应用中功能表面的理性设计开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Secondary electron emission (SEE), arising from the interaction of energetic electrons with material surfaces, can induce deleterious effects in vacuum radio-frequency (RF) systems, notably multipacting, thereby degrading performance and threatening device integrity. Despite extensive efforts, effective and widely adopted mitigation strategies remain limited. Here, we report an approach to simultaneously tailor SEE and electrical conductivity using nanometric multi-element multilayers deposited by atomic layer deposition (ALD). The design and fabrication of these heterostructures are presented, and a range of films is systematically characterized to establish correlations between emission properties, electrical conductivity, chemical and structural features. We demonstrate that such heterostructures enable combinations of total electron emission yield (TEEY) and conductivity unattainable in single-phase materials, including regimes combining high TEEY with relatively high conductivity and low TEEY with low conductivities. These results open new avenues for the rational design of functional surfaces for vacuum electronic applications.

</details>


### [161] [24-hr Solid-State Power Generation with Self-Adaptive Tunable Radiative Coatings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36983)
*Ken Araki,Liping Wang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文实验验证了一种集成可调辐射涂层（tunable radiative coating）的温差发电器件，可实现昼夜连续固态发电（solid-state power generation）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实现昼夜连续、无需外部控制的固态发电，并提升相对于黑体吸收器（black absorber）的发电性能。

Method: 采用热致变色VO2薄膜（thermochromic VO2 thin film）置于重掺杂硅衬底（heavily doped silicon substrate）上，中间加入相移硅间隔层（phase-shifting silicon spacer），构成随温度自适应切换的辐射涂层，并集成商用温差发电模块；通过高真空与自然环境户外测试评估性能，并阐明其物理机制。

Result: 涂层白天太阳吸收率0.90、红外发射率0.23，夜间发射率0.85，大气透明窗口（atmospheric transparency window）内发射率变化达0.62。真空测试中，35 mm²涂层在正午产生1.64 W/m²功率，较黑体吸收器高32%；夜间产生26 mW/m²，优于黑体发射器63%。自然对流环境下，2英寸圆形涂层正午发电0.48 W/m²，较同尺寸黑体高14%。24小时连续户外测试中，开路电压（open circuit voltage）在真空由433 mV至-62 mV，在自然环境由300 mV至-51 mV。

Conclusion: 该温度自适应辐射涂层可无需外部控制地在昼夜间切换吸收与辐射功能，实现连续固态发电并优于黑体方案。

Abstract: Day-night solid-state power generation is experimentally demonstrated with thermoelectric generators integrated with a tunable radiative coating, which switches between a selective solar absorber during daytime and a radiative cooler during nighttime by temperature without any external control. The self-adaptive radiative coating is carefully designed with thermochromic VO2 thin film on heavily doped silicon substrate along with a phase-shifting silicon spacer in between, whose physical mechanism is elucidated. The fabricated coating exhibits a high solar absorptivity of 0.90 and a low infrared emissivity of 0.23 during daytime, and a high emissivity of 0.85 at night, resulting in large emissivity change of 0.62 within the atmospheric transparency window upon metal-to-insulator phase transition. From outdoor tests in a high vacuum apparatus, the tunable radiative coating in 35-mm squared size achieves 1.64 W/m2 power generation around noon time from a stack of commercial thermoelectric modules of 15 mm squared, which is 32% more from that with a black absorber. At night, it produces 26 mW/m2 power, outperforming the black emitter by 63%. Even exposed the ambient with convective loss, the 2-inch-round tunable coating generates power of 0.48 W/m2 at noon time, 14% more than the black absorber of the same size. The tunable radiative coating covered thermoelectric modules produces open circuit voltages from 433 mV to -62 mV in vacuum and from 300 mV to -51 mV in ambient through 24-hr continuous outdoor tests.

</details>


### [162] [HyTopo A High-Performance Hierarchical Algorithm for Mapping the Structural Evolution and Transition Networks of Gas Hydrates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37036)
*Zherui Chen,Shuquan Huang,Ke Xu,Zhanbo Li,Xinyu Song,Haijun Wang,Dingyuan Tang,Qingxia Liu,Yajun Deng*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出以笼拓扑（cage topology）为核心的分析框架 HyTopo，用于识别水合物（hydrate）形成与解离过程中的笼结构演化。该框架结合水合物序参量、分级环/笼识别与笼转变网络分析，揭示了水合物演化受异质且路径依赖的笼动力学控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解析水合物形成与解离机制，不仅需要识别类水合物水（hydrate-like water），还需确定无序液态水重组为或逃逸出晶态水合物网络时所依赖的具体笼拓扑。现有分析视角对此拓扑信息的刻画不足，故需发展以笼拓扑为中心的解析工具。

Method: 提出 HyTopo 框架，整合水合物序参量、分级环与笼识别（hierarchical ring and cage recognition）以及笼转变网络分析（cage-transition network analysis）。基于局部取向关联（local orientational correlations），将水分子分类为类液态、类前驱体、类水合物与类冰环境，并以此引导对晶态水合物笼及缺陷中间体的高效拓扑搜索。

Result: 应用于甲烷水合物（methane hydrate）生长、均相成核（homogeneous nucleation）与减压诱导解离（depressurization-induced dissociation）表明：水合物演化由异质且路径依赖的笼动力学支配。生长过程中 EA-WPU 抑制笼状结构整体增殖并改变缺陷笼与晶态 sI 笼的平衡；成核早期由不完整笼基元主导，并作为结构储备生成少量稳定的 5¹² 与 5¹²6² 笼；解离经界面塌缩、体相分解与溶解气体析出进行，笼寿命与转变路径强烈依赖笼拓扑。

Conclusion: HyTopo 提供了物理可解释、拓扑分辨的途径，将局部水有序化、笼形成、结构缺陷与水合物相变联系起来，适用于复杂分子模拟中的相关研究。

Abstract: Resolving the formation and dissociation mechanisms of hydrate requires identifying not only hydrate-like water, but also the specific cage topologies by which disordered liquid water reorganizes into, or escapes from, crystalline hydrate networks. Here we introduce HyTopo, a cage-topology-centered analysis framework that combines a hydrate order parameter, hierarchical ring and cage recognition, and cage-transition network analysis. Based on local orientational correlations, HyTopo classifies water molecules into liquid-like, precursor-like, hydrate-like, and ice-like environments, and uses this information to guide efficient topological searches for crystalline hydrate cages and defective intermediates. Applications to methane hydrate growth, homogeneous nucleation, and depressurization-induced dissociation reveal that hydrate evolution is governed by heterogeneous and pathway-dependent cage dynamics. During hydrate growth, EA-WPU suppresses the overall proliferation of cage-like structures while altering the balance between defective cages and crystalline sI cages. During nucleation, incomplete cage motifs dominate the early pathway and provide a structural reservoir from which a small fraction of stable 512 and 51262 cages. Hydrate dissociation proceeds through interfacial collapse, bulk decomposition, and dissolved-gas exsolution, with cage lifetimes and transition pathways strongly dependent on cage topology. These results show that HyTopo provides a physically interpretable and topology-resolved route to connect local water ordering, cage formation, structural defects, and hydrate phase transitions in complex molecular simulations.

</details>


### [163] [Linear-Scaling Quantum Transport from Machine-Learning Density Functional Theory Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37058)
*Bang Liu,Yang Zhong,Zhi-Xin Guo,Xin-Gao Gong,Hongjun Xiang*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作提出 HamGNN-NEGF，一个线性标度（linear-scaling）且具备 DFT 级精度（DFT-level fidelity）的量子输运模拟框架，用 E(3) 等变图神经网络（E(3)-equivariant graph neural network）预测大器件哈密顿量（Hamiltonian），并结合电极自能（self-energy）、非正交核多项式方法（nonorthogonal kernel polynomial method）与递归格林函数（recursive Green's function）实现器件长度线性标度的输运计算，可模拟超过 10,000 原子的体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将密度泛函理论（DFT）与非平衡格林函数（nonequilibrium Green's function, NEGF）结合的 DFT-NEGF 模拟对现代技术重要，但其标度（scaling）性能差，长期将预测性模拟限制在远小于真实器件万原子量级的小型理想化体系。因此需要兼顾第一性原理精度与器件级规模的计算框架。

Method: 核心是 HamGNN-NEGF 框架：以在小型结构常规 DFT 哈密顿量上训练的 E(3) 等变图神经网络预测大器件哈密顿量，从而避免昂贵的 DFT-NEGF 训练数据；将预测哈密顿量与 DFT 导出的电极自能、用于费米能级（Fermi level）确定的非正交核多项式方法以及递归格林函数算法结合，使固定截面（cross section）下计算成本随器件长度线性增长。

Result: 即使器件原子数少于 500，HamGNN-NEGF 相对完全自洽（fully self-consistent）DFT-NEGF 实现超过三个数量级的加速，且优势随体系规模增大而增强。在原始 Pt-Si-Pt、掺杂 Pt-Si:P-Pt 与 Pt-分子-Pt 结（junction）上的基准测试显示 meV 级哈密顿量精度、可信的透射谱（transmission spectra）以及超过 10,000 原子的预测性模拟。消除输运自洽性（transport self-consistency）还使 HSE06 等杂化泛函（hybrid functional）无需额外 NEGF 开销即可使用；零偏压哈密顿量近似（zero-bias Hamiltonian approximation）将框架扩展到弱非线性区（weakly nonlinear regimes）的有限偏压输运。

Conclusion: HamGNN-NEGF 弥合了第一性原理精度与器件级模拟之间的差距，为真实纳米电子与量子器件的预测性建模提供了实用途径。

Abstract: Quantum transport simulations that combine density functional theory (DFT) with the nonequilibrium Green's function formalism (DFT-NEGF) are important to modern technology, yet their unfavorable scaling has long confined predictive simulations to small, idealized systems far below the ten-thousand-atom scale of realistic devices. Here, we introduce HamGNN-NEGF, a linear-scaling framework with DFT-level fidelity. An E(3)-equivariant graph neural network trained on conventional DFT Hamiltonians of small structures predicts Hamiltonians for large devices, avoiding costly DFT-NEGF training data. The predicted Hamiltonians are integrated with DFT-derived electrode self-energies, a nonorthogonal kernel polynomial method for Fermi-level determination, and a recursive Green's function algorithm, yielding a computational cost that scales linearly with device length at fixed cross section. Even for devices containing fewer than 500 atoms, HamGNN-NEGF achieves speedups exceeding three orders of magnitude over fully self-consistent DFT-NEGF, with the advantage increasing further with system size. Benchmarks on pristine Pt-Si-Pt, doped Pt-Si:P-Pt, and Pt-molecule-Pt junctions demonstrate meV-level Hamiltonian accuracy, faithful transmission spectra, and predictive simulations beyond 10,000 atoms. Eliminating transport self-consistency also enables hybrid functionals such as HSE06 without additional NEGF overhead, while a zero-bias Hamiltonian approximation extends the framework to finite-bias transport in weakly nonlinear regimes. HamGNN-NEGF thus bridges first-principles accuracy and device-scale simulation, providing a practical route toward predictive modeling of realistic nanoelectronic and quantum devices.

</details>


### [164] [Defect-controlled electrical and optical properties of CrN thin films: experiment and first-principles study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37075)
*J. Bulíř,U. D. Wdowik,J. More-Chevalier,P. Hubík,E. de Prado,M. Vondráček,L. Fekete,M. Novotný,J. Lančok,D. Legut*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 采用射频磁控溅射（RF magnetron sputtering）在 Ar-N$_2$ 反应气氛中沉积 CrN 薄膜，系统考察氮含量与衬底温度对结构、电学和光学性质的影响；结合密度泛函理论（DFT）计算，揭示空位缺陷对电子结构与导电类型转变的调控作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探究氮含量与衬底温度对 CrN 薄膜结构、电学与光学性质的调控规律，并阐明实验中观察到的导电类型转变（p 型至 n 型）的微观机制。

Method: 在 400–800 °C 下，以 Ar-N$_2$ 反应气氛通过射频磁控溅射在熔融石英与 MgO (001) 衬底上沉积薄膜；采用 X 射线衍射（XRD）与原子力显微镜（AFM）表征结构，紫外-可见-近红外及红外光谱椭偏（spectroscopic ellipsometry）测量介电函数与吸收边，并辅以密度泛函理论（DFT）计算。

Result: 过化学计量比（overstoichiometric）CrN 薄膜电阻率低于 6 mΩ·cm 并呈现稳定 p 型行为；衬底温度高于 600 °C 时发生向 n 型导电的转变。介电函数与吸收边强烈依赖于化学计量比与沉积条件，结构表征确认结晶性与晶粒生长受衬底影响。

Conclusion: DFT 计算表明阳离子与阴离子空位显著改变电子结构与光学响应，可解释实验观测的导电性转变；沉积参数为通过缺陷工程（defect engineering）调控 CrN 薄膜提供了有效途径。

Abstract: Thin chromium nitride (CrN) films were deposited by RF magnetron sputtering in a reactive atmosphere of Ar-N$_2$ on fused silica and MgO (001) substrates at temperatures between 400 $^o$C and 800 $^o$C. The influence of nitrogen content and substrate temperature on structural, electrical, and optical properties was systematically investigated. The overstoichiometric CrN films exhibited a reduced resistivity below 6 m$Ω\cdot$cm with stable p-type behavior, while increasing the substrate temperature above 600 $^o$C induced a transition to n-type conductivity. Spectroscopic ellipsometry in the UV-Vis-NIR and infrared spectral ranges revealed a strong dependence of the dielectric function and the absorption edge on film stoichiometry and deposition conditions. Structural characterization using X-ray diffraction and atomic force microscopy confirmed substrate-dependent crystallinity and grain growth. Density functional theory calculations showed that cation and anion vacancies strongly modify the electronic structure and optical response, explaining the experimentally observed conductivity transitions. The results demonstrate that the deposition parameters provide an effective means to tailor CrN thin films via defect engineering.

</details>


### [165] [Engineering Synaptic Dynamics in Ag-Modified TaO$_x$ Memristive Devices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37204)
*R. Leal Martir,W. Quiñonez,A. J. T. van der Ree,M. H. Aguirre,G. Palasantzas,M. J. Sánchez,D. Rubi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过 Ag 修饰 TaO$_x$ 忆阻器（memristor），在突触抑制（synaptic depression）中引入电可选的自由度；仅调节脉冲幅度即可在同一器件上实现渐进—突变—渐进的三种更新机制，并在 MNIST/Fashion-MNIST 任务中验证其功能影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 忆阻神经形态硬件（memristive neuromorphic hardware）的挑战不仅在于调控突触权重更新的幅度，还在于调控其动力学（dynamics）。研究动机是寻找一种可电控的方式，使抑制动力学从固定的器件特性转变为可编程属性。

Method: 对 TaO$_x$ 忆阻器进行 Ag 修饰，利用 Ag 相关动力学与氧空位（oxygen-vacancy）动力学之间的相互作用；通过改变脉冲幅度驱动器件状态。并构建最小耦合态模型（minimal coupled-state model），其中空位相关开关变量与较慢的 Ag 相关内部自由度相互作用。功能评估采用基于忆阻器的多层感知机（multilayer perceptron）。

Result: 参考器件表现为常规双极开关（bipolar switching）；Ag 修饰器件呈现对称的“桌腿”型迟滞回线（table-with-legs hysteresis loops）、亚稳中间态（metastable intermediate states）以及抑制动力学对编程幅度的强非单调依赖。仅改变脉冲幅度可使同一器件在三种机制间切换：渐进 S 型抑制、集中于少数脉冲内的突变更新、以及跨越一百个以上脉冲的广泛分布演化。MNIST 上突变、S 型与缓慢演化响应分别达到约 50%、85%、88% 的准确率，渐进机制在 Fashion-MNIST 上约 72%。

Conclusion: Ag 相关动力学与氧空位动力学的耦合，可将突触抑制从固定的器件特性转变为电可编程属性，为忆阻神经形态硬件提供额外的设计自由度。

Abstract: Engineering not only the magnitude but also the dynamics of synaptic weight updates is an important challenge for memristive neuromorphic hardware. Here, we show that Ag modification of TaO$_x$ memristors introduces an electrically selectable degree of freedom in synaptic depression through the interplay between Ag-related and oxygen-vacancy dynamics. Reference devices exhibit conventional bipolar switching, whereas Ag-modified devices display symmetric table-with-legs hysteresis loops, metastable intermediate states, and a strongly non-monotonic dependence of depression dynamics on programming amplitude. By varying only the pulse amplitude, the same device can be driven among three regimes: gradual sigmoidal depression, an abrupt update concentrated within a few pulses, and a broadly distributed evolution extending over more than one hundred pulses. A minimal coupled-state model, in which a vacancy-related switching variable interacts with a slower Ag-related internal degree of freedom, reproduces this gradual--abrupt--gradual crossover using two individually monotonic field-activated processes. The functional impact is assessed in a memristor-based multilayer perceptron. For MNIST, the abrupt, sigmoidal, and slowly evolving responses yield accuracies of approximately 50%, 85%, and 88%, respectively, while the gradual regime reaches approximately 72% for Fashion-MNIST. These results show that coupling Ag-related and oxygen-vacancy dynamics can transform synaptic depression from a fixed device characteristic into an electrically programmable property.

</details>


### [166] [Vapor phase growth and characterization of van der Waals BiSbTeSe platelets on semiconducting MoS2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37303)
*M. Zhezhu,V. Torosyan,V. Mehrabyan,A. Vasilev*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究实现了三元拓扑绝缘体 BSTS 纳米片在半导体 MoS2 衬底上的直接范德华气相生长，并建立了厚度、成分与拉曼光谱之间的关联。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BiSbTeSe（BSTS）等 tetradymite 族三维拓扑绝缘体因补偿型体绝缘特性，适用于自旋电子学与量子器件；但其纳米结构生长通常依赖复杂设备、有机金属前驱体或绝缘衬底，在技术相关的半导体衬底上的简单生长尚缺乏探索。

Method: 以预合成的 BiSbTe1.5Se1.5 晶体为源材料，采用气相生长（vapor phase growth）在半导体 MoS2 上直接生长范德华（van der Waals）BSTS 纳米片，并结合 AFM 厚度测量、拉曼光谱（Raman spectra）与 EDS 成分分析进行表征。

Result: 生长的 BSTS 纳米片厚度为 4–246 nm，单个纳米片平均横向尺寸约 1.4 μm；拉曼谱同时包含 BSTS 与底层 MoS2 的贡献，其中 A21g 模式对厚度与局域成分最敏感；EDS 显示成分随厚度系统性变化，在约 49 nm 处出现交叉，与 E2g 拉曼带低波数分量厚度依赖关系的转变相吻合。

Conclusion: 成分变化被初步归因于生长过程中组成元素的差异挥发性（differential volatility）；MoS2 可作为 BSTS 沉积的潜在线范德华平台，并为后续 BSTS-MoS2 异质结研究提供结构与成分基线。

Abstract: Three dimensional topological insulators of the tetradymite family, such as BiSbTeSe (BSTS), are attractive for spintronic and quantum device applications because of their compensated, bulk-insulating character. Nanostructured BSTS platelets are promising for practical TI based devices. However, their growth often requires complex equipment, organometallic precursors, or electrically insulating substrates. Simple growth on technologically relevant semiconducting substrates remains largely unexplored. Here, we report the vapor phase growth of van der Waals BSTS platelets directly on semiconducting MoS2 using a presynthesized BiSbTe1.5Se1.5 crystal as the source material. The grown BSTS platelets exhibit thicknesses ranging from 4 nm to 246 nm, with an average lateral size of 1.4 mkm for individual platelets. Raman spectra of platelets, acquired in correlation with AFM thickness measurements, reveal contributions from both BSTS and the underlying MoS2 substrate. Among the characteristic BSTS modes, A21g mode shows the highest sensitivity to platelet thickness and local composition. EDS analysis reveals a systematic thickness dependence of the platelet composition, with an apparent crossover around 49 nm that coincides with a change in the thickness dependence of the low-wavenumber component of the E2g Raman band. This correlation suggests a composition-related origin of the Raman behavior, with the compositional variations tentatively attributed to the differential volatility of the constituent elements during growth. These results demonstrate that MoS2 is a promising van der Waals platform for BSTS deposition and provide a structural and compositional baseline for future studies of BSTS-MoS2 heterostructures.

</details>


### [167] [Topological Hall Effect Induced by Chiral Spin Textures at the Ferroelectric/Ferromagnetic Interface](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37406)
*Jingkuan Xiao,Yaqing Han,Jianfeng Guo,Renjun Du,Jiawei Jiang,Baoshan Cui,Runnong Zhou,Siqin Wang,Siqi Jiang,Fuzhuo Lian,Di Zhang,Guodong Ma,Jiabei Huang,Zhaochen Qu,Wanting Xu,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Alexander S. Mayorov,Jinsheng Wen,Haifeng Ding,Gong Chen,Ahmet Avsar,Hongxin Yang,Lihong Bao,Hong-Jun Gao,Shiyu Zhu,Lei Wang,Geliang Yu*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究在少层 Fe$_3$GeTe$_2$/$α$-In$_2$Se$_3$ 范德华异质结中观察到由界面 Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya 相互作用（DMI）稳定的手性自旋织构，并通过铁电极化实现非易失调控，获得四种可编程霍尔电阻态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 手性自旋织构因手性与拓扑稳定性在下一代计算技术中具潜力，而铁电/铁磁范德华异质结可结合界面反演对称性破缺、自旋轨道耦合与可切换铁电极化，成为实现与调控界面 DMI 的理想平台。

Method: 对少层 Fe$_3$GeTe$_2$/$α$-In$_2$Se$_3$ 异质结进行输运测量（拓扑霍尔信号与厚度依赖输运）、低温磁力显微镜（magnetic force microscopy）成像，并结合原子尺度自旋动力学模拟（atomistic spin-dynamics simulations）与第一性原理计算（first-principles calculations）。

Result: 在矫顽场（coercive field）上下识别出两组拓扑霍尔信号；厚度依赖输运显示临界温度随 Fe$_3$GeTe$_2$ 层数增加而显著降低；低温磁力显微镜在矫顽场附近观察到两类磁对比相反的磁泡（magnetic bubbles），分别对应不同拓扑霍尔信号；切换 $α$-In$_2$Se$_3$ 铁电极化可非易失调制反常与拓扑霍尔效应。

Conclusion: 结果支持界面 DMI 稳定的手性自旋织构的形成；铁电与磁的双稳定性产生四种可由电场和磁场编程的霍尔电阻态，凸显范德华界面在先进器件应用中的潜力。

Abstract: Chiral spin textures, largely driven by the Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction, offer significant potential for next-generation computing technologies due to their chirality and topological stability. Ferroelectric/ferromagnetic van der Waals heterostructures are particularly appealing because they can combine interfacial inversion-symmetry breaking and spin-orbit coupling to promote interfacial Dzyaloshinskii-Moriya interaction, while switchable ferroelectric polarization provides a nonvolatile tuning knob. This study investigates interfacial chiral spin textures in few-layer Fe$_3$GeTe$_2$/$α$-In$_2$Se$_3$ heterostructures. Two groups of topological Hall signals are identified just below and above the coercive field, and thickness-dependent transport reveals a notable reduction in critical temperature with increasing Fe$_3$GeTe$_2$ layer thickness. Low-temperature magnetic force microscopy images reveal two types of magnetic bubbles with opposite magnetic contrasts near the coercive field, each associated with distinct topological Hall signals. Together with atomistic spin-dynamics simulations and first-principles calculations, these results support the formation of interfacial DMI-stabilized chiral spin textures. Switching the ferroelectric polarization of the $α$-In$_2$Se$_3$ layer further enables nonvolatile modulation of both anomalous and topological Hall effects. The resulting ferroelectric and magnetic bistabilities generate four distinguishable Hall resistance states programmable by electric and magnetic fields. These findings highlight the potential of van der Waals interfaces for advanced device applications.

</details>


### [168] [Operando Surface Probe Microscopy Reveals Electrochemical Origins of Reliability Variability in Hafnia Ferroelectrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37516)
*Anudeep Tullibilli,Kartick Biswas,Shubham Kumar Parate,Pavan Nukala*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究利用原位（operando）纳米尺度表面测量，揭示铪基铁电体（hafnia-based ferroelectrics）器件可靠性由界面电化学过程主导，并通过调整工艺条件在不改变铁电相的前提下实现100%器件良率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 器件间与样品间变异性（device-to-device and sample-to-sample variability）仍是铪基铁电体可靠应用的主要障碍，即使在名义相同的结构与工艺条件下亦然，因此需要能提供工艺相关反馈的原位诊断探针（operando diagnostic probes），而非仅依赖常规结构相分析。

Method: 以生长于La0.67Sr0.33MnO3（LSMO）缓冲SrTiO3衬底上的外延菱方相Y:HfO2薄膜为模型体系，联合使用原位原子力显微镜（AFM）、导电AFM、压电响应力显微镜（PFM）与关联STEM-EELS，将表面演化与界面电化学直接关联。

Result: 呈现wake-up行为的器件表面高度均匀增加，源于LSMO电极的均匀氧化及极化翻转过程中氧向铁电层迁移；而漏电器件则出现明显表面粗化与起泡（blister）现象，源于原始LSMO电极中空间不均匀的氧化态在LSMO/Y:HfO2界面引发竞争性析氧反应（oxygen evolution reactions），其伴随的电子产生导致瞬态漏电。

Conclusion: 基于上述机理认识，对工艺条件作简单修改（不改变已优化的铁电相）即可抑制竞争性电化学路径，可重复获得100%铁电器件。该工作确立原位表面演化为探测隐藏电化学过程的纳米尺度诊断手段，并为提升铪基铁电器件的可靠性与可重复性提供实用路径。

Abstract: Device-to-device and sample-to-sample variability remains a major obstacle to the reliable implementation of hafnia-based ferroelectrics, even in nominally identical structures fabricated under similar processing conditions. This highlights the need for operando diagnostic probes that provide process-relevant feedback beyond conventional structural phase analysis. Here, we demonstrate that operando nanoscale surface measurements serve as sensitive probe of the local electrochemical processes governing reliability in hafnia ferroelectric devices. Using epitaxial rhombohedral Y:HfO2 films grown on La0.67Sr0.33MnO3 (LSMO)-buffered SrTiO3 as a model system, we combine operando atomic force microscopy, conductive AFM, piezoresponse force microscopy, and correlative STEM-EELS to directly link surface evolution with interfacial electrochemistry. Devices exhibiting wake-up show a spatially uniform increase in surface height, arising from homogeneous oxidation of the LSMO electrode followed by oxygen migration into the ferroelectric layer during polarization switching. In contrast, leaky devices display pronounced surface roughening and blister formation, originating from spatially inhomogeneous oxidation states in the pristine LSMO electrode that promote competing oxygen evolution reactions at the LSMO/Y:HfO2 interface. The accompanying electron generation produces leakage which is transient. Guided by these mechanistic insights, we introduce simple modifications to the processing conditions, without altering the optimized ferroelectric phase, that suppress the competing electrochemical pathways and reproducibly yield 100% ferroelectric devices. More broadly, this work establishes operando surface evolution as a powerful nanoscale diagnostic of hidden electrochemical processes and provides a practical route for improving the reliability and reproducibility of hafnia-based ferroelectric devices.

</details>


### [169] [Screening of Coulomb Interactions in MoS2 Nanoribbons: Enhanced Coulomb interactions, Antiscreening, and Edge Magnetism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37547)
*A. Montaghemi,H. Hadipour,A. Khademi,A. Yazdani*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该工作基于第一性原理（first-principles）结合约束随机相近似（constrained random-phase approximation），系统研究 MoS2 的 1H 与 1T' 相在不同维度下长程库仑相互作用（Coulomb interactions）的屏蔽行为，重点考察纳米带（nanoribbons）中量子限域对库仑相互作用的增强效应，并与 h-BN、黑磷烯（black phosphorene）、石墨烯纳米带进行对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoS2 因激子（excitons）与三子（trions）的高稳定性而在光电子学中受到关注，其大束缚能源于适中的带隙与低维体系中非常规的库仑屏蔽。为理解低维化如何调控库仑相互作用，需系统研究不同相与不同维度（尤其纳米带）下的屏蔽行为及其对电子与磁性的影响。

Method: 采用第一性原理计算结合约束随机相近似（constrained random-phase approximation）方法，分析 MoS2 1H 与 1T' 相在一维至二维不同维度下长程库仑相互作用的屏蔽，并将 MoS2 纳米带与 h-BN、黑磷烯、石墨烯纳米带进行对比研究。

Result: 在一维纳米带中，量子限域（quantum confinement）显著增强库仑相互作用：Hubbard U 从二维体系的 2.6 eV 增至半导体纳米带的近 3.5 eV；库仑相互作用呈长程特征，其尾部延伸超过 100 Å，约为二维结构中的两倍。未氢钝化（non-hydrogen-passivated）的锯齿型（zigzag）MoS2 纳米带存在涉及 d 与 p 轨道的非对称边缘态（asymmetric edge states），与磷烯、h-BN、石墨烯纳米带不同，从而产生有限净磁化（finite net magnetization）。

Conclusion: 低维化（尤其形成一维纳米带）会显著增强并延长 MoS2 中的库仑相互作用，且边缘态轨道特性可诱导有限净磁化；对比研究揭示了 MoS2 纳米带在电子与磁性行为上相较于 h-BN、黑磷烯与石墨烯纳米带的独特性，凸显库仑相互作用在决定其电子与磁性质中的关键作用。

Abstract: MoS2 has attracted significant attention for its promising applications in optoelectronics, owing to the remarkable stability of its excitons and trions. These quasiparticles have large binding energies that arise from the MoS2 moderate band gap and the unconventional screening of Coulomb interactions in low dimensions. Here, we investigate the screening of long-range Coulomb interactions in the 1H and 1T' phases of MoS2 across different dimensionalities, with particular emphasis on nanoribbons. Our analysis is based on first-principles calculations combined with the constrained random-phase approximation. This work presents a comparative study of MoS2, h-BN, black phosphorene, and graphene nanoribbons, with emphasis on the role of Coulomb interactions in shaping their electronic and magnetic properties. In one-dimensional nanoribbons, quantum confinement leads to a substantial enhancement of Coulomb interactions relative to 2D MoS2. Calculations show that the Hubbard U increases from 2.6 eV in the 2D system to nearly 3.5 eV in the semiconducting nanoribbons. In these systems, the Coulomb interaction is long-ranged, with its tail extending over more than 100 A, nearly twice of that in the 2D structure. The presence of asymmetric edge states involving d and p orbitals in non-hydrogen-passivated zigzag MoS2 nanoribbons, in contrast to phosphorene, h-BN, and graphene nanoribbons, results in a finite net magnetization.

</details>


### [170] [Switching Anomalous Hall and Nernst Responses by Nonmagnetic N Occupation at Fixed Noncoplanar Mn Antiferromagnetic Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37689)
*Xin Liu,Jiyuan Xu,Li Ma,Guoke Li,Dewei Zhao,Congmian Zhen,Denglu Hou*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过改变非磁性 N 子晶格占据、保持 Mn 非共面全进全出（all-in-all-out）磁序不变，在 γ-Mn、Mn₄N、MnN 系列中实现反常霍尔与反常能斯特响应“零—有限—零”的演化，表明非磁子晶格占据可作为补偿反铁磁体中贝里曲率驱动输运的对称性调控参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常认为反常横向输运主要由磁序决定，本文关注能否在不改变磁序的前提下通过非磁性原子占据来调控磁对称性。研究旨在分离“磁序”与“非磁占据”两个因素，厘清贝里曲率（Berry curvature）受对称性约束的机制。

Method: 选取晶格常数相同、Mn 非共面全进全出（all-in-all-out）磁构型一致的 γ-Mn、Mn₄N、MnN 作为参照态，仅改变 N 子晶格的占据情况，比较其反常霍尔（anomalous Hall）与反常能斯特（anomalous Nernst）响应及布里渊区积分贝里曲率。

Result: 该系列呈现“零—有限—零”的反常输运演化。γ-Mn 与 MnN 中高磁对称性导致布里渊区积分贝里曲率完全抵消；Mn₄N 中 N 占据降低磁对称性，在保持空间反演的同时破坏相关二重旋转对称性，从而解除抵消约束，在 [111] 方向产生未补偿的贝里曲率贡献，费米能级附近的有限反常霍尔电导率达 −126 S/cm。

Conclusion: 非磁性子晶格占据可作为一种对称性控制参数，用于调控补偿反铁磁体中由贝里曲率驱动的输运，且该调控独立于磁序的改变。

Abstract: Nonmagnetic atomic occupation can control anomalous transverse transport by modifying magnetic symmetry without changing the underlying magnetic order. We demonstrate this effect using controlled $γ$-Mn, Mn$_4$N, and MnN reference states with the same lattice constant and identical noncoplanar all-in-all-out Mn magnetic configurations, while varying only the occupation of the N sublattice. The anomalous Hall and anomalous Nernst responses exhibit a pronounced zero--finite--zero evolution across the series despite the unchanged Mn spin order. In $γ$-Mn and MnN, the high magnetic symmetry enforces complete cancellation of the Brillouin-zone-integrated Berry curvature. In Mn$_4$N, N occupation lowers the magnetic symmetry while preserving inversion and breaking the relevant twofold rotational symmetries, thereby lifting the cancellation constraint and permitting an uncompensated Berry-curvature contribution along the [111] direction. The resulting finite anomalous Hall conductivity reaches $-126$~S/cm near the Fermi level. These results establish nonmagnetic sublattice occupation as a symmetry-control parameter for Berry-curvature-driven transport in compensated antiferromagnets, independent of changes in the magnetic order.

</details>


### [171] [Chemical-Disorder-Induced Non-metallic Transport in Thermodynamically Metallic Mo4TGa16Ge (T = Co, Rh or Ir)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37707)
*Chaoguo Wang,Jiaqi Tian,Xin Gui*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究报告了由强关联平带超导体（flat-band superconductor）Mo4PtGa17派生出的新化合物系列Mo4TGa16Ge（T = Co、Rh、Ir），其总价电子数（total valence electron counts）名义上保持不变，但性质与母体材料显著不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 平带电子态（flat-band electronic states）对化学扰动高度敏感，可借此获得母体材料之外的更广泛电子行为；因此希望通过化学调控探索平带金属间化合物（flat-band intermetallics）的新性质。

Method: 通过名义上保持总价电子数的方式，从Mo4PtGa17衍生合成Mo4TGa16Ge（T = Co、Rh、Ir）；结合X射线晶体学（X-ray crystallography）与光谱学进行成分与化学键分析、电子结构理论计算、低温热容及电输运测量。

Result: 三种材料均结晶于非中心对称立方空间群F-43m，Ge选择性占据母体中一个Ga位。理论计算预测其具有金属性电子结构，费米能级（Fermi energy）处以窄的Mo-d态为主；低温热容显示有限Sommerfeld系数。但电输运测量显示以非金属行为为主，并伴有较小的激活能，排除了常规半导体行为。

Conclusion: 非金属输运特征与热力学金属行为的共存，可归因于窄带材料对化学无序（chemical disorders）的高度敏感性。Mo4TGa16Ge由此提供了一个可化学调控的平台，用于研究平带金属间化合物中无序调控的输运（disorder-controlled transport）。

Abstract: Flat-band electronic states can be highly sensitive to chemical perturbations, offering opportunities to access a broader range of electronic behaviors beyond that of parent materials. Here, we report the discovery of a new series of compounds, Mo4TGa16Ge (T = Co, Rh, or Ir), derived from the strongly correlated flat-band superconductor, Mo4PtGa17, via nominally preserving the total valence electron counts. All three materials crystallize in a noncentrosymmetric cubic space group, F-43m, with Ge selectively occupying one of the Ga sites in the parent compound. Although the total electron count remains the same, Mo4TGa16Ge exhibits significantly distinct properties from Mo4PtGa17. Theoretical calculations predict metallic electronic structures with narrow, dominant Mo-d states at the Fermi energy, while low-temperature heat capacity results demonstrate finite Sommerfeld coefficients. However, electrical transport measurements show predominantly non-metallic behaviors with small observed activation energies, excluding the possibilities of conventional semiconducting behaviors. Combining chemical bonding analysis, electronic structure and compositional determination from X-ray crystallography and spectroscopy, we propose that the coexistence of the non-metallic transport features and the thermodynamically metallic behaviors can be attributed to the high sensitivity of the narrow-band materials to chemical disorders. Thus, the discovery and investigation of Mo4TGa16Ge provide a chemically tunable platform for studying disorder-controlled transport in flat-band intermetallics.

</details>


### [172] [Pronounced Site Preference in Cr-Doped Mn-Based M-Type Hexaferrites and Its Chemical Origins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37720)
*Dylan Correll,Susheng Tan,Evan Wang,Xin Gui*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究以首个 Mn 基 M 型六方铁氧体（hexaferrite）KSb3Mn9O19 为模型体系，通过 Cr3+ 掺杂系列揭示阳离子占位选择由晶体场效应、化学键合与局域结构应变共同决定，且单一格位取代可引发邻近亚晶格的耦合重排与无序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 阳离子分布（cation distribution）决定晶态氧化物的性质，尤其影响受抑磁体（frustrated magnets）的磁基态，因此理解其化学调控因素对理性设计阳离子有序材料至关重要。既有研究对 M 型六方铁氧体中阳离子分布的起源与演化尚缺乏化学层面的系统认识。

Method: 以首个 Mn 基 M 型六方铁氧体 KSb3Mn9O19 为模型，制备 Cr3+ 掺杂系列 KSb3(Mn1-xCrx)9O19，综合采用粉末与单晶 X 射线衍射、电子显微术及能量色散 X 射线谱进行结构与成分表征，并分析局域配位环境随 Cr 含量的演化，辅以热容测量考察磁性质。

Result: Cr3+ 对 Mn Kagome 亚晶格表现出显著占位偏好，并伴随 Mn 空位及邻近格位上 Mn3+/Sb3+ 无序的演化。局域配位环境分析表明，晶体场效应、化学键合与局域结构应变共同主导该位点选择性。掺杂化合物的磁性与未掺杂母体相似，仅在高 Cr 浓度下出现掺杂诱导的自旋玻璃态（spin-glass state），并由热容测量支持。

Conclusion: 研究建立了 M 型六方铁氧体中阳离子位点分布与选择的化学图像，表明一个晶体学格位上的取代可诱导邻近亚晶格的耦合重分布与无序。局域配位化学可作为理解并最终调控复杂氧化物阳离子分布的途径，为结构明确的受抑磁性材料提供化学设计原则。

Abstract: Cation distribution plays a critical role in determining the properties of crystalline oxides. Understanding the chemical factors that govern cation distribution is therefore essential for the rational design of cation-ordered materials, particularly frustrated magnets in which structural disorder can strongly affect the magnetic ground state. Here, we investigate the origin and evolution of cation distribution in the extensively studied M-type hexaferrite structure using the first Mn-based M-type hexaferrite, KSb3Mn9O19, as a model system. A systematic Cr3+-doped series, KSb3(Mn1-xCrx)9O19, was examined using powder and single-crystal X-ray diffraction, electron microscopy, and energy-dispersive X-ray spectroscopy. Cr3+ exhibits a pronounced preference for the Mn Kagome sublattice, accompanied by the evolution of Mn vacancies and Mn3+/Sb3+ disorder on neighboring sites. Analysis of the local coordination environments and their evolution with Cr content suggests that crystal-field effects, chemical bonding, and local structural strain collectively govern this site selectivity. The magnetic properties of the Cr-doped compounds show similar behaviors as the undoped parent compound, other than the doping-induced spin-glass state at the highest Cr concentration, supported by heat capacity measurements. These results establish a chemical picture of cation site distribution and selection in M-type hexaferrites and demonstrate how substitution at one crystallographic site can induce coupled redistribution and disorder across neighboring sublattices. Moreover, this work establishes local coordination chemistry as a route toward understanding and ultimately controlling cation distribution in complex oxides, providing chemical design principles for structurally well-defined frustrated magnetic materials.

</details>


### [173] [Nonrelativistic and Relativistic Contributions to Spin-Momentum Locking and Spin Photogalvanic Effect in an Altermagnetic Multiferroic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37763)
*Giuseppe Cuono,Subhadeep Bandyopadhyay,Amar Fakhredine,Mathews Benny,Xujia Gong,Paolo Barone,Andrea Droghetti,Carmine Autieri,Silvia Picozzi*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该论文通过第一性原理计算与对称性分析，研究交替磁多铁材料 BaCuF4 中非相对论性交替磁 d 波自旋-动量锁定（spin-momentum locking, SML）与相对论性自旋结构的来源区分问题，并揭示该层级结构可编码于非线性自旋-光电导响应中。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交替磁多铁（altermagnetic multiferroics）中，非相对论性自旋-动量锁定与相对论性自旋织构（spin texture）共存，但二者来源的区分仍具挑战性。该研究旨在建立厘清这些不同物理起源的方法。

Method: 采用第一性原理密度泛函理论（density-functional theory, DFT）计算，并结合自旋极化多极展开（multipolar expansion of the spin polarization）的对称性分析，研究交替磁多铁 BaCuF4。同时利用自旋群（spin groups）与磁空间群（magnetic space groups）之间的群-子群关系区分不同贡献。

Result: 主导的非相对论性交替磁 d 波 SML 主要源于 CuF6 八面体旋转与极性畸变的耦合，故可通过铁电翻转（ferroelectric switching）实现反转；自旋-轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling）产生额外自旋多极，并依赖 Néel 矢量取向诱导自旋倾斜（spin canting）与弱铁磁性。研究据此区分了非相对论性交替磁、倾斜相关及纯相对论性三类 SML 贡献。

Conclusion: 该层级结构可编码于非线性自旋-光电导响应（nonlinear spin-photogalvanic response）中，其自旋投影可选择性探测不同来源的贡献，从而在交替磁多铁中建立了微观自旋织构与非线性自旋输运之间的直接联系。

Abstract: Altermagnetic multiferroics provide a platform where non-relativistic spin-momentum locking (SML) coexists with relativistic spin textures, yet disentangling their different origins remains challenging. Here, using first-principles density-functional-theory calculations and symmetry analysis of the multipolar expansion of the spin polarization, we investigate the altermagnetic multiferroic BaCuF4. Its dominant non-relativistic altermagnetic d-wave SML originates primarily from a CuF6 octahedral rotation coupled to the polar distortion and can therefore be reversed by ferroelectric switching. We show that spin-orbit coupling generates additional spin multipoles and, depending on the Neel-vector orientation, induces spin canting and weak ferromagnetism. By exploiting the group-subgroup relations between spin groups and magnetic space groups, we then distinguish non-relativistic altermagnetic, canting-associated, and purely relativistic contributions to the SML. We further show that this hierarchy is encoded in the nonlinear spin-photogalvanic response, whose spin projection selectively probes contributions of different origins. These results establish a direct connection between the microscopic spin texture and nonlinear spin transport in altermagnetic multiferroics.

</details>


### [174] [From Chemical Complexity to Tunable Magnetic Ordering in Highly Disordered High-Entropy Spinel Oxides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37792)
*Neha Sharma,Sushanta Mandal,Nikita Sharma,Amritpal,Sangeeta Thakur,Viktor Ukleev,Chen Luo,Florin Radu,S. D. Kaushik,Tirthankar Chakraborty,Sanjoy Kr. Mahatha,Denis Pelloquin,Sourav Marik*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本研究通过固定高熵 A 位、系统改变尖晶石型高熵氧化物 B 位阳离子组成，发现磁转变温度 T_C 与对应单 B 位高熵体系 T_C 呈显著线性关系，且该规律在高度复杂的高熵体系中依然成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高熵稳定化学（high-entropy stabilization chemistry）将构型无序（configurational disorder）转化为热力学优势，但极端无序与竞争相互作用下的磁有序演化规律及其可预测性尚不明确，本文旨在探究高熵氧化物中磁性有序的演变。

Method: 在固定高熵 A 位基体 (Ni0.2Mg0.2Co0.2Cu0.2Zn0.2)B2O4 中系统调控 B 位阳离子组成，并采用磁化测量、低温 X 射线磁圆二色性（X-ray magnetic circular dichroism）以及中子粉末衍射（neutron powder diffraction）等手段表征磁有序。

Result: 引入多组分 B 位后，磁转变温度（T_C）与相应单 B 位高熵体系 T_C 呈显著的线性依赖；即便在 (Ni0.2Mg0.2Co0.2Cu0.2Zn0.2)(Cr0.2Mn0.2Fe0.2Ga0.2X0.2)2O4（X = Al、Ti）这类高度复杂体系中该趋势仍然保持，并观测到稳健的长程亚铁磁有序（long-range ferrimagnetic ordering）。

Conclusion: 在构型极度无序、无主导磁性离子且缺乏明确超交换路径（superexchange pathway）的条件下，稳健的亚铁磁有序可由极端构型无序催生而非被其抑制，从而为高熵氧化物中磁转变温度的预测性调控提供了超越常规有序体系的路径。

Abstract: High-entropy stabilization chemistry is redefining materials design by transforming configurational disorder, arising from the deliberate incorporation of multiple principal cations, into a thermodynamic advantage that promotes phase stability and enables emergent functionalities. In this work, we investigate the evolution of magnetic ordering in spinel-type high entropy oxides by systematically varying the cation composition of the B site within a fixed high-entropy A-site matrix, (Ni$_{0.2}$Mg$_{0.2}$Co$_{0.2}$Cu$_{0.2}$Zn$_{0.2}$)B$_2$O$_4$. Upon introducing multicomponent B-site configurations, we uncover a strikingly linear dependence of the magnetic transition temperature (T$_C$) on the T$_C$s of the corresponding single B-site high-entropy systems. Remarkably, this trend persists even in highly complex (Ni$_{0.2}$Mg$_{0.2}$Co$_{0.2}$Cu$_{0.2}$Zn$_{0.2}$)(Cr$_{0.2}$Mn$_{0.2}$Fe$_{0.2}$Ga$_{0.2}$X$_{0.2}$)$_2$O$_4$, X = Al and Ti. Despite the material's extremely high degree of disorder, absence of a dominant magnetic ion or a straightforward superexchange pathway, detailed magnetization measurements, low-temperature X-ray magnetic circular dichroism, and neutron powder diffraction studies reveal robust long-range ferrimagnetic ordering. These results reveal an emergent predictability in ferrimagnetic high-entropy spinel oxides, where, despite extreme configurational disorder and competing interactions, robust ferrimagnetic order can arise from, rather than be hindered by, extreme configurational disorder. This establishes a pathway for predictively tuning magnetic transition temperatures in high-entropy oxides beyond conventional ordered systems.

</details>


### [175] [Hierarchical Fourier Phase Projection for Local Electronic Observables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37814)
*Tao Hu,Weiqing Zhou,Zhichang Fu,Yechen Chen,Shengjun Yuan*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 本文提出分层傅里叶相位投影（hierarchical Fourier phase projection, HPP）方法，通过将傅里叶探针（Fourier probes）组织为可复用的空间层次结构，在无需显式构造全部占据轨道的前提下高效获取大规模电子结构计算中的局域可观测量，并实现从低成本局部估计到投影精确极限的系统性细化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模电子结构计算（large-scale electronic-structure calculations）需要高效访问局域可观测量（local observables），但显式构造全部占据轨道（occupied orbitals）代价过高。因此需要一种不依赖完整轨道构造、且能利用密度矩阵局域性（density-matrix locality）的求解途径。

Method: 提出分层傅里叶相位投影（HPP），将傅里叶探针（Fourier probes）组织为可复用的空间层次结构，逐步消除短程混叠（short-range aliasing），并利用密度矩阵局域性。该方法支持从低成本局部估计到所选数值占据算符（numerical occupation operator）投影精确极限的系统性细化（systematic refinement），且不丢弃已计算的响应。

Result: 在冻结 Kohn–Sham 哈密顿量（frozen Kohn–Sham Hamiltonians）下对半导体与金属体系的测试表明：电子密度（electron densities）与非局域赝势力（nonlocal pseudopotential forces）的收敛可控；在固定局部精度下，所需探测分辨率（probing resolution）对体系尺寸依赖较弱；在固定探测工作量（probing workload）下，直接计算成本近似线性增长；层间变化（inter-level changes）可为在有限精度下终止细化提供实用信息。

Conclusion: HPP 在单一层次框架内将电子局域性（electronic locality）、可观测量精度（observable accuracy）与计算代价（computational effort）相联结，为大规模电子结构计算中的局域量（local quantities）提供了一条可扩展（scalable）路径。

Abstract: Large-scale electronic-structure calculations require efficient access to local observables without explicitly constructing all occupied orbitals. We develop hierarchical Fourier phase projection (HPP), which organizes Fourier probes into a reusable spatial hierarchy that progressively removes short-range aliasing while exploiting density-matrix locality. The method provides systematic refinement from low-cost local estimates to the projection-exact limit of the chosen numerical occupation operator, without discarding previously evaluated responses. Tests using frozen Kohn--Sham Hamiltonians for semiconducting and metallic systems demonstrate controllable convergence of electron densities and nonlocal pseudopotential forces, weak size dependence of the probing resolution required for a fixed local accuracy, and near-linear growth of the direct computational cost at fixed probing workload. Inter-level changes further provide practical information for terminating the refinement at finite accuracy. HPP connects electronic locality, observable accuracy, and computational effort within a single hierarchical framework, providing a scalable route to local quantities in large-scale electronic-structure calculations.

</details>


### [176] [Electronic Coupling and Charge-Transfer Landscape of Graphene on Ge(001)/Si(001): Multiscale Analysis Assisted by Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37827)
*Pawel Dabrowski,Przemysław Przybysz,Maciej Rogala,Iaroslav Lutsyk,Paweł Krukowski,Witold Kozłowski,Michał Piskorski,Piotr Milczarski,Iwona Pasternak,Jakub Sitek,Marek Kopciuszyński,Ryszard Zdyb,Jagoda Sławińska,Pawel J. Kowalczyk*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究结合 UPS、KPFM、ARPES、STS 与 DFT，解析石墨烯（graphene）在 Ge(001)/Si(001) 上的功函数调制与电子耦合，揭示纳米刻面形貌导致的局域非均匀性与掺杂极性差异机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解并调控石墨烯-半导体界面的电荷转移（charge transfer），对二维材料与硅兼容技术（silicon-compatible technologies）的集成至关重要。文献中关于该界面掺杂极性（doping polarity）的报道不一致，也构成待解释的问题。

Method: 实验上联合使用紫外光电子能谱（UPS）、开尔文探针力显微镜（KPFM）、角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）、扫描隧道谱（STS），并结合密度泛函理论（DFT）计算；同时采用机器学习辅助的 STS 数据分类。

Result: UPS 与 KPFM 显示功函数（work function）呈空间非均匀分布，并与衬底纳米刻面形貌（nanofaceted morphology）相关；ARPES 与 DFT 对原始界面一致表明 n 型掺杂（n-type doping）及电子由 Ge 向石墨烯转移。氧化界面建模则预测掺杂反转为 p 型（p-type doping），可解释文献中掺杂极性的分歧。STS 分类解析出从纳米刻面顶部的近自由悬浮石墨烯到刻面间强耦合区及纳米带（nanoribbon）状区域等不同局域电子区域。

Conclusion: 研究识别了支配局域石墨烯-衬底相互作用与电荷转移的机制，为优化合成工艺、减少剥离（delamination）过程中的缺陷形成提供指导，并支持制备用于电子器件及作为空气敏感材料保护涂层转移的高质量石墨烯层。

Abstract: Understanding and controlling charge transfer at graphene-semiconductor interfaces is essential for the integration of two-dimensional materials into silicon-compatible technologies. Here, we combine ultraviolet photoelectron spectroscopy (UPS), Kelvin probe force microscopy (KPFM), angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES), scanning tunneling spectroscopy (STS) and density functional theory (DFT) to resolve work-function modulation and electronic coupling in graphene grown on Ge(001)/Si(001). UPS and KPFM reveal a spatially non-uniform work-function landscape correlated with the nanofaceted morphology of the substrate. ARPES and DFT calculations for the pristine interface consistently indicate n-type doping and electron transfer from Ge to graphene. In contrast, modeling of the oxidized interface predicts a reversal to p-type doping, providing a plausible explanation for the different doping polarities reported in the literature. Machine-learning-assisted classification of the STS data resolves distinct local electronic regimes, ranging from nearly free-standing graphene on nanofacet tops to more strongly coupled inter-facet regions and nanoribbon-like regions with distinct local electronic responses. By identifying the mechanisms governing local graphene-substrate interactions and charge transfer, our study provides guidelines for tailoring the synthesis process and minimizing defect formation during delamination. These insights support the production of high-quality graphene layers for electronic devices and for transfer as protective coatings for air-sensitive materials.

</details>


### [177] [Disentangling octahedral distortion and symmetry breaking in the ordered double perovskite SrLaCoNbO$_6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37835)
*Ajay Kumar,Clemens Ulrich,Yaroslav Mudryk,Rajendra S. Dhaka*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 结合变温拉曼光谱（Raman spectroscopy）与X射线衍射（XRD），在25–900 °C范围内对B位有序双钙钛矿（double perovskite）SrLaCoNbO₆开展结构研究，识别出两类温度驱动异常。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在区分复杂双钙钛矿氧化物中的同构八面体畸变（isostructural octahedral distortion）与对称性破缺相变（symmetry-breaking phase transition），并验证无需同步辐射或中子衍射设施的可行表征路径。

Method: 在25–900 °C范围内联用变温拉曼光谱与实验室XRD，并结合群论分析（group-theoretical analysis）、Rietveld精修及定量八面体畸变分析。

Result: 在T_OD = 300±25 °C与T_PT = 750±25 °C观测到声子频率与线宽的重整化及特征XRD反射的演化与抑制；T_OD对应单斜P2₁/n相内的同构八面体畸变，CoO₆与NbO₆的径向与角度畸变参数降至近零后因面内Co–O与Nb–O键长层级反转而回升；T_PT对应P2₁/n向单斜I2/m的相变。

Conclusion: 变温拉曼与实验室XRD结合定量八面体畸变分析，可有效区分同构八面体畸变与对称性破缺相变，为复杂双钙钛矿氧化物结构研究提供不依赖同步辐射或中子衍射的稳健方案。

Abstract: We report a comprehensive high temperature structural investigation of the B-site ordered double perovskite SrLaCoNbO$_6$ using combined Raman spectroscopy and x-ray diffraction (XRD) over the range 25--900~$^\circ$C. Two distinct temperature-driven anomalies are identified at $T_\mathrm{OD} = 300\pm 25~^\circ$C and $T_\mathrm{PT} = 750\pm 25~^\circ$C through pronounced renormalizations in the phonon frequency and linewidth, accompanied by the evolution and suppression of characteristic XRD reflections. The low-temperature anomaly at $T_\mathrm{OD}$ is associated with an isostructural octahedral distortion within the monoclinic $P2_1/n$ phase. At $T_\mathrm{OD}$, the radial and angular distortion parameters of the CoO$_6$ and NbO$_6$ octahedra decrease to near-zero values before reversing upon further heating due to an inversion of the in-plane Co--O and Nb--O bond-length hierarchy. The high-temperature anomaly at $T_\mathrm{PT}$ corresponds to a symmetry-breaking structural phase transition from $P2_1/n$ to the monoclinic $I2/m$ phase, confirmed by group-theoretical analysis and Rietveld refinement. These results demonstrate that integrating temperature-dependent Raman spectroscopy with laboratory-based XRD, complemented by quantitative octahedral distortion analysis, provides a robust approach to disentangle isostructural octahedral distortions from symmetry-breaking phase transitions in complex double perovskite oxides without recourse to synchrotron or neutron diffraction facilities.

</details>


### [178] [Irreducible Weyl Semimetals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37927)
*Ke-Xin Pang,Yan Gao*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该文提出“不可约外尔半金属（irreducible Weyl semimetals, IWSMs）”概念，即在费米能级附近满足电荷中性且晶体学上不可再分的完整外尔节点构型，并在全部1651个磁空间群中系统分类，发现16种“不可约外尔分子（irreducible Weyl molecules, IWMs）”中仅10种可实现单拷贝相，其中含5种新拓扑相。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外尔半金属（Weyl semimetal, WSM）研究的目标之一是寻找由最少外尔点（Weyl points, WPs）构成的相，以研究手性拓扑荷的本征物理。Pang等已证明可实现的外尔半金属可分解为16种不可约外尔分子的线性组合，但哪些不可分解构型本身能形成独立晶相仍属未解问题。

Method: 结合全部1651个磁空间群（magnetic space groups）、外尔轨道多重度与能带相容性关系，对由晶体对称性保护的二重外尔点构成的不可约外尔半金属进行分类；并对全部十类构型以对称性约束的晶格模型进行验证。

Result: 16种原始清单中仅10种允许单拷贝实现，包括4种Pair、4种Split与2种Mixed电荷节点构型。分类揭示了5种非常规且此前未认识的拓扑相：电荷为三的Pair IWSM {3, -3}；Split IWSM {3, -1, -1, -1}与{4, -1, -1, -1, -1}；以及Mixed IWSM {3, 1, -2, -2}与{3, 3, -2, -2, -2}。对称性约束晶格模型验证了全部十类的节点清单、陈数荷与表面手性流。

Conclusion: 研究确立了“晶体不可约性（crystalline irreducibility）”作为超越单个外尔节点的相级组织原则，并为在电子、声子与光子系统中识别最小外尔复合体提供了对称性分辨的框架。

Abstract: A key objective in Weyl-semimetal (WSM) research is to identify phases built from minimal configurations of Weyl points (WPs), which provide the simplest settings for investigating the intrinsic physics of chiral topological charges. Pang et al. [Phys. Rev. Res. 8, 033304 (2026)] recently established that crystallographically realizable WSMs can be decomposed into linear combinations of 16 minimal "irreducible Weyl molecules" (IWMs). A fundamental question remains unresolved: which of these non-decomposable configurations can themselves form standalone crystalline phases? Here we introduce irreducible Weyl semimetals (IWSMs), in which two bands crossing near the Fermi level form an elementary symmetry-compatible complex whose complete Weyl-node configuration is charge neutral and crystallographically indivisible. Combining all 1651 magnetic space groups with Weyl-orbit multiplicities and band-compatibility relations, we classify IWSMs formed by crystalline-symmetry-protected twofold WPs. Only ten of the sixteen primitive inventories admit one-copy realizations, comprising four Pair, four Split, and two Mixed charge-node architectures. Remarkably, this classification uncovers five unconventional and previously unrecognized topological phases: the charge-three Pair IWSM {3, -3}; the Split IWSMs {3, -1, -1, -1}, and {4, -1, -1, -1, -1}; and the Mixed IWSMs {3, 1, -2, -2} and {3, 3, -2, -2, -2}. For all ten classes, symmetry-constrained lattice models verify the complete node inventories, Chern charges, and surface chiral-flow incidence. Our results establish crystalline irreducibility as a phase-level organizing principle beyond individual Weyl nodes and provide a symmetry-resolved framework for identifying minimal Weyl complexes in electronic, phononic, and photonic systems.

</details>


### [179] [3/8 charge-density wave instability in the kagome metal CsV$_3$Sb$_5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38071)
*Luca Buiarelli,Hyeonseo H. Park,Ethan T. Ritz,Rafael M. Fernandes,Turan Birol*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 通过密度泛函微扰理论（DFPT）计算，预测CsV₃Sb₅在压力下出现波矢为(3/8, 0, 1/2)的新电荷有序相，与近期X射线衍射实验一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: kagome金属CsV₃Sb₅存在电荷密度波（CDW）与超导转变，二者均受压力显著影响；近期X射线衍射实验在超导穹顶（superconducting dome）凹陷处压力下发现新电荷有序相，其波矢与对称性不同于常压下的2×2×2与2×2×4序，需从理论上予以解释。

Method: 采用密度泛函微扰理论（density functional perturbation theory, DFPT）在精细倒空间网格上计算晶格不稳定性，并结合有限位移（finite-displacement）计算与非谐效应（anharmonic effects）分析，辅以嵌套函数（nesting function）与朗道自由能（Landau free energy）分析。

Result: DFPT预测CsV₃Sb₅的主导晶格不稳定性位于该波矢；有限位移计算表明非谐效应在常压下稳定常规L₂⁻ CDW，而竞争的3/8不稳定性在约1 GPa以上变为能量有利，与实验观测一致；嵌套函数在同一波矢处呈现峰值。

Conclusion: 朗道自由能分析解释了X射线衍射中观测到的单斜（monoclinic）畸变的出现，并给出若干可能与超导T_c变化趋势相关的键序（bond-ordering）模式。

Abstract: The kagome metal CsV$_3$Sb$_5$ exhibits charge density wave (CDW) and superconducting transitions, both of which are substantially affected by pressure. Recent x-ray diffraction experiments identify a new charge-ordered phase at a pressure coinciding with the dip of the superconducting dome. This CDW phase displays a distinctive wavevector $\mathbf{Q} = (3/8, 0, 1/2)$ and monoclinic symmetry, in contrast to the $2\times2\times 2$ and $2\times2\times 4$ orders reported at ambient pressure. In this letter, we show that density functional perturbation theory (DFPT) calculations on a fine reciprocal-space grid predict the leading lattice instability of CsV$_3$Sb$_5$ to be at this wavevector. Finite-displacement calculations reveal that while anharmonic effects stabilize the conventional $L_2^-$ CDW at ambient pressure, the competing $3/8$ instability becomes energetically favorable above $\sim$1 GPa, consistent with experimental observations. Interestingly, the nesting function displays a peak at the same wavevector. A Landau free energy analysis explains the emergence of the monoclinic distortion observed in x-ray diffraction, in addition to several bond-ordering patterns that may be relevant to the observed trends in the superconducting $T_c$.

</details>


### [180] [How Thermodynamically Accessible are Quaternary Mixed-Metal Chalcohalides?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38125)
*Pascal Henkel,G. Krishnamurthy Grandhi,Jarno Laakso,David Rovira Ferrer,Edgardo Saucedo,Jingrui Li,Paola Vivo,Miguel A. L. Marques,Patrick Rinke*

Main category: cond-mat.mtrl-sci

TL;DR: 该研究结合密度泛函理论（density functional theory, DFT）与 Alexandria Materials Database，快速评估了 54 种 M(II)$_2$M(III)Ch$_2$X$_3$ 型四元混合金属硫卤化物（quaternary mixed-metal chalcohalides, MMCHs）的热力学可及性（thermodynamic accessibility），并通过 Sn 基化合物的溶液加工验证了预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MMCHs 是一类有前景的混合阴离子（mixed-anion）半导体家族，但其热力学可及性此前基本未被探索，缺乏对合成可行性的系统判断。
