Method: 将密度泛函理论与 Alexandria Materials Database 相结合，对 54 种 M(II)$_2$M(III)Ch$_2$X$_3$ 化合物的热力学稳定性进行快速而可靠的评估，并通过六种 Sn 基 MMCHs 的溶液加工（solution processing）实验进行验证。

Result: 所有被评估的 MMCHs 均被预测为热力学不稳定（thermodynamically unstable），但相当一部分落在凸包（convex hull）的计算不确定度范围内；其预测分解遵循五条不同反应路径，最常见的是分解为 M(III)$_2$Ch$_3$、M(II)X$_2$ 和 M(II)Ch。溶液加工结果支持该分析，并首次合成了此前未报道的 Sn$_2$InS$_2$Br$_3$（多相薄膜）。

Conclusion: 该方法确定了凸包邻近度（convex hull proximity）与竞争相形成（competing phase formation）是溶液加工中限制 MMCH 合成可及性的关键因素，可为未来 MMCH 的探索提供指导。

Abstract: Quaternary mixed-metal chalcohalides (MMCHs) are a promising mixed-anion semiconductor family, but their thermodynamic accessibility remains largely unexplored. We combine density functional theory with the Alexandria Materials Database to carry out a rapid and reliable evaluation of the thermodynamic accessibility of 54 M(II)$_2$M(III)Ch$_2$X$_3$ compounds. All evaluated MMCHs are predicted to be thermodynamically unstable, but a considerable fraction of MMCHs lie within computational uncertainty of the convex hull. Their predicted decomposition follows five distinct reaction pathways, most commonly into M(III)$_2$Ch$_3$, M(II)X$_2$, and M(II)Ch. Solution processing of six Sn-based MMCHs supports our analysis. Notably, this includes the first synthesis of the previously unreported Sn$_2$InS$_2$Br$_3$ in a multiphase film. Our approach identifies convex hull proximity and competing phase formation as key constraints on MMCH synthetic accessibility during solution processing, thereby guiding future MMCH exploration.

</details>

### [181] [An adaptive fractional state links circuit mechanisms to cortical dynamics across the visual hierarchy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37355)
*Brendan Harris,Pulin Gong*

Main category: q-bio.NC

TL;DR: 该研究结合小鼠六个视觉区的 Neuropixels 记录与机制性回路建模，识别出一种重尾超扩散涨落与长程时间依赖、振荡共存的动力学状态，并将其形式化为“自适应分数阶（adaptive fractional, AF）状态”。AF 状态的动力学指数沿视觉层级系统性变化，高层视觉区表现为更弱超扩散与更强时间记忆，该层级转变在回路模型中源于有效抑制的逐步减弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 皮层回路需在灵活响应新输入的同时整合过去信息，但神经活动如何协调这两类相互竞争的需求尚不明确。该研究旨在刻画能够同时支持快速响应与长期时间整合的神经动力学机制，并检验其在视觉层级上的组织规律。

Method: 方法上，将小鼠六个视觉区的 Neuropixels 记录与机制性回路建模相结合；并引入有效平均场理论（effective mean-field theory），其中空间与时间分数阶导数（fractional derivatives）分别刻画重尾偏移与长程记忆。

Result: 研究发现存在一个重尾超扩散（superdiffusive）涨落与长程时间依赖及振荡共存的动力学状态，即 AF 状态；其特征动力学指数沿视觉层级系统性变化：较高视觉区超扩散较弱、时间记忆较强。回路模型显示，这一层级性转变由有效抑制（effective inhibition）的逐步减弱所产生。

Conclusion: 研究将经典的时间尺度层级（hierarchy of timescales）拓展为动力学状态层级（hierarchy of dynamical regimes），并提示 AF 状态可使皮层区域同时表达快速响应与长期时间整合。

Abstract: Cortical circuits must respond flexibly to new inputs while integrating information about the past, yet the way in which neural activity reconciles these competing demands remains unclear. Combining Neuropixels recordings from six mouse visual areas with mechanistic circuit modeling, we identify a dynamical regime in which heavy-tailed superdiffusive fluctuations coexist with long-range temporal dependence and oscillations. We formalize this regime as the adaptive fractional (AF) state, using an effective mean-field theory in which spatial and temporal fractional derivatives capture heavy-tailed excursions and long-range memory, respectively. We find that dynamical exponents characterizing the AF state vary systematically across the visual hierarchy: higher visual areas exhibit weaker superdiffusion and stronger temporal memory. In the circuit model, this hierarchical shift emerges from a progressive weakening of effective inhibition. These findings extend the classical hierarchy of timescales to a hierarchy of dynamical regimes and suggest that the AF state allows cortical areas to jointly express rapid responses and long-term temporal integration.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.stat-mech'></div>

# cond-mat.stat-mech [[Back]](#toc)

### [182] [Memory-induced optimal switching in a dynamic Ising-Kuramoto model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35838)
*Reon Hayashi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究分析一个全局耦合的随机系统，其中每个单元同时携带 Ising 自旋与连续相位，揭示复合相干模式在普通相位相干较弱时仍可有序化，并给出同步阈值及切换速率最优化的条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索自旋动力学与相位动力学耦合时产生的复合相干模式（composite coherent mode），尤其是当普通相位相干不明显时，系统是否仍可通过自旋-相位相互作用实现有序化，并理解切换速率与记忆效应对同步阈值的影响。

Method: 方法上，自旋经历弛豫速率为 γ 的 Glauber 切换（Glauber switching），相位由关联时间为 τ 的 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck 噪声（Ornstein-Uhlenbeck noise）驱动；围绕非磁非相干态作线性响应分析，得到同步阈值 K_c=[(μ+β_sγ)χ_γ]^{-1}，其中 χ_γ 为自由相位关联函数的记忆加权拉普拉斯变换（memory-weighted Laplace transform）；并推导内部最优的一般充分条件，结合有限尺寸模拟验证。

Result: 结果显示，Glauber 响应增强与记忆敏感度下降之间的竞争使 K_c 在某一有限切换速率处达到最小；有限时间 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck 关联满足所需的快切换渐近条件，而白噪声极限（white-noise limit）则未必满足。有限尺寸模拟重现了非单调阈值、随 τ 增大阈值向更慢切换移动、非磁态中的反相位复合有序（antiphase composite order）以及磁化背景上的模式混合（mode mixing）。

Conclusion: 结论是，该全局耦合自旋-相位系统存在可由有限切换速率调控的同步最优化，且有限关联时间的噪声对复合有序的形成具有关键作用，为理解自旋与相位耦合下的相干现象提供了理论框架。

Abstract: We study a globally coupled stochastic system in which each unit carries an Ising spin and a continuous phase. The spins undergo Glauber switching with relaxation rate $γ$, while the phases are driven by Ornstein-Uhlenbeck noise with correlation time $τ$. A spin-dependent phase interaction supports a composite coherent mode, $Z_m=N^{-1}\sum_jσ_j e^{iθ_j}$, that can order even when the ordinary phase coherence is small. Linear response about the nonmagnetic incoherent state gives the synchronization threshold $K_{\mathrm c}=[(μ+β_sγ)χ_γ]^{-1}$, where $χ_γ$ is a memory-weighted Laplace transform of the free phase correlation function. The competition between the increasing Glauber response and the decreasing memory susceptibility produces a finite switching rate at which $K_{\mathrm c}$ is minimized. We derive a general sufficient condition for such an interior optimum and show that finite-time Ornstein-Uhlenbeck correlations satisfy the required fast-switching asymptotics, whereas the white-noise limit need not. Finite-size simulations reproduce the nonmonotonic threshold, its shift toward slower switching as $τ$ increases, antiphase composite order in a nonmagnetic state, and mode mixing on a magnetized background.

</details>


### [183] [Nonreciprocal Chiral Automata](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35984)
*Andrew A. Allocca,Armin Rahmani,Pouyan Ghaemi,Sriram Ganeshan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文基于含静止粒子（rest particles）与手性碰撞（chiral collisions）的 FHP-II 格气元胞自动机（lattice-gas cellular automaton）模型，通过 Chapman-Enskog 粗粒化构造出非零奇压强（odd pressure）输运系数，并给出其与 Hall 黏度、奇异力矩的关系式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 奇压强作为宇称奇（parity-odd）输运系数，可在可压缩二维流体中产生对涡量的各向同性压强响应，并通过涡旋-压缩模式的不对称耦合提供类似若干生物系统的非互易流体力学响应机制；然而实现该系数的微观模型相对稀缺。

Method: 构建基于 Frisch-Hasslacher-Pomeau II（FHP-II）模型的格气元胞自动机，引入静止粒子与非对称（手性）碰撞规则；核心是存在静止粒子时对运动粒子施加弱旋转的局域宇称破缺碰撞，随后采用 Chapman-Enskog 粗粒化方法提取流体力学尺度的输运系数。

Result: Chapman-Enskog 粗粒化给出非零奇压强系数；在流体力学尺度上，该微观碰撞规则等效于一个有效磁场（effective magnetic field），将模型的体黏度（bulk viscosity）转化为奇压强。此外，推导出 Hall 黏度、奇压强与奇异力矩（odd torque）同其宇称偶（parity-even）对应量之间的关系式。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个以格气元胞自动机实现奇压强输运系数的微观模型，表明局域宇称破缺碰撞可经由有效磁场机制生成奇输运现象，并给出了奇输运系数与常规输运系数的一般性对应关系。

Abstract: Odd pressure is a parity-odd transport coefficient that gives an isotropic pressure response to vorticity in compressible two-dimensional fluids. By asymmetrically coupling vortical and compressional modes, it provides a direct mechanism for a nonreciprocal hydrodynamic response reminiscent of several biological systems. Yet microscopic models realizing odd pressure as a transport coefficient are relatively scarce. Here, we construct one such model using a lattice-gas cellular automaton based on the Frisch-Hasslacher-Pomeau II model with rest particles and chiral collisions, whose Chapman-Enskog coarse-graining produces a nonzero odd pressure coefficient. The key ingredient is a local parity-breaking collision that weakly rotates moving particles in the presence of a rest particle. At hydrodynamic scales, this microscopic rule acts as an effective magnetic field, converting the model's bulk viscosity into odd pressure. More generally, we derive expressions relating Hall viscosity, odd pressure, and odd torque to their parity-even counterparts in our model.

</details>


### [184] [Autonomous self-harmonic drift in Langevin dynamics causes a compaction of underlying domain for the density of Stochastic Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36232)
*Ken Sekimoto*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究指出，具有自谐漂移与经典自旋的朗之万动力学与“随机局部化”概念存在强关联，前者可视为后者在紧致化域上的版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机在于揭示朗之万动力学（Langevin dynamics）与随机局部化（Stochastic Localization）这两个分别在自旋系统与数学几何、数据科学中受到关注的概念之间的深层联系。

Method: 方法为对比两类框架：将后者的定义域——$n$ 维欧氏空间——紧致化（compactification）为 $n$ 维球面，并论证该紧致化源于对漂移项（drift）自治性（autonomous）的要求。

Result: 结果表明，自谐漂移与经典自旋的朗之万动力学可被理解为随机局部化在球面域上的对应版本，两者的差异可归因于漂移自治性的约束。

Conclusion: 结论认为，这两种方法之间的关联有望拓展随机局部化的适用范围。

Abstract: Our recent research on Langevin dynamics with self-harmonic drift and classical spins has revealed a strong connection to ``Stochastic Localization," a concept attracting attention in the fields of mathematical geometry and data science. Roughly speaking, the former can be viewed as a version of the latter in which the $n$-dimensional Euclidean space - the domain of the probability distribution function appearing in the latter - is ``compactified'' to the surface of an $n$-dimensional sphere. By comparing the two frameworks, we argue that this compactification is a consequence of requiring the drift to be autonomous. The relationship between the two approaches may expand the scope of Stochastic Localization.

</details>


### [185] [Derivation of Fokker-Planck equation and its entropy production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36260)
*Tânia Tomé,Mário J. de Oliveira*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文从主方程（master equation）出发，在离散状态空间引入含两项的跃迁速率（transition rate），在连续空间极限下推导出 Fokker-Planck 方程及其熵产生（entropy production）与熵流（entropy flux），并讨论相空间（phase space）情形下多热库接触系统与孤立系统的相应方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望建立主方程与 Fokker-Planck 方程之间在熵产生描述上的联系。为此需要一个合适的状态空间与跃迁速率设定，使连续极限既能给出 Fokker-Planck 方程，也能给出对应的熵产生率与熵流。

Method: 在定义主方程的离散状态空间中引入跃迁速率，该速率包含两部分：与间距倒数成正比的一般项，以及与间距平方倒数成正比、对应独立随机变量方差（variance）的项。随后取连续空间极限进行推导。

Result: 在连续空间极限下，得到 Fokker-Planck 方程、熵产生率以及熵流。文中还考察了状态空间为相空间（phase space）的情形，并讨论了适用于与多个热库（heat reservoirs）接触的系统以及孤立系统（isolated systems）的方程。

Conclusion: 由主方程经上述跃迁速率设定并在连续极限下，可统一导出 Fokker-Planck 方程及其熵产生与熵流，该框架亦被推广到相空间，以处理多热库接触系统与孤立系统。

Abstract: We derive the Fokker-Planck equation and its entropy production from the master equation. To this end we consider a discrete space of states where the master equation is defined and appropriate transition rates are introduced. A transition rate has two parts, one of which is the ordinary term proportional to the inverse of the spacing and the other is inversely proportional to the square of the spacing corresponding to the variance of the independent stochastic variable. In the continuous space limit, we obtain the Fokker-Planck as well as the rate of entropy production and the flux of entropy. We also consider the case where the space of states is the phase space and discuss the equations appropriate for systems in contact with several heat reservoirs and for isolated systems.

</details>


### [186] [Localization Transition in Kinetically Deformed one-dimensional Aubry-André Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36446)
*Arpita Goswami*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该工作提出一种单粒子动能项的 $q$-形变（$q$-deformation），在一维 Aubry-André（AA）模型中研究其对局域化（localization）的调控作用，揭示形变破坏精确自对偶性（self-duality）后出现的新局域化结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统 AA 模型的所有本征态在 $λ_c=2$ 处同时局域化，缺乏可调控的中间相。该文旨在通过单一参数 $q$ 引入可控的长程跃迁机制，从理论上突破精确自对偶性，从而调控局域化转变结构。

Method: 构造厄米（Hermitian）的 $q$-形变动能算符，将其写为晶格平移算符（lattice translation operator）的非线性函数，保持均匀晶格并在 $q\to1$ 极限下连续还原常规 AA 哈密顿量。该形变生成由单一参数 $q$ 控制的无限组关联奇程动能过程，并在对偶变换（dual transformation）下表现为对偶准周期势（quasiperiodic potential）的高次谐波。作者还提出在周期驱动 AA 模型中实现该层级结构的实验方案。

Result: 与常规 AA 模型在 $λ_c=2$ 处所有本征态同时局域化不同，形变模型在 $λ_c=2$ 之外仍保留一部分非局域态（delocalized states）。在 $q$-$λ$ 平面中出现中间区域（intermediate regime），其中局域与扩展本征态在谱内共存。

Conclusion: $q$-形变提供了一种以单参数控制、非唯象的长程跃迁机制，可定向破坏 AA 模型的精确自对偶性，并产生可调的局域化相图与共存相，且具备周期性驱动系统实验实现的可能性。

Abstract: We propose a $q$-deformation in the single-particle kinetic energy and investigate how it modifies the localization in the one-dimensional Aubry-André (AA) model. We construct a Hermitian $q$-deformed kinetic operator as a nonlinear function of the lattice translation operator, preserving the uniform lattice and recovering the conventional AA Hamiltonian continuously in the undeformed limit $q\to1$. The deformation generates an infinite set of correlated odd-range kinetic processes, controlled by a single parameter q, rather than phenomenologically involving long-range hopping. Under the dual transformation, this long-range hopping appears as higher harmonics of the dual quasiperiodic potential, providing a controlled route for breaking the exact self-duality of the AA model, with $q$ as the control parameter, consequently modifying the localization structure for $q\neq1$. In contrast to the conventional AA model, where all eigenstates localize simultaneously at $λ_c=2$, the deformed model exhibits a fraction of delocalized states even beyond $λ_c =2$. An intermediate regime also emerges in the $q-λ$ plane where localized and extended eigenstates coexist across the spectrum. We also propose a possible experimental realization of the hierarchy produced in the $q$-deformed kinetic setting in a periodically driven AA model.

</details>


### [187] [Derivation of the General Solution of the Black-Scholes Boundary-Value Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36447)
*ByoungSeon Choi,M. Y. Choi*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文指出满足 Black-Scholes 偏微分方程（partial differential equation）及欧式看涨期权（European call option）终端条件（terminal condition）的函数有无穷多个，因而 Black-Scholes 公式并非唯一解，并对此类解给出严格推导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有认识将 Black-Scholes 公式视为满足相应偏微分方程与终端条件的唯一解。作者意在指出该唯一性假设不成立，并由此质疑其与一价定律（law of one price）的相容性。

Method: 以 Black-Scholes 边值问题（boundary value problem）为对象，对满足该偏微分方程与欧式看涨期权终端条件的多解进行严格推导。

Result: 满足 Black-Scholes 偏微分方程及对应终端条件的解有无穷多个，故 Black-Scholes 公式并非唯一解；该结论与先前假设相悖。

Conclusion: Black-Scholes 公式的非唯一性意味着其违反一价定律这一经济学与金融学基本定律之一；文章提供了该边值问题多解的严格推导。

Abstract: There are infinitely many functions that satisfy the Black-Scholes partial differential equation and the terminal condition corresponding to the European call option. This means that the Black-Scholes formula, which led to the award of the 1997 Nobel Prize in Economic Sciences, is not the unique solution, as was once assumed. Consequently, it violates the law of one price, one of the fundamental laws of economics and finance. In this article, we present a rigorous derivation of these solutions to the Black-Scholes boundary value problem.

</details>


### [188] [Crossover of Scaling Behaviors of Work Cumulants in a Driven Gaussian Field Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36476)
*Yanbo Qiao,Ruohan Xu,H. T. Quan*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文推导了驱动 $O(N)$ 高斯场论的有限温功特征函数（CFW），并给出所有零温过剩功累积量的闭式表达，系统刻画了绝热微扰论（APT）与 Kibble–Zurek（KZ）机制之间的交叉竞争。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 驱动态量子系统中的功统计与缺陷激发是量子热力学与非平衡相变的核心问题，但绝热微扰论（APT）与 Kibble–Zurek（KZ）标度在可解模型中的竞争机制尚缺乏统一的解析刻画。该工作旨在以可解的高斯场论为平台，建立二者交叉的系统分析基础。

Method: 针对驱动 $O(N)$ 高斯场论，推导有限温功特征函数（characteristic function of work, CFW）的闭式表达与零温过剩功累积量。对带隙（gapped）协议采用绝热微扰论（adiabatic perturbation theory, APT）分析边界导数行为；对趋近临界点、指数为 $p$ 的幂律（power-law）协议，则比较临界激发与正则贡献的量级竞争。

Result: 对带隙协议，当边界一阶导数非零时，APT 给出 $τ_Q^{-2}$ 标度（$τ_Q$ 为协议时长），边界越光滑衰减越快。对幂律协议，第 $n$ 阶累积量的临界贡献呈 Kibble–Zurek（KZ）标度 $τ_Q^{-p(d+n)/(p+2)}$（当 $d+n<2p+4$），在 $d+n=2p+4$ 处获得对数修正 $τ_Q^{-2p}\logτ_Q$，超过该条件后按 $τ_Q^{-2p}$ 标度；其中 $d$ 为空间维度。

Conclusion: 该可解模型中 APT–KZ 交叉的解析研究，为在相互作用场论（interacting field theories）中探讨二者的竞争提供了基准与出发点。

Abstract: We derive the finite-temperature characteristic function of work (CFW) for a driven $O(N)$ Gaussian field theory and obtain closed-form expressions for all zero-temperature excess-work cumulants. For gapped protocols, we use adiabatic perturbation theory (APT) to derive the $τ_Q^{-2}$ scaling for protocols with a nonzero first derivative at either boundary, where $τ_Q$ is the protocol duration; smoother boundaries lead to faster decay. For power-law protocols approaching the critical point with exponent $p$, we determine the competition between critical excitations and the regular contribution. The $n$th cumulant's critical contribution follows Kibble--Zurek (KZ) scaling $τ_Q^{-p(d+n)/(p+2)}$ for $d+n<2p+4$, acquires a logarithmic correction $τ_Q^{-2p}\logτ_Q$ at $d+n=2p+4$, and scales as $τ_Q^{-2p}$ above this condition, where $d$ is the spatial dimension. This analytical study of the APT--KZ crossover in a solvable model provides a basis for studying their competition in interacting field theories.

</details>


### [189] [Thermodynamics of the space of trajectories governed by a combination of two additive boundary functionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37102)
*V. V. Ryazanov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文将随机路径空间热力学（stochastic path-space thermodynamics）的形式体系推广至包含边界泛函与相对泛函的变量谱，并以具非对称线性漂移与原点的阶跃惩罚势的一维 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck 过程为模型，借助 Feynman-Kac 形式（Feynman-Kac formalism）与抛物柱函数理论（parabolic cylinder theory）数值求解有效哈密顿量，进而建立 Gallavotti-Cohen 型修正涨落定理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于系统扩展热力学变量空间，引入随机过程的边界泛函与相对泛函，以刻画分子布朗马达等待阶段等非平衡场景中的涨落统计。该工作是对预印本 arXiv:2607.24078 与 arXiv:2609.08477 所提出方法的推广。

Method: 采用 Feynman-Kac 形式与抛物柱函数理论，构造基于割线法（secant method）匹配对数导数的数值方案，获得有效哈密顿量的高精度本征值，用以定义累积量生成函数（cumulant generating function）。模型为一维 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck 过程，含非对称线性漂移与原点处的阶跃惩罚势。

Result: 获得了定义累积量生成函数的高精度有效哈密顿量本征值；针对耗散半平面中系统固定积分占据时间下的共轭漂移流，建立了 Gallavotti-Cohen 型修正路径空间涨落定理；并阐明新变量的物理与热力学意义。

Conclusion: “停留时间”（residence time）与“积分电流”（integral current）泛函的组合被用于描述对机械或电刺激敏感的离子通道，表明所引入变量具有实际建模价值。

Abstract: This paper extends the formalism of stochastic path-space thermodynamics by systematically expanding the space of thermodynamic variables with a spectrum of boundary and relative functionals of random processes. Generalizing the approaches introduced in preprints arXiv:2607.24078 and arXiv:2609.08477, we investigate the fluctuation statistics of a one-dimensional Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process with an asymmetric linear drift and a step penalty potential at the origin, which models the waiting phase of a molecular Brownian motor. Utilizing the Feynman-Kac formalism and the parabolic cylinder theory, a numerical scheme based on matching logarithmic derivatives via the secant method is constructed. A high-precision eigenvalues of the effective Hamiltonian is obtained, which define the cumulant generating function. We formulate a modified path-space fluctuation theorem of the Gallavotti-Cohen type for the conjugate drift current at a fixed integral occupation time of the system in the dissipative half-plane. The physical and thermodynamic significance of the new variables is elucidated. A combination of "residence time" and "integral current" functionals is applied to describe an ion channel sensitive to mechanical or electrical stimuli.

</details>


### [190] [Static versus dynamic universality in the site-diluted kagome Ising model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36886)
*Alexandros Vasilopoulos,Zeynep Demir Vatansever,Erol Vatansever,Gerard T. Barkema,Nikolaos G. Fytas*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该研究用Wolff单簇算法考察kagome晶格上位置稀释Ising模型（site-diluted Ising model）的平衡与动态临界性质，发现静态临界指数在所有稀释浓度下保持纯Ising精确值，而动态指数z随稀释下降并在强稀释下趋于饱和，且不同晶格间存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于检验无序（disorder）对Ising模型临界行为的影响，尤其是澄清稀释是否改变静态临界指数与动态普适性，并比较kagome与square两种晶格的行为差异。该问题涉及无序边际不相关性与动态普适类（dynamic universality）是否成立。

Method: 采用Wolff单簇算法（Wolff single-cluster algorithm）对kagome晶格上的位置稀释Ising模型进行模拟，考察不同自旋浓度p下的平衡临界性质与动态临界性质，并与square晶格结果进行对比。

Result: 平衡临界指数ν=1与γ/ν=7/4在所有考虑的p下保持纯Ising精确值，符合无序在α=0处的边际不相关性，比热在稀释后由对数增长过渡为双对数增长；这些静态结果在kagome与square晶格间一致。动态指数z在两种晶格上均随稀释下降，与局域动力学（local dynamics）下的观测相反，并在强稀释下呈现饱和至与p无关值的证据，暗示存在稀释动态不动点（diluted dynamic fixed point）。纯情形下两晶格z一致，但稀释区存在差异。

Conclusion: 两晶格在稀释区z的差异被归因于它们位于Ising不动点与percolation不动点之间重整化群流（renormalization-group flow）上的不同位置，而非动态普适性在渐近意义上的真正破缺。

Abstract: We study the site-diluted Ising model on the kagome lattice using the Wolff single-cluster algorithm, focusing on both equilibrium and dynamic critical properties. The equilibrium critical exponents $ν= 1$ and $γ/ν= 7/4$ retain their exact pure Ising values for all spin concentrations $p$ considered, consistent with the marginal irrelevance of disorder at $α= 0$, with the specific heat crossing over from logarithmic to double-logarithmic growth upon dilution. These static results are shared between the kagome and square lattices. On the dynamic side, $z$ decreases upon dilution on both lattice, opposite to what is observed under local dynamics, with evidence of saturation to a $p$-independent value at strong dilution, suggesting a diluted dynamic fixed point. While $z$ is consistent between the two lattices in the pure case, it differs in the diluted regime, an effect we attribute to the two lattices sitting at different positions along the renormalization-group flow between the Ising and percolation fixed points, rather than to a true asymptotic breakdown of dynamic universality.

</details>


### [191] [Exact anomalous current fluctuations in the strong-anisotropy limit of the XXZ chain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37512)
*Taiki Ishiyama,Taiga Kurose,Kazuya Fujimoto,Tomohiro Sasamoto*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究折叠 XXZ 链（folded XXZ chain）在无穷温度下的自旋流涨落，获得时间积分自旋流矩生成函数的精确表达式，并证明其长时间极限收敛于由 M-Wright 函数描述的非高斯分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 折叠 XXZ 链刻画 XXZ 链的强各向异性极限（strong-anisotropy limit），其自旋流涨落性质此前缺乏精确刻画，故本文旨在以无穷温度下的矩生成函数（moment-generating function）为切入点，解析地确定长时间输运统计。

Method: 通过非局域映射（nonlocal map）将折叠 XXZ 链对应到一维 t-0 模型（one-dimensional t-0 model），并利用其精确的自旋-电荷分离（spin-charge separation）；在此基础上对矩生成函数作长时间渐近分析，并将该途径推广至相邻两格点自旋求和的动力学关联函数。

Result: 得到时间积分自旋流矩生成函数的精确表达式；长时间渐近分析表明重标度后的时间积分电流收敛于由 M-Wright 函数（M-Wright function）描述的非高斯分布；同时，相邻两格点自旋求和的动力学关联函数（dynamical correlation function）在长时间下呈扩散型高斯轮廓。

Conclusion: 折叠 XXZ 链在无穷温度下的长时间自旋输运呈现非高斯统计，而相邻格点自旋求和的关联表现出扩散高斯行为；二者均可由映射至 t-0 模型及自旋-电荷分离的精确方法统一导出。

Abstract: We study spin-current fluctuations of the folded XXZ chain, which describes the strong-anisotropy limit of the XXZ chain. We obtain an exact expression for the moment-generating function of the time-integrated spin current at infinite temperature. A long-time asymptotic analysis establishes that the rescaled time-integrated current converges to a non-Gaussian distribution described by the M-Wright function. The derivation relies on a nonlocal map to the one-dimensional t-0 model and its exact spin-charge separation. By extending this approach, we also show that the dynamical correlation function of the spins summed over two adjacent sites exhibits a diffusive Gaussian profile at long times.

</details>


### [192] [Weakly interacting Bose-Einstein condensate with stochastic resetting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37110)
*Nikhil Mesquita,Manas Kulkarni,Satya N. Majumdar,Sanjib Sabhapandit*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文研究弱排斥玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体（Bose-Einstein condensate）在共享随机环境下，随机重置（stochastic resetting）产生的动力学涌现关联（dynamically emergent correlations, DEC）与内在（直接）相互作用共存的效应，并解析给出其非平衡稳态的密度分布、边缘统计与全计数统计（full counting statistics）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机重置可通过共享涨落环境在多体系统中产生强关联，但这类动力学涌现关联与系统内在直接相互作用如何共存尚缺乏可解析研究的模型。本文旨在为此类相互作用竞争提供一个可处理的量子多体框架。

Method: 在 Gross-Pitaevskii 框架下描述弱排斥玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚体，系统从初始 Thomas-Fermi 态自由膨胀，并以恒定速率被随机重置回该初始态；通过解析方法获得稳态密度分布、边缘统计与全计数统计。

Result: 重置协议驱动系统进入唯一的非平衡稳态；稳态密度在核心区保持倒抛物形式，外部呈现指数衰减尾部；重置诱导凝聚体边缘与粒子数的非平庸涨落，并给出边缘分布与全计数统计的精确标度形式。

Conclusion: 该模型为探索量子多体系统中内在排斥相互作用与由共同随机环境产生的吸引性动力学涌现关联之间的相互作用，提供了可解析处理的研究场景。

Abstract: Stochastic resetting can generate strong correlations in many-body systems through a shared fluctuating environment. Here, we investigate how such dynamically emergent correlations (DEC) coexist with intrinsic (direct) interactions in a weakly repulsive Bose-Einstein condensate described within the Gross-Pitaevskii framework. The condensate undergoes free expansion from an initial Thomas-Fermi state and is stochastically reset to this initial state at a constant rate. We show that the resetting protocol drives the system into a unique nonequilibrium steady state and obtain its density profile, edge statistics, and full counting statistics analytically. The steady-state density retains an inverted-parabolic form within the core, while developing exponentially decaying tails outside it. We further show that resetting induces nontrivial fluctuations of the condensate edge and particle number, yielding exact scaling forms for the edge distribution and the full counting statistics. Our results provide a tractable setting for exploring the interplay between intrinsic repulsive interactions and attractive DEC generated by a common stochastic environment in quantum many-body systems.

</details>


### [193] [Accurate ground-state entropies from population Monte Carlo: The antiferromagnetic Ising model on the Shastry-Sutherland lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37174)
*Denis Gessert,Wolfhard Janke,Martin Weigel*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文展示用群体退火蒙特卡罗（population annealing Monte Carlo）方法高精度计算基态熵，并在 Shastry-Sutherland 晶格上的经典反铁磁 Ising 模型（对角耦合为最近邻两倍）中验证，得到每格点基态熵为 0.45877778(10)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高精度获取宏观简并基态（macroscopically degenerate ground state）的熵在数值上具有挑战性，需要可靠方法并与已有估计交叉验证。

Method: 采用群体退火蒙特卡罗模拟（population annealing Monte Carlo）计算基态熵；模型为 Shastry-Sutherland 晶格上的经典反铁磁 Ising 模型（classical antiferromagnetic Ising model），对角耦合强度取最近邻相互作用的两倍；并对采样基态构型分析键构型、局域能量构型与自旋-自旋关联（spin-spin correlations）。

Result: 在无外磁场时模型在所有温度下无序；所得每格点基态熵为 0.45877778(10)，与近期角转移矩阵重整化群（corner transfer matrix renormalization group）研究的估计吻合良好。所有观测与基态无序性质一致，但数值数据显示局域能量偏离基态能级的格点团簇尺寸分布呈幂律衰减（power-law decay）。

Conclusion: 群体退火蒙特卡罗可高精度计算基态熵，并在该模型中与角转移矩阵重整化群结果一致；局域能量团簇尺寸分布的幂律行为提示基态中存在值得关注的标度特征。

Abstract: We demonstrate how the population annealing Monte Carlo simulation method can be used to compute the ground-state entropy to high precision. This approach is tested for the classical antiferromagnetic Ising model on the Shastry-Sutherland lattice with diagonal couplings chosen twice as strong as the nearest-neighbor interactions. In the absence of an external magnetic field, the model is known to be disordered at all temperatures, with a macroscopically degenerate ground state. We find the ground-state entropy to be $0.458\,777\,78(10)$ per site, which is in good agreement with the estimate of a recent study using the corner transfer matrix renormalization group method. The sampled ground-state configurations are analyzed by considering the bond configurations, the local-energy configurations, and the spin-spin correlations. While all observations are consistent with the disordered nature of the ground state, the numerical data suggest a power-law decay of the size distribution of clusters of sites with local energies different from the ground-state level.

</details>


### [194] [The Spectral Cost of Detecting Nonequilibrium at Finite Temporal Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37205)
*Murad Aznagulov*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该摘要指出，即便探测器的最小采样间隔为 τ₀、无法获取更密集的快照，只要采样间隔由两个时钟随机化，仍可对完全观测的有限马尔可夫生成元（finite Markov generator）实现精确辨识；同时强调“辨识（identification）”不等于“检测（detection）”。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究的动机在于辨析采样受限条件下的两个常被混淆的目标：精确辨识生成元与拒斥隐藏的平衡态解释。作者关注探测器时间分辨率下限 τ₀ 对这两类任务的不同影响。

Method: 方法上，利用两个时钟对采样间隔进行随机化（randomized gaps），使间隔小于 τ₀ 的快照限制被规避；并针对非平衡网络（nonequilibrium network）分析拒斥隐藏平衡态（hidden equilibrium）解释所需的快照数量，同时考察自适应采样策略（adaptive schedule）与单条相依轨迹检验（test on one dependent trajectory）。

Result: 对正规非平衡网络，拒斥所有隐藏平衡态解释所需快照数随 e^{2γ_irr τ₀} 增长，其中 γ_irr 为旋转模式（rotating modes）最慢的阻尼率；任何自适应调度都无法超越该速率，而单条相依轨迹上的检验可以达到该速率。

Conclusion: 结论是：受时间分辨率限制的探测器仍可在间隔随机化下精确辨识有限马尔可夫生成元，但辨识与检测并不等价；拒斥隐藏平衡态解释的分辨代价由 γ_irr 与 τ₀ 共同决定的指数标度所支配，且该标度已达最优。

Abstract: A detector that cannot take two snapshots closer than a time $τ_0$ still identifies a fully observed finite Markov generator exactly, provided its gaps are randomized by two clocks. Identification is not detection. For a normal nonequilibrium network, the number of snapshots needed to reject every hidden equilibrium explanation grows as $e^{2γ_{\mathrm{irr}}τ_0}$, where $γ_{\mathrm{irr}}$ is the slowest damping of rotating modes. No adaptive schedule beats this rate, and a test on one dependent trajectory attains it.

</details>


### [195] [Scale invariance, fractal dynamics, and critical exponents at the phase transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37275)
*Henrique Alves de Lima*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文提出临界点处平衡动力学实际发生在有效分形子空间（effective fractal subspace）而非整个欧氏空间（Euclidean space），并借助分数阶微积分（fractional calculus）将反常临界指数 η 几何化为与关联分形维数（correlation fractal dimension）相联系的量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文旨在为临界现象中反常临界指数 η 提供一个几何解释，将其与有效分形子空间及关联分形维数相关联，从而连接标度律（scaling laws）、临界指数、关联函数与分形几何。

Method: 方法上从 Fisher 的序参量关联函数（order-parameter correlation function）表述出发，使用分数阶微积分工具推导关联函数形式，并建立 η 与 Riesz 分数阶导数（Riesz fractional derivative）所联系的分形维数 d_R 的显式关系，同时考察 Rushbrooke 标度关系（Rushbrooke scaling relation）以及非整数维与无序系统（disordered systems）中的临界行为。

Result: 结果给出可复原上临界维（upper critical dimension）以下临界行为的关联函数形式，并得到 η 与 d_R 的显式关系；在由参数 σ 控制的无序情形中，检验了所提几何解释保持有效的范围。

Conclusion: 结论认为临界点观察到的反常行为可理解为动力学被约束在有效分形子空间上的结果，表明标度律、临界指数、关联与分形几何之间存在内在联系。

Abstract: We propose that, at criticality, equilibrium dynamics effectively evolves on a fractal subspace rather than throughout the full Euclidean space. Starting from Fisher's formulation of the order-parameter correlation function, we interpret the anomalous critical exponent $η$ geometrically through a correlation fractal dimension associated with this effective subspace. Using fractional-calculus tools, we derive a form of the correlation function that recovers critical behavior below the upper critical dimension and obtain an explicit relation between $η$ and a fractal dimension $d_R$ linked to the Riesz fractional derivative. We also examine the Rushbrooke scaling relation and investigate critical behavior in non-integer-dimensional and disordered systems. In the disordered case, controlled by a parameter $σ$, we test the range over which the proposed geometric interpretation remains valid. The results connect scaling laws, critical exponents, correlations, and fractal geometry, suggesting that the anomalous behavior observed at criticality can be understood as a consequence of dynamics constrained to an effective fractal subspace.

</details>


### [196] [Anomalous transport in periodic systems driven by active fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37289)
*Karol Białas*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文研究周期势场中由主动涨落（active fluctuations）驱动的布朗粒子输运，聚焦白泊松散粒噪声（white Poisson shot noise）及其非高斯振幅统计，系统揭示巨输运增强、绝对负迁移率（absolute negative mobility）与棘轮效应（ratchet effect）三大反常输运机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热噪声（thermal noise）满足细致平衡（detailed balance）与涨落耗散定理（fluctuation-dissipation theorem），而主动涨落打破这些约束，可刻画生物与合成微系统中的自驱动机制及活性浴（active-bath）相互作用，因而需要专门研究其驱动的输运行为。

Method: 结合蒙特卡洛模拟（Monte Carlo simulations）与解析建模；采用带双向偏正态振幅分布（bidirectional skew-normal amplitude distribution）的白泊松散粒噪声，并提出唯象跳跃-弛豫模型（jump-relaxation model）将位移分解为瞬时随机跳跃与向势阱极小值的确定性弛豫，并推广至惯性动力学。

Result: 遵从偏正态振幅分布的主动涨落使定向速度相比自由粒子提升数个数量级；有限质量 m 可依噪声方差增强、减弱、诱导甚至完全抑制该增强。在一维纯过阻尼系统中建立绝对负迁移率的极简模型，无需粒子惯性、空间周期势非线性或显式含时周期驱动；在平均随机力的另一种标度下，振幅分布的不对称性通过倾斜棘轮效应（tilting ratchet effect）在零偏置极限产生净定向输运。

Conclusion: 主动涨落（含散粒噪声非高斯振幅统计）可在周期势中产生多种反常输运现象，其机制可由跳跃-弛豫与极简过阻尼模型统一刻画，质量与噪声方差构成关键调控参数。

Abstract: This dissertation investigates the transport of Brownian particles driven by active fluctuations in periodic potential landscapes. Unlike thermal noise, active fluctuations break detailed balance and violate the fluctuation-dissipation theorem. They model self-propulsion mechanisms and active-bath interactions in biological and synthetic micro-systems. By focusing on white Poisson shot noise with non-Gaussian amplitude statistics, this work systematically uncovers the mechanisms governing three major transport anomalies: giant transport enhancement, absolute negative mobility, and the ratchet effect.
  Combining Monte Carlo simulations with analytical modeling, this work demonstrates that active fluctuations adhering to a bidirectional skew-normal amplitude distribution enhance directed velocity compared to the free-particle by several orders of magnitude. This giant transport enhancement is fully quantified via a phenomenological jump-relaxation model that decouples particle displacement into instantaneous stochastic jumps and deterministic relaxation towards potential minimum. Extending this model to inertial dynamics reveals that finite particle mass m acts as a dynamic control parameter capable of strengthening, weakening, constructively inducing, or completely suppressing transport enhancement depending on noise variance.
  Furthermore, an ultra-minimal model for absolute negative mobility is established in a one-dimensional, purely overdamped system, showing that discontinuous shot noise drives transport opposite to an applied bias without requiring particle inertia, nonlinearity of spatially periodic potential, or explicit time-dependent periodic driving. Conversely, under an alternative scaling of the average stochastic force, an asymmetric distribution of fluctuation amplitudes generates net directed transport through a tilting ratchet effect in the zero-bias limit.

</details>


### [197] [Phase separation, morphology, and metastability in the three-dimensional active Potts model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37436)
*Aditya Kumar Dutta,Raja Paul*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 本文在立方晶格上研究具有 2、4、6 个取向态的三维活性 Potts 模型（active Potts model, APM），刻画其稳态相图与非平衡相分离形貌，并检验极性液体的亚稳性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在将此前二维活性 Potts 模型的相行为拓展至三维，考察取向态数目与推进速度如何影响稳态相图及致密相形貌。

Method: 采用立方晶格上的三维活性 Potts 模型，设置 2、4、6 个取向态；结合微观模拟（microscopic simulations）与粗粒化流体力学方程（coarse-grained hydrodynamic equations），并通过人为引入液滴来检验极性液体的稳定性。

Result: 稳态相图呈现低密度气态、高密度极性液态与液–气共存相，相行为受密度、温度和推进速度控制，并保留二维 APM 的主要特征。弱推进时致密相形成垂直于运动方向的板状（slab）形貌；随推进速度增大，2 态模型保持该形貌，而 4、6 态的致密相发生重取向并沿纵向传播，6 态模型还进一步发展出柱状（cylindrical）形貌。

Conclusion: 高推进速度下，反向传播液滴仅能部分改变初始极性态，而横向液滴可完全改变之，表明三维活性 Potts 模型中极性液体具有亚稳（metastable）性质。

Abstract: We study the three-dimensional active Potts model (APM) on a cubic lattice with 2, 4, and 6 orientational states. The steady-state phase diagrams exhibit low-density gaseous, high-density polar liquid, and liquid--gas coexistence phases, with the phase behavior controlled by density, temperature, and propulsion speed and retaining the main features of the previously studied two-dimensional APM. At weak propulsion, the dense phase forms a slab perpendicular to its direction of motion. With increasing propulsion speed, this morphology persists for the 2-state model, whereas the dense phases for 4 and 6 states reorient and propagate along their longitudinal direction. For the 6-state model, the reoriented dense phase further develops a cylindrical morphology. We also examine the stability of polar liquids against artificially introduced droplets using microscopic simulations and coarse-grained hydrodynamic equations. At high propulsion speeds, counter-propagating droplets can partially modify the initial polar state, while transverse droplets can completely alter it, demonstrating the metastable nature of polar liquids in the three-dimensional active Potts model.

</details>


### [198] [Quantum Statistical Thermal Engine at the BCS-BEC crossover](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37592)
*Santiago Henríquez Lira,Felipe Isaule,Martín HvE Groves,Francisco J. Peña,Patricio Vargas,Thomás Fogarty*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文提出一种基于双组分费米气体（two-component Fermi gas）的量子热机，利用s波接触相互作用（s-wave contact interaction）跨越多体BCS-BEC渡越（crossover）运行，并以统计性质而非单纯压缩-膨胀过程作为功来源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统量子热机多依赖压缩与膨胀等机械循环过程获取功，而对相互作用本身所携带的统计热力学资源利用不足。本文旨在探索配对关联（pairing correlations）可否作为可调控的热力学资源，从而构建新型量子热机架构。

Method: 采用泛函重整化群（functional renormalization group）方法，在BCS-BEC渡越区域内以非微扰形式求取状态方程（equation of state），并涵盖超流-正常相变（superfluid-normal phase transition）。所设计循环由等熵密度冲程、等容热化与等温相互作用扫描组合而成。

Result: 该循环融合了奥托循环（Otto cycle）与卡诺循环（Carnot cycle）的特征，使系统在不使净功消失的前提下同时逼近两者的效率极限。在无密度变化时，循环退化为类斯特林统计热机（statistical Stirling-like engine），其功完全源于相互作用，效率可达38%。渡越过程呈现显著不对称性，使同一架构经相图中不同路径可实现热机、制冷机、加速器或加热器功能。

Conclusion: 研究结果凸显配对关联作为量子热机通用热力学资源的潜力，并表明相互作用扫描可为量子热力学循环提供独立于密度调控的功能维度。

Abstract: We propose a quantum heat engine based on a two-component Fermi gas with s-wave contact interaction, operating across the BCS-BEC crossover. The work is extracted from the statistical properties of the gas, which are controlled by the interaction, rather than relying solely on compression and expansion stages. Using the functional renormalization group formalism, we obtain the equation of state in a non-perturbative form along the crossover, encompassing the superfluid-normal phase transition. The cycle combines isentropic density strokes, isochoric thermalization, and isothermal interaction sweeps. This construction makes it possible to integrate features of both Otto and Carnot cycles, in which the system simultaneously saturates both efficiency limits without the net work vanishing. In the absence of density variations, the cycle reduces to a statistical Stirling-like engine, in which the work generated arises exclusively from the interaction, achieving efficiencies up to $38\%$. A pronounced asymmetry emerges through the crossover, giving rise to distinct operating regimes depending on the trajectory followed in the phase diagram. Consequently, the same architecture can be tuned to function as an engine, refrigerator, accelerator, or heater. These findings highlight pairing correlations as a versatile thermodynamic resource for quantum heat machines.

</details>


### [199] [Blind directions of linear response and the limits of finite-perturbation bounds on efficiency fluctuations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37940)
*Badr Farih*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文研究随机效率（stochastic efficiency）这一非对称性变量在有限扰动下的可认证方差。作者采用能值比 η=W/(W+T_0S)，指出常规比值 -W/Q_h 各阶矩不存在。由于线性响应（linear response）对 η 的 Cramér-Rao 界在某一超平面上为零，作者改用 Barankin 多点界（倾斜路径测度）得到正的下界。在三态马达模型中，该界可恢复 η 方差的 75%，且为最佳线性响应界的 1.25-1.57 倍。作者强调这是一种面向已知模型的计算工具，而非不确定性关系，且该增益是短时间窗口效应，不能用于统计推断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 热力学不确定性关系（thermodynamic uncertainty relations）用于约束时间反对称流（time-antisymmetric currents）的涨落，但随机效率作为两个奇性量之比在路径反转下为偶，并不属于此类流。传统比值 -W/Q_h 的矩不存在，因此需要选取矩存在的观测量并考察有限扰动能认证多少方差，尤其在非线性观测量上线性响应失效的情形。

Method: 研究对象为能值比 η=W/(W+T_0S)。对每个流及每个关于跃迁计数与驻留时间线性的观测量，多参数 Cramér-Rao 界已等于方差；而对 η 该界在扰动方向的超平面上为零。作者转而使用 Barankin 多点界（Barankin multi-point bound），并作用于倾斜路径测度（tilted path measures）。将其 Gram 矩阵表述为 Feynman-Kac 矩阵指数，其势为跃迁强度（jump intensities）的 Hellinger 被积函数，扰动代价为 χ² 散度（χ² divergence）。并对 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck 过程给出精确可解构造。

Result: 在三态马达模型（three-state motor model）中，所有线性响应界均为零时，Barankin 界在超平面上保持为正，并恢复 η 方差的 75%。该界达到最佳线性响应界的 1.25-1.57 倍，且增益在最多十六个状态的网络上持续存在。对 Ornstein-Uhlenbeck 过程，构造精确可解：最优倾斜按 T^{-1/2} 缩放，三个测试点几乎达到上确界。

Conclusion: 该构造确立了两种极限：增益是短时间窗口效应，当计数趋于高斯（Gaussian）时消失；且该界无法转化为推断，因为粗粒化测量仅从下方约束 χ² 散度。作者结论为：这是一种针对手头模型的计算工具（computational instrument），而非不确定性关系。

Abstract: Thermodynamic uncertainty relations bound the fluctuations of time-antisymmetric currents. Stochastic efficiency is not one: a ratio of two odd quantities, it is even under path reversal, and for the conventional ratio $-W/Q_h$ no moment exists. We work with the exergetic ratio $η=W/(W+T_0S)$, whose moments exist, and ask how much of its variance a finite perturbation of the dynamics can certify when linear response cannot. The question is specific to nonlinear observables: for every current, and every observable linear in transition counts and residence times, the multiparameter Cramér-Rao bound over rate and site perturbations already equals the variance. For $η$ it does not. The Cramér-Rao bound vanishes on a hyperplane of perturbation directions, while the Barankin multi-point bound, applied to tilted path measures, stays positive there and recovers 75% of the variance of $η$ in a three-state motor model where every linear-response bound is zero. The Gram matrix of tilted Markov-jump path measures is a Feynman-Kac matrix exponential whose potential is the Hellinger integrand of the jump intensities, and the cost of a perturbation is its $χ^2$ divergence, which no dissipation bound controls. The bound captures 1.25-1.57 times the best linear-response bound, and the gain persists on networks of up to sixteen states. For an Ornstein-Uhlenbeck process the construction is exactly solvable: the optimal tilt scales as $T^{-1/2}$ and three test points nearly reach the supremum. Two limits are established. The gain is a short-window effect that disappears as the counts become Gaussian, and the bound cannot be turned into inference: coarse measurements bound $χ^2$ only from below. The construction is a computational instrument for a model in hand, not an uncertainty relation.

</details>


### [200] [The Principle of Minimum Justified Correlation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38124)
*John H Van Drie*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该文（两部分论文的第一部分）论证 Shannon 熵（Shannon entropy）、Fisher 信息（Fisher information）与量子力学动能（kinetic energy）均可视为相关性（correlation）的度量，并提出"最小可证成相关性原理"（principle of minimum justified correlation）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文试图为 Shannon 熵、Fisher 信息与量子动能建立统一的相关性解释，并据此给出一个在给定物理约束下选择分布的变分原理。

Method: 以"破坏相关性的基本映射"（fundamental correlation-destroying map）为核心：将联合概率密度 ρ(x,y) 替换为边缘分布之积 ρ_x(x)ρ_y(y)；离散情形下考察 Shannon 熵在该映射下的单调性，连续情形下引入坐标不变的连续相关性度量 I[ρ] = h[ρ_x] + h[ρ_y] − h[ρ] 以及相对 Fisher 信息 J(ρ‖ρ_xρ_y)。

Result: 离散情形下 Shannon 熵在该基本映射下非递减；连续情形下由该映射得到行为良好且坐标不变的相关性度量 I[ρ]，相对 Fisher 信息亦呈现类似行为。对归一化实量子波函数，动能的减少量与相对 Fisher 信息严格成正比：⟨T⟩_ψ − ⟨T⟩_Φ = (ℏ²/8m) J(ρ‖ρ_xρ_y)，其中 Φ = √(ρ_x ρ_y)。

Conclusion: 结果支持 Jaynes 意义上的"最小可证成相关性原理"：在给定物理约束及满足约束的可能分布（或相应振幅）集合中，应选取相关性最小者。本文为两部分论文的第一部分，推导上述熵、Fisher 信息与动能恒等式并陈述该原理；第二部分将处理多重解、时间依赖、自旋作用、通往 Schrödinger 方程的路径以及一项提议的实验检验。文中注明使用了 AI。

Abstract: It is shown that Shannon entropy, Fisher information, and quantum-mechanical kinetic energy may all be viewed as measures of correlation. We begin with a fundamental correlation-destroying map: a joint probability density $ρ(x,y)$ is replaced by $ρ_x(x)ρ_y(y)$, the product of its marginal distributions. In the discrete case, Shannon's entropy is nondecreasing under this map. For continuous distributions, this fundamental map leads to a well-behaved, coordinate-invariant correlation measure, analogous to the discrete Shannon entropy: \[ I[ρ] = h[ρ_x] + h[ρ_y] - h[ρ]. \] In the continuous case, however, another measure appears: Fisher information. The relative Fisher information $J(ρ\|ρ_xρ_y)$ behaves similarly under the fundamental map. For a normalized real quantum wavefunction, this decrease is exactly proportional to the decrease in mean kinetic energy, \[ \langle T\rangle_ψ- \langle T\rangle_Φ= \frac{\hbar^2}{8m} J(ρ\|ρ_xρ_y), \qquad Φ= \sqrt{ρ_x ρ_y}. \] In Jaynes's language, the result supports a principle of minimum justified correlation: given physical constraints and a set of possible distributions or related amplitudes satisfying those constraints, select from that set those with the least correlation.
  This is Part I of a two-part paper. Here we develop the entropy, Fisher-information, and kinetic-energy identities above, and state the principle they support. Part II addresses questions which Part I raises but leaves unanswered: multiple solutions, time dependence, the role of spin, a route to the Schrödinger equation itself, and a proposed experimental test. AI has been used.

</details>


### [201] [Classification of topological phases of matter in stochastic systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38171)
*Dexin Li,Evelyn Tang*

Main category: cond-mat.stat-mech

TL;DR: 该论文针对随机（stochastic）系统中的拓扑相（topological phases）缺乏与马尔可夫约束（Markovian constraints）相符的分类问题，引入新的同伦方法（homotopy approach）并重新定义点间隙（point gap），建立了严格分类框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑相已在随机系统及生化过程中被发现，但现有分类方法基于量子系统，无法适配马尔可夫约束导致的矩阵空间改变，因而缺乏一致分类，阻碍了对新场景的推广。

Method: 提出新的同伦方法（homotopy approach），并重新定义点间隙（point gap），以刻画马尔可夫约束下的矩阵空间及其拓扑性质。

Result: 在马尔可夫约束下，仅有两类对称类（symmetry classes）保持稳健：无对称性（no symmetry）与伪厄米性（pseudo-Hermiticity）；并确定了具有拓扑非平凡相的维度及其群结构（group structure），同时指出非厄米性（non-Hermiticity）对非平凡拓扑相的必要性。

Conclusion: 该工作为预测活性物质（active matter）与生命物质（living matter）中的稳健行为提供了严格框架。

Abstract: Topological phases of matter support edge states protected from noise and perturbations, and the classification of which symmetry and dimension support such phases was developed for quantum systems and analogous platforms. While topological phases have also been discovered in stochastic systems and posited as mechanisms for biochemical processes, a classification consistent with their Markovian constraints remains lacking, impeding generalization to new scenarios. These constraints alter the matrix space preventing the application of previous classification methods, while hosting new properties such as the necessity of non-Hermiticity for non-trivial topological phases. We introduce new methods including a new homotopy approach and redefinition of the point gap and find that only two symmetry classes remain robust: no symmetry and pseudo-Hermiticity. In these symmetry classes, we identify the dimensions with topologically non-trivial phases and their group structure, creating a rigorous framework for predicting robust behavior in active and living matter.

</details>


<div id='math.DG'></div>

# math.DG [[Back]](#toc)

### [202] [Positive Sectional Curvature on All Smooth 7-spheres](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.29426)
*Yang-Hui He,Ziran Liu,Shing-Tung Yau*

Main category: math.DG

TL;DR: 该论文在每一个光滑同伦七维球面（smooth homotopy seven-sphere）上构造出截面曲率（sectional curvature）严格为正的光滑黎曼度量（smooth Riemannian metric），覆盖全部 28 个有向微分同胚类（oriented diffeomorphism classes）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正截面曲率度量的存在性是黎曼几何（Riemannian geometry）中的核心问题，而同伦七维球面的光滑结构多样性（28 类）使其成为检验该问题的关键对象。论文旨在为所有此类奇异光滑结构建立正曲率度量。

Method: 方法为分块构造与粘合：针对每个光滑结构，在两张七维圆盘（seven-dimensional disks）上分别构造正曲率度量，使二者的诱导边界度量（induced boundary metrics）在给定贴合映射（attaching map）下相符，再通过粘合定理（gluing theorem）得到闭流形上的光滑度量。

Result: 所得度量在闭流形上光滑，且截面曲率严格为正；构造对全部 28 个有向微分同胚类均成立。

Conclusion: 结论表明，同伦七维球面的所有光滑结构均可承载严格正截面曲率的黎曼度量。

Abstract: We construct a smooth Riemannian metric with strictly positive sectional curvature on every smooth homotopy seven-sphere. For each smooth structure, we construct positively curved metrics on two seven-dimensional disks whose induced boundary metrics agree under the prescribed attaching map. Under this identification, a gluing theorem then yields the required smooth metric on the closed manifold. The construction applies to all 28 oriented diffeomorphism classes.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.quant-gas'></div>

# cond-mat.quant-gas [[Back]](#toc)

### [203] [Multicomponent anyons in one-dimensional optical lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36554)
*Sagarika Basak,Xi-Wen Guan,Han Pu*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 本文研究一维（1D）光晶格中多分量相互作用任意子（anyons）的基态与动力学性质，采用任意子—哈伯德模型（Anyon--Hubbard model）分析自旋与电荷关联。结果表明分数量子交换统计与多分量效应共同导致动量分布的结构重构及反铁磁自旋关联，且统计相位参数可作为调控淬火动力学（quench dynamics）的有效旋钮。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探讨多分量效应与分数量子交换统计（fractional exchange statistics）如何共同影响一维光晶格中任意子的基态关联与动力学行为，并推动对自旋任意子体系中统计驱动的相变的认识。

Method: 采用任意子—哈伯德模型（Anyon--Hubbard model）描述一维光学晶格中受限的多分量相互作用任意子，考察自旋与电荷关联随相互作用强度和任意子统计参数的变化，并借助结构因子与淬火动力学（quench dynamics）分析展开研究。

Result: 分数量子交换统计使原本对称的壳层结构及自旋费米子动量分布中的显著奇异性发生结构重构，出现不对称性、新谱峰与展宽的奇异特征；作为任意子极限情形的赝玻色子（pseudobosons）不再显示主导的零动量峰，而呈现有限动量峰的准费米子壳层结构，其位置受晶格占据数与相互作用强度调制。自旋关联的结构因子揭示了反铁磁序（antiferromagnetic ordering），其关联强度可由任意子交换统计调节；淬火动力学显示统计相位参数可切换偶极振荡的欠阻尼与过阻尼弛豫机制并调控云团膨胀。

Conclusion: 研究结果展示了统计参数驱动的基态与动力学相变（phase transitions）在自旋任意子体系中的可能性，为探索统计驱动的量子相变与动力学行为开辟了途径。

Abstract: We investigate the ground-state and dynamical properties of multicomponent interacting anyons confined in a one-dimensional (1D) optical lattice. Adopting the Anyon--Hubbard model, we explore spin and charge correlations as functions of interaction strength and the anyonic statistical parameter. Our results demonstrate that multicomponent effects combined with fractional exchange statistics substantially reshape both charge and spin correlations. For fractional exchange statistics, the canonical symmetric shell structure with prominent singularities in the spinor fermionic momentum distribution undergoes notable structural reconstruction, exhibiting emergent asymmetry, new spectral peaks, and broadened singular features. Unlike the Tonks--Girardeau bosonic gas, pseudobosons, representing a limiting case of anyons, cease to display a dominant zero-momentum peak. Instead, a quasi-fermionic shell structure emerges with finite-momentum peaks, whose locations are modulated by lattice site occupancy and interaction strength. The structure factor of spin correlations uncovers antiferromagnetic ordering in spinor anyon systems, with correlation magnitudes tunable by anyonic exchange statistics. Furthermore, quench dynamics analysis reveals the statistical phase parameter as an effective tuning knob. It enables the switching of dipole oscillations between underdamped and overdamped relaxation regimes and governs overall cloud expansion dynamics. Our findings pave the way for exploring statistics-driven ground-state and dynamical phase transitions in spinor anyon systems.

</details>


### [204] [Generalized Effective Spin-Chain formalism for multicomponent anyons in one-dimensional optical lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36555)
*Sagarika Basak,Xi-Wen Guan,Han Pu*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该工作提出广义有效自旋链（generalized effective spin-chain, GESC）形式化框架，用于描述一维光晶格中强相互作用的多组分任意子（anyons）。框架将粒子运动映射为无自旋费米子、将自旋态映射为有序链，从而以自旋—电荷分离（spin–charge separation）视角刻画分数交换统计的物理效应，并将统计相位主要编码于可观测量的任意子结构与自旋交换过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强相互作用多组分任意子体系中，分数交换统计（fractional exchange statistics）如何在强关联条件下存续并体现，缺乏高效且物理透明的理论描述。作者希望通过自旋—电荷分离的映射，将统计效应从电荷自由度中解耦，从而厘清统计相位在静态与动力学行为中的具体作用位置。

Method: 构建广义有效自旋链（GESC）形式化：在强相互作用区，将粒子运动映射为无自旋费米子，自旋态映射为有序链。该映射使领头阶电荷哈密顿量（charge Hamiltonian）不再依赖统计相位，而统计相位通过双占据态（doubly occupied states）的虚隧穿（virtual tunneling）直接印刻到自旋交换系数上。基于该框架分析任意子—哈伯德模型（Anyon–Hubbard model）的基态与非平衡动力学，包括连续自旋交换导致的相位积累与干涉效应。

Result: 领头阶电荷哈密顿量与统计相位无关，统计相位经由虚隧穿进入自旋交换系数。GESC 复现了已有任意子—哈伯德模型（Anyon–Hubbard model）报告的基态性质，并表明其统计依赖主要源于可观测量的显式任意子结构，而非自旋基态本身。非平衡情形下，连续自旋交换累积方向依赖相位，其干涉导致杂质输运由扩散型向局域化转变，并在中间统计下产生反演不对称（inversion-asymmetric）传播；相同与可区分杂质均出现膨胀受抑，传播差异由杂质身份与相互作用各向异性之间的相互作用决定。

Conclusion: GESC 提供了通用且计算高效的理论框架，将宏观动力学与微观虚交换过程相联系，为解析分数交换统计如何在强相互作用下存续、并在静态与动力学区域呈现不同表现提供了独特的自旋—电荷分离视角。

Abstract: We develop a generalized effective spin-chain (GESC) formalism for strongly interacting multicomponent anyons in a one-dimensional (1D) optical lattice. By mapping particle motion onto spinless fermions and spin states onto an ordered chain, this framework provides a spin--charge-separated perspective on the physical effects of fractional exchange statistics. In the strong-interaction regime, the leading-order charge Hamiltonian becomes independent of the statistical phase; instead, virtual tunneling through doubly occupied states directly imprints this phase on the spin-exchange coefficients. The GESC formalism captures ground-state properties of the full Anyon--Hubbard model reported in S.~Basak, X.-W.~Guan, and H.~Pu, unpublished manuscript (2026), and reveals that their statistical dependence is predominantly encoded in the explicit anyonic structure of the observables rather than the underlying spin ground state. This formalism uncovers that, out of equilibrium, successive spin exchanges accumulate direction-dependent phases; the resulting interference drives crossover from dispersive to localized impurity transport and generates inversion-asymmetric propagation at intermediate statistics. Suppressed expansion persists for identical and distinguishable impurities, with differences in propagation governed by the interplay between impurity identity and interaction anisotropy. Ultimately, GESC offers a versatile, computationally efficient theoretical framework that connects macroscopic dynamics to microscopic virtual exchange processes, conferring a unique spin--charge-separated vantage for resolving how fractional exchange statistics survives strong interactions and manifests distinctly across static and dynamical regimes.

</details>


### [205] [Topological Pseudo-Goldstone Modes from Weakly Broken Dipole Conservation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36994)
*Yan-Guang Yue,Jie Lou,Yan Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 弱破坏有序相中的连续对称性可将戈德斯通模式（Goldstone modes）转化为拓扑集体能带。本文在具有两个独立戈德斯通相位模式的中性偶极凝聚体中确立了该机制：弱实味混合（real flavor mixing）固定相位并选出手征凝聚体，其陀螺耦合作用于缠绕极化织构，产生含拓扑最低带的赝戈德斯通部门。恢复偶极守恒后两模式在零频相遇，各自的陈数（Chern number）不再有定义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有序相中连续对称性的弱破缺通常被视为低能激发的微扰效应，但其是否可作为一种机制、将原本无能隙的戈德斯通模式重塑为具有拓扑性质的集体能带，尚缺乏明确的理论框架。本文以中性偶极凝聚体为平台，探索这一机制成立的条件与后果。

Method: 以守恒的母体偶极电荷为基础，该凝聚体支持两个独立的戈德斯通相位模式；引入弱实味混合固定相位并选出手征凝聚体，使陀螺耦合（gyroscopic coupling）作用于两模式的缠绕极化织构（winding polarization texture）。此外，利用母体偶极电荷定义的张量流（tensor current）的圆响应，进行模型辅助的低能嵌入局域贝里曲率（Berry curvature）重构，并给出解析稳定性边界与大占据数极限。

Result: 弱实味混合驱动的陀螺耦合产生一个赝戈德斯通部门，其中包含一条拓扑最低带。恢复偶极守恒迫使两个模式在零频处相遇，此时二者各自的能带陈数（Chern number）失去定义。张量流的圆响应可用于在基准区间内模型辅助地重构低能嵌入局域贝里曲率。解析稳定性边界与大占据数极限为该机制提供了可控的实现途径。

Conclusion: 连续对称性的弱破缺可作为将戈德斯通模式转化为拓扑集体能带的一般机制，并在中性偶极凝聚体中获得受控实现。偶极守恒的恢复消除了各模式独立陈数的定义，同时张量流的圆响应为探测嵌入贝里曲率提供了可行手段。

Abstract: Weakly breaking a continuous symmetry in an ordered phase can convert its Goldstone modes into topological collective bands. We establish this mechanism in a neutral dipole condensate whose conserving parent supports two independent Goldstone phase modes. Weak real flavor mixing pins the phases and selects a chiral condensate. The resulting gyroscopic coupling acts on their winding polarization texture, producing a pseudo-Goldstone sector containing a topological lowest band. Restoring dipole conservation forces the two modes to meet at zero frequency, where their separate band Chern numbers cease to be defined. The same parent dipole charges define a tensor current whose circular response permits model-assisted reconstruction of low-energy embedded local Berry curvature in the benchmark regime. Analytical stability bounds and a large-occupation limit provide a controlled realization of this mechanism.

</details>


### [206] [Finite-momentum trimers and Cooper quartets in a one-dimensional Fermi gas with coexistent $s$- and $p$-wave interactions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37032)
*Yixin Guo*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 在同时存在 $s$ 波与 $p$ 波相互作用的一维双组分费米气体（Fermi gas）中，通过保持伽利略不变性（Galilean invariance）的配对正则化，研究有限动量三聚体（trimer）与库珀四聚体（Cooper quartet）；发现四聚体在所有取样的耦合下均位于各分支之下，且其成键不能由最强束缚对或主导相互作用能单独决定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有工作多关注零质心动量束缚态或单一分波，本文旨在考察 $s$ 波与 $p$ 波共存时有限总动量的三聚体与四聚体，并厘清泡利阻塞（Pauli blocking）导致的色散偏移如何改变束缚态相图。

Method: 采用一维双组分费米气体模型，对每一对粒子的相对动量进行正则化以保证伽利略不变性；考虑泡利阻塞对三聚体色散的影响，并结合变分束缚（variational bound）分析与多种耦合、散射长度及密度的取样计算。

Result: 允许三聚体有限动量运动后，$abb$ 区域在配对与三聚体层面均扩大，并消除了奇波配对能量最低的区域；$aabb$ 四聚体在所有取样耦合下低于上述各分支，在始于两个双聚体通道交叉处的条带内其最低解离通道为运动 $abb$ 三聚体加一个原子。弱种间吸引下，两个奇波库珀对结合成四聚体，其结合能随吸引减弱可比共存三聚体高若干数量级；较强吸引下，关闭奇波相互作用后四聚体相对两个异种双聚体的结合消失，尽管偶波相互作用主导相互作用能。相对于真空情形，费米海（Fermi sea）增强弱耦合四聚体结合；固定散射长度时四聚体结合随密度增大，而共存三聚体显著变浅。

Conclusion: 四聚体是否成键不由最强束缚对或主导相互作用能单独决定；有限质心动量、泡利阻塞与费米海效应共同塑造了一维 $s$-$p$ 混合体系的多体束缚态结构。

Abstract: We study finite-momentum trimers and Cooper quartets in a one-dimensional two-component Fermi gas with coexistent $s$- and $p$-wave interactions, regularized in the relative momentum of each pair so that they remain Galilean invariant. Pauli blocking alone then makes the trimer dispersion deviate from free center-of-mass motion, so the trimer energy can be lowest at finite total momentum. Allowing this motion expands the $abb$ region among pairs and trimers and removes the region where an odd-wave pair is lowest. The $aabb$ quartet lies below all these branches at every sampled coupling, and in a strip starting on the crossing of the two dimer-pair channels its lowest breakup channel is a moving $abb$ trimer and an atom. At weak interspecies attraction, a variational bound shows that two odd-wave Cooper pairs bind into a quartet, whose binding exceeds that of the coexisting trimer by orders of magnitude as the attraction weakens. At stronger interspecies attraction, the quartet binding relative to two unlike-species dimers disappears when the odd-wave interaction is switched off, although the even-wave interaction dominates the interaction energy. Relative to the in-vacuum case, the Fermi sea enhances the weak-coupling quartet binding, and at fixed scattering lengths the quartet binding grows with density while the coexisting trimer becomes much shallower. These results show that neither the most strongly bound pair nor the dominant interaction energy alone determines whether the quartet binds.

</details>


### [207] [Quantum Simulation of Detuning-Controlled Proximity-Induced Localization and Reentrant Delocalization in a Quasiperiodic Ladder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37059)
*Mrinal Kanti Giri,Shih Tai-Ming,Pinaki Sengupta,Pochung Chen*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 在失谐（detuned）准周期梯子（quasiperiodic ladder）中，仅对一条腿施加交错失谐即可通过腿间杂化（inter-leg hybridization）在另一条腿上诱导局域化（localization），且随失谐继续增大出现再入退局域（re-entrant delocalization）。该现象在 IBM 量子硬件上以动力学特征得到验证，表明腿选择性失谐可作为可编程的局域化调控手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探索无需直接改动准周期势（quasiperiodic potential）本身即可诱导或逆转局域化的机制，即通过耦合系统中邻近腿的失谐实现邻近诱导局域化（proximity-induced localization）与再入退局域。

Method: 方法上采用失谐准周期梯子模型，在弱准周期区域对仅一条腿施加交错失谐（staggered detuning），借助腿间杂化影响相邻腿；以静态局域化诊断量（static localization diagnostics）刻画各区域，并在 IBM Quantum 硬件上测量波函数扩散的动力学特征。

Result: 结果表明，在未耦合的 Aubry-André 链（Aubry-André chains）原本处于扩展态的弱准周期区域，单腿交错失谐可在相邻腿诱导局域化；进一步增大失谐后，纯净链（pure chain）重新进入退局域区域，形成退局域—局域—退局域的序列。实验上观察到邻近局域区域中波函数扩散被强烈抑制，并在较大失谐下再入恢复。

Conclusion: 结论是腿选择性失谐（leg-selective detuning）可作为耦合准周期系统中诱导与逆转局域化的可编程控制旋钮（programmable control knob）。

Abstract: We demonstrate proximity-induced localization and re-entrant delocalization in a detuned quasiperiodic ladder. In the weak-quasiperiodic regime, where uncoupled Aubry-André chains are extended, a staggered detuning applied to only one leg induces localization in the neighboring leg through inter-leg hybridization. With further increasing detuning, the pure chain re-enters a delocalized regime, producing a delocalized-localized-delocalized sequence without directly modifying its quasiperiodic potential. We identify these regimes using static localization diagnostics and demonstrate experimentally accessible dynamical signatures on IBM Quantum hardware: strongly suppressed wavefunction spreading in the proximity-localized regime and its re-entrant recovery at larger detuning. These results establish leg-selective detuning as a programmable control knob for inducing and reversing localization in coupled quasiperiodic systems.

</details>


### [208] [Second-order coherence properties of ultrafast polariton dynamics in plasmonic lattices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37142)
*Evgeny A. Mamonov,Lukas Freter,Sioneh Eyvazi,Elliot W. Lloyd,Päivi Törmä*

Main category: cond-mat.quant-gas

TL;DR: 该研究报告了等离激元晶格（plasmonic lattice）中极化激元（polariton）发射的高度相干态，其二阶相干函数（second-order coherence function）在 Γ 点与非 Γ 点辐射分别测量，偏离值 $g^{(2)}(τ=0)-1<10^{-4}$，并展示超快（>1 THz）动力学，可解释为亚 50 fs 热化的 BEC，或具有振动自由度的超辐射（superradiance）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强耦合系统中极化激元激光与 BEC 的一阶相干性（first-order coherence）研究结论一致，但二阶相干性在不同系统中差异明显，即使在阈值以上仍出现偏离 1 的超泊松（super-Poissonian）涨落，尤其在非平衡（non-equilibrium）区域，需更深入理解光基凝聚体的发射统计与动力学。

Method: 在等离激元晶格中测量极化激元发射，分别针对 Γ 点与非 Γ 点辐射（基态与高能尾部）测定二阶相干函数，并考察其超快动力学时间尺度。

Result: 观测到高度相干态，$g^{(2)}(τ=0)-1<10^{-4}$；并证实动力学具有超快性质（>1 THz），对应亚 50 fs 的热化过程。

Conclusion: 该过程可解释为具有超快、亚 50 fs 热化的 BEC；作者同时提出另一种可能解释，即在发射体具备振动自由度的系统中的超辐射（superradiance）。

Abstract: The first-order coherence properties of strongly coupled systems supporting polariton lasing and Bose-Einstein condensation (BEC) have been thoroughly studied and show consistent results. In contrast, second-order coherence properties have been found to vary for different systems, and the second-order coherence function can deviate from the value of one, typical for atomic BEC, revealing super-Poissonian fluctuations even above the BEC/polariton lasing threshold. This calls for a deeper understanding of the emission statistics and dynamics in light-based condensates, especially in the non-equilibrium regime. Here we demonstrate a coherent state of polariton emission from a plasmonic lattice measured as the second-order coherence function value separately for $\mathrmΓ$- and non-$\mathrmΓ$-point radiation (ground state and high-energy tail). We observe a high degree of coherence with the value $g^{(2)}(τ=0)-1<10^{-4}$. We also demonstrate the ultrafast ($>$1 THz) nature of the dynamics. The process can be interpreted as a BEC with ultrafast, sub-50-fs thermalization, but we also suggest an alternative explanation as superradiance in a system where the emitters have vibrational degrees of freedom.

</details>


<div id='math.FA'></div>

# math.FA [[Back]](#toc)

### [209] [Stability of isometries of quantum states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37133)
*Sam Looi*

Main category: math.FA

TL;DR: 该论文证明了正迹类锥（positive trace-class cone）上满射近似等距（surjective approximate isometries）在迹度量（trace metric）与 Bures 度量下具有与维度无关的 Hyers-Ulam 稳定性（Hyers-Ulam stability），并用 Wigner 对称（Wigner symmetry）一致逼近，误差分别为 9ε/2 与 2√2ε；同时在无穷维中构造了反例，说明态空间上不存在独立于维度的稳定性模数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于考察近似等距能否被精确对称（Wigner 对称）稳定逼近，并厘清该稳定性是否依赖于空间维度；此前对量子态空间上 Hyers-Ulam 稳定性的维度依赖性尚不明确。

Method: 方法上，作者在正迹类锥上针对迹度量与 Bures 度量分别建立定量逼近定理：设 ε≥0 为保距加性误差的一致上界，构造 Wigner 对称（酉或反酉共轭，unitary or antiunitary conjugation）作为逼近；并针对固定零点的映射与 δ-满射（δ-surjectivity）假设给出改进界；此外通过无穷维构造反例。

Result: 主要结果：任意此类近似等距可被 Wigner 对称一致逼近，迹度量误差至多 9ε/2、Bures 度量误差至多 2√2ε，即使该映射不固定零点；对固定零点的映射，在 δ-满射假设下迹界改进为 3ε，且与 δ 无关。无穷维中存在全态空间（含混合态）的双射，其两种度量下的畸变（distortion）趋于零，却与每个 Wigner 对称保持固定的一致距离。态空间上稳定性在每个固定有限维成立，模数依赖维度并随 ε→0 趋于零，但不存在独立于维度的此类模数。

Conclusion: 结论：正迹类锥上的 Hyers-Ulam 稳定性是维度无关的，并可给出显式误差常数；但态空间的对应稳定性本质上依赖维度，无穷维情形下无法保证接近 Wigner 对称，从而刻画了稳定性的适用边界。


Abstract: We prove dimension-independent Hyers-Ulam stability for surjective approximate isometries of the positive trace-class cone in both the trace and Bures metrics. Here $\varepsilon\geq0$ is a uniform upper bound on the additive error in preserving distances. Such an approximate isometry is uniformly approximated by a Wigner symmetry (a unitary or antiunitary conjugation), with error at most $9\varepsilon/2$ in the trace metric and $2\sqrt2\varepsilon$ in the Bures metric, even if it does not fix zero. For maps fixing zero, the trace bound improves to $3\varepsilon$ even under the hypothesis of $δ$-surjectivity, independently of $δ$. In infinite dimensions, we construct bijections of the full state space, including mixed states, whose distortions tend to zero in both metrics but which stay a fixed uniform distance from every Wigner symmetry. For state spaces, stability holds in each fixed finite dimension with a dimension-dependent modulus tending to zero as $\varepsilon\to0$, but no such modulus can be chosen independently of dimension.

</details>


<div id='physics.soc-ph'></div>

# physics.soc-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [210] [Multiscale Reconstruction of Weighted Networks from Coarse-Grained Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37957)
*Mattia Marzi,Frank P. Pijpers,Diego Garlaschelli*

Main category: physics.soc-ph

TL;DR: 该研究提出一个基于多尺度加权网络（multiscale weighted networks）模型的重建框架，可在任意聚合层级间迁移参数，从而仅凭粗粒度信息推断细尺度网络结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统网络重建通常在约束可观测的同一分辨率层级进行，但当仅有粗粒度（coarse-grained）信息而相关过程发生在更细尺度时，该方法失效，因而需要一个能跨聚合层级重建的框架。

Method: 采用配套论文中的多尺度加权网络模型，将其转化为概率框架（probabilistic framework），用于跨任意聚合层级重建加权网络；模型在粗粒化（coarse-graining）下保持函数形式不变，使在可观测聚合层标定的全局参数可迁移至细层而无需重新拟合。

Result: 在国际贸易网络（International Trade Network，国家聚合为地理宏观区域）与荷兰生产网络（Dutch production network，跨行业分类层级）两个实证系统中测试。尽管相较直接在同尺度上标定的先进加权重建模型存在信息劣势，本方法仍高精度恢复细尺度二值结构，提升了精确率（precision）、特异性（specificity）、准确率（accuracy）与最大度误差（maximum degree-error）指标，敏感度（sensitivity）几乎相当。

Conclusion: 结果表明显式多尺度重建方法可在信息受限条件下有效推断细尺度网络，并在地理与行业两类聚合机制下均优于在目标分辨率直接标定的方法。

Abstract: Network reconstruction from partial information is usually performed at the same resolution level at which constraints are observable. This becomes problematic when only coarse-grained information is available, while the relevant process occurs at a finer scale. Here we employ the multiscale model of weighted networks introduced in a companion paper and turn it into a probabilistic framework for reconstructing weighted networks across arbitrary aggregation levels. The model is built to preserve its functional form under coarse-graining, so that global parameters calibrated on an observable aggregate layer can be transferred to finer layers without refitting. We test the method on two empirical systems with different aggregation mechanisms. In the International Trade Network, countries are aggregated into geographic macro-regions and the observed coarse-grained layer is used to infer the underlying country-level network. In the Dutch production network, sectoral flows are reconstructed across the hierarchical industrial classification, using coarser sectoral layers to infer finer ones. In both geographical and sectoral settings, we benchmark our genuinely multiscale reconstruction method against a state-of-the-art weighted reconstruction model calibrated directly at the target resolution, and therefore using additional information available at the same (finer) scale at which performance is evaluated. Remarkably, despite this informational disadvantage, our method recovers the fine-scale binary structure with high accuracy, improving precision, specificity, accuracy and maximum degree-error diagnostics, while remaining nearly equivalent in sensitivity.

</details>


<div id='physics.comp-ph'></div>

# physics.comp-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [211] [Optimization Using Pathwise Algorithmic Derivatives of Electromagnetic Shower Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.07944)
*Max Aehle,Mihály Novák,Vassil Vassilev,Nicolas R. Gauger,Lukas Heinrich,Michael Kagan,David Lange*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该工作将算法微分（algorithmic differentiation）集成到基于 G4HepEm 的电磁簇射模拟代码 HepEmShow 中，用于研究路径导数（pathwise derivative）对采样量能器中能量沉积关于束流与几何参数的导数的近似效果，并展示了其在随机梯度优化中的可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 蒙特卡洛（Monte-Carlo）模拟期望值的导数近似方法中，路径导数平均通常最易实施，且通过算法微分一般无需大幅手动分析或改写代码；但当程序存在不连续点时，路径导数估计量可能有偏（biased），从而削弱其应用价值。

Method: 将算法微分集成进基于 G4HepEm 的电磁簇射（electromagnetic shower）模拟代码 HepEmShow，并在采样量能器（sampling calorimeter）场景下，考察路径导数对能量沉积关于束流和几何参数的导数的近似程度；同时在一个模型示例中测试该梯度估计量用于随机梯度（stochastic gradient）优化。

Result: 当模拟中关闭多次散射（multiple scattering）时，路径导数的均值快速收敛到其期望值，且这些期望值与能量沉积的实际导数接近；此外，该新型梯度估计量在模型示例中被证明可用于随机梯度优化。

Conclusion: 算法微分可低成本地嵌入高能物理（high-energy physics）探测器模拟代码并产生可用的路径导数梯度；在无多次散射条件下该估计量偏差小、收敛快，并具备支撑随机梯度优化的潜力。

Abstract: Among the well-known methods to approximate derivatives of expectancies computed by Monte-Carlo simulations, averages of pathwise derivatives are often the easiest one to apply. Computing them via algorithmic differentiation typically does not require major manual analysis and rewriting of the code, even for very complex programs like simulations of particle-detector interactions in high-energy physics. However, the pathwise derivative estimator can be biased if there are discontinuities in the program, which may diminish its value for applications.
  This work integrates algorithmic differentiation into the electromagnetic shower simulation code HepEmShow based on G4HepEm, allowing us to study how well pathwise derivatives approximate derivatives of energy depositions in a sampling calorimeter with respect to parameters of the beam and geometry. We found that when multiple scattering is disabled in the simulation, means of pathwise derivatives converge quickly to their expected values, and these are close to the actual derivatives of the energy deposition. Additionally, we demonstrate the applicability of this novel gradient estimator for stochastic gradient-based optimization in a model example.

</details>


### [212] [Label-Permutation Symmetry and Stability in Oscillator Potts Machines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36716)
*E. M. Hasantha Ekanayake,Nikhil Shukla*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究振荡器 Potts 机（oscillator Potts machines, OPMs）的动力学特性，指出其作为振荡器伊辛机（oscillator Ising machines, OIMs）的多态扩展，存在二态情形所不具备的动力学性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPMs 提供了基于物理启发的能量最小化框架，用于求解由 q 态 Potts 哈密顿量（q-state Potts Hamiltonian）描述的组合优化问题；尽管可视为 OIMs 的多态推广，但其动力学是否与二态情形一致尚需考察。

Method: 针对近期提出的多谐波（multiharmonic）OPM 形式，推导了依赖构型的局部稳定性条件（configuration-dependent local-stability condition），并分析 Potts 标签置换对雅可比谱（Jacobian spectrum）的影响。

Result: 研究表明，具有相同 Potts 能量的构型未必在动力学上等价；特别地，当 q≥4 时，Potts 标签的置换可改变雅可比谱，进而改变使给定 Potts 构型局部稳定所需的正则化强度（regularization strength）。

Conclusion: 同一 Potts 解的不同相位编码（phase encodings）尽管具有相同的 Potts 能量，却可能呈现不同的局部稳定性；这说明 OPMs 的动力学性质不能简单由二态 OIM 类推。

Abstract: Oscillator Potts machines (OPMs) provide a physics-inspired, energy-minimization framework for solving combinatorial optimization problems described by the $q$-state Potts Hamiltonian. Although OPMs may be viewed as multistate extensions of oscillator Ising machines (OIMs), here, we show that they exhibit dynamical properties absent in the binary case. Specifically, we derive a configuration-dependent local-stability condition for a recently proposed multiharmonic OPM formulation and show that configurations with the same Potts energy need not be dynamically equivalent. In particular, for $q\geq4$, permutations of the Potts labels can alter the Jacobian spectrum and, consequently, the regularization strength required to locally stabilize a given Potts configuration. Thus, different phase encodings of the same Potts solution can exhibit different local stability properties despite having identical Potts energies.

</details>


### [213] [From Bellissard's gap labeling to atomistic quasiperiodic bilayers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36981)
*Thomas D. Kühne,Vladislav Efremkin,Emil Prodan*

Main category: physics.comp-ph

TL;DR: 本文在 Bellissard 的非周期固体协变观测框架下，探讨扭转双层体系中蝴蝶状能谱与体隙标签及拓扑响应的关联，并通过有限蜂窝双层的第二矩恒等式区分层间耦合贡献与体隙标签。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bellissard 框架以协变观测（covariant observables）描述非周期固体；本文试图厘清扭转双层中蝴蝶状能谱（butterfly-like spectra）如何与体隙标签（bulk gap labels）及拓扑响应（topological response）相联系，并明确原子尺度计算所需的条件。

Method: 采用有限蜂窝双层（finite honeycomb bilayer）作为示例，利用精确的第二矩恒等式（second-moment identity）分离层间耦合对谱方差的贡献；结合角度分辨谱、有限态计数、未耦合参考体系以及尺寸与展宽检验。

Result: 体隙（bulk gap）可给出谱投影算子（spectral projector）与积分态密度（integrated density of states），但拓扑响应还需对该投影算子作额外配对；示例显示耦合会重新分配谱权重，而并不赋予体隙标签。

Conclusion: 论文进一步给出原子尺度电子结构计算的要求，包括非正交轨道（nonorthogonal orbitals）、投影态密度（projected densities of states）以及相对滑移过程中随动的基函数，从而界定将原子尺度谱与体态计数及拓扑响应相衔接所需的步骤。

Abstract: Bellissard's framework uses covariant observables to describe aperiodic solids. We examine how butterfly-like spectra in twisted bilayers can be related to bulk gap labels and topological response. A bulk gap permits a spectral projector and an integrated density of states. A topological response requires an additional pairing of that projector. In an illustrative finite honeycomb bilayer, an exact second-moment identity isolates the interlayer contribution to spectral variance. Angle-resolved spectra, finite state counts, an uncoupled reference, and size and broadening checks show how coupling redistributes spectral weight without assigning a bulk gap label. We then examine the requirements for atomistic electronic-structure calculations, including nonorthogonal orbitals, projected densities of states, and basis functions that move during relative sliding. The resulting framework specifies the steps needed to connect atomistic spectra with bulk state counting and topological response.

</details>


<div id='astro-ph.CO'></div>

# astro-ph.CO [[Back]](#toc)

### [214] [Current and Future Constraints on the Primordial Power Spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35964)
*Zachary Cheslog,Emily Finson,Amanda MacInnis,Neelima Sehgal,Niayesh Afshordi,Simran K. Nerval,Renée Hložek*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 通过放松轻遗迹种类数（$N_{\rm eff}$）与中微子质量之和（$\sum m_\nu$），原初标量功率谱（primordial scalar power spectrum）的约束轮廓被拓宽，使 Starobinsky 暴胀与双热大爆炸（Bi-thermal Big Bang）模型重新与数据一致；未来 CMB 实验将显著收紧对 $n_s$、$\alpha_s$ 及分箱原初功率谱的测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原初标量功率谱是探测早期宇宙物理（early-universe physics）的重要窗口，可检验广泛暴胀场景所预言的标量谱指数（scalar spectral index, $n_s$）、非零跑动（running, $\alpha_s$）等偏离纯幂律（pure power-law）的特征。现有六参数 $Λ$CDM 模型将若干关键模型排除，需检验这一排除结论对额外自由度的稳健性。

Method: 在九参数 $Λ{\rm CDM} + \alpha_s + N_{\rm eff} + \sum m_\nu$ 模型框架下，放宽轻遗迹种类数与中微子质量之和，并联合使用 Planck、ACT、SPT 的 CMB 数据（CMB-PAS）与 DESI DR2，对 $n_s$ 与 $\alpha_s$ 进行参数约束。此外开展对未来 CMB 设施的预报（forecast）分析，并更新 CMB-HD 似然（likelihood）与 Fisher 代码以支持 CLASS 与 CAMB。

Result: 在六参数 $Λ$CDM 中被约 95% 置信水平排除的 Starobinsky 暴胀与双热大爆炸模型，在九参数模型中与数据一致：CMB-PAS 结合 DESI DR2 给出 $n_s = 0.9758 \pm 0.0064$、$\alpha_s = 0.0080 \pm 0.0064$。CMB-PAS 将 $e^{-2\tau}\mathcal{P}(k = 0.2\,{\rm Mpc}^{-1})$ 约束至 0.5%，SO 类与 CMB-HD 类巡天可收紧至 0.1%；CMB-HD 还可将测量延伸至 $k = 30\,{\rm Mpc}^{-1}$ 等更小尺度，约束精度约一个数量级（factor of ten）以内。

Conclusion: 原初功率谱相关模型的排除结论对 $N_{\rm eff}$ 与 $\sum m_\nu$ 自由度敏感，放宽这些参数可显著拓宽 $n_s$ 的误差轮廓，使部分早期宇宙模型摆脱被排除状态。未来 CMB 设施将在 $n_s$、$\alpha_s$ 及分箱原初功率谱 $\mathcal{P}(k)$ 上提供大幅改进的约束，相关预报代码与公开工具已同步更新。

Abstract: The primordial scalar power spectrum provides a powerful window onto early-universe physics, including a broad class of inflationary scenarios. These models often predict a characteristic scalar spectral index $(n_s)$, nonzero running $(α_s)$, or more general deviations from a pure power-law form. We find that freeing the number of light relic species $(N_{\rm eff})$ and the sum of the neutrino masses $(\sum m_ν)$ broadens the $n_s$ error contours enough that key early-Universe models are no longer excluded. In particular, while both Starobinsky inflation and the Bi-thermal Big Bang model are ruled out at about the 95% confidence level in the six-parameter $Λ$CDM model, we find that in the nine-parameter $Λ{\rm CDM} + α_s + N_{\rm eff} + \sum m_ν$ model, CMB data from Planck, ACT, and SPT (CMB-PAS) combined with DESI DR2 give $n_s = 0.9758 \pm 0.0064$ and $α_s = 0.0080 \pm 0.0064$, consistent with both models. Future CMB facilities will sharply improve constraints on $n_s$, $α_s$, and, more generally, the binned primordial power spectrum $\mathcal{P}(k)$. For example, CMB-PAS constrains $e^{-2τ}\mathcal{P}(k = 0.2\,{\rm Mpc}^{-1})$ to 0.5%, while SO-like and CMB-HD-like surveys would tighten this to 0.1%; CMB-HD would also extend this measurement to smaller, previously unprobed scales, constraining $e^{-2τ}\mathcal{P}(k = 30\,{\rm Mpc}^{-1})$ to within a factor of ten. We release our forecast code and update the public CMB-HD likelihood and Fisher codes to support CLASS in addition to CAMB.

</details>


### [215] [Carving out the Multifield Cosmological Collider Landscape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37570)
*Denis Werth*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该论文指出，在具有大量场（large number of fields）的暴胀（inflation）模型中，宇宙对撞机信号（cosmological collider signals）在双谱（bispectrum）压缩极限下会普遍表现为近局域（almost-local）形状，这是极值统计（extreme value statistics）与随机矩阵普适性（random-matrix universality）的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 具体紫外嵌入（ultraviolet embeddings）所预言的暴胀通常涉及大量场，因此需要理解这类多场情形下宇宙学关联函数（cosmological correlators）的软极限行为，并判断增强的压缩极限信号是否为普遍现象而非精细调节（fine-tuned）结果。

Method: 构造曲率扰动（curvature perturbation）与 N_f 个额外场之间最一般的破boost混合（boost-breaking mixings），并提取决定宇宙学关联函数软极限的晚期标度维度（scaling dimensions）。

Result: 当场数较少时，增强的压缩极限（可能带振荡）属于精细调节信号；而当 N_f 很大（约100）时，混合普遍将标度维度推向幺正边界（unitarity boundary）。

Conclusion: 大 N_f 下标度维度趋向幺正边界是极值统计与随机矩阵普适性的直接结果，从而将近局域双谱形状转变为对几乎所有质量和混合都成立的普适预言。

Abstract: We show that inflation with a large number of fields, as predicted by concrete ultraviolet embeddings, generically produces almost-local cosmological collider signals in the bispectrum squeezed limit. We construct the most general boost-breaking mixings between the curvature perturbation and $N_{\rm f}$ additional fields, and extract the late-time scaling dimensions that dictate the soft limits of cosmological correlators. For a handful of fields, an enhanced squeezed limit (with potential oscillations) is a fine-tuned signal. At large $N_{\rm f}\sim100$, however, mixings generically drive the scaling dimensions toward the unitarity boundary. We show that this is a direct consequence of extreme value statistics and random-matrix universality, turning an almost-local bispectrum shape into a generic prediction for almost any masses and mixings.

</details>


### [216] [Gravitational-wave dark sirens as astrophysical probes: inferring galaxy-merger relations and identifying individual hosts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38110)
*Alessandro Agapito,Michele Mancarella*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 该工作将引力波“暗汽笛”（dark siren）技术从宇宙学工具拓展为天体物理探针，在单一层级框架内同时推断致密双星在星系中的分布规律与个别事件宿主星系身份。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 以往暗汽笛方法仅把引力波事件与星系表的统计关联用于宇宙学参数测量，而该关联本身所承载的天体物理信息——致密双星如何分布于星系、以及具体宿主星系的身份——长期未被利用。

Method: 构建单一层级贝叶斯框架（hierarchical framework），同时推断星系光度权重（galaxy luminosity weighting）与宿主身份，将众多潜在宿主压缩为按概率排序的紧凑目标列表，作为后续观测的决策工具。

Result: 对 GW170817 进行盲测分析，该方法将 NGC 4993 恢复为排名最高的宿主星系，并对星系光度权重给出约束，温和偏向非均匀的宿主权重；反之，若固定星系权重，则会降低宿主识别的准确性，甚至倾向于错误的宿主。

Conclusion: 该框架将暗汽笛重新定位为探测电磁暗弱的致密双星合并天体物理的潜在独特探针（unique probe）。

Abstract: Gravitational-wave (GW) ``dark siren'' techniques, namely the statistical association of GW events with a galaxy catalog, have so far been used as a cosmological tool. Yet this association carries distinctive astrophysical information that remains unused---specifically, how compact binaries populate galaxies and the identities of individual hosts. In this work, we turn the dark-siren program into an astrophysical probe by inferring both simultaneously within a single hierarchical framework. The method reduces the many potential hosts to a compact, probabilistically ranked target list, providing a direct follow-up decision tool. Applied blindly to GW170817, it recovers NGC~4993 as the highest-ranked host and constrains the galaxy luminosity weighting, mildly favoring non-uniform host weights. Fixing the galaxy weighting can instead degrade host identification and even favor the wrong host. This framework recasts dark sirens as a potentially unique probe of the astrophysics of electromagnetically dark compact-binary mergers.

</details>


<div id='nlin.SI'></div>

# nlin.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [217] [Generalized Charlier Recurrence Coefficients: Hankel Formulas, Laguerre--Freud Dynamics, and Painlevé V Connections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35895)
*Mahouton Norbert Hounkonnou*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 该论文研究离散权重 ρ_μ(k)=μ^k/(k!)^2 所对应的广义 Charlier 多项式（generalized Charlier polynomials）的首一递推系数（monic recurrence coefficients）β_n、γ_n，给出互补矩（moment）、行列式（determinant）、离散动力（discrete dynamical）与连续可积（continuous integrable）四种描述，并进行数值交叉验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 广义 Charlier 多项式递推系数的刻画在正交多项式理论中具有基础意义，但需要统一不同框架（矩/行列式、动力系统、可积系统）下的规范化与相互联系。该工作旨在为相应权重建立自洽的规范化并实现跨方法验证。
