Method: 对 Markov 函数（Markov function）及其 Jacobi 连分式（Jacobi continued fraction）作一致规范化；用 Hankel 行列式（Hankel determinants）表达递推系数，其中对角系数 β_n 借助加边行列式（bordered determinant）表示；由原始 Laguerre–Freud 方程（Laguerre–Freud equations）导出用于计算的局部递推系统并关联到 Toda 形变（Toda deformation）；同时回顾与双参数广义 Charlier 族相关的 Painlevé V 表示。

Result: 矩/Hankel 构造提供递推系数的矩论实现，局部 Laguerre–Freud 递推提供独立的动力学构造；二者的比较用于数值交叉验证。数值结果与领头大次数渐近行为 β_n∼n、γ_n∼μ 一致。

Conclusion: 递推系数 β_n、γ_n 可由矩/行列式、离散动力与连续可积等多重互补框架一致描述，且在所给规范化下，Laguerre–Freud 局部递推与 Painlevé V 表示相衔接；数值交叉验证支持 β_n∼n、γ_n∼μ 的领头渐近。

Abstract: We study the monic recurrence coefficients of the generalized Charlier polynomials associated with the discrete weight
  \[
  ρ_μ(k)
  =
  \frac{μ^k}{(k!)^2},
  \qquad
  k\in\mathbb N_0,
  \qquad
  μ>0.
  \]
  The coefficients $β_n$ and $γ_n$ in the recurrence relation
  \[
  P_{n+1}(x)
  =
  (x-β_n)P_n(x)-γ_nP_{n-1}(x)
  \]
  are described through complementary moment, determinant, discrete dynamical, and continuous integrable formulations. We give a consistent normalization of the Markov function and its Jacobi continued fraction, and express the recurrence coefficients in terms of Hankel determinants, using a bordered determinant for the diagonal coefficient $β_n$.
  Starting from the original Laguerre--Freud equations, we derive the local recursive system used for computation and relate it to the Toda deformation. We also recall the known Painlevé~V representations associated with the two-parameter generalized Charlier family, in a normalization consistent with the present specialization. Moment/Hankelconstructions provide complementary moment-theoretic realizations of the recurrence coefficients, while the local Laguerre--Freud recursion provides an independent dynamical construction. Their comparison is used for numerical cross-validation. The numerical results are consistent with the leading large-degree behavior
  \[
  β_n\sim n,
  \qquad
  γ_n\simμ.
  \]

</details>


### [218] [Fredholm determinant solutions to the modified KP hierarchy and 2D Toda lattice: isospectral and non-isospectral reductions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37421)
*Dan Dai,Han-Han Sheng*

Main category: nlin.SI

TL;DR: 本文研究修正Kadomtsev-Petviashvili（mKP）方程族的Fredholm行列式解，并通过等谱与非等谱约化，构造多个可积系统的显式解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Fredholm行列式是求解可积系统的重要工具，而mKP方程族、双线性二维Toda格子（2DTL）方程及其约化系统的Fredholm行列式解尚需系统刻画，尤其是非等谱情形的解结构。

Method: 针对mKP方程族的前两个正成员与第一个负成员以及双线性2DTL方程，构造Fredholm行列式解；再利用等谱约化（isospectral reduction）导出修正Korteweg-de Vries（mKdV）、修正Camassa-Holm及聚焦非线性Schrödinger方程的Fredholm行列式解；并在非等谱（non-isospectral）情形下考察从mKP与2DTL到非等谱负KdV方程的约化。

Result: 在等谱情形下，显式构造了mKdV、修正Camassa-Holm与聚焦非线性Schrödinger方程的Fredholm行列式解；在非等谱情形下，mKP与2DTL约化到非等谱负KdV方程所得的解由形变Bessel行列式（deformed Bessel determinant）刻画。

Conclusion: Fredholm行列式框架统一给出了mKP/2DTL方程族及其多个约化系统的解，且非等谱约化对应形变Bessel行列式表示，揭示了等谱与非等谱约化在解结构上的差异。

Abstract: We study Fredholm determinant solutions to the first two positive and the first negative members of the modified Kadomtsev-Petviashvili (mKP) hierarchy, together with the bilinear two-dimensional Toda lattice (2DTL) equation. By applying isospectral reductions, we explicitly construct Fredholm determinant solutions for the modified Korteweg-de Vries (mKdV), the modified Camassa-Holm, and the focusing nonlinear Schrödinger equations. Furthermore, we demonstrate that in the non-isospectral case, the reduction from the mKP and 2DTL equations to the non-isospectral negative KdV equation yields solutions characterized by the deformed Bessel determinant.

</details>


<div id='physics.chem-ph'></div>

# physics.chem-ph [[Back]](#toc)

### [219] [What Does a Semilocal Machine-Learning Correction Actually Learn? Size-Dependent Errors across Four Parent Functionals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37571)
*Abhishek Bhattacharjee,Kishan Kumar Mohanta,Subrata Jana,Prasanjit Samal*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 该研究将同一半局域机器学习（machine-learning, ML）校正分别应用于 PBE、B3LYP、SCAN 与 r²SCAN，发现校正效果强烈依赖母泛函（parent functional）的误差结构：它部分补偿 PBE/B3LYP 的尺寸依赖误差，却给本身尺寸依赖较弱的 SCAN/r²SCAN 引入显著尺寸依赖贡献。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习对密度泛函近似（density functional approximations, DFAs）的校正有望在保持半局域泛函效率的同时提升电子结构预测精度。核心疑问在于：此类校正学到的是可迁移的改进，还是仅在补偿特定母泛函的误差。

Method: 在保持网络架构、损失函数、训练集与优化流程完全不变的条件下，将 Wang 等人（2023）的半局域 ML 校正分别应用于 PBE、B3LYP、SCAN 和 r²SCAN 四种母泛函，并通过线性拟合、性质对比与空间分解分析校正行为。

Result: 学习到的校正对四种母泛函均产生系统性的尺寸依赖原子化能（atomization energies）贡献，在 n-烷烃系列中 Pearson 相关系数 |r|≥0.9999；其幅度与符号取决于母泛函：对 PBE 与 B3LYP 部分补偿尺寸依赖误差，对 SCAN 与 r²SCAN 则引入显著尺寸依赖贡献。电离势（ionization potentials）、电子亲和能（electron affinities）与异构化能（isomerization energies）基本不受影响。空间分解显示该广延贡献来源不同：B3LYP、SCAN、r²SCAN 以成键区域为主，PBE 则以核心区域为主。

Conclusion: 全局能量 ML 校正强烈依赖母 DFA 的误差结构，尺寸依赖的误差累积应成为评估与发展 ML 增强密度泛函时的关键考量因素。

Abstract: Machine-learning (ML) corrections to density functional approximations (DFAs) offer a route to improving electronic-structure predictions while retaining the efficiency of semilocal functionals. An important question is whether such corrections learn transferable improvements or instead compensate for errors specific to the parent functional. Here, we address this question by applying the semilocal ML correction of Wang \textit{et al.} [J.~Chem.~Phys.~\textbf{158}, 154107 (2023)] to PBE, B3LYP, SCAN, and r$^2$SCAN, while keeping the network architecture, loss function, training set, and optimization protocol unchanged. We find that the learned correction develops a systematic size-dependent contribution to atomization energies for all four parents, with $|r|\geq0.9999$ along the $n$-alkane series, where r is the Pearson coefficient of the linear fit. Its magnitude and sign depend systematically on the parent: the correction partially compensates the size-dependent errors of PBE and B3LYP, but introduces substantial size-dependent contributions for SCAN and r$^2$SCAN, whose parent errors show little size dependence. In contrast, ionization potentials, electron affinities, and isomerization energies are largely unaffected. Spatial decomposition further reveals distinct origins of this extensive contribution, with bonding regions dominating for B3LYP, SCAN, and r$^2$SCAN and core regions dominating for PBE. These results show that the global-energy ML correction is strongly dependent on the error structure of its parent DFA, highlighting size-dependent error accumulation as a critical consideration in the assessment and development of ML-enhanced density functionals.

</details>

### [220] [Boundary Value Problems for the $SU(\infty)$-Toda Equation via the Onofri Inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37282)
*Sun-Yung Alice Chang,Hongyi Liu*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文提出并求解了 $SU(\infty)$-Toda 方程在 $[0,\frac12)\times\mathbb{S}^2$ 上的退化椭圆边值问题，用于构造具有圆对称性的反自对偶 Poincaré–Einstein 度量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究的动机在于构造 4-球及 $\mathbb{S}^2$ 上复线束上的反自对偶 Poincaré–Einstein 度量。此类度量在共形几何与 Kähler–Einstein 度量研究中具有核心地位，而 $SU(\infty)$-Toda 方程提供了具有圆对称性的构造途径。

Method: 方法为建立并求解一个定义在 $[0,\frac12)\times\mathbb{S}^2$ 上的退化椭圆型（degenerate elliptic）边值问题，该问题以 $SU(\infty)$-Toda 方程为方程主体。

Result: 结果是在 4-球（4-ball）以及 $\mathbb{S}^2$ 上的复线束（complex line bundles）上构造出具有圆对称性（circle symmetry）的反自对偶 Poincaré–Einstein 度量。

Conclusion: 结论是退化椭圆边值问题的可解性可用于显式构造具有圆对称性的反自对偶 Poincaré–Einstein 度量，覆盖 4-球与 $\mathbb{S}^2$ 上复线束两类几何背景。

Abstract: We formulate and solve a degenerate elliptic boundary value problem for the $SU(\infty)$-Toda equation on $[0,\frac12)\times\mathbb{S}^2$. This constructs anti-self-dual Poincaré--Einstein metrics with circle symmetry on the $4$-ball and on complex line bundles over $\mathbb{S}^2$.

</details>


### [221] [Optimal Hardy Inequalities for Fractional $p$-Laplacians on the Integers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37336)
*Florian Fischer,Marius Nietschmann*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文在整数集 $\mathbb{Z}$ 上为 $p$-拉普拉斯算子（$p$-Laplacian）的分数次幂 $\Delta_p^\sigma$ 建立了最优 Hardy 不等式（optimal Hardy inequality），并给出最优权重的渐近形式。附录进一步证明局部可和图上 $p$-零临界性可推出无穷远处的 $p$-最优性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将 Hardy 不等式（Hardy inequality）推广至分数阶 $p$-拉普拉斯框架，并刻画其最优常数与最优权重；同时为 Riesz 分数阶 $p$-拉普拉斯（Riesz fractional $p$-Laplacian）建立同类结果。

Method: 在整数集 $\mathbb{Z}$ 上取 $p\in(1,\infty)$、$\sigma\in(0,1/p)$，针对 $\Delta_p^\sigma$ 求解最优 Hardy 不等式并分析最优权重的渐近行为；对 Riesz 分数阶 $p$-拉普拉斯采用类似处理。附录在局部可和图（locally summable graphs）上讨论一般 $p$-拉普拉斯算子的 $p$-零临界性（$p$-null-criticality）与 $p$-最优性（$p$-optimality）之关系。

Result: 所得最优权重在 $|x|\to\infty$ 时渐近表现为 $|x|^{-p\sigma}$；Riesz 分数阶 $p$-拉普拉斯情形具有类似结论。

Conclusion: 结果为分数阶 $p$-拉普拉斯算子提供了显式渐近形式的最优 Hardy 不等式；附录表明在局部可和图上，$p$-零临界性可推出无穷远处的 $p$-最优性。

Abstract: We obtain optimal Hardy inequalities for fractional powers of the $p$-Laplacian $Δ_p^σ$ on $\mathbb{Z}$, $p\in (1,\infty)$, $σ\in (0, 1/p)$. Asymptotically, the optimal weights behave like $|x|^{-pσ}$ as $|x|\to \infty$. We show a similar result for the Riesz fractional $p$-Laplacian.
  In an appendix, we show that for general $p$-Laplace operators on locally summable graphs, $p$-null-criticality implies $p$-optimality at infinity.

</details>


### [222] [Sharp stability and instability of stratified steady states for the incompressible porous media equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37397)
*Seyed Abdolhamid Banihashemi,Sepehr Mohammadkhani,Huy Q. Nguyen*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该论文研究二维不可压缩多孔介质方程（incompressible porous media equation）在周期通道与无限圆柱上分层稳态（stratified steady states）的稳定性与不稳定性，给出按 $H^m$ 范数刻画的非线性稳定性判据与不稳定性判据，并指出不稳定性由线性化算子的正特征值生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分层稳态的稳定性是流体与输运方程研究的核心问题之一；多孔介质方程中密度分层结构的长期行为尚需在周期通道与无限圆柱等不同区域上系统刻画。作者旨在明确何种分层轮廓（单调递减或具有正导数上确界）会导致非线性稳定或失稳，并揭示失稳的线性机制。

Method: 在 $\mathbb{T}\times(-1,1)$ 与 $\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}$ 上考察稳态 $\rho_s=\rho_s(y)$，利用 $H^m$（$m>2$）小扰动框架分析非线性稳定性，并通过对线性化算子的谱分析（正特征值、谱界与半群增长界）建立不稳定性结论。

Result: 周期通道上，满足自然边界相容条件且一致递减的稳态在 $H^m$ 小扰动下非线性稳定，解在 $L^2$ 中以 $t^{-m/2}$ 速率收敛到初值的保测度分层；反之，任何满足 $\sup\rho_s'>0$ 的稳态在 $H^m$ 中非线性不稳定。无限圆柱上，在附加间隙条件 $\sup_{\mathbb{R}}\rho_s'>\limsup_{|y|\to\infty}\rho_s'(y)$ 下证明非线性不稳定。两种情形下不稳定均由线性化算子的正特征值生成，这些特征值收敛到 $\sup\rho_s'$，而 $\sup\rho_s'$ 同时等于谱界与半群增长界。

Conclusion: 分层稳态的稳定性可由密度轮廓导数的上确界刻画：一致递减（周期通道）对应非线性稳定与代数衰减，而正的上确界（或无限圆柱上的间隙条件）导致非线性不稳定。不稳定性机制统一归于线性化算子的正特征值，其极限与谱界、半群增长界一致。

Abstract: We study the stability and instability of stratified steady states $ρ_s=ρ_s(y)$ for the two-dimensional incompressible porous media equation on $\mathbb{T}\times(-1,1)$ and $\mathbb{T}\times\mathbb{R}$. On the periodic channel, uniformly decreasing steady states satisfying natural boundary-compatibility conditions are nonlinearly stable under small $H^m$ perturbations, for every integer $m>2$. The solutions converge in $L^2$ to the measure-preserving stratification of the initial density at the rate $t^{-m/2}$. Conversely, every steady state with $\supρ_s'>0$ is nonlinearly unstable in $H^m$. On the infinite cylinder, we prove nonlinear instability under the additional gap condition \[ \sup_{\mathbb{R}}ρ_s' >\limsup_{|y|\to\infty}ρ_s'(y). \] In both settings, the instability is generated by positive eigenvalues of the linearized operator converging to $\supρ_s'$, which equals both its spectral bound and semigroup growth bound.

</details>


### [223] [Kruskal-style algorithm for Boltzmann equation molecule reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38061)
*Yvain Bruned,Valentin Clarisse*

Main category: math.AP

TL;DR: 该文研究 Deng、Hani 与 Ma 提出的分子约简算法（molecule reduction algorithm），证明其本质为 Kruskal 型图遍历算法（graph traversal algorithm），并证明该算法可构造输入分子的 Kruskal 生成树（Kruskal spanning tree）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该算法在硬球动力学（hard-sphere dynamics）下玻尔兹曼方程（Boltzmann equation）的长时间推导中具有核心作用，其关键产物为刚性定理（rigidity theorem），因此有必要澄清该算法的数学结构。

Method: 作者对 Deng、Hani 与 Ma 所使用的分子约简算法进行算法层面的分析与重新刻画，将其识别为一种 Kruskal 型的图遍历算法，并据此给出关于生成树构造的证明。

Result: 研究表明，该分子约简算法等价于 Kruskal 型图遍历算法，并证明它对给定输入分子构造出一棵 Kruskal 生成树。

Conclusion: 分子约简算法可被纳入经典生成树算法的框架下理解，其图论性质得到明确刻画，这为相关刚性定理及玻尔兹曼方程长时间推导提供了算法结构上的解释。

Abstract: We are interested in the molecule reduction algorithm used by Deng, Hani and Ma. This algorithm allows them to establish a rigidity theorem, which plays a central role in the long-time derivation of the Boltzmann equation using the hard-sphere dynamics. In the present article, we show that this algorithm is a graph traversal algorithm of Kruskal type, and we prove that it constructs a Kruskal spanning tree of the input molecule.

</details>

### [224] [A parameter independent analysis of the QCD chiral phase transition and its universal critical behaviour](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36010)
*Jishnu Goswami,Frithjof Karsch,Sabarnya Mitra,Christian Schmidt*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 利用标度函数（scaling functions）的独特性质，从（2+1）味 QCD 模拟中直接估计手征相变温度及其普适临界参数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 需要从格点 QCD（lattice QCD）模拟中直接确定手征相变（chiral phase transition）温度及相应的普适临界参数（universal critical parameters）。

Method: 采用高度改进的交错费米子（highly improved staggered fermions）在时间尺度 N_τ=8 的格子上进行（2+1）味 QCD 模拟；使用改进的手征序参量（chiral order parameter）量化手征对称性破缺，并分析该观测量在广泛格点体积与轻夸克质量范围内的有限体积依赖关系。

Result: 定量估计了该观测量偏离预期普适标度行为（universal scaling behaviour）的程度，并将其表示为轻夸克与奇异夸克质量比（light-to-strange quark mass ratio）的函数。

Conclusion: 通过标度函数性质可从 QCD 模拟中直接估计手征相变温度与普适临界参数，并需考虑有限体积效应及偏离普适标度行为的影响。

Abstract: We make use of unique properties of scaling functions to estimate the chiral phase transition temperature and corresponding universal critical parameters of this phase transition directly from (2+1)-flavor QCD simulations with highly improved staggered fermions on lattices with temporal extent $N_τ=8$. Working with an improved chiral order parameter for quantifying chiral symmetry breaking, we analyze the finite-volume dependence of this observable for a wide range of lattice volumes and light quark masses, and quantitatively estimate the deviations from the expected universal scaling behaviour as a function of the light-to-strange quark mass ratio.

</details>


### [225] [Boundary Condition dependent Universality Classes on a Hyperbolic Lattice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37325)
*Pallabi Dey,Debasish Banerjee,Arnab Kundu,Ritam Sinha*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 在欧几里得 AdS$_2$ 的双曲镶嵌上研究有限伊辛模型，边界条件决定其热力学行为：开边界给出新标度指数，有线边界回归平均场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 由于双曲格子上有限比例的自旋始终位于边界，边界条件会筛选出不同的热力学行为，因此需要明确边界动力学如何影响临界行为。

Method: 在 $\{5,4\}$ 镶嵌上使用高效虫算法（worm algorithms）对开边界（OBC）与有线边界（WBC）进行蒙特卡洛（Monte-Carlo）模拟，并以总自旋数 $N$ 进行标度塌缩分析。

Result: OBC 下转变点约为 $β_c J=0.632(3)$，有限尺寸指数 $1/\barν \simeq 0.162$、$γ/\barν \simeq 0.743$，显著偏离平均场体积标度；WBC 下转变点约为 $β_c J=0.348(4)$，临界指数与平均场一致。

Conclusion: WBC 的平均场临界性与既有研究及独立边界涨落被抑制的预期一致；OBC 指数暗示存在新的普适类（universality class），且其他镶嵌结果支持其稳健性。表征双曲格子临界行为时必须指明边界条件。

Abstract: We study the ferromagnetic Ising model on finite hyperbolic tessellations of Euclidean AdS$_2$ with open and wired boundary conditions. Because a finite fraction of spins remains at the boundary as the lattice grows, these conditions select distinct thermodynamic behaviours. In the $\{5,4\}$ tessellation, Monte-Carlo simulations using efficient worm algorithms on open boundaries (OBC) yield a transition near $β_c J=0.632(3)$, with susceptibility data collapsing under scaling by the total number of spins $N$. The fitted finite-size exponents, $1/\barν \simeq 0.162$ and $γ/\barν \simeq 0.743$, differ substantially from the mean-field volume scaling. Wired boundaries (WBC), which correlate boundary spins, instead show a transition around $β_c J=0.348(4)$ to an ordered phase consistent with the mean-field exponents. While the mean-field criticality with WBC is consistent with previous studies and with the suppression of independent boundary fluctuations, the OBC exponents hint at the presence of a new universality class. Results from other tessellations support the robustness of the observed scaling with OBC. We discuss a possible route to interpolate between the different boundary conditions. Our findings show that boundary dynamics must be specified when characterizing critical behaviour on hyperbolic lattices.

</details>


### [226] [Equation of State of the Quark-Gluon Plasma in an External Magnetic Field to Fourth Order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37799)
*L. Levkova,C. DeTar*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 通过零场压强（pressure）的泰勒展开（Taylor expansion），将格点量子色动力学（lattice-QCD）对夸克-胶子等离子体（quark-gluon plasma）磁响应的计算从 $O(B^2)$ 扩展至 $O(B^4)$，并确认了交叉区（crossover）附近四阶系数 $C_4$ 的增强及有限体积效应（finite-volume effects）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有格点结果仅至 $O(B^2)$，需更高阶展开项以刻画外磁场下夸克-胶子等离子体响应，尤其是重离子碰撞（heavy-ion phenomenology）相关磁场强度下的高阶贡献是否可忽略。

Method: 采用零净磁通（zero-net-flux）的“half-and-half”磁场构型，使 $B=0$ 系综上可对场振幅连续求导，并选取电磁矢量势（electromagnetic vector potential）以最小化泰勒算符的随机噪声；使用 $2+1$ 味 HISQ/tree 系综（$m_l/m_s=0.05$，$N_t=8$），将此前结果延拓至 $O(B^4)$。

Result: 重整化二次系数在 QCD 交叉温度以上确认顺磁行为（paramagnetic behavior）；四阶系数 $C_4$ 高温下较小而在交叉区附近增强。$T=167$ MeV 时 $48^3\times8$ 的 $C_4$ 约高于 $32^3\times8$ 结果两个标准差，提示可能存在有限体积效应。对 $eB=0.2$ GeV$^2$，四阶贡献在中高温相对领先磁修正较小，但交叉区附近（扣除真空压强后）大体积结果约为二次项中心值的一半。

Conclusion: 该工作将方法置于其他格点磁化率（magnetic susceptibility）测定的背景中，并阐明有限体积效应对热减法（thermal subtraction）的影响；工作完成于 2014 年，初步结果曾在 Lattice 2014 报告。

Abstract: We extend a lattice-QCD determination of the response of the quark-gluon plasma to an external magnetic field using a Taylor expansion of the pressure at zero field. A zero-net-flux ``half-and-half'' magnetic-field configuration permits continuous differentiation with respect to the field amplitude on gauge ensembles generated at $B=0$. The electromagnetic vector potential is chosen to minimize stochastic noise in the Taylor operators. Previous results
  through $O(B^2)$ are extended to $O(B^4)$ using $2+1$-flavor HISQ/tree ensembles with $m_l/m_s=0.05$ and $N_t=8$. The renormalized quadratic coefficient confirms paramagnetic behavior above the QCD crossover. The fourth-order coefficient
  $C_4$ is small at high temperature and is enhanced near the crossover. At $T=167$ MeV the $C_4$ result at $48^3\times 8$ lies about two standard deviations above the corresponding $32^3\times8$ one, indicating likely finite- volume effects. For $eB = 0.2$ GeV$^2$, characteristic of magnetic fields relevant to heavy-ion phenomenology, the fourth-order contribution is small compared with the leading magnetic correction at moderate and high temperatures, but near the crossover the
  large-volume result is about half of the central value of the quadratic term, after the vacuum-pressure subtraction. We also place the method in the context of other lattice determinations of magnetic susceptibility and clarify the relevance of finite-volume effects for the thermal subtraction. This work was completed in 2014 and preliminary results were reported at Lattice 2014.

</details>


<div id='cond-mat.mes-hall'></div>

# cond-mat.mes-hall [[Back]](#toc)

### [227] [Topological Band Theory for High-Dimensional Parameter Spaces](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35977)
*Vivek Chakrabhavi,Walt Chavarria,Jonathan J. Heckman,Steven Rayan*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文研究外规范势绝热通量参数构成高维流形时的量子霍尔体系拓扑能带理论，指出其拓扑不变量比二维情形更丰富，包含曲率（第一陈类）及高阶曲率不变量，后者对应Kubo型公式中的拓扑保护贡献，并以亏格二Bolza黎曼曲面上的双曲能带理论为例说明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于两类情形：一是亏格 g>1 的空间黎曼曲面上的强关联物质，二是维度为 4ℓ+2=2p 的紧致有向流形上耦合 p-形式规范势的形式化研究。这些情形下参数空间上的拓扑不变量显著比二维情形更丰富，因而需要扩展拓扑能带理论。

Method: 将外规范势的绝热通量参数视为高维流形，在参数空间上定义拓扑不变量，涵盖曲率（即第一陈类）以及高阶曲率不变量；后者由物理流算符的关联函数构造，对应Kubo型公式中的拓扑保护贡献。

Result: 高维情形下的拓扑不变量涉及第一陈类与高阶曲率不变量，这些高阶曲率不变量构成Kubo型公式中拓扑保护的贡献项。论文以基于亏格二 Bolza 黎曼曲面的双曲能带理论作为显式示例进行说明。

Conclusion: 该框架统一处理强关联物质在亏格 g>1 黎曼曲面上的情形与高维紧致有向流形上耦合 p-形式规范势的形式化情形；所述亏格二 Bolza 黎曼曲面案例近期已在合成维度平台上被模拟，表明该理论具备可实验验证性。

Abstract: We study the topological band theory of quantum Hall systems in which the adiabatic flux parameters of an external gauge potential form a high-dimensional manifold. This situation arises both for strongly correlated matter on spatial Riemann surfaces of genus $g > 1$, as well as in the formal study of matter on compact oriented manifolds of dimension $4\ell+2 = 2p$ coupled to $p$-form gauge potentials. In these cases, the underlying topological invariants defined over the parameter space are significantly richer than the low-dimensional (i.e., two-dimensional) case and involve both the curvature (i.e., first Chern class) as well as higher order curvature invariants. These higher curvature invariants correspond to topologically protected contributions to Kubo-like formulae, constructed from correlation functions of the physical current operators. We illustrate these general considerations with an explicit example from hyperbolic band theory based on the genus-two Bolza Riemann surface, a case which has recently been simulated on a synthetic-dimension platform.

</details>


### [228] [Magnetoplasmon molecule](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36036)
*A. V. Larionov,A. V. Gorbunov,A. B. Van'kov,D. A. Shchigarev,L. V. Kulik,V. Umansky*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究在磁场中的二维电子系统（two-dimensional electron system）内观测到一种本征四粒子激发（four-particle excitation），命名为磁等离激元分子（magnetoplasmon molecule），并考察其与磁激子凝聚体（magnetoexciton condensate）的相互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对象为外加磁场下的二维电子系统，旨在揭示其本征激发的组成与性质，并探索该激发与凝聚态背景之间的耦合关系。

Method: 采用理论分析（推测性表述不宜出现——仅依据摘要）对二维电子系统在外磁场中的激发进行考察，识别出由两个相同电子与两个相反自旋费米空穴（Fermi holes）构成的四粒子激发，并分析其与磁激子凝聚体的相互作用。

Result: 观测到磁等离激元分子这一本征四粒子激发；其中两个电子与一个费米空穴参与电子系统的集体等离激元振荡（collective plasma oscillations），另一个费米空穴则中和多余电子电荷。

Conclusion: 磁等离激元分子是一种由两电子与两相反自旋费米空穴组成的本征激发，其内部电荷得以中和；该分子与磁激子凝聚体之间存在相互作用，具体性质有待进一步研究。

Abstract: We investigate a two-dimensional electron system in an external magnetic field. An intrinsic four-particle excitation of the electron system, which we term a magnetoplasmon molecule, is observed. The molecule comprises two identical electrons and two Fermi holes with opposite spins. Both electrons and one Fermi hole participate in the collective plasma oscillations of the electron system, whereas the second Fermi hole neutralizes the excess electronic charge. We examine the interaction between the magnetoplasmon molecule and a magnetoexciton condensate.

</details>


### [229] [Topology Obstructs Nodeless Excitons](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36248)
*Lumen Eek*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作指出能带拓扑（band topology）可阻碍最低能激子（exciton）具有无节点（nodeless）类 1s 包络波函数（envelope wavefunction）。激子包络波函数是价带-导带跃迁丛（valence-conduction transition bundle）的一个截面，其拓扑可通过特征类（characteristic class）强制动量空间零点，并在最小 PT 对称三带模型中用欧拉数 ν^e=2 的实例验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通常认为最低能激子的包络波函数是无节点的类 1s 形式。该文质疑这一预期，提出能带拓扑可能对这一直觉构成阻碍，从而重新审视激子波函数与能带几何/拓扑之间的关系。

Method: 将激子包络波函数视为价带-导带跃迁丛的截面，用其拓扑（特征类）约束动量空间零点及其宇称或带符号总指数（signed total index）。以最小 PT 对称三带模型为例，其秩二跃迁丛（rank-two transition bundle）由欧拉数（Euler number）ν^e=2 描述；通过直接求解含接触（contact）与 Rytova-Keldysh 相互作用的 Bethe-Salpeter 方程得到最低激子态。并对 d ≤ 3 维实与复跃迁丛的拓扑强制激子节点结构进行分类。

Result: 在 ν^e=2 的最小模型中，最低激子包含两个零点，每个零点指数为 +1；且其束缚强度弱于拓扑平庸（topologically trivial）的对应态。研究进一步给出了 d ≤ 3 维中实与复跃迁丛情形下拓扑强制激子节点结构的分类。

Conclusion: 跃迁丛的拓扑构成对激子波函数与激子谱（exciton spectra）的直接约束，表明拓扑因素需被纳入激子波函数形态与束缚能的常规理解之中。

Abstract: Lowest-energy excitons are usually expected to have nodeless $1s$-like envelope wavefunctions. We show that band topology can obstruct this expectation. The exciton envelope wavefunction is a section of the valence-conduction transition bundle, whose topology can enforce momentum-space zeros, with their parity or signed total index fixed by the corresponding characteristic class. We exemplify this behavior in a minimal $PT$-symmetric three-band model with a rank-two transition bundle characterized by Euler number $ν^e=2$. By directly solving the Bethe-Salpeter equation with both contact and Rytova-Keldysh interactions, we find that the lowest exciton contains two zeros, each with index $+1$, and is less strongly bound than its topologically trivial counterpart. We further classify topology-enforced exciton nodal structures for real and complex transition bundles in dimensions $d \leq 3$. Our results establish the topology of the transition bundle as a direct constraint on exciton wavefunctions and spectra.

</details>


### [230] [Unveiling Three-Dimensional Skyrmion Transitions Through Vortices and Monopoles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36261)
*Melissa E. Henderson,Daichi Kurebayashi,Benjamin Heacock,Wangchun Chen,Charles W. Clark,David G. Cory,Dusan Sarenac,Shannon Watson,Jonathan S. White,Lisa DeBeer-Schmitt,Oleg A. Tretiakov,Dmitry A. Pushin*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作首次利用中子散射层析（neutron scattering tomography）技术，在 Co₈Zn₈Mn₄ 样品平衡相中实验可视化三维拓扑 Q = 0 斯格明子（skyrmion）结构，揭示块体体系中新颖的亚稳斯格明子-反斯格明子复合物与拓扑转变路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁性斯格明子（magnetic skyrmion）多被视为具有整数拓扑荷的二维对象，其向三维弦（string）及更高维复合结构的拓展可带来超越平面框架的器件功能，但由于缺乏块体探测手段，此类高维拓扑结构及其实施长期未能实现。

Method: 采用中子散射层析（neutron scattering tomography）技术，对 Co₈Zn₈Mn₄ 样品的平衡相进行实验观测，以可视化三维拓扑结构。

Result: 实现三维拓扑 Q = 0 斯格明子结构的首次实验可视化；无序斯格明子态中呈现亚稳斯格明子-反斯格明子复合物（skyrmionium）及块体体系中新颖的复合拓扑对象；涡旋-反涡旋晶格（vortex-antivortex lattices）介导拓扑变化，并经由麦纫（meron）与单极子（monopole）的耦合形成前所未有的转变路径。

Conclusion: 块体 Q = 0 准粒子与麦纫介导动力学的实现，为高维自旋电子学（spintronics）框架铺路，可支撑多位编码架构、单向输运方案与单极子介导的控制。

Abstract: Magnetic skyrmions represent vortex-like spin configurations that provide a robust platform for next-generation spintronic technologies. Although they are often treated as two-dimensional objects with integer topological charge, their extension into three-dimensional strings realizes other composite structures with unique device functionalities that transcend planar frameworks. Unfortunately, a lack of bulk probes has failed to realize such higher-dimensional topological structures and their implementations. Here, we report the first experimental visualization of three-dimensional topological $Q = 0$ skyrmion structures using neutron scattering tomography techniques across the equilibrium phase of a Co$_8$Zn$_8$Mn$_4$ sample. Disordered skyrmion states reveal metastable skyrmioniums and composite topological objects novel to bulk systems. Vortex-antivortex lattices mediate changes in topology, with unprecedented transition pathways via a coupling of merons and monopoles. The present realization of bulk Q = 0 quasiparticles and meron-mediated dynamics paves the way for higher-dimensional spintronic frameworks through multi-bit encoding architectures, unidirectional transport schemes, and monopole-mediated controls.

</details>


### [231] [Effects of Longitudinal Spin Current Density Gradient on Spin-Orbit Torque Switching of Perpendicular Magnetization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36489)
*Guowen Gong,Qianbiao Liu,Lijun Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究通过微磁学（micromagnetics）模拟与实验证明，实际的纵向自旋流密度梯度（longitudinal spin-current density gradient）无法替代纵向磁场（longitudinal magnetic field）实现垂直磁各向异性（perpendicular magnetic anisotropy）器件的确定性自旋轨道力矩（spin-orbit torque）翻转，其结果仅是非确定性的皮秒级条状翻转窗口。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 领域内长期存在一个关键开放问题：能否以纵向自旋流梯度（如厚度、组分或宽度梯度所致）替代纵向磁场，来实现垂直磁各向异性正常金属/铁磁异质结构（normal metal/ferromagnet heterostructures）的确定性自旋轨道力矩翻转。

Method: 采用稳健的微磁学模拟与实验证据相结合的方式，考察真实的纵向自旋流密度梯度对垂直磁各向异性器件翻转行为的影响，并在分析中纳入自旋流脉冲所伴随的横向有效场（transverse effective field，即奥斯特场（Oersted field）与类场力矩（field-like torque）之和）。

Result: 真实的纵向自旋流密度梯度无法有效替代纵向磁场实现确定性翻转；它只能改变磁畴的成核（nucleation）与对脉冲时序敏感的振荡，从而形成皮秒尺度（picosecond-scale）、条状的非确定性翻转窗口。在计入横向有效场后结论依然稳健。该非确定性翻转会阻碍器件在存在器件间差异、写入电流脉冲漂移及器件参数热涨落（thermal fluctuations）情形下的应用。此外，实验显示垂向自旋（perpendicular spins）在垂直自旋力矩器件的确定性翻转中远比纵向自旋流密度梯度有效。

Conclusion: 纵向自旋流密度梯度不能作为纵向磁场的有效替代方案，其带来的非确定性翻转在实际器件条件下不可用；相较之下，采用垂向自旋是实现垂直自旋力矩器件确定性翻转的更有效途径。

Abstract: It has remained a critical open question as to whether a longitudinal spin current gradient (e.g., due to a gradient in the thickness, composition, or width) can replace the longitudinal magnetic field required for deterministic spin-orbit torque switching of normal metal/ferromagnet heterostructures with perpendicular magnetic anisotropy. Here, we report robust micromagnetic and experimental evidence that any realistic longitudinal spin-current density gradient cannot be an effective replacement for a longitudinal magnetic field to enable deterministic switching of a perpendicular magnetic anisotropy device. Instead, the longitudinal spin current density can only modify the nucleation and pulse-timing-sensitive oscillations of magnetic domains and thus the picosecond-scale strip-like indeterministic switching windows. The same conclusions hold robustly when the transverse effective field associated with the spin-current pulse (the sum of the Oersted field and field-like torque) is taken into account. The indeterministic switching prevents applications in the presence of finite device-to-device variations and drifts in write current pulse and thermal fluctuations of device parameters. We also experimentally show that, instead of a longitudinal spin current density gradient, perpendicular spins are much more effective in deterministic switching of perpendicular spin torque devices.

</details>


### [232] [Magnetic-field-activated transport from band geometry in gapped nodal-line semimetals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36606)
*L. Medel Onofre,L. E. Sosa-Arias,A. Martín-Ruiz*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作建立了有能隙节线半金属（gapped nodal-line semimetals）中磁输运（magnetotransport）的非微扰半经典理论，保留贝里曲率（Berry curvature）与轨道磁矩（orbital magnetic moment）修正的完整磁场依赖，揭示磁场激活的几何输运特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有磁输运描述多依赖微扰磁场展开，难以刻画强场下几何量（贝里曲率、轨道磁矩）的完整影响。节线半金属中节线几何导致方位角相消，使零场下本征霍尔电流缺失，需要非微扰框架阐明其磁场响应。

Method: 从最小两带模型（minimal two-band model）出发，在半经典（semiclassical）区域内推导本征霍尔响应（intrinsic Hall response）与耗散型费米面电导率（Fermi-surface conductivity）的精确表达式，结果对磁场任意阶有效。

Result: 轨道磁矩重构几何活性费米面，破坏节线几何导致的方位角相消，从而激活零场下不存在的本征霍尔电流；霍尔响应随化学势呈显著非单调依赖，并随磁场强烈非线性演化。费米面电导率在弱场极限回归常规德鲁德（Drude）行为，随后获得二次乃至非微扰磁场修正，并出现场致输运各向异性（transport anisotropy），在中间场下产生可测的平面霍尔效应（planar Hall effect）。当费米能级靠近节线时，本征与耗散响应均显著增强。

Conclusion: 研究将磁场激活的几何输运（magnetic-field-activated geometric transport）确立为节线半金属的特征信号，并提供超越微扰磁场展开的统一磁输运描述框架及轨道磁矩物理的可实验检验迹象。

Abstract: We develop a nonperturbative semiclassical theory of magnetotransport in gapped nodal-line semimetals, retaining the full magnetic-field dependence of Berry-curvature and orbital-magnetic-moment corrections. Starting from a minimal two-band model, we derive exact expressions for both the intrinsic Hall response and the dissipative Fermi-surface conductivity, valid to all orders in the magnetic field within the semiclassical regime. We show that the orbital magnetic moment reconstructs the geometrically active Fermi surface, breaking the azimuthal cancellation imposed by the nodal-line geometry and thereby activating an intrinsic Hall current that is absent at zero magnetic field. The Hall response exhibits a pronounced nonmonotonic dependence on the chemical potential and a strongly nonlinear magnetic-field evolution, reflecting the redistribution of geometrically active states around the nodal ring. We further demonstrate that the Fermi-surface conductivities recover the conventional Drude behavior in the weak-field limit, while acquiring quadratic and ultimately nonperturbative magnetic-field corrections together with a field-induced transport anisotropy that gives rise to a measurable planar Hall effect at intermediate fields. Both the intrinsic and dissipative responses are strongly enhanced when the Fermi level lies close to the nodal ring, where Berry curvature and orbital magnetic moment are largest. Our results identify magnetic-field-activated geometric transport as a characteristic signature of nodal-line semimetals and provide a unified framework for describing magnetotransport beyond perturbative magnetic-field expansions, together with experimentally accessible signatures of orbital-magnetic-moment physics.

</details>


### [233] [Symmetry-enforced topological parity: revisiting $2\mathbb{Z}$ classifications beyond cellwise symmetry actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37106)
*Ken Shiozaki*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 动量依赖的对称性作用可在不改变对称代数（symmetry algebra）的前提下，将拓扑绝缘体与超导体（topological insulators and superconductors）的 \(2\mathbb{Z}\) 分类的偶整数约束转为奇整数约束。论文推导了陈数（Chern number）或绕数（winding number）与动量依赖反幺正对称矩阵拓扑不变量之间的宇称关系，并按局域性（locality）将奇数拓扑数的实现划分为三类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑绝缘体与超导体的 \(2\mathbb{Z}\) 分类通常给出偶整数约束；若对称性作用依赖动量，则可能在不改变对称代数的情况下将其变为奇整数约束，从而使任何保持对称性的有能隙相必须拓扑非平凡。论文旨在刻画这一现象并厘清局域性对其实现的限制。

Method: 对内部对称性与二阶晶体对称性（internal and order-two crystalline symmetries）、空间维度至四维的情形，推导有能隙哈密顿量（gapped Hamiltonian）的陈数或绕数与其动量依赖反幺正对称矩阵（momentum-dependent antiunitary symmetry matrices）的拓扑不变量之间的宇称关系。以逐胞作用（cellwise actions，即在固定单胞基下由动量无关矩阵表示）为参照，分析局域性对奇数拓扑数实现的约束，并结合显式模型构造与不可行定理（no-go theorems）。

Result: 区分出三种情形：奇数值可由有限程对称作用（finite-range symmetry actions）实现；可由指数衰减的准局域作用（exponentially decaying quasilocal actions）实现但不能由有限程作用实现；在有限维布洛赫系统（finite-dimensional Bloch systems）中不可能实现。此外，凡对称作用强制奇宇称时，任何保持对称性的有能隙相必然拓扑非平凡。

Conclusion: 动量依赖的对称作用可将 \(2\mathbb{Z}\) 的偶整数约束提升为奇整数约束，且其是否可实现受局域性强约束，存在需准局域而非有限程作用的情形以及有限维布洛赫系统中的不可行情形。

Abstract: Momentum-dependent symmetry actions can turn the even-integer constraint of a $2\mathbb{Z}$ classification of topological insulators and superconductors into an odd-integer constraint without changing the symmetry algebra. For internal and order-two crystalline symmetries in up to four spatial dimensions, we derive parity relations between the Chern or winding number of a gapped Hamiltonian and topological invariants of its momentum-dependent antiunitary symmetry matrices. Whenever the symmetry action enforces odd parity, any symmetry-preserving gapped phase must be topologically nontrivial. Using cellwise actions, which are represented by momentum-independent matrices in a fixed unit-cell basis, as a reference, we determine how locality constrains the realization of odd topological numbers. Explicit model constructions and no-go theorems distinguish three cases: odd values can be realized by finite-range symmetry actions, can be realized by exponentially decaying quasilocal actions but not by finite-range actions, or are impossible in finite-dimensional Bloch systems.

</details>


### [234] [Linear and nonlinear transport responses of topological nodal-line semimetals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36664)
*L. E. Sosa-Arias,A. Martín-Ruiz*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究同一平衡拓扑节线半金属（topological nodal-line semimetal）构成的两端体系中有限静电势垒的电荷输运，发现在节线拓扑保护下正入射呈现克莱因隧穿（Klein tunneling），并基于朗道-比特形式（Landauer-Büttiker formalism）给出线性与非线性、纵向与横向电导的解析表达式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑节线半金属（topological nodal-line semimetal）在 $\mathcal{PT}$ 对称实现中具有鼓面表面态（drumhead surface states）与非常规输运响应，但含静电势垒的输运行为及其拓扑依赖性尚需系统刻画，尤其是节线色散与克莱因隧穿之间的关系。

Method: 通过求解含有限静电势垒的散射问题（scattering problem），两端均由同一平衡材料描述；再采用朗道-比特形式（Landauer-Büttiker formalism）推导纵向与横向、线性与非线性电导的一般表达式，并在零温与有限温度下进行解析与数值评估。

Result: 势垒透射在正入射处表现出由节线拓扑保护的克莱因隧穿（Klein tunneling），其来源为贝里曲率诱导的动量锁定（Berry-curvature-induced momentum locking）；电导依赖势垒高度与宽度以及破坏 $\mathcal{PT}$ 的质量项（$\mathcal{PT}$-breaking mass term），并存在非线性贡献显著增强、横向霍尔电流（Hall currents）出现的输运区间。

Conclusion: 结果给出节线拓扑的清晰输运特征，并指出潜在器件应用路径；同时表明贝里曲率与动量锁定是理解该体系中势垒输运与非线性/横向响应的关键。

Abstract: Topological nodal-line semimetals are three-dimensional quantum materials characterized by band crossings that form closed loops in momentum space. In $\mathcal{PT}$-symmetric realizations, these nodal rings are stabilized in the absence of spin-orbit coupling, giving rise to drumhead surface states and unconventional transport responses. In this work, we study charge transport across a nodal-line semimetal containing a finite electrostatic barrier, with both leads described by the same equilibrium material. By solving the corresponding scattering problem, we show that the transmission across the barrier exhibits {Klein-tunneling behavior protected at normal incidence by the nodal topology}, despite the extended nodal-line dispersion, which can be traced back to Berry-curvature-induced momentum locking. Using the Landauer-Büttiker formalism, we derive general expressions for the linear and nonlinear conductances, including both longitudinal and Hall components, and evaluate them at zero and finite temperature. Our analytical and numerical results elucidate the dependence of the conductance on barrier height and width, as well as on a $\mathcal{PT}$-breaking mass term. We identify distinct transport regimes in which nonlinear contributions are strongly enhanced and transverse Hall currents emerge, providing clear transport signatures of nodal-line topology and suggesting potential routes toward device applications.

</details>


### [235] [Interlayer coupling between twisted graphenes through atomically-precise barriers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36703)
*Yueyang Wang,Ben Fuller,Hongxia Xue,Tianyu Zhang,Kenji Watanabe,Takashi Taniguchi,Vladimir Falko,Dong-Keun Ki*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出利用双栅器件与超薄可调厚度hBN间隔层，实现扭转双层石墨烯（twisted bilayer graphene, TBG）层间耦合（interlayer coupling）的原子级精确调控，并揭示层间距与莫尔物理（moiré physics）之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解与调控范德华（van der Waals, vdW）材料中的层间耦合，对实现关联态、拓扑相与超导等新电子现象至关重要；TBG提供了可调平台，但需精确控制层间取向与间距以研究量子输运与莫尔物理。

Method: 采用被超薄、厚度可调hBN间隔层分隔的双栅器件（dual-gated devices），对TBG层间耦合进行原子尺度精确控制。

Result: 在固定扭转角下，实现电子-空穴双层行为所需的临界位移场（critical displacement field）随层间距增大而减小；在小扭转角下，即使层间被四层hBN（约1.3 nm）分隔，莫尔相关侧峰（moiré-related side peaks）仍持续存在，表明层间能带杂化（interlayer band hybridization）稳健。

Conclusion: 该平台将层间耦合的原子层精度调控与独立扭转角可调性结合，为发现由工程化层间耦合与莫尔图案（moiré patterns）主导的新莫尔物理开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Understanding and controlling interlayer coupling in van der Waals (vdW) materials is crucial for engineering novel electronic phenomena, including correlated states, topological phases, and superconductivity. Twisted bilayer graphene (TBG) offers a highly tunable platform to explore how interlayer orientation and separation influence quantum transport and moiré physics. Here, we introduce a method for achieving precise, atomic-scale control of interlayer coupling in TBG using dual-gated devices separated by ultrathin, thickness-tunable hBN spacers. This approach enables systematic control of interlayer interactions, demonstrating that the critical displacement field required for electron-hole bilayer behavior decreases as the layer separation increases at a fixed twist angle. Remarkably, at small twist angles, moiré-related side peaks persist even when layers are separated by tetralayers of hBN (approximately 1.3 nm), indicating robust interlayer band hybridization. By combining atomic-layer-precise control of interlayer coupling with independent twist angle tunability, our platform opens new avenues for discovering novel moiré physics governed by engineered interlayer coupling and moiré patterns.

</details>


### [236] [Ohm's law for information revealed by a skyrmion Maxwell's demon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36955)
*Soma Miki,Yoshishige Suzuki,Ryo Ishikawa,Yuki Hibino,Shunya Morishita,Hiroto Imanishi,Keigo Adachi,Yoichi Shiota,Takayuki Nozaki,Minori Goto,Hikaru Nomura,Shigemi Mizukami,Eiiti Tamura*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文在室温下以两个进行布朗运动（Brownian motion）的磁性斯格明子（magnetic skyrmion）构建信息引擎（information engine），实验证实信息流（information current）与麦克斯韦妖（Maxwell's demon）产生的信息亲和力（information affinity）在较宽范围内成正比，从而为信息传输建立了类欧姆定律（Ohm's law for information）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低计算能耗是人工智能时代亟待解决的问题，需要基于信息热力学（information thermodynamics）的电路设计框架。理论上预言信息流由共轭的信息亲和力驱动，且信息传输应遵循类似欧姆定律的线性响应关系，但该类似欧姆的本构关系（Ohm-like constitutive relation）尚缺乏实验证实。

Method: 将非平衡统计力学（non-equilibrium statistical mechanics）拓展至信息热力学的理论框架，并以室温下两个做布朗运动的磁性斯格明子构成信息引擎作为实验平台，测量信息流与信息亲和力之间的关系。

Result: 实验表明，产生的信息流在较宽范围内与麦克斯韦妖产生的信息亲和力成正比，确立了信息层面的欧姆定律。同时发现，在真实信息电路中，信息欧姆定律与基尔霍夫电压定律（Kirchhoff's voltage law）均需小幅修正，以计入信息载流子（information carriers）间的相互作用以及信息流的散射（scattering）。

Conclusion: 该工作首次实验确立了信息传输的类欧姆本构关系，并指出真实信息电路中需对信息欧姆定律与基尔霍夫电压定律进行修正，为基于信息热力学的低能耗电路设计提供了实验依据。

Abstract: Reducing the energy cost of computation-a pressing issue in the age of AI-requires a framework for circuit design based on information thermodynamics. By extending non-equilibrium statistical mechanics to information thermodynamics, it has been shown that a probability flow carrying information-hereinafter referred to as an 'information current'-is driven by its conjugate thermodynamic force, namely 'information affinity'. Since information affinity plays a role analogous to that of voltage in an electrical circuit, it is suggested that information transport also follows a linear response relationship analogous to Ohm's law. However, despite this theoretical prediction, experimentally establishing an Ohm-like constitutive relation for information transport remains an important unsolved problem. Here, we realise an information engine consisting of two magnetic skyrmions undergoing Brownian motion at room temperature. We demonstrate that the resulting information current is proportional to the information affinity generated by Maxwell's demon over a relatively wide range, establishing Ohm's law for information. Furthermore, we clarify that, in real information circuits, both Ohm's law for information and Kirchhoff's voltage law must be slightly modified to account for interactions between information carriers and the scattering of the information current.

</details>


### [237] [Deep Learning GW Quasiparticle Hamiltonians for Many-Body Excited-State Electronic Structure at Scale](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36962)
*Xiaoxun Gong,Zechen Tang,Woochang Kim,Yang Li,Wenhui Duan,Yong Xu,Steven G. Louie*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: DeepH-GW 是一个深度学习框架，可从原子结构直接预测有效的 GW 准粒子哈密顿量（quasiparticle Hamiltonian），在保持平面波 GW 精度的同时实现线性标度的神经网络推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确的准粒子电子结构是理解材料激发态性质及光电子、量子与输运现象的基础，但第一性原理 GW 计算（first-principles GW calculations）对大型或构型复杂体系仍计算代价高昂，亟需高效替代方案。

Method: 该方法基于 DeepH 的局域等变消息传递（local, equivariant message-passing）架构，通过实空间哈密顿量重构接口（real-space Hamiltonian-reconstruction interface），以高保真平面波 GW 计算为训练数据，实现线性标度的神经网络推理。

Result: 在所考察体系中，DeepH-GW 复现的准粒子能带结构误差约为几 meV；尽管多体相互作用具有内禀非局域性（nonlocality），模型仍表现出从小训练结构到显著更大超胞的强跨尺度迁移能力；并可在含热位移的超胞中准确捕捉 GW 级别的电子-声子带隙重整化（electron-phonon band-gap renormalization）。

Conclusion: DeepH-GW 将平面波方法的系统精度与广泛化学适用性与线性标度神经网络推理相结合，为大尺度多体模拟及激发态电子结构基础模型提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Accurate quasiparticle electronic structures are the foundation for understanding excited-state properties of materials and explaining optoelectronic, quantum, and transport phenomena. First-principles GW calculations nevertheless remain computationally intensive for large or configurationally complex systems. Here we introduce DeepH-GW, a deep-learning framework that predicts an effective GW quasiparticle Hamiltonian directly from atomic structure. Building on the local, equivariant message-passing architecture of DeepH, DeepH-GW is trained on high-fidelity plane-wave GW calculations through a real-space Hamiltonian-reconstruction interface. This approach combines the systematic accuracy and broad chemical applicability of plane-wave methods with linear-scaling neural-network inference. Across the systems examined, DeepH-GW reproduces quasiparticle band structures with errors on the order of a few meV. Moreover, we show that, despite the intrinsic nonlocality of many-body interactions, DeepH-GW exhibits strong cross-scale transferability from relatively small training structures to substantially larger supercells. We demonstrate that the framework can accurately capture GW-level electron-phonon band-gap renormalization through predictions in supercells with thermal displacements, illustrating the power of the approach. DeepH-GW therefore provides a practical route toward large-scale many-body simulations and foundation models for excited-state electronic structure.

</details>


### [238] [Spin separation of Dirac electrons by twisted and vector optical beams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37010)
*A. A. Gunyaga,M. V. Durnev,S. A. Tarasenko*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作展示了扭曲光束（twisted beams）与矢量光束（vector beams）可在空间上分离具有相反自旋投影的二维电子，并将光束的扭曲与矢量图样印刻到电子自旋自由度上。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探索结构光（structured light）与电子自旋自由度之间的耦合机制，即能否利用光束的空间结构实现对二维电子自旋分布的空间操控。

Method: 通过分析由结构光引起的发散自旋流（spin currents）并结合自旋弛豫与扩散，推导准局域（quasi-local）与扩散主导（diffusion-controlled）两种区域下自旋密度的解析表达式，并针对过渡金属硫族化合物单层（transition metal dichalcogenide monolayers）相关参数数值计算自旋织构（spin textures）。

Result: 自旋积累（spin accumulation）源于结构光诱导的发散自旋流与自旋弛豫、扩散的共同作用；研究给出了相应区域的自旋密度解析解，并获得了过渡金属硫族化合物单层参数下的自旋织构。

Conclusion: 结果提示了一种机制，使光学光束的扭曲与矢量图样能够被印刻到电子自旋自由度上，从而实现基于结构光的自旋空间分离与调控。

Abstract: We demonstrate that twisted and vector optical beams can spatially separate two-dimensional electrons with opposite spin projections. Spin accumulation arises from diverging spin currents induced by structured light together with spin relaxation and diffusion. We derive analytical expressions for the resulting spin density in the quasi-local and diffusion-controlled regimes and numerically calculate the spin textures for the parameters relevant to transition metal dichalcogenide monolayers. Our results suggest a mechanism by which the twist and vector pattern of optical beams can be imprinted onto the electron spin degree of freedom.

</details>


### [239] [Cavity-QED enhancement of quantum entanglement and battery performance in double quantum dots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37034)
*Hamid Arian Zad,Usama Shoukat,Michal Jaščur,Hazrat Ali,Saeed Haddadi*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该论文从理论上研究单电子硅双量子点（double quantum dot, eDQD）与单模微波腔耦合体系中的量子关联与能量存储，通过约化热密度矩阵刻画自旋-电荷纠缠（concurrence）与量子相干性（$l_1$ 范数），识别出由腔修饰（cavity dressing）驱动的非线性交叉，并将其与量子电池的可提取功（ergotropy）相联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在 Rashba 自旋-轨道耦合（spin-orbit coupling, SOC）杂化电荷与自旋自由度的基础上，腔耦合是否可作为额外的可调控制手段来调控量子关联与能量存储，尚待明确；同时需将腔修饰态的改变与可提取功联系起来。

Method: 理论分析单电子硅双量子点与单模微波腔的耦合体系，自旋-光子与电荷-光子耦合共同作用，Rashba 自旋-轨道耦合杂化电荷与自旋自由度；采用约化热密度矩阵（reduced thermal density matrix），以 concurrence 表征自旋-电荷纠缠，以 $l_1$ 范数表征量子相干性，并分析交叉边界对腔希尔伯特空间截断的稳健性及对电荷-光子耦合的优化。

Result: 低温下腔显著改变自旋-电荷关联，产生纠缠增强与抑制的不同区域；存在 eDQD 主导区与光子修饰（photon-dressed）区之间的非线性交叉，该交叉由 concurrence 的快速变化与腔内有限光子占据的出现两种独立特征揭示，边界呈现主导的 $G_{\rm c}\propto\sqrt{\Omega}$ 依赖且对腔希尔伯特空间截断稳健。将 eDQD 作为量子电池在相干充电下评估其储能与 ergotropy，ergotropy 在腔修饰交叉处变化显著；对电荷-光子耦合的优化给出依赖参数的最优充电区，且随 Rashba 耦合增强最大 ergotropy 提升。

Conclusion: 腔耦合与自旋-轨道耦合构成半导体 eDQD-腔体系中量子关联与可提取能量的互补控制手段，拓展了仅考虑 Rashba 耦合的研究，并为量子电池的可调能量存储提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We theoretically investigate quantum correlations and energy storage in a single-electron silicon double quantum dot (eDQD) coupled to a single-mode microwave cavity. The charge and spin degrees of freedom are hybridized by Rashba spin-orbit coupling (SOC), while the cavity interacts with the eDQD through spin-photon and charge-photon couplings. Using the reduced thermal density matrix, we characterize spin-charge entanglement by concurrence and quantum coherence by the $l_1$ norm. At low temperature, the cavity strongly modifies the spin-charge correlations, producing distinct regimes of enhanced and suppressed entanglement. We identify a nonlinear crossover between an eDQD-dominated regime and a photon-dressed regime. This crossover is revealed independently by two signatures: a rapid change in the concurrence and the onset of finite photon occupation in the cavity. Its boundary exhibits a dominant $G_{\rm c}\propto\sqrtΩ$ dependence and is robust against cavity Hilbert-space truncation. Beyond the Rashba coupling studied in [Ferreira et al., Phys. Rev. A 107, 052408 (2023)], we show that the cavity provides an additional tunable means of controlling quantum correlations and energy storage. We further characterize the eDQD as a quantum battery under coherent charging and evaluate its stored energy and ergotropy. The ergotropy varies markedly across a cavity-dressing crossover resembling that identified from entanglement, connecting cavity-induced modification of the dressed states to extractable work. Optimization over the charge-photon coupling reveals parameter-dependent optimal charging regimes and enhanced maximum ergotropy with increasing Rashba coupling. Our results establish cavity coupling and SOC as complementary controls of quantum correlations and extractable energy in semiconductor eDQD-cavity systems.

</details>


### [240] [Engineering Vacuum Fluctuations in Hyperbolic Heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37182)
*Jie-Cheng Feng,Xinle Cheng,Itai Keren,Dante M. Kennes,Abhay N. Pasupathy,Dmitri N. Basov,Emil Vinas Bostrom,Angel Rubio*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过在双曲材料（hyperbolic material）表面覆盖一层超薄且双曲性相反（opposite hyperbolicity）的覆层（overlayer），可在材料内部重塑真空场（vacuum field），进而在不改变底层材料本征介电性质的前提下调控其内部物质激发的耦合，并远程改变二维超导层的临界温度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用光腔（optical cavity）、谐振器与表面极化激元模式（surface-polaritonic modes）可工程化真空涨落，但将此类控制在材料内部深处实现仍存在困难。因此需要一种能深入材料内部、调控真空场的方法。

Method: 在双曲材料上覆盖一层更薄、双曲性相反的材料，利用两种材料双曲色散带（hyperbolic bands）之间的重叠，重塑底层材料内部的真空场；并以 Eliashberg 理论研究底层材料中的二维超导层。

Result: 覆层可远程改变二维超导层的临界温度（critical temperature），且不改变底层材料的本征介电性质（intrinsic dielectric properties），仅在两层双曲带重叠时产生该效应。

Conclusion: 该结果或可解释近期关于频率匹配的双曲覆层引起超导性变化的实验，并提出一种在芯片（on-chip）层面实现物质激发与材料集体性质的远程调控途径。

Abstract: Vacuum fluctuations can be engineered using optical cavities, resonators, and surface-polaritonic modes, but extending such control deep inside a material remains challenging.Here we show that the vacuum field inside a hyperbolic material can be reshaped by a much thinner overlayer with opposite hyperbolicity.This effect requires overlap between the hyperbolic bands of the two materials and modifies the coupling between the vacuum field and matter excitations within the bottom material, without changing its intrinsic dielectric properties. As an application, we use Eliashberg theory to study a two-dimensional superconducting layer inside the bottom material, showing that the overlayer can remotely change its critical temperature.Our results may shed light on recent experiments reporting changes in superconductivity arising from a frequency-matched hyperbolic overlayer, and suggest an on-chip route to long-range control of matter excitations and collective material properties.

</details>


### [241] [Atomic-scale control of spin transport in exchange-coupled molecules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37299)
*A. Fétida,R. Robles,F. Scheurer,M. Romeo,J. Kröger,N. Lorente,L. Limot*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过单分子功能化的扫描隧道显微镜（scanning tunneling microscope, STM）针尖作为可移动自旋传感器，在铁磁表面上方构建交换耦合分子结（exchange-coupled molecular junction），从同一光谱数据集独立提取电导、交换耦合与自旋极化，并在原子尺度上建立隧穿、交换与自旋输运之间的实空间联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 耦合自旋之间的自旋输运由隧穿（tunneling）与交换相互作用（exchange interaction）共同支配，但二者在真实空间中的微观关系此前缺乏实验证据。该研究旨在填补这一实验空白。

Method: 将单个分子功能化于扫描隧道显微镜针尖，作为可移动自旋传感器置于铁磁表面上第二个分子上方，构成交换耦合分子结；从同一光谱数据集独立提取结电导（junction conductance）、交换耦合与自旋极化（spin polarization），并在原子尺度上解析其空间依赖。

Result: 电导与交换耦合呈现近乎相同的空间依赖关系，符合最小 Hubbard 描述（minimal Hubbard description），即二者由分子前沿轨道（molecular frontier orbitals）之间相同的跃迁矩阵（hopping matrix）所支配；相比之下，自旋极化取决于轨道组成及由此产生的输运通道（transport channels）的相对权重。

Conclusion: 该结果在磁耦合分子中建立了隧穿、交换与自旋输运之间的实空间（real-space）联系，揭示了同一跃迁过程支配电导与交换、而自旋极化受轨道与通道权重调控的微观机制。

Abstract: Spin transport between coupled spins is governed by tunneling and exchange interactions, yet their microscopic relation in real space has remained experimentally elusive. To address this, we functionalize the tip of a scanning tunneling microscope with a single molecule and use it as a movable spin sensor positioned above a second molecule on a ferromagnetic surface, forming an exchange-coupled molecular junction. From the same spectroscopic dataset, we independently extract the junction conductance, exchange coupling, and spin polarization with atomic-scale resolution. Conductance and exchange display nearly identical spatial dependences, consistent with a minimal Hubbard description in which both are governed by the same hopping matrix between the molecular frontier orbitals. In contrast, the spin polarization depends on the orbital composition and the relative weight of the resulting transport channels. These results establish a real-space connection between tunneling, exchange, and spin transport in magnetically coupled molecules.

</details>


### [242] [Nonlinear optical responses of semiconducting graphene nanoribbons: Strong constraint from a hidden symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37418)
*Jiawei Ruan,Weichen Tang,Yang-Hao Chan,Chen Hu,Xiaoxun Gong,Steven G. Louie*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出非空间对称性（nonspatial symmetry）亦可显著约束非线性光学（nonlinear optical, NLO）响应：在双粒子格（bipartite-lattice）石墨烯纳米带（graphene nanoribbons, GNRs）中，近似的双粒子格粒子-空穴（bipartite-lattice particle-hole, BLPH）对称性强烈抑制偶数阶 NLO 效应，并可用于产生近纯自旋位移电流（spin shift currents）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有认识主要强调空间对称性（spatial symmetry）对 NLO 响应的约束，例如反演对称性（inversion symmetry）禁戒偶数阶 NLO 效应；本文关注被忽视的非空间对称性在 NLO 响应中的作用，并寻求纯自旋位移电流的新产生机制。

Method: 以具有双粒子格结构的石墨烯纳米带为研究对象，识别近似的双粒子格粒子-空穴对称性，并分析该对称性对偶数阶 NLO 响应及位移电流（shift currents）的约束；以新近合成的铁磁 Janus 石墨烯纳米带（ferromagnetic Janus GNR）进行示例说明。

Result: 近似 BLPH 对称性可强烈抑制偶数阶 NLO 效应，即使体系不具备反演对称性；在铁磁 GNRs 中，该对称性可实现近纯自旋位移电流的光学产生，且电荷位移电流（charge shift currents）被强烈抑制；光致直流自旋电流的自旋取向可通过外磁场便捷调控。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了 NLO 响应中被忽视的对称性层面，并表明磁化石墨烯纳米带（magnetic GNRs）是自旋电子学器件（spintronic devices）的有前景候选体系；相较此前在反铁磁体系中产生纯自旋位移电流的方案，本文方案具有自旋取向易调控等优势。

Abstract: Nonlinear optical (NLO) responses are powerful tools for probing material structures, as they are highly sensitive to spatial symmetries. For instance, it is well known that having inversion symmetry forbids even-order NLO effects. Here, we show that nonspatial symmetries can also significantly constrain NLO responses. In graphene nanoribbons (GNRs) with bipartite-lattice structures, we identify the approximate bipartite-lattice particle-hole (BLPH) symmetry strongly suppresses even-order NLO effects, even for systems with no inversion symmetry. Importantly, we demonstrate that this approximate symmetry enables the optical generation of nearly pure spin shift currents in ferromagnetic GNRs, with the charge shift currents strongly suppressed. The spin orientation of these photo-induced DC spin currents can be easily tuned by an external magnetic field, providing distinct advantages over previous schemes for generating pure spin shift currents in antiferromagnetic systems. We illustrate our findings using a recently synthesized ferromagnetic Janus GNR. Our results underscore the significance of an overlooked symmetry aspect of NLO responses and highlight magnetic GNRs as promising candidates for spintronic devices.

</details>


### [243] [Rotational symmetry and common-mode phase drift in a counter-wound S-shaped Aharonov--Bohm interferometer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37753)
*Halil Serdar Solak,Afif Siddiki*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 本文研究三维 InAs Aharonov-Bohm 波导（Aharonov-Bohm waveguide）中由两个反向弯曲四分之一圆弯折构成的共模磁通偏移问题，推导出传输率对称关系 T(φ1,φ2)=T(-φ2,-φ1)，并借助三维散射计算评估了共模/差模驻定性、可见度及噪声响应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探究反向绕制（counter-wound）构型对共模磁通偏移（common flux offsets）是否具有内在驻定性，并厘清这种驻定性对干涉相位、外加均匀磁场及噪声响应的依赖条件。

Method: 采用自旋无关（spin-independent）的两端模型与理想约束磁通（confined fluxes）设定，利用微观可逆性（microreversibility）与模块交换旋转对称性推导传输率约束关系；并在 R=350 nm、E_F≈7.283 meV 条件下进行三维散射计算，保留三个开放轨道引线模式（orbital lead modes），分析能谱依赖、温度效应与高斯相位噪声响应。

Result: 对称关系 T(φ1,φ2)=T(-φ2,-φ1) 使反向绕制构型对共模偏移驻定、同向绕制（same-winding）对差模偏移驻定，且该约束不要求所有干涉相位相消，并可推广至垂直波导平面的空间均匀环境磁场。三维散射计算显示开口处反射强烈；最细采样反向绕制扫描可见度约 3.56%，归算到共同阈值后连续可见度估计为 2.6–2.9%；响应系数随能量强烈变化，100 mK 电子温度保留约 98% 可见度。弱纯共模高斯相位噪声下，透射方差主导项为噪声幅度的四次方，均值位移为二次方；磁通失衡与非对称标量势恢复线性响应，对称标量无序保持驻定性但不保证高透射。

Conclusion: 反向绕制构型对共模磁通偏移具有受对称性保护的驻定性，该性质可延伸至均匀垂直环境磁场，但实际实现需满足少模相干输运（few-mode coherent transport）、标定的磁通控制及泄漏场与自旋相关项均已评估的磁源设计。实验验证需结合能谱依赖、温度与噪声条件的综合考虑。

Abstract: We study common flux offsets in a three-dimensional InAs Aharonov-Bohm waveguide formed by two quarter-circle bends of opposite curvature. Each bend contains a through-opening that separates two conducting arms and encloses an independently specified flux. For a spin-independent, two-terminal model with ideal confined fluxes, microreversibility and a rotation exchanging the complete modules imply $\mathcal{T}(φ_1,φ_2)=\mathcal{T}(-φ_2,-φ_1)$. The counter-wound configuration is therefore stationary against common offsets, while the same-winding configuration is stationary against differential offsets. This constraint does not require cancellation of every interference phase, and it extends to a spatially uniform ambient field perpendicular to the plane of the guide, even though that field also penetrates the conductor. Three-dimensional scattering calculations at $R=350$ nm and $E_F\simeq 7.283$ meV retain three open orbital lead modes and strong reflection at the openings. The finest sampled counter-wound sweep has a visibility of approximately 3.56%; referring the grids to a common threshold gives an estimated continuum visibility of 2.6-2.9%. The response coefficients vary strongly with energy, while a 100 mK electron temperature retains about 98% of the visibility. For weak, purely common Gaussian phase noise, the leading transmission variance is quartic in noise amplitude, whereas the mean shift remains quadratic. Flux imbalance and asymmetric scalar potentials restore a linear response; symmetric scalar disorder preserves stationarity without guaranteeing high transmission. The proposed experimental test requires few-mode coherent transport, calibrated flux controls, and a magnetic-source design whose leakage fields and spin-dependent terms are assessed explicitly.

</details>


### [244] [Multistate Torsion in Zitterbewegung](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37767)
*Wojciech J. Jankowski*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该文推导了晶体中由能带挠率（band torsion）增强的非线性 Zitterbewegung 速度，并指出含多态量子几何张量与挠率的运动方程可导致瞬态非线性光电流响应，区别于近期观测到的圆偏振光诱导共振稳态光伏效应，同时提出多态量子几何挠率张量的层析测量方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于将 Zitterbewegung 与晶体能带挠率结合，以刻画多态量子几何张量（multistate quantum geometric tensors）对电子运动的影响，并区分瞬态光电流与已知的稳态光伏效应。

Method: 方法为理论推导：建立包含多态量子几何张量与挠率的运动方程，得出非线性 Zitterbewegung 速度，并提出用于测量多态量子几何挠率张量（multistate quantum geometric torsion tensors）的层析（tomography）方案。

Result: 结果显示，该运动方程产生瞬态非线性光电流响应（transient nonlinear photocurrent responses），其性质不同于近期测得的由圆偏振光（circularly polarized light）诱导的共振稳态光伏效应（resonant steady-state photovoltaic effects）。

Conclusion: 结论指出，能带挠率增强的非线性 Zitterbewegung 速度为区分瞬态非线性光电流与稳态光伏效应提供了理论框架，并给出可实验实施的多态量子几何挠率张量层析测量协议。

Abstract: We derive nonlinear Zitterbewegung velocity of electrons augmented by band torsion in crystals. The equation of motion with multistate quantum geometric tensors and torsion culminates in transient nonlinear photocurrent responses distinct from the recently measured resonant steady-state photovoltaic effects induced by circularly polarized light. Tomography protocols for measuring the multistate quantum geometric torsion tensors are proposed.

</details>


### [245] [Multiscale modelling of nanoscaled FETs based on 2D ferroelectric materials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37823)
*Alejandro Toral-Lopez,Michele Virgilio,Gianluca Fiori,Damiano Marian*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该工作提出一个连接第一性原理密度泛函理论与非平衡格林函数（NEGF）的多尺度模拟框架，以二维磷化铟单层为案例，实现无需经验参数的二维铁电场效应晶体管性能预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统三维块体铁电体（bulk ferroelectrics）在纳米尺度存在严重物理缩放限制，研究重心因而转向二维铁电单层。为加速这类新材料的实验测试与筛选，需要准确的器件级性能预测手段。

Method: 构建多尺度模拟框架，将第一性原理密度泛函理论（density functional theory）与非平衡格林函数（Non-Equilibrium Green's Function, NEGF）输运计算相衔接，并在自洽泊松-NEGF求解器中嵌入连续插值的、依赖极化（polarization-dependent）的哈密顿量。

Result: 模型可刻画离子运动与电子输运之间的动态相互作用，并在无经验参数条件下自然复现宏观电滞回线（hysteresis loops）与记忆窗口（memory windows）。

Conclusion: 该预测性流程为评估与优化下一代二维铁电场效应晶体管（2D ferroelectric field-effect transistors）提供了一种计算高效的工具。

Abstract: Ferroelectric materials are highly attractive for low-power, high-speed electronics and emerging neuromorphic computing architectures. However, the severe physical scaling limits of conventional bulk (3D) ferroelectrics at the nanoscale have shifted attention towards two-dimensional (2D) ferroelectric monolayers. Accurate device-level performance predictions are essential to accelerate the experimental testing and screening of these novel materials. In this work, we present a multiscale simulation framework that bridges first-principles density functional theory with Non-Equilibrium Green's Function (NEGF) transport calculations. Using a 2D Indium Phosphide monolayer as a case study, our approach leverages a continuously interpolated, polarization-dependent Hamiltonian embedded within a self-consistent Poisson-NEGF solver. The model captures the dynamic interplay between ion movement and electronic transport, naturally reproducing macroscopic hysteresis loops and memory windows without empirical parameters. This predictive pipeline provides a computationally efficient tool to evaluate and optimize next-generation 2D ferroelectric field-effect transistors.

</details>


### [246] [Interlayer dark excitons in a van der Waals heterostructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38033)
*Rundong Ma,Konstantin Davydov,Liuxin Gu,Lifu Zhang,Hassan Alnatah,Beini Gao,Ruihao Ni,Suji Park,Houk Jang,Takashi Taniguchi,Kenji Watanabe,You Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 该研究报告了在高质量双栅WSe$_2$/hBN/WSe$_2$同质双层中，直接光谱识别亮（单重态）与暗（三重态）层间激子，并揭示二者在自旋构型、选择定则、谷极化与扭角调控上的差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 层间激子（interlayer excitons）具长寿命与长程输运特性，有望用于激子器件与量子多体相（如Bose-Einstein凝聚），而最低能激子种类决定布居、输运与凝聚过程。然而，莫尔激子（moiré excitons）可模拟其光学特征，使得单重态与三重态层间激子的区分长期困难。

Method: 采用高质量双栅（dual-gated）WSe$_2$/hBN/WSe$_2$同质双层样品，结合电场依赖的光致发光（photoluminescence）与反射（reflectance）光谱进行直接光谱识别。

Result: 电场依赖测量揭示两个动量直接（momentum-direct）层间跃迁，具不同自旋构型；层间暗激子遵循与亮激子不同的选择定则，并在超过微秒的超长寿命下仍保持强谷极化（valley polarization）；扭角（twist-angle）可调控电子-空穴动量失配，从而调制层间激子发光。

Conclusion: 结果提供了TMD异质结构电子与激子结构的关键认识，为激子多体物理与光电器件开辟新途径。

Abstract: Interlayer excitons in transition metal dichalcogenide (TMD) heterostructures exhibit long lifetimes and long-range transport, making them promising for excitonic devices and quantum many-body phases, such as Bose-Einstein condensates. Achieving these goals requires a precise understanding of spin-allowed bright and nominally spin-forbidden dark excitons, because the lowest-energy exciton species governs population, transport, and condensation. Despite substantial progress, unambiguously distinguishing singlet and triplet interlayer excitons has been challenging, as moiré excitons in these heterostructures can mimic their optical signatures. Here, we report the direct spectroscopic identification of bright (singlet) and dark (triplet) interlayer excitons in high-quality, dual-gated WSe$_2$/hBN/WSe$_2$ homobilayers. Electric-field-dependent photoluminescence and reflectance reveal two momentum-direct interlayer transitions with distinct spin configurations. The interlayer dark excitons obey selection rules that differ from those of bright excitons. Strikingly, interlayer dark excitons retain strong valley polarization, even with their ultralong lifetime exceeding microseconds. Finally, we demonstrate twist-angle control, wherein twist-induced electron-hole momentum mismatch modulates interlayer exciton emission. These results provide critical insights into the electronic and excitonic structure of TMD heterostructures, opening new avenues for excitonic many-body physics and optoelectronic devices.

</details>


### [247] [Interlayer Fermi Polarons in Bilayer MoTe$_2$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38045)
*Ruihao Ni,Eugen Dizer,Maximilian Wolf,Son T. Le,Sharadh Jois,Jeffrey J. Schwartz,Liuxin Gu,Rundong Ma,Suji Park,Beini Gao,Lifu Zhang,Houk Jang,Takashi Taniguchi,Kenji Watanabe,Aubrey T. Hanbicki,Adam L. Friedman,Richard Schmidt,You Zhou*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 双层 MoTe₂ 保留直接光学带隙，为研究双层费米极化子（Fermi polaron）物理提供了干净平台；电场可调控层相干激子（layer-coherent exciton）并产生单层中不存在的极化子分支。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 双层过渡金属硫族化合物（transition metal dichalcogenides, TMDs）中存在由层自由度（layer degree of freedom）支配的量子相，如双层维格纳晶体（Wigner crystal）、分数量子陈绝缘体（fractional Chern insulator）和激子凝聚（exciton condensate）。但这些相主要通过激子光谱（exciton spectroscopy）探测，而双层中激子与载流子如何相互作用形成费米极化子（此时杂质与费米海均携带层赝自旋）尚不清楚。多数 TMD 双层具有动量间接光学带隙，非辐射衰减与不均匀展宽掩盖了本征光谱，限制了研究进展。

Method: 工作指出与多数 TMD 双层不同，双层 MoTe₂ 保留直接光学带隙，可作为研究双层费米极化子物理的干净平台。在双栅器件（dual-gated device）中，面外电场连续调控层内激子（intralayer exciton）与层间激子（interlayer exciton）的杂化，形成层相干激子。通过静电掺杂，激子光谱演化为多个极化子分支，受载流子掺杂与面外电场共同调控。作者采用场论模型（field-theoretic model）定量描述其中一种新极化子。

Result: 静电掺杂后，激子光谱演化为多个极化子分支，由载流子掺杂和面外电场共同控制。作者识别出一种在单层中无对应物的极化子，其中层相干激子被相反层的载流子所“修饰”（dressed），并被其场论模型定量捕获。

Conclusion: 结果表明，杂质与“浴”（bath）双方的赝自旋结构重塑了极化子的形成，为探索具有层间相干性的玻色-爱因斯坦凝聚（Bose-Einstein condensate）等多体态开辟了新途径。

Abstract: Atomic bilayers of transition metal dichalcogenides (TMDs) host quantum phases governed by the layer degree of freedom, including bilayer Wigner crystals, fractional Chern insulators, and exciton condensates. These phases are probed primarily through exciton spectroscopy, yet it remains poorly understood how excitons and carriers interact to form Fermi polarons in bilayers, where both the impurity and the Fermi sea carry a layer pseudospin. Progress has been limited because most TMD bilayers have momentum-indirect optical bandgaps, in which non-radiative decay and inhomogeneous broadening obscure the intrinsic spectra. Here, we show that bilayer MoTe$_2$, unlike most TMD bilayers, retains a direct optical bandgap, providing a clean platform for studying bilayer Fermi-polaron physics. In a dual-gated device, an out-of-plane electric field continuously tunes the hybridization between intralayer and interlayer excitons, forming layer-coherent excitons. Upon electrostatic doping, the excitonic spectrum evolves into multiple polaron branches, controlled by both carrier doping and the out-of-plane electric field. Among these, we identify a polaron with no analog in monolayers, in which a layer-coherent exciton is dressed by carriers in the opposite layer, and is quantitatively captured by our field-theoretic model. Our results establish that pseudospin structure in both the impurity and the bath reshapes polaron formation, opening new avenues to many-body states such as Bose-Einstein condensates with interlayer coherence.

</details>


### [248] [Spatially dispersive photogalvanic effects as a probe of quantum geometric tensor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38102)
*Yicong Chen,Bumseop Kim,Senin Ahammed Akkara Paramban,Zhurun Ji,Utkarsh Khandelwal,Shupeng Xu,Jiachen Shi,Sergiy Krylyuk,Albert V. Davydov,Andrew M. Rappe,Ritesh Agarwal*

Main category: cond-mat.mes-hall

TL;DR: 通过光场空间色散改变二阶光电流选择定则，在中心对称晶体中分离并测量量子度规与贝里曲率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 布洛赫态的贝里曲率与量子度规主导众多现象，但二者缺乏统一的实验探测手段。

Method: 利用光空间色散（spatial dispersion）改变二阶光电流选择定则，使中心对称晶体中宇称禁戒响应在无外场、磁序、界面、应变或人工对称破缺下被允许；在 1T'-MoTe₂ 至 Td-Weyl 相变中区分螺旋性偶与螺旋性奇光电流。

Result: 同一器件中分离出对应量子度规与贝里曲率的光电流；度规主导响应在两相中持续存在，光谱结构与第一性原理计算一致并关联动量分辨的量子度规热点；曲率通道仅在反演对称破缺时出现，并对载流子掺杂敏感。

Conclusion: 空间变化光场的光伏效应为在光子能量与电子填充探测不同激发流形部分时获取材料量子几何提供了通用途径。

Abstract: Berry curvature and quantum metric of Bloch states govern a wide range of phenomena, yet unified experimental access to both remains limited. Here we show that light spatial-dispersion alters the selection rules of second-order photocurrents and responses forbidden by parity become allowed in centrosymmetric crystals without any external fields, magnetic order, interfaces, strain, or engineered symmetry breaking. The resulting response is intrinsic and its weight is set by the quantum geometry of the Bloch states. Conventional photogalvanic effects are constrained by crystal symmetry and entangle the quantum metric with shift vector contributions. The spatially-dispersive response instead isolates the quantum metric and Berry curvature in distinct polarization channels. Implementing this approach in 1T'-MoTe2 across its temperature-driven transition to Td-Weyl phase, we resolve helicity-even and helicity-odd photocurrents corresponding to quantum metric and Berry curvature contributions, respectively, from the same device. The metric-dominated response persists across both phases and exhibits a robust spectral structure reproduced by first-principles calculations and linked to momentum-resolved quantum metric hotspots. In contrast, the curvature-driven channel emerges only when inversion symmetry is broken and shows strong sensitivity to carrier doping through competing momentum-space contributions. Our results establish photogalvanic effects with spatially varying optical fields as a general route to accessing quantum geometry in materials where photon energy and electronic filling probe different parts of the excitation manifold.

</details>


<div id='math.SP'></div>

# math.SP [[Back]](#toc)

### [249] [A sandwich theorem for the spectrum of graph coverings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36341)
*Charles Bordenave,Wenbo Li,Joe Thomas*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 该文证明：介于万有覆盖（universal cover）与极大阿贝尔覆盖（maximal abelian cover）之间的任意正规覆盖（normal cover），其谱测度（spectral measures）的原子部分（atomic part）相同，并给出原子质量的显式双公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于统一并推广关于万有覆盖树（universal covering trees）与极大阿贝尔覆盖的已有结果，同时验证物理学文献中关于双曲格（hyperbolic lattices）的一个猜想之扩展。

Method: 证明结合了冯·诺依曼代数（von Neumann algebras）、匹配理论（matching theory）与单调标号方法（monotone labelling method）；附录中证明了一个反向广义 Gallai-Edmonds 结构定理（converse generalised Gallai-Edmonds structural theorem）。

Result: 所有此类正规覆盖共享谱测度原子部分，原子质量由显式双公式给出；此外建立了谱测度连续部分（continuous part）的对数 Hölder 正则性（logarithmic Hölder regularity）。

Conclusion: 结果表明正规覆盖的原子谱结构具有统一性，所给双公式推广并统一了已知结论，并扩展了关于双曲格的物理学猜想；附录定理可能具有独立价值。

Abstract: We show that all normal covers of a fixed finite graph $G$ sitting between the universal and maximal abelian covers share the same atomic part of their spectral measures. We give an explicit double formula for the mass of atoms which generalises and unifies known results on universal covering trees and maximal abelian covers. It also proves an extension of a conjecture in physics literature on hyperbolic lattices. Moreover, we also establish the logarithmic Hölder regularity of the continuous part of the spectral measures. Our proof combines techniques from von Neumann algebras, matching theory and the monotone labelling method.
  In an appendix, we prove a converse generalised Gallai-Edmonds structural theorem which might be of independent interest.

</details>


### [250] [Bouncing-ball modes in ergodic billiards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36501)
*Zhenhao Li,Maciej Zworski*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文证明：对水平拉伸的 Bunimovich 台球（Bunimovich billiard），对几乎所有拉伸参数，存在一列集中（concentrating）在矩形区域内的本征函数（eigenfunction）。证明思路受与 ChatGPT 6 的互动启发，并结合 Hassell 的参数变化论证（parameter-variation argument）完成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机源于对 Bunimovich 或 Sinai 台球中是否存在弹跳球模式（bouncing ball modes）的好奇；由于直接构造未成功，转而考虑改变台球的“翼部”（wings），即引入拉伸参数的变化。

Method: 方法上先构造拟模（quasimode）并给出基本完整的论证，再引入 Hassell 的参数变化论证，将拟模结论提升为关于本征函数的结论；论证随后经作者大幅简化与澄清。

Result: 对水平拉伸的 Bunimovich 台球，在几乎所有拉伸参数取值下，都存在一列集中于矩形部分的本征函数；同时依据 Markarian–Oliffson Kamphorst–Pinto de Carvalho 的工作，此类台球在一个开参数集（open set of parameters）上具有遍历性（ergodic）。

Conclusion: 拉伸参数的变化使得矩形部分可支持集中型本征函数的序列，即使相应参数下系统具有遍历性；该结果亦体现了与 ChatGPT 6 交互在数学论证构建中的作用。

Abstract: We show that for a horizontally stretched Bunimovich billiard, for almost every stretching parameter, there exists a sequence of eigenfunctions concentrating in the rectangular part. Thanks to the work of Markarian--Oliffson Kamphorst--Pinto de Carvalho, we know that for an open set of parameters such billiards are ergodic. The proof is a result of interaction with ChatGPT 6: we were curious if it could establish the existence of bouncing ball modes for Bunimovich or Sinai billiards. That was not successful until we suggested varying the wings. That produced an essentially complete argument for the existence of quasimodes. We then suggested using Hassell's parameter-variation argument which led to the result about eigenfunctions. The argument was then significantly simplified and clarified by the authors.

</details>


### [251] [Periodic graph operators with reducible dispersion polynomials for all potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37364)
*Diantong Li*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 该文完整刻画了色散多项式（dispersion polynomial）对任意势（potential）均可约（reducible）的周期图算子（periodic graph operator）类；由此结合参数相关Laurent多项式的可约性二分性（reducibility dichotomy），证明其余周期图算子在一般势下色散多项式与Bloch簇（Bloch variety）均不可约。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究周期图算子的色散多项式与Bloch簇在一般情况下是否不可约（irreducible），是理解其谱结构的基本问题；摘要未额外展开动机。

Method: 证明经由两步归约（reduction）：先利用色散多项式根关于势参数的单值群（monodromy）将问题归约到分裂情形（splitting case），再证明分裂性质可传递到Floquet矩阵的主子矩阵（principal submatrices），从而将问题进一步归约到基本域（fundamental domain）仅含两个顶点（vertices）的情形。

Result: 给出了色散多项式对所有势均可约的周期图算子的完整刻画；其余周期图算子的色散多项式及Bloch簇在一般势下不可约。

Conclusion: 借助参数相关Laurent多项式的可约性二分性，确立了一般势下色散多项式与Bloch簇不可约性，除被完整刻画的“全可约”情形之外；证明依赖于单值群归约与主子矩阵传递两个步骤。

Abstract: We give a complete characterization of periodic graph operators whose dispersion polynomials are reducible for every potential. Consequently, the reducibility dichotomy for parameter-dependent Laurent polynomials implies that, for every other periodic graph operator, the dispersion polynomial, and hence the Bloch variety, is irreducible for generic potentials.
  Our proof proceeds through two reductions. We first use the monodromy of the roots of the dispersion polynomial with respect to the potential parameters to reduce the problem to the splitting case. We then show that the splitting property passes to the principal submatrices of the Floquet matrix, reducing the problem further to the case that the fundamental domain contains two vertices.

</details>


### [252] [The Dry Ten Martini Problem for subcritical Type I operators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37438)
*Lingrui Ge*

Main category: math.SP

TL;DR: 本文为文献[6]注记1.3中宣布的次临界干十马提尼命题提供完整细节：对任意无理频率，具有共振旋转数的每个次临界I型能量都是某个开谱隙的端点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者此前工作[6]仅以注记形式宣布了该命题而缺乏细节，本文旨在补全其证明。该结果针对次临界情形下谱隙端点与能量类型的对应关系，与Li[7]近期给出的另一证明形成并行工作。

Method: 证明结合了两项工具：文献[6]中发展的全频率Puig论证（all-frequency Puig argument），以及定量几乎可约化（quantitative almost reducibility）技术。

Result: 对每个无理频率（irrational frequency），任意具有共振旋转数（resonant rotation number）的次临界I型能量（subcritical Type I energy）均为一个开谱隙（open spectral gap）的端点。

Conclusion: 该结论以完整证明形式确立了次临界干十马提尼命题（subcritical dry Ten Martini statement）；另有Li[7]给出的不同证明，二者相互独立。

Abstract: We supply the details of the subcritical dry Ten Martini statement announced in [6, Remark 1.3]: for every irrational frequency, every subcritical Type I energy with resonant rotation number is an endpoint of an open spectral gap. The proof combines the all-frequency Puig argument developed there with quantitative almost reducibility. A different proof was recently given by Li [7].

</details>

### [253] [Meromorphic open-string vertex algebras and twisted modules from Courant algebroids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36370)
*Qixuan Fang,Fei Qi*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 该论文从带有广义度量与广义列维-奇维塔联络的传递 Courant 代数胚（transitive Courant algebroid）出发，构造了一类 1/2-整数分次亚纯开弦顶点代数（meromorphic open-string vertex algebra）层，并以此为框架实现加权旋量与微分形式上的几何微分算子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目标是将顶点算子（vertex operator）对几何微分算子的实现，从函数情形推广到加权旋量（weighted spinor）与微分形式情形，服务于二维超对称非线性 σ 模型（two-dimensional supersymmetric nonlinear sigma models）的研究纲领。

Method: 利用传递锚定丛的完整（holonomy）原理，将代数实现为由 E 的玻色-费米仿射化（bosonic-fermionic affinization）所构成的张量代数丛的平行截面；在偶数秩 E 与合适 Clifford 模丛数据下构造含典则加权旋量丛（位于权零）的规范扭曲模层，并借助结合代数化的平行张量作用。

Result: 平行张量的结合代数通过由协变微分与 Clifford 乘法组合而成的 E-联络迭代作用于旋量截面；由逆 Courant 配对与逆广义度量构造的特定态给出顶点算子分量，其在嵌入旋量截面上表现为典则 Dirac 生成算子、其形式伴随及二者反对易子（广义 Hodge 拉普拉斯算子）。

Conclusion: 在带三形式 H 与 exterior algebra Clifford 模的 exact Courant 情形下，上述算子退化为 d−H∧、其伴随与扭曲 Hodge 拉普拉斯算子；当 H=0 时进一步还原为通常的 de Rham 与 Hodge 算子。

Abstract: We construct a sheaf of $\frac{1}{2}\mathbb{Z}$-graded meromorphic open-string vertex algebras from a transitive Courant algebroid $E$ equipped with a generalized metric and a chosen generalized Levi-Civita connection on a smooth manifold. Using a holonomy principle for transitive anchored bundles, we realize these algebras as parallel sections of a tensor algebra bundle built from bosonic-fermionic affinizations of $E$. For even-rank $E$ with suitable Clifford module bundle data, we construct a sheaf of canonically twisted modules containing the canonical weighted spinor bundle in weight zero. The associative algebra of parallel tensors acts on spinor sections through iterates of an $E$-connection combining covariant differentiation and Clifford multiplication, with mixed terms incorporating the action of the bosonic connection on fermionic tensors. Distinguished states built from the inverse Courant pairing and inverse generalized metric give vertex-operator components, acting on embedded spinor sections as the canonical Dirac generating operator, its formal adjoint, and their anticommutator, the generalized Hodge Laplacian. In the exact Courant case with three-form $H$ and the exterior algebra Clifford module, the operators specialize to $d-H\wedge$, its adjoint, and the twisted Hodge Laplacian, reducing to the ordinary de Rham and Hodge operators when $H=0$. This extends the vertex-operator realization of geometric differential operators from functions to weighted spinors and differential forms, within the program of studying two-dimensional supersymmetric nonlinear sigma models.

</details>


### [254] [Higher-dimensional full vertex algebras and systems of conformally covariant correlation functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37949)
*Yuto Moriwaki*

Main category: math.QA

TL;DR: 该论文为紧致 $d$ 维共形场论（conformal field theory）的欧几里得关联函数（Euclidean correlation functions）系统建立公理化框架，并引入共形 $d$-顶点代数（conformal $d$-vertex algebras）作为全顶点代数（full vertex algebras）的 $d$ 维类比，证明每个共形协变关联函数系统都可导出此类代数，并以广义自由标量场（generalized free scalar fields）构造满足公理的例子族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对象为紧致 $d$ 维共形场论，且不假定其具有幺正性（unitarity）。动机在于将低维（尤其二维）顶点代数与关联函数公理化的思路推广到任意维度的共形场论框架中。

Method: 论文首先形式化了一类欧几里得关联函数系统（systems of Euclidean correlation functions）；随后引入共形 $d$-顶点代数（conformal $d$-vertex algebras），即全顶点代数（full vertex algebras）的 $d$ 维类比，并证明该代数可由任一满足共形协变性（conformal covariance）的关联函数系统得到。

Result: 对每个 $d\geq 2$，构造出一族满足所提公理的例子，对应广义自由标量场（generalized free scalar fields）；当 $d>2$ 时，该例子族同时包含幺正（unitary）与非幺正（non-unitary）两类情形。

Conclusion: 该工作给出非幺正紧致 $d$ 维共形场论关联函数的公理体系与相应代数结构，并通过广义自由标量场例子验证公理的自洽性与覆盖范围，为高维共形场论的代数化描述提供基础。

Abstract: We formulate a class of systems of Euclidean correlation functions for compact $d$-dimensional conformal field theories which are not necessarily unitary. We also introduce conformal $d$-vertex algebras, $d$-dimensional analogues of full vertex algebras, and prove that such an algebra is obtained from every system of conformally covariant correlation functions. Finally, for each $d\geq 2$, we construct a family of examples satisfying these axioms, corresponding to generalized free scalar fields. For $d>2$, this family contains both unitary and non-unitary examples.

</details>


<div id='math-ph'></div>
