### [255] [Coherent energy cascades in nonlinear disordered Hamiltonians](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36009)
*Anxo Biasi,Brad Cownden,Oleg Evnin,Alvaro Iturbe Jabaloyes*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该研究证明：在具有完全共振模谱与随机非线性耦合的无穷维共振哈密顿系统（resonant Hamiltonian systems）中，极少量的结构化耦合子集即可支撑相干级联（coherent cascades），表现为相位对齐、幂律谱（power-law spectra）及 Sobolev 范数的无界增长或有限时间爆破。数值模拟显示随机初始数据可通过锁相（phase locking）过程自发涌现相干级联，表明无序模耦合可维持而非阻断跨任意远尺度的相干能量输运。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统波湍流理论（wave kinetic theory）以各简正模（normal modes）随机相位为特征的湍流能量级联为核心；而近年发现的相干级联此前仅在模相互作用代数结构被严格控制的确定性系统（如非线性薛定谔方程）中确立。因此，相干级联是否依赖于特定的耦合结构、能否在耦合被大幅改变后依然存在，成为待解决的问题。

Method: 研究对象为具有完全共振简正模谱与随机非线性耦合的无穷维共振哈密顿系统；通过严格证明说明，仅占总耦合极小比例的一个结构化耦合子集，即可支撑相干级联。同时结合数值模拟，考察随机初始数据条件下相干级联的涌现过程。

Result: 相干级联在模耦合被显著改变时依然稳健：结构化耦合子集（占总量极小比例）足以产生相位对齐、幂律谱形成，以及 Sobolev 范数的无界增长或有限时间爆破两种可能结果之一。数值模拟进一步显示，随机初始数据可通过锁相过程涌现出相干级联。

Conclusion: 无序的模相互作用（disordered mode interactions）不仅不会阻断、反而能够维持跨任意远尺度的相干能量输运；相干级联的存在对耦合结构的改变具有稳健性，并为更广泛初始数据与系统中相干级联的涌现提供了可能机制。

Abstract: The familiar wave kinetic theory revolves around turbulent energy cascades characterized by random phases of the individual normal modes. By contrast, recent years have revealed turbulent cascades where the transfer of energy is mediated by phase-coherent dynamics. Such coherent cascades have been previously established in deterministic systems with a tightly controlled algebraic structure of the mode interactions, such as nonlinear Schrödinger equations.
  Here, we show that the presence of phase-coherent cascades is robust with respect to substantial alterations of the mode couplings. We prove that, in infinite-dimensional resonant Hamiltonian systems with fully resonant normal mode spectra and random nonlinear couplings, a structured subset of couplings --- an infinitesimal fraction of the total --- suffices to support coherent cascades characterized by phase alignment, the formation of power-law spectra, and either unbounded growth of Sobolev norms or a finite-time blow-up. Numerical simulations further demonstrate the emergence of coherent cascades from random initial data through a process of phase locking, suggesting a mechanism for their emergence in broader classes of initial data and systems. We thus show that disordered mode interactions can sustain, rather than block, coherent energy transport across arbitrarily distant scales.

</details>


### [256] [Combinatorics and loop equations for antisymmetrised and Hermitised matrix product ensembles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36332)
*Stephane Dartois,Anas. A Rahman*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究 $m$ 阶反对称矩阵乘积系综（antisymmetrised matrix product ensemble）与 $m$ 阶厄米化矩阵乘积系综（Hermitised matrix product ensemble）的特征值统计，通过构造带状图（ribbon graphs）与星座图（constellations），用混合累积量（mixed cumulants）对其计数，并在 $m=1$ 时给出生成函数的圈方程（loop equation）刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 上述两类系综近期被证明与特定 Muttalib–Borodin 系综及 Harish-Chandra–Itzykson–Zuber 型积分相关，这一联系促使对其特征值统计进行更深入的研究。

Method: 构造由系综混合累积量所计数的带状图与星座图；并在 $m=1$ 情形下，对相应累积量的生成函数给出圈方程刻画。

Result: 建立了带状图与星座图同两类矩阵乘积系综混合累积量之间的枚举对应关系；获得 $m=1$ 时累积量生成函数的圈方程表征。

Conclusion: 带状图/星座图枚举与圈方程方法可用于刻画反对称及厄米化矩阵乘积系综的混合累积量结构，为后续特征值统计研究提供组合与解析工具。

Abstract: The order $m$ antisymmetrised matrix product ensemble is represented by the product $X_1^T\cdots X_m^TJX_m\cdots X_1$, where $X_1,\ldots,X_m$ are independent real Ginibre matrices and $J$ is the elementary antisymmetric matrix, while the order $m$ Hermitised matrix product ensemble is represented by $X_1^\dagger\cdots X_m^\dagger HX_1\cdots X_m$, where $X_1,\ldots,X_m$ are now independent complex Ginibre matrices and $H$ is a Hermitian matrix drawn from the Gaussian unitary ensemble. These ensembles have recently been shown to be related to certain Muttalib--Borodin ensembles and integrals of Harish-Chandra--Itzykson--Zuber type, thereby motivating further investigation into their eigenvalue statistics. In this work, we construct ribbon graphs and constellations that are enumerated by the mixed cumulants of these ensembles and give loop equation characterisations for the generating functions of said cumulants when $m=1$.

</details>


### [257] [Analysis of Delay Differential Equations Using the Offset Linear Canonical Transform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36792)
*Gita Rani Mahato Manab Kundu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出基于偏移线性正则变换（offset linear canonical transform, OLCT）的框架，用于求解一类延迟微分方程（delay differential equations）。通过将原方程在变换域转化为 Volterra 型延迟积分方程并数值求解，验证了方法的精度与有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 受 Ohira 工作启发，并利用偏移线性正则变换（OLCT）相对傅里叶变换（Fourier transform, FT）的优势，本文旨在为延迟微分方程提供新的变换域求解途径。其动机在于借助 OLCT 的算子性质提升对该类方程的处理能力。

Method: 利用 OLCT 的运算性质，将原始延迟微分方程转换到变换域中的 Volterra 型延迟积分方程，并采用 Brunner 的步进法（method of steps）进行数值求解。此外，针对特殊情形推导出显式解析解，并通过关联 OLCT 方法与傅里叶变换对应形式，建立统一的变换域表述。

Result: 数值与图形结果表明所提方法具有精度和有效性，并通过与 Ohira 基于傅里叶变换的表述进行对比得到验证。针对特殊情形，还分析了延迟参数的影响。

Conclusion: 该框架可为求解含超越项的非线性延迟微分方程提供有前景的基础，因为 OLCT 与所得 Volterra 积分方程均具备处理非线性与超越项所需的本质性质。其中统一的变换域表述亦加强了该方法的理论一致性。

Abstract: Motivated by the work of Ohira and the advantages of the offset linear canonical transform (OLCT) over the Fourier transform (FT), this paper proposes an OLCT based framework for solving a class of delay differential equations. By exploiting the operational properties of the OLCT, the original delay differ- ential equation is transformed into a Volterra-type delay integral equation in the transform domain and solved numerically using Brunners method of steps . An explicit analytical solution is derived for a special case to investigate the effect of the delay parameter. A unified transform-domain formulation is also established by relating the proposed OLCT approach to its Fourier transform counterpart. Numerical and graphical results demonstrate the accuracy and effectiveness of the proposed method and validate it through comparisons with the Fourier transform based formulation of Ohira . The proposed framework may provide a promising foundation for solving non linear delay differential equations involving transcendental terms, as both the OLCT and the resulting Volterra integral equation possess the essential properties required to handle non linearities and transcendental terms.

</details>


### [258] [Reduced Solutions of Toda Systems for Arbitrary Complex Simple Lie Algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36897)
*Yiqian Shi,Chunhui Wei,Bin Xu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究由球面度规（spherical metrics）生成的 Toda 系统（Toda systems）的约化解，针对任意复单李代数（complex simple Lie algebra），给出约化族的本原中心化子参数化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注球面度规所生成 Toda 系统的约化解结构，旨在为约化族提供内在的参数化刻画，并说明其与单值群（monodromy group）中心化子的关系，从而连接球面度规存在性结果与 Toda 源元组。

Method: 研究对象为任意复单李代数上的 Toda 系统；主要结果为内在的中心化子参数化（intrinsic centralizer parametrization），即约化族等于由单值群闭包的主像（principal image）的中心化子所截出的正 Iwasawa 切片（positive Iwasawa slice）。

Result: 证明约化族可刻画为受中心化子约束的正 Iwasawa 切片；作为应用，将已有的球面度规存在性结果迁移至对角 Toda 源元组（diagonal Toda source tuple）。

Conclusion: 该参数化给出约化解的内在描述，并将球面度规存在性结论用于对角 Toda 源元组。

Abstract: We study reduced solutions of Toda systems generated by spherical metrics for an arbitrary complex simple Lie algebra. The main result is an intrinsic centralizer parametrization: for a spherical metric, the reduced family is the positive Iwasawa slice cut out by the centralizer of the principal image of closure of monodromy group. As an application, we transfer existing spherical-metric existence results to the diagonal Toda source tuple.

</details>


### [259] [Exact Elastodynamic Homogenization of Laminated Composites Revisited](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37060)
*Chunlin Wu,Huiming Yin*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文重新审视Willis关于周期性层合复合材料（periodic laminate composites）的弹性动力学均匀化（elastodynamic homogenization）方法，修正已发表公式中关于Floquet数（Floquet number）及有效属性的代数差异，并利用基于等效夹杂（equivalent inclusion-based method, EIM）的方法以特征场（eigen-fields）确定唯一的有效本构属性，两种方法结果高度一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Willis的精确均匀化方法依赖微结构特定的Green函数（microstructure-specific Green's function），但其已发表公式在Floquet数及相关有效属性上存在若干代数差异，需要重新检验与修正。同时，为将该框架拓展至多维Willis型均匀化，需要一个经过验证的替代均匀化方案作为基础。

Method: 本文重新推导周期性层合板的微结构特定Green函数，并识别与修正已发表公式中Floquet数及有效属性的代数差异；同时提出基于等效夹杂法（EIM）的均匀化方案，采用含两个特征场的比较介质（comparison medium）来模拟非均匀性：特征应变（eigenstrain）对应刚度失配，特征动量（eigen-momentum）对应质量密度失配，从而可直接使用一般Green函数。

Result: 使用更新后特定Green函数的精确均匀化方法与所提出的含特征场EIM均匀化方案进行对比，二者获得极好的一致性（excellent agreement）。

Conclusion: 本文修正了Willis框架中关于Floquet数与有效属性的公式差异，并验证了EIM特征场方案可等效复现精确均匀化结果，为将该均匀化框架拓展至多维Willis型均匀化（multi-dimensional Willis-type homogenization）提供了基础。

Abstract: This paper revisits Willis' elastodynamic homogenization (Mechanics of Materials, 41 (2009) 385-393) of periodic laminate composites and uses the equivalent inclusion-based method (EIM) to evaluate the unique effective constitutive properties with eigen-fields. The microstructure-specific Green's function is essential in Willis's exact homogenization method, which was derived from the corresponding periodic laminates. Our re-examination identifies several algebraic differences in the published formulae for the Floquet number and the related effective properties. In parallel, this paper simulates inhomogeneities with a comparison medium containing two eigen-fields, namely eigenstrain and eigen-momentum, to simulate stiffness and mass density mismatch, respectively. Therefore, the general Green's function can be directly used. The exact homogenization method with the updated specific Green's function is compared to the proposed EIM with eigen-fields homogenization scheme, and excellent agreement is obtained. The present paper provides a base to extend the homogenization framework to multi-dimensional Willis-type homogenization.

</details>


### [260] [Tripartite System Dynamics of Cooperation Ecological Resources and Adaptive Regulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37333)
*Linjie Liu,Yike Li,Shijia Hua*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文构建一个整合社会行为、生态资源动态与制度监管的三方协同演化系统（tripartite coevolutionary system），将制度干预建模为响应合作与生态目标偏差的自适应反馈机制。通过均衡、稳定性与相空间分析，识别系统的主要运行区制与转迁机制，揭示可持续均衡、双稳性（bistability）、极限环（limit cycles）及"镇压—反弹"政策陷阱（crackdown rebound policy trap）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于应对高成本社会合作（costly social cooperation）情境下的公地困境（commons dilemmas），即个体合作成本与集体资源可持续性之间的张力。作者试图超越单一维度分析，从系统层面解释治理失灵（governance failure）的成因，并为耦合人—环境系统中的自适应制度设计提供分析依据。

Method: 方法上建立三方协同演化系统，耦合社会行为、生态资源动态与制度监管三个子系统；其中制度干预被建模为自适应反馈机制（adaptive feedback mechanism），其强度随对既定合作目标与生态目标的偏差而调整。分析手段包括均衡分析（equilibrium analysis）、稳定性分析（stability analysis）与相空间刻画（phase space characterization），用以识别主要运行区制及转迁机制。

Result: 结果显示系统存在可持续运行均衡，即合作、生态资源与制度监管三者共存；并进一步确立了"完全合作、资源峰值可得性与持续最小干预"这一理想运行状态的结构稳定性（structural stability）。此外，系统还呈现双稳性与极限环，表明不同初始条件与参数配置可导致不同的长期结果。这些非线性机制产生持续的"镇压—反弹"政策陷阱：干预加强可短期改善合作与资源状况，但最终引发反复振荡并阻碍系统范围的共识（consensus）。

Conclusion: 结论指出，该框架为治理失灵提供了系统层面的解释，并为耦合人—环境系统（coupled human-environment systems）中自适应制度监管的设计提供分析性洞见。其核心启示在于：非线性反馈可能使强化干预适得其反，制度设计需关注长期动态而非短期矫正效果。

Abstract: To address commons dilemmas under costly social cooperation, this paper develops a tripartite coevolutionary system that integrates social behavior, ecological resource dynamics, and institutional regulation. Institutional intervention is modeled as an adaptive feedback mechanism whose intensity responds to deviations from prescribed cooperation and ecological targets. Through equilibrium analysis, stability analysis, and phase space characterization, we identify the principal operating regimes and transition mechanisms of the coupled system. The results show that the system admits a sustainable operating equilibrium in which cooperation, ecological resources, and institutional regulation coexist. We further establish the structural stability of a desirable operating state characterized by full cooperation, peak resource availability, and persistently minimal intervention. In addition to these stable regimes, the system exhibits bistability and limit cycles, indicating that alternative long term outcomes may arise from different initial conditions and parameter configurations. These nonlinear mechanisms generate a persistent crackdown rebound policy trap, in which intensified intervention temporarily improves cooperation and resource conditions but eventually produces recurrent oscillations and prevents system wide consensus. The proposed framework provides a system level explanation of governance failure and offers analytical insights for designing adaptive institutional regulation in coupled human environment systems.

</details>


### [261] [A posteriori controllability of non-linear remodelling in linear elastic continua](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37411)
*Salvatore Di Stefano,Marta Zoppello*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文将连续介质中的重构（remodelling）问题纳入几何控制理论（Geometric Control Theory）框架，基于等容 Bilby--Kroner--Lee 分解推导主重构伸长与取向变量的非线性状态空间表示，并分析其欠驱动可控性。结果显示三维情形可通过少量独立控制实现内部状态完全操控，而平面约化情形则丧失可控性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在对连续介质系统内部结构重构的事后可控性（a posteriori controllability）进行系统考察。作者将此问题置于几何控制理论中，以推动连续介质控制理论（Continuum Control Theory）作为受控内部结构变换分析与设计框架的发展。

Method: 采用等容 Bilby--Kroner--Lee 分解描述重构，其中内部结构变换由重构张量（remodelling tensor）表示，其演化由应力驱动的本构律（constitutive law）控制。假设小变形与小重构伸长但保留主重构方向的有限转动，推导以主重构伸长和取向变量表示的非线性状态空间（state-space）模型，将重构过程视为以材料位置为参数化的有限维非线性控制系统，并用几何控制理论工具研究欠驱动可控性问题。

Result: 研究刻画了平衡构型（equilibrium configurations），并对欠驱动可控性进行了分析。三维情形表明，重构伸长与取向之间的非线性耦合使得通过较少数量的独立控制即可实现内部状态的完全操控（complete steering）；而平面约化情形尽管存在非线性，仍表现出可控性的丧失。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了对重构可控性的首次系统性研究，并为连续介质控制理论作为连续介质中受控内部结构变换分析与设计框架的发展提供了进一步支持。

Abstract: We investigate the a posteriori controllability of remodelling within the framework of Geometric Control Theory applied to continuum systems. Remodelling is described through an isochoric Bilby--Kroner--Lee decomposition, where the internal structural transformation is represented by a remodelling tensor and its evolution is governed by a stress-driven constitutive law. Assuming infinitesimal deformation and remodelling stretches, while retaining finite rotations of the principal remodelling directions, we derive a nonlinear state-space representation in terms of principal remodelling stretches and orientation variables. This formulation allows the remodelling process to be interpreted as a finite-dimensional nonlinear control system parametrised by the material position. Equilibrium configurations are characterised, and under-actuated controllability problems are studied by means of the tools of Geometric Control Theory. The three-dimensional setting shows that the nonlinear coupling between remodelling stretches and orientations enables complete steering of the internal state through a reduced number of independent controls, while the planar reduction reveals a loss of controllability despite the presence of nonlinearity. The results provide a first systematic investigation of remodelling controllability and further support the development of Continuum Control Theory as a framework for the analysis and design of controlled internal structural transformations in continuous media.

</details>


### [262] [Distribution of Scattering Matrix Elements in the case of Symplectic Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37696)
*Nils Gluth,Thomas Guhr*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于超对称方法，在辛对称（symplectic symmetry）情形下推导了散射矩阵元的实部与虚部分布以及相应截面分布，从而补全了戴森三重分类（Dyson's threefold way）下的相关推导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 散射理论是获取量子系统信息的核心，但解析求解通常困难，因此借助统计方法研究普适特征颇具价值。既有基于海德堡方法（Heidelberg approach）的研究多针对无自旋或总角动量守恒系统，而近期实验进展使研究半整数自旋系统变得必要。

Method: 采用海德堡方法，以随机矩阵（random matrix）刻画目标系统的哈密顿量；并运用超对称（supersymmetry）技术，在辛对称（symplectic symmetry）情形下进行推导。

Result: 推导得到散射矩阵元的实部与虚部（real and imaginary parts of the scattering matrix elements）的分布，以及对应的截面（cross sections）分布；该结果补全了戴森三重分类（Dyson's threefold way）下的推导。

Conclusion: 辛对称情形（对应半整数自旋系统）的散射统计结果得以建立，使戴森三重分类中各类对称情形的推导体系趋于完整。

Abstract: Scattering theory is at the core of gaining information about most quantum systems. In general, analytic progress is difficult and the investigation of universal features using statistical methods is of interest. Commonly this is carried out within the Heidelberg approach which models the Hamiltonian of the target by a random matrix. Hitherto, such investigations were performed for systems that are effectively spinless or have total angular momentum conservation. In light of recent experimental progress it has become relevant to also investigate systems with half-integer spin. Using supersymmetry, we derive the distribution for the real and imaginary parts of the scattering matrix elements and the corresponding cross sections in the case of symplectic symmetry. These results complete the derivations for Dysons threefold way.

</details>


### [263] [Influence of Time Reversal Invariance Breaking on the Distribution of Off-Diagonal Scattering Matrix Elements and Cross Sections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37724)
*Nils Gluth,Ahmed Aldabag,Thomas Guhr*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在随机矩阵理论（Random Matrix Theory）框架下研究时间反演不变性（time reversal invariance）破缺对散射矩阵非对角元与截面分布普适行为的影响，利用超对称方法（supersymmetry）导出无其他对称性时半整数自旋与整数/无自旋系统的精确分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 散射理论是量子系统研究的关键工具，普适方法尤受关注。Dyson三分类（Threefold way）按时间反演行为划分系统，但许多实际系统（如可调外磁场中的系统）既非完全时间反演不变也非完全破缺，因此需研究部分破缺情形下的普适分布。

Method: 采用超对称方法（supersymmetry），在无进一步对称性条件下，针对半整数自旋及整数/无自旋系统，推导非对角散射矩阵元与截面分布的精确解析表达式。

Result: 成功获得非对角散射矩阵元与截面分布的精确表达式；发现这些分布具有对称性，而散射矩阵元本身并不对称。

Conclusion: 研究刻画了时间反演不变性破缺对前述分布普适行为的影响，所得结果可能显著便利于时间反演不变性破缺的数据分析。

Abstract: Scattering theory is a key tool for the investigation of quantum systems. Universal approaches are of interest, particularly in the framework of Random Matrix Theory. Dyson's Threefold way classifies systems according to their behavior under time reversal. In these three cases the distribution of off-diagonal scattering matrix elements and cross sections were recently calculated. However, in many applications systems neither display full nor fully broken time reversal invariance, such as systems in tuneable external magnetic fields. The study of these systems has a long history, also in Random Matrix Theory. We investigate the influence of time reversal invariance breaking on the universal behavior of the mentioned distributions. Using supersymmetry we succeed in deriving exact expressions for the distribution of off-diagonal scattering matrix elements and cross sections in systems with half-integer or integer/no spin in the absence of further symmetries. We find that the distributions are symmetric while the scattering matrix elements are not. Furthermore, our results might considerably facilitate the data analysis of time reversal invariance breaking.

</details>


### [264] [Breaking the Spin Degeneracy for Distributions of Off-Diagonal Scattering Matrix Elements in the Case of Symplectic Symmetry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37738)
*Nils Gluth,Thomas Guhr*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文提出可在实验上实现的模型，通过在散射通道（channels）中引入自旋偏好（spin preference）来打破散射矩阵元（scattering matrix elements）分布的全自旋取向简并，并借助超对称（supersymmetry）推导出相应的非线性 sigma 模型（nonlinear sigma model），在一阶近似下精确计算了实部与虚部分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新近得到的散射矩阵元分布精确表达式显示，所有入射与出射自旋取向组合的分布是等同的，即存在自旋简并（spin degeneracy）。作者希望以可实验实现的模型来主动打破这一简并，从而检验简并破缺对分布的影响。

Method: 在通道中引入自旋偏好以打破简并，构建适合实验实现的模型；利用超对称（supersymmetry）方法推导对应的非线性 sigma 模型（nonlinear sigma model）；以衡量偏好程度的参数做一阶展开，精确计算实部与虚部的分布。

Result: 打破自旋简并使受偏好与不受偏好的自旋取向得到不同的分布；但值得注意的是，这些改变后的分布在实部与虚部上与未破缺自旋简并情形下的分布相同。

Conclusion: 在可实验实现的自旋偏好模型中，自旋简并破缺虽然改变了不同自旋取向的分布形式，却在一阶近似下保留了实部与虚部分布与简并情形一致的显著特征。

Abstract: Recently derived exact expressions for the distributions of scattering matrix elements show that all combinations of ingoing and outgoing spin orientations are equally distributed. We propose a model suitable for experimental realization that breaks this degeneracy by introducing a spin preference in the channels. With the help of supersymmetry we derive a corresponding nonlinear sigma model. We exactly calculate the distributions of real and imaginary part in first order of the parameter measuring the preference. Quite remarkably, while breaking the spin degeneracy leads to different distributions for the favored and unfavored spin orientation, the real and imaginary parts of these altered distributions are the same as in the case of unbroken spin degeneracy.

</details>


### [265] [Continuum Limit for One-Dimensional Moiré Relaxation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37943)
*Hao Lan,Michael Hott,Jeff Calder,Alexander B. Watson*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文证明了一维原子尺度莫尔（moiré）弛豫模型的最小化子在连续极限下收敛于常用连续泛函的最小化子。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为实践中常用于建模莫尔材料弛豫的连续泛函提供原子尺度的严格基础，弥合离散原子模型与连续模型之间的理论缺口。

Method: 构建含 N 与 N+1 原子/共同胞的两条周期链，在原子尺度上精确莫尔周期；能量由谐波最近邻层内相互作用与偶数 C^2、具多项式衰减的层间对势生成的非局部作用构成；再取连续极限，使线性弹性（linear elasticity）与周期错配势（misfit potential）耦合。

Result: 原子尺度模型的最小化子收敛到相应一维连续极限泛函的最小化子，该泛函即弹性与周期错配势的耦合形式。

Conclusion: 结果为常用连续弛豫模型的合理性提供了严格的变分收敛依据，并揭示错配势源自层间对势的周期化。

Abstract: We establish convergence of minimizers for a minimal one dimensional atomic-scale model for relaxation in moiré materials to those of a one-dimensional continuum limit functional analogous to those commonly used in practice to model relaxation. The atomic-scale model consists of two periodic chains containing $N$ and $N+1$ atoms per common cell and is exactly moiré-periodic at the atomic scale. Its energy combines harmonic nearest-neighbor intralayer interactions with nonlocal interlayer interactions generated by an even $C^2$ pair potential with polynomial decay. The associated continuum energy couples linear elasticity to a periodic misfit potential, obtained by periodizing the pair potential, which penalizes unfavorable local stackings.

</details>


### [266] [Quantum percolation conjecture on regular trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38017)
*Simon Becker,Izak Oltman*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该文研究正则树（regular tree）上伯努利键渗流（Bernoulli bond percolation）的临界阈值比较，证明无限簇（infinite cluster）的出现严格早于绝对连续谱（absolutely continuous spectrum）的出现，从而结合 Bordenave 定理给出 \(p_c<p_{ac}<1\)。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 渗流阈值（percolation threshold）与谱阈值（spectral threshold）之间的关系此前已知部分结果，例如 Bordenave 定理给出 \(p_{ac}\le 1\) 一侧的界，但两者之间的严格分离尚未明确。该文旨在确定无限簇出现与绝对连续谱出现这两个相变点的先后次序。

Method: 在度数至少为三的任意正则树上，考察伯努利键渗流；在每个开簇（open cluster）上取邻接算子（adjacency operator），并构造一个显式的超临界区间（supercritical interval），在该区间内分析该算子几乎必然的谱类型。

Result: 证明在显式的超临界区间上，每个开簇的邻接算子几乎必然不含绝对连续分量（absolutely continuous component）。由此得到无限簇的出现严格早于绝对连续谱的出现，即在无限簇已出现的参数区间内，谱仍可保持无绝对连续部分。

Conclusion: 对所有度数至少为三的正则树上的伯努利键渗流，均有 \(p_c<p_{ac}<1\)，即渗流临界概率严格小于绝对连续谱出现的临界概率，且后者严格小于 1。

Abstract: For Bernoulli bond percolation on every regular tree of degree at least three, we prove that infinite clusters appear strictly before absolutely continuous spectrum. Throughout an explicit supercritical interval, the adjacency operator of every open cluster almost surely has no absolutely continuous component. Together with Bordenave's theorem, this gives $p_c<p_{ac}<1$.

</details>


### [267] [On the Spectrum of Some Temperley-Lieb Spin Chains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38074)
*Robert Ferydouni,Bruno Nachtergaele*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述自旋-$s$链在单态投影近邻相互作用下的谱理论结果，并给出若干新结果，包括跨越对称扇区的能级铁磁有序、有限体积谱间隙的一致下界，以及完全极化态 GNS 哈密顿量精确谱隙 $1-2/d$，并讨论基态高简并导致的对称破缺与间隙不稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该相互作用在归一化后生成环参数（loop parameter）$d=2s+1$ 的 Temperley-Lieb 代数（Temperley-Lieb algebra），其谱性质具有独立数学意义。此前对自旋-$\tfrac12$ $XXZ$ 链已有能级铁磁有序结果，作者希望将其推广至一般自旋-$s$ 情形并确定谱间隙。高简并基态与连续对称破缺、间隙稳定性之间的关系亦构成研究动机。

Method: 主要工具为 Temperley-Lieb 代数结构分析。作者证明该模型在对称扇区之间满足能级铁磁有序（ferromagnetic ordering of energy levels），据此导出有限体积谱间隙（finite volume spectral gap）的一致下界；将变分估计（variational estimate）与 Knabe 有限尺寸判据（Knabe's finite size criterion）结合，确定完全极化态 GNS 哈密顿量的精确谱隙；并证明开链基态投影算子满足 Jones-Wenzl 递推（Jones-Wenzl recursion）。

Result: 证明了跨越对称扇区的能级铁磁有序，推广了 Nachtergaele、Spitzer 与 Starr（2004）关于自旋-$\tfrac12$ $XXZ$ 链的结果。得到有限体积谱间隙的一致下界。完全极化态 GNS 哈密顿量的精确谱隙为 $1-2/d$，该值同时是最大混合基态（maximally mixed ground state）间隙的下界。开链基态投影算子满足 Jones-Wenzl 递推，从而恢复已知结论：简并度为量子整数 $[L+1]_q$，其中 $q+q^{-1}=d$。

Conclusion: 基态的高度简并使模型能够在保持有隙（gapped）的同时破坏连续对称性，并导致该间隙不稳定（unstable）。

Abstract: We review mathematical results about the spectrum of spin-$s$ chains with a nearest neighbor interaction given by the projection onto the singlet state of two spin-$s$ degrees of freedom. Up to normalization, these interaction terms generate a Temperley-Lieb algebra at loop parameter $d=2s+1$. We also present several new results. First, we prove a ferromagnetic ordering of energy levels across symmetry sectors of the model, thus generalizing a previous result of Nachtergaele, Spitzer and Starr (2004) for the spin-$\tfrac12$ $XXZ$ chain. This enables us to determine a uniform lower bound on the finite volume spectral gap for these spin chains. By combining a variational estimate with Knabe's finite size criterion, we show that the exact spectral gap of the GNS Hamiltonian of the fully polarized state is $1-2/d$. Furthermore, this value serves as a lower bound for the gap of the maximally mixed ground state. Finally, we show that the ground state projectors on open chains satisfy the Jones-Wenzl recursion, thereby recovering a known result that the degeneracies are the quantum integers $[L+1]_q$ for $q+q^{-1}=d$. We discuss how the high degeneracy of the ground states allows the model to break a continuous symmetry while remaining gapped, and renders that gap unstable.

</details>

### [268] [Advancing Fuzzy-Sphere CFTs: $\mathrm{SO}(3)$-Rotation-Resolving Exact Diagonalization, 3D Ising Heavy Primaries, and Quasi-Hole-Space Projection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.35960)
*Zheng Zhou,Yin-Chen He*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作通过利用对称性与希尔伯特空间结构改进模糊球正则化框架，发展出保持完整SO(3)旋转对称的精确对角化算法，在三维Ising CFT中识别出约100个初级算子，并给出与拓扑序交织的CFT研究策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模糊球正则化（fuzzy-sphere regularization）已成为研究三维共形场论（conformal field theories, CFTs）的有力工具，但计算成本与对称性利用不足限制了其应用范围；同时此前模糊球与自举（bootstrap）结果在宇称奇（parity-odd）标量初级算子上存在分歧，需要厘清。

Method: 利用对称性与希尔伯特空间（Hilbert-space）结构，发展出能解析完整SO(3)旋转对称的精确对角化（exact-diagonalization）算法；并将该方法应用于三维Ising CFT，此外通过相应模型量子霍尔波函数（如Laughlin态）的准空穴（quasi-hole）态构造显著缩减的希尔伯特空间来研究涉及分数量子霍尔态的临界点。

Result: 新算法将计算成本降低数百倍；在三维Ising CFT中识别出约100个初级算子，包含大量此前未知的重标量初级算子，标度维度（scaling dimension）最高至Δ≈16.5；解决了关于宇称奇标量初级算子的模糊球与自举结果之间的张力；并识别出与φ^4拉格朗日描述中φ^n算子相关的标量初级算子分支（始于identity、σ、ε、ε'、σ'），其波函数呈现与球面保面积微分同胚（area-preserving diffeomorphisms）不变态密切相关的半经典结构。

Conclusion: 研究显示涉及分数量子霍尔态的临界点可在由准空穴态张成的显著缩减希尔伯特空间内研究，这为探索与拓扑有序相（topologically ordered phases）交织的CFT提供了一般性策略。

Abstract: Fuzzy-sphere regularization has recently emerged as a powerful framework for studying three-dimensional conformal field theories (CFTs). In this work, we exploit symmetry and Hilbert-space structures to substantially advance this framework. We develop an exact-diagonalization algorithm that resolves the full $\mathrm{SO}(3)$ rotational symmetry, reducing computational costs by factors of several hundred. For the three-dimensional Ising CFT, we identify around 100 primary operators, including a large number of previously unknown heavy scalar primaries up to scaling dimension $Δ\approx16.5$. We also resolve the tension between previous fuzzy-sphere and bootstrap results regarding the parity-odd scalar primary. Furthermore, we identify a branch of scalar primaries associated with $φ^n$ operators in the $φ^4$ Lagrangian description, beginning with the identity, $σ$, $ε$, $ε'$, and $σ'$. Their wave functions exhibit a semi-classical structure closely related to states invariant under area-preserving diffeomorphisms of the sphere. Finally, we show that critical points involving fractional quantum Hall states can be studied within a substantially reduced Hilbert space spanned by quasi-hole states of the corresponding model quantum Hall wave functions like the Laughlin state. This suggests a general strategy for exploring CFTs intertwined with topologically ordered phases.

</details>


### [269] [Spectroscopy of phonon-coupled integer and fractional Chern insulators: emergence of polarons and chirality deficit of graviton mode](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36027)
*Min Long,Yuzhu Wang,Zi Yang Meng*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过密度矩阵重整化群（DMRG）结合局域基优化，研究平带 Haldane 模型与动态 Holstein 声子的耦合，揭示了声子主要通过修饰电荷中性集体模式来驱动 Chern 绝缘体（CI）与分数量子 Chern 绝缘体（FCI）的失稳，并给出统一的基于激发的图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强电子-声子耦合（electron-phonon coupling）可使 Chern 绝缘体与分数量子 Chern 绝缘体失稳并转向电荷有序，但两者失稳是否共享同一微观机制尚不清楚，本文即针对此问题展开研究。

Method: 在整数与分数填充下，研究耦合动态 Holstein 声子（Holstein phonons）的平带 Haldane 模型，采用带局域基优化（local basis optimization）的密度矩阵重整化群（density-matrix renormalization group, DMRG）方法。

Result: 声子的低能效应主要由其对电荷中性集体模式的修饰主导：在 CI 与 FCI 中，声子分别将激子（exciton）与磁旋子（magnetoroton）修饰为激子极化子（exciton polaron）与磁旋子极化子（magnetoroton polaron）。这些模式在接近相变时显著软化，其能量极小值动量预示了电荷有序相的序波矢；声子谱同时获得源自激子与磁旋子模式的色散特征。在 FCI 相内，电子-声子耦合还增强引力子响应（graviton response）的相反手性谱权重，导致手性亏损（chirality deficit）。

Conclusion: 本文为 CI 与 FCI 中声子驱动的失稳提供了统一的、基于激发的图像，并确立晶格动力学（lattice dynamics）作为探测其中性集体模式的探针。

Abstract: Strong electron-phonon coupling can destabilize both Chern insulators (CIs) and fractional Chern insulators (FCIs) in favor of charge order, but whether the instabilities in CI and FCI share a common microscopic mechanism remains unclear. In this paper, we address this question by studying a flat-band Haldane model coupled to dynamical Holstein phonons at integer and fractional fillings using density-matrix renormalization group with local basis optimization. We find that the low-energy effects of phonons are dominated by their dressing of charge-neutral collective modes. In the CI and FCI, phonons dress excitons and magnetorotons into composite modes that we identify as exciton polarons and magnetoroton polarons, respectively. These modes soften strongly upon approaching the transition, while the momenta of their energy minima anticipate the ordering wave vectors of the charge-ordered phases. The phonon spectrum, in turn, acquires dispersive features inherited from the exciton and magnetoroton modes, providing direct lattice signatures of these neutral excitations. Within the FCI phase, electron-phonon coupling also enhances the opposite-chirality spectral weight of the graviton response, thereby resulting in a chirality deficit. Our results provide a unified excitation-based picture of phonon-driven instabilities in CIs and FCIs and establish lattice dynamics as a probe of their neutral collective modes.

</details>


### [270] [Excitonic Stripe Order in the Two-Orbital Hubbard-Kanamori Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36051)
*Rafael D. Soares,Luke Staszewski,Chunhan Feng,Alexander Wietek*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文在方格子双轨道 Hubbard-Kanamori 模型中研究激子序的条纹类似物，揭示其在掺杂下形成激子条纹与螺旋激子密度波，并说明 Hund 交换与晶场劈裂如何选择激子通道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激子凝聚（excitonic condensation）与条纹形成（stripe formation）是电子关联（electronic correlations）的两种不同表现，前者天然出现在多轨道体系，后者是掺杂关联电子模型的显著特征；本文旨在探究二者的类比与统一。

Method: 采用无限制实空间 Hartree-Fock（unrestricted real-space Hartree-Fock）计算，并结合随机相近似不稳定性分析（random-phase-approximation instability analysis），在方格子双轨道 Hubbard-Kanamori 模型中确定 Kanamori 相互作用对激子通道的选取。

Result: Hund 交换（Hund's exchange）符号控制凝聚体自旋性质：铁磁 Hund 耦合倾向三重态激子序（triplet excitonic order），反铁磁 Hund 耦合稳定单态激子序（singlet excitonic order）。掺杂后，公度激子密度波（commensurate excitonic density waves）发展为非公度织构，包括激子条纹及三重态区的螺旋激子密度波（spiral excitonic density waves）。晶场劈裂（crystal-field splitting）通过改变轨道间嵌套（inter-orbital nesting）重组激子不稳定性，并可在无对跳跃时稳定三重态激子序。

Conclusion: 研究确立激子条纹（excitonic stripes）为多轨道关联体系的一种独特对称破缺态，并指出其稳定化的微观途径。

Abstract: Excitonic condensation and stripe formation are two distinct manifestations of electronic correlations. While excitonic order naturally arises in multi-orbital systems, stripe order is a prominent feature of doped correlated-electron models. Here, we investigate an excitonic analogue of stripe order in the two--orbital Hubbard-Kanamori model on the square lattice, characterized by a spatial modulation of inter-orbital particle-hole coherence and, in the orbital-parity-symmetric limit, spontaneous breaking of a relative orbital $\mathbb{Z}_2$ symmetry. Using unrestricted real-space Hartree-Fock calculations complemented by random-phase-approximation instability analysis, we determine how the Kanamori interactions select different excitonic channels. The sign of Hund's exchange controls the spin character of the condensate: ferromagnetic Hund coupling favors triplet excitonic order, whereas antiferromagnetic Hund coupling stabilizes singlet excitonic order. Upon doping, commensurate excitonic density waves develop incommensurate textures, including excitonic stripes and, in the triplet sector, spiral excitonic density waves. We further show that crystal-field splitting strongly reorganizes the excitonic instability by modifying inter-orbital nesting and can stabilize triplet excitonic order even in the absence of pair hopping. Our results establish excitonic stripes as a distinct symmetry-broken state of multi-orbital correlated systems and identify microscopic routes for their stabilization.

</details>


### [271] [Magnetic excitations of the strongly-anisotropic triangular XXZ model: a projected spin-product state approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36140)
*Achille Mauri,Frédéric Mila*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文提出并分析了一种"投影"相干态近似（projected coherent-state approximation），用于研究强Ising型各向异性下自旋-1/2三角晶格XXZ模型（triangular XXZ model）的基态与激发谱，并与钴氧化物K2Co(SeO3)2（KCSO）的实验结果进行比较，较线性自旋波理论（linear spin-wave theory）有显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近期实验激发了对强Ising型各向异性（Ising-like anisotropy）下自旋-1/2三角XXZ模型基态性质与激发谱的研究兴趣，需要发展能够刻画强约束希尔伯特空间（Hilbert space）内低能物理的近似方法。

Method: 构造"投影"相干态近似：将自旋乘积态（spin-product states）投影到三角Ising反铁磁体（triangular Ising antiferromagnet）的低能构型上；随后由含时变分原理（time-dependent variational principle）导出的运动方程进行线性化，以获得激发谱。

Result: 该近似给出了基态有序结构与纵向磁场（longitudinal field）下磁化曲线（magnetization curve）的描述，并自然导出强约束希尔伯特空间内的类自旋波激发（spin-wave-like excitations）。零场下变分近似呈现准简并态（quasi-degenerate states）构成的极平坦能量景观，导致激发谱中出现低能赝Goldstone模式（pseudo-Goldstone mode）。与KCSO实验相比，该近似较线性自旋波理论有显著改进。

Conclusion: 该方法能自然描述强约束希尔伯特空间内的激发，并较线性自旋波理论显著改善与实验的符合度；但赝Goldstone能隙（pseudo-Goldstone gap）的数值及低场下M点附近的色散（dispersion）仍存在偏差。

Abstract: Recent experiments have sparked interest on the ground-state properties and on the excitation spectrum of the spin-$1/2$ triangular XXZ model with strong Ising-like anisotropy. In this work, we introduce and analyze a ``projected'' coherent-state approximation to the model, constructed by projecting spin-product states onto the low-energy configurations of the triangular Ising antiferromagnet. Within this approximation, we discuss the structure of the ground-state ordering and the magnetization curve under a longitudinal field. We then discuss the excitation spectrum by linearizing equations of motion deriving from a time-dependent variational principle. This leads to a natural description of spin-wave-like excitations within the strongly constrained Hilbert space. At zero field, the variational approximation leads to a very flat landscape of quasi-degenerate states, which results in a low-energy pseudo-Goldstone mode in the excitation spectrum. We compare our results to experimental measurements on the cobaltite K$_{2}$Co(SeO$_{3}$)$_{2}$ (KCSO). Our approximation leads to a dramatic improvement as compared to linear spin-wave theory. However, there remain deviations in the value of the pseudo-Goldstone gap and in the dispersion near $M$ points at low field.

</details>


### [272] [Magnetic properties and Fermi-surface topology of kagome magnet HoV$_6$Sn$_6$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36258)
*Tucker Beekmann,Ganesh Pokharel,Wyatt Edward Ackerman,Stephen D. Wilson,David E. Graf,Keshav Shrestha*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该研究结合磁化测量、隧道二极管振荡（TDO）与第一性原理计算，系统表征了笼目磁体（kagome magnet）HoV₆Sn₆的磁性与费米面拓扑，揭示其磁各向异性、多带费米面结构以及V笼目层主导的低能电子性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为深入理解笼目磁体（kagome magnet）HoV₆Sn₆中磁性与电子结构之间的关系，特别是其磁各向异性来源与费米面（Fermi surface）拓扑特征。

Method: 采用磁化测量与隧道二极管振荡（tunnel-diode-oscillator, TDO）技术，在最高41.5 T磁场、最低0.35 K温度下探测量子振荡；同时通过第一性原理计算电子能带、态密度（density of states, DOS）与费米面。

Result: 高温磁化呈居里-外斯（Curie-Weiss）行为，T_c≈2.4 K以下出现长程磁序并表现出显著磁各向异性，面外与面内饱和磁矩分别约9.5与7 μ_B/Ho；TDO信号呈现多频率量子振荡，频率可达约9 kT。计算显示费米能级附近存在平带、范霍夫奇点（van Hove singularities）与类狄拉克交叉，态密度由V-d轨道主导，并支持Ho磁矩沿c轴的铁磁基态，其计算振荡频率与实验总体吻合。

Conclusion: 实验与理论结果共同揭示了HoV₆Sn₆的磁各向异性与电子结构，并表明其具有多带费米面（multiband Fermi-surface）拓扑特征。

Abstract: We report a study of the magnetic properties and Fermi-surface topology of the kagome magnet HoV$_6$Sn$_6$ using magnetization and tunnel-diode-oscillator (TDO) measurements. The temperature-dependent magnetization exhibits Curie-Weiss behavior at high temperatures and reveals long-range magnetic order below $T_c$ $\approx$ 2.4 K. Below Tc, the field-dependent magnetization reveals pronounced magnetic anisotropy, with saturation moments of $\approx$ 9.5 and 7 $μ_B$ /Ho for fields applied along the out-of-plane and in-plane directions, respectively. To probe the Fermi surface, we perform TDO measurements in magnetic fields up to 41.5 T and at temperatures down to 0.35 K. The TDO signal exhibits well-defined quantum oscillations with multiple frequencies extending up to $\sim$ 9 kT. Angular- and temperature-dependent measurements are used to determine the Fermi-surface geometry and cyclotron effective masses, respectively. To elucidate the electronic structure, we perform first-principles calculations of the electronic bands, density of states (DOS), and Fermi surface. The calculated band structure exhibits flat-band features, van Hove singularities, and Dirac-like crossings near the Fermi level. The DOS near the Fermi level is dominated by the V-d orbitals, indicating that the V kagome layers play a major role in determining the low-energy electronic properties of HoV6Sn6. Our calculations further support a ferromagnetic ground state with the Ho moments oriented along the c axis, consistent with neutron-scattering measurements. The calculated quantum-oscillation frequencies from the Fermi-surface pockets of HoV6Sn6 are in overall good agreement with the experimentally observed frequencies. Our combined experimental and theoretical results provide insight into the magnetic anisotropy and electronic structure of HoV$_6$Sn$_6$ and reveal its multiband Fermi-surface topology.

</details>


### [273] [Quantum metric induced nonlinear transport in the hidden loop-current phase of kagome metal RbV$_3$Sb$_5$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36353)
*Jia-Chen Shi,Shupeng Xu,Utkarsh Khandelwal,Nashra Pistawala,Luminita Harnagea,Steven J. May,Ritesh Agarwal*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作利用三阶非线性输运（third-order nonlinear transport）在 RbV₃Sb₅ 中探测被提出的隐藏低温相，发现约 35 K 处纵向与横向三次谐波响应同时出现异常，并将其归因于具有时间反演对称性破缺（time-reversal symmetry breaking）的电子重构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: kagome 金属 AV₃Sb₅（A=K、Rb、Cs）中可能存在的环流序（loop-current order）隐藏低温相，其现有实验特征依然微弱且间接，因此需要更灵敏的探测手段。

Method: 采用三阶非线性输运测量 RbV₃Sb₅，分析纵向三次响应随温度与方向的变化及其对常规弛豫时间标度（relaxation time scaling）的偏离，同时考察横向三次响应中磁场奇宇称（field-odd）分量；并结合环流序电荷密度波（charge-density-wave）模型进行解释。

Result: 在约 35 K 处，纵向三次响应出现强拐点与显著方向各向异性，明显偏离常规弛豫时间标度；同一温度下横向三次响应获得磁场奇宇称分量，二者起始温度一致。量子度规四极（quantum metric quadrupole）被证实直接贡献于纵向三阶响应，其在环流序模型中的方向选择性增强可解释观测到的角度重构。

Conclusion: 高阶非线性输运可将电子对称性与量子度规（quantum metric）的细微变化转化为可测电学信号，为研究量子材料中复杂电子相提供有效手段。

Abstract: A hidden low-temperature phase with possible loop-current order has been proposed in the kagome metals AV$_3$Sb$_5$ (A=K, Rb or Cs), but its experimental signatures remain subtle and indirect. Here, we use third-order nonlinear transport to probe this hidden phase in RbV$_3$Sb$_5$. At ~35 K, the longitudinal cubic response develops a strong kink and pronounced directional anisotropy with a strong departure from common relaxation time scaling, while the transverse cubic response acquires a magnetic field-odd component at the same temperature. Their coincident onset identifies the third-harmonic response as a sensitive marker of the low-temperature electronic reconstruction with time-reversal broken symmetry. Crucially, we find that the quantum metric quadrupole contributes directly to the longitudinal third-order response. In a loop-current charge-density-wave model, its direction selective enhancement captures the observed angular reconstruction. Our work shows how higher-order nonlinear transport translates subtle changes in electronic symmetry and quantum metric into measurable electrical signatures in quantum materials that is important for studying complex electronic phases of matter.

</details>


### [274] [Artificial versus Natural Atoms: The uncanny capability of the many-body Schrödinger equation to produce emergent behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36356)
*Constantine Yannouleas*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文综述过去25年关于“人造原子与分子”（artificial atoms and molecules）物理的研究进展，其核心机制是有限个被束缚的费米子或玻色子形成维格纳分子（Wigner molecules, WMs），即体相维格纳晶体的有限量子类比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 体相维格纳晶体早有研究，但其有限量子类比——由相互排斥的束缚粒子构成的人造原子与分子——在近25年随量子纳米器件制造而成为重要课题。该方向既面向量子信息与量子计算的技术应用，也服务于多体物理基础问题（如强关联与分数量子霍爾效应）。

Method: 采用综述（review）方式，梳理理论与实验进展；物理来源归结为多体薛定谔方程（many-body Schrödinger equation, MBSE）在强粒子间关联区域的解，该区域由势能主导动能、或由高磁场、或由快速旋转所致。研究对象涵盖二维半导体与莫尔过渡金属二硫族化物量子点，以及囚禁的超冷中性原子或离子等多种人工量子器件与装置（单阱或多阱、可变形状排布）。

Result: 维格纳分子化（Wigner molecularization）的主要量子效应包括：谱能隙的强烈淬灭、类天然分子的转动-振动谱（rovibational spectra）的出现、纠缠，以及由外部微扰导致的钉扎（pinning）。在高磁场或快速旋转条件下，WMs 为分数量子霍爾效应（fractional quantum Hall effect）提供了替代理论。

Conclusion: 维格纳分子物理源于强关联区域的多体薛定谔方程解，为理解人造原子与分子的新奇物理提供了统一框架，并对量子信息、量子计算及多体基础物理具有重要前景。

Abstract: The paper reviews the theoretical and experimental progress achieved in the last 25 years in understanding the novel physics of artificial atoms and molecules as arising from the formation of Wigner molecules (WMs) of localized (to a stronger or lesser extent) fermionic or bosonic particles, which are finite quantum analogs of the more familiar bulk Wigner crystal. The term artificial atoms, as used here, encompasses a broad range of recently fabricated quantum nanodevices and experimental apparatuses consisting of a finite number of mutually repelling confined particles, including two-dimensional semiconductor and moiré transition metal dichalcogenide quantum dots, as well as trapped ultracold neutral atoms or ions. These nano-sized or micro-sized artificial devices and apparatuses (in single well or multi-well of variable-shape arrangements) hold a great promise for technological applications in the field of quantum information and quantum computers, as well as for advances in fundamental many-body physics. Prominent quantum effects of Wigner molecularization are the strong quenching of the spectral energy gaps, the appearance of rovibational spectra (in analogy with natural molecules), entanglement, and pinning due to an external perturbation. In high magnetic fields or at rapid rotation, WMs provide an alternative theory to the fractional quantum Hall effect. The physics of Wigner molecules is shown to derive from the solutions of the many-body Schrödinger equation (MBSE) in the regime of strong interparticle correlations arising from the dominance of the potential over the kinetic energy, or from a high magnetic field, as well as from a rapid rotation... (continues in the paper).

</details>


### [275] [Probing the electronic structure of $\mathrm{UTe}_2$ with ARPES and high-energy spectroscopy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36706)
*Shin-ichi Fujimori*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文综述了$\mathrm{UTe}_2$的谱学研究进展，涵盖动量分辨角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）与多种X射线谱学手段，发现所推断的电子结构存在显著的测量技术依赖性，并支持其具有$5f^2$与$5f^3$构型混合的中间价（intermediate-valence）基态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $\mathrm{UTe}_2$因非常规超导（unconventional superconductivity）及可能实现的自旋三重态（spin-triplet）、拓扑非平庸配对态而成为强关联材料研究热点。核心悬而未决的问题是$\mathrm{U}\,5f$电子的性质及其对低能准粒子态的参与方式。

Method: 综述近期谱学研究，包括动量分辨角分辨光电子能谱（ARPES）以及元素与构型敏感的X射线探针，如X射线吸收谱（XAS）、X射线吸收近边结构（XANES）、X射线磁圆二色性（XMCD）、共振X射线发射谱（RXES）与共振非弹性X射线散射（RIXS）；并与密度泛函理论加动力学平均场理论（DFT+DMFT）等现代电子结构计算相结合。

Result: 关键发现是所推断的电子结构存在明显的技术依赖性（pronounced technique dependence），不同谱学手段给出的图像并不一致。

Conclusion: 综合结果支持$\mathrm{UTe}_2$具有含显著$5f^2$与$5f^3$构型混合的中间价基态；并明确了将正常态电子结构与超导机制相联系所需的关键实验与理论基准。

Abstract: $\mathrm{UTe}_2$ has emerged as one of the most intensively studied strongly correlated materials in recent years owing to its unconventional superconductivity and the possible realization of a spin-triplet, topologically nontrivial pairing state. A central open issue concerns the nature of the $\mathrm{U}\,5f$ electrons and their participation in low-energy quasiparticle states. In this review, we summarize recent spectroscopic studies of $\mathrm{UTe}_2$ using momentum-resolved angle-resolved photoemission spectroscopy (ARPES) and element- and configuration-sensitive X-ray probes, including X-ray absorption spectroscopy (XAS), X-ray absorption near-edge structure (XANES), X-ray magnetic circular dichroism (XMCD), resonant X-ray emission spectroscopy (RXES), and resonant inelastic X-ray scattering (RIXS). A key finding is the pronounced technique dependence of the inferred electronic structure. We synthesize the present spectroscopic picture by integrating these results with modern electronic-structure calculations such as density functional theory plus dynamical mean-field theory (DFT+DMFT). These findings support an intermediate-valence ground state with significant admixture of $5f^2$ and $5f^3$ configurations in $\mathrm{UTe}_2$, and they delineate key experimental and theoretical benchmarks needed to connect the normal-state electronic structure to the superconducting mechanism.

</details>


### [276] [Quantized Hyperfine Field at an Implanted $μ^+$ Site in PrPb$_3$: Interplay between Localized $f$ Electrons and an Interstitial Charged Particle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36868)
*T. U. Ito,W. Higemoto,K. Ohishi,N. Nishida,R. H. Heffner,Y. Aoki,A. Amato,T. Onimaru,H. S. Suzuki*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 在PrPb₃中利用μ⁺自旋旋转与弛豫（μSR）研究间隙氢样粒子对局域f电子的影响，首次在f电子化合物中观测到自发μ⁺自旋进动，证实其源于μ⁺与最近邻¹⁴¹Pr超精细增强核自旋的耦合，并指示间隙带电粒子改变了f电子态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于探究间隙氢样粒子（interstitial hydrogen-like particle）对局域f电子（localized f electrons）的局部影响，该问题关系到带电杂质如何改变f电子化合物中的电子态。

Method: 采用μ⁺自旋旋转与弛豫（μ⁺ spin rotation and relaxation，μSR）测量PrPb₃中间隙粒子周围的局域磁性环境。

Result: 首次在f电子化合物中观测到具有谐波频率的自发μ⁺自旋进动（spontaneous μ⁺ spin precession）；信号源自μ⁺自旋与最近邻（nn）¹⁴¹Pr超精细增强核自旋（hyperfine-enhanced nuclear spin）之间的耦合，该核自旋具有Ising型各向异性；且相较于体态（bulk）¹⁴¹Pr，最近邻¹⁴¹Pr的自旋动力学（spin dynamics）被显著抑制。

Conclusion: 上述结果强烈表明，间隙带电粒子（interstitial charged particle）导致了f电子态的改。变。

Abstract: The local effect of an interstitial hydrogen-like particle on localized $f$ electrons was studied in PrPb$_3$ by means of $μ^+$ spin rotation and relaxation. Spontaneous $μ^+$ spin precession with harmonic frequencies was observed for the first time in $f$ electron compounds. We demonstrate that the signal is derived from a coupling between the $μ^+$ spin and the hyperfine-enhanced nuclear spin of nearest neighbor (nn) $^{141}$Pr with Ising-like anisotropy. The signal also suggests a marked suppression of spin dynamics of the nn $^{141}$Pr in comparison with that of the bulk $^{141}$Pr. These facts strongly indicate modification of the $f$ electronic state due to the interstitial charged particle.

</details>


### [277] [Ergodicity in a hole-dopped Anderson spin insulator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36936)
*M. S. Bahovadinov,A. A. Markov,G. V. Shlyapnikov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究一维随机磁场中双组分费米子（$t-J_{XX}$ 模型）在掺入单个空穴（hole）后自旋部门的稳定性，指出单个空穴在弱无序极限下可使自旋链热化（thermalization），并导致空穴离域化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多体局域化（many-body localization）系统中，相互作用系统可能无法充当自身的热浴（bath）；而在电子系统中，电荷与自旋自由度可互为热浴。因此需澄清：在自旋部门处于 Anderson 局域化的绝缘体中，引入电荷掺杂（charge doping）是否会破坏其局域性。

Method: 采用一维晶格中的双组分费米子模型，取大在位排斥极限（$t-J_{XX}$ 模型），并引入单个空穴及随机磁场；结合解析论证与数值计算，考察自旋部门的局域化与热化行为。

Result: 在无空穴时，自旋部门在任意弱的随机磁场下均局域化；存在单个空穴时，弱无序极限下自旋链被热化，整体系统呈现遍历性（ergodicity）且空穴离域化。在较强磁场下，空穴最终以有限的局域化长度（localization length）局域化，Anderson 自旋绝缘体（Anderson spin insulator）状态得以恢复。

Conclusion: 单个空穴即可在弱无序区充当自旋链的热浴并驱动其热化，从而破坏自旋部门的 Anderson 局域化；而当无序增强至一定程度后，空穴自身局域化，自旋绝缘体恢复，表明掺杂效应对局域化的影响依赖于无序强度。

Abstract: An interacting many-body system being localized in the presence of disorder can fail to serve as its own bath. In an electronic system, the charge and spin degrees of freedom can act as thermal baths for each other. In this paper, we study the stability of an Anderson insulator in the spin sector with respect to charge doping. We consider one-dimensional two-component fermions in a lattice with large onsite repulsion ($t-J_{XX}$ model) and with a single hole in a random magnetic field. In the absence of the hole the spin sector is localized in arbitrarily weak random magnetic fields. We argue that a single hole thermalizes the spin chain in the weak disorder limit. In this regime our numerical results indicate ergodicity of the system as a whole and delocalization of the hole. At larger magnetic fields the hole eventually localizes with a finite localization length, and the Anderson spin insulator is restored.

</details>


### [278] [Boundary Criticality in (2+1)-dimensional U(1) Dirac Quantum Spin Liquid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38088)
*Huan Jiang,Zhiming Pan,Xue-Jia Yu,Shao-Kai Jian*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该文研究（2+1）维 U(1) 狄拉克自旋液体（Dirac spin liquid）的边界临界行为，发现涌现规范场（emergent gauge field）的 Neumann 与 Dirichlet 边界条件导致不同的边界普适类（boundary universality classes）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在探究涌现规范场能否在不改变体态量子自旋液体（quantum spin liquid）的前提下调控边界临界行为，并厘清边界临界性的普适类归属问题。

Method: 针对低能物理由无质量量子电动力学（massless quantum electrodynamics）描述的（2+1）维 U(1) 狄拉克自旋液体，在半空间（half-space）中开展微扰重整化群（perturbative renormalization-group）分析。

Result: 结果表明，涌现规范场的 Neumann 与 Dirichlet 边界条件对应两类不同的边界普适类；研究还确定了基本场及规范不变算符（gauge-invariant operators）的边界标度维度（boundary scaling dimensions），可作为可观测特征。

Conclusion: 涌现规范场可在不改变体态量子自旋液体的条件下控制边界临界行为；作者进一步提出具有可调边界相互作用的费米子-规范场晶格模型（fermion-gauge lattice model），作为实现与探测这些边界普适类的微观平台。

Abstract: An emergent gauge field can control boundary critical behavior without changing the bulk quantum spin liquid. We investigate the boundary criticality in $(2+1)$-dimensional $U(1)$ Dirac spin liquid, whose low-energy physics is described by massless quantum electrodynamics. Using a perturbative renormalization-group analysis in a half-space, we show that Neumann and Dirichlet boundary conditions for the emergent gauge field lead to distinct boundary universality classes. We determine the boundary scaling dimensions of the fundamental fields and gauge-invariant operators that provide observable signatures. We further propose a fermion-gauge lattice model with tunable boundary interactions as a microscopic setting for realizing and probing these boundary universality classes.

</details>


### [279] [Evolution of Magnetism in Ce$_4$Ge$_7$ under Magnetic Field and Pressure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37129)
*Kaixin Ye,Qihe Yu,Yongjian Li,Yanan Zhang,Ye Chen,Rui Li,Lin Jiao,M. Smidman,Yongjun Zhang,Yu Liu,Huiqiu Yuan*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 单晶 Ce₄Ge₇ 在 7.3 K 发生反铁磁转变，b 轴磁场下 1.3 T 出现由本征 Karplus-Luttinger 机制主导的异常霍尔效应；静水压下磁有序在 P_c = 10.2 GPa 以上消失，并伴随非费米液体行为等量子临界点特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文关注非中心对称正交结构（non-centrosymmetric orthorhombic structure，空间群 C222₁）Ce₄Ge₇ 的磁性与输运性质，并借助压力调控探索其反铁磁量子临界行为。

Method: 采用单晶样品的磁性与输运测量；施加沿 b 轴磁场以研究 metamagnetic transition（变磁转变）及异常霍尔效应；并通过静水压（hydrostatic pressure）实验追踪 T_N 的演化。

Result: T_N = 7.3 K；2 K 下 b 轴磁场于 1.3 T 出现变磁转变，并对应异常霍尔效应，主要由本征 Karplus-Luttinger 机制主导。压力下 T_N 先略增后被逐渐抑制，P_c = 10.2 GPa 以上无磁有序；P_c 附近出现 A 系数发散、剩余电阻率最大值及非费米液体行为。

Conclusion: 上述量子临界点（quantum critical point）相关特征表明 Ce₄Ge₇ 中可能存在反铁磁量子临界点。

Abstract: We report the magnetic and transport properties of single-crystalline Ce$_4$Ge$_7$, which crystallizes in a non-centrosymmetric orthorhombic structure (space group C222$_1$). It undergoes an antiferromagnetic transition at $T_N$ = 7.3 K. When a magnetic field is applied along the $b$-axis, a metamagnetic transition occurs at 1.3 T (at 2 K). Correspondingly, this transition gives rise to an anomalous Hall effect, which is dominated by the intrinsic Karplus-Luttinger mechanism. Under hydrostatic pressure, $T_N$ of Ce$_4$Ge$_7$ initially increases slightly and is then gradually suppressed. For pressures above $P_c$ = 10.2 GPa, no magnetic order is observed. The divergence of the $A$ coefficient, the maximum of the residual resistivity, and the non-Fermi liquid behavior around $P_c$ indicate the possible existence of an antiferromagnetic quantum critical point in Ce$_4$Ge$_7$.

</details>


### [280] [Uniform-Charge-Density $d$-Wave Superconductivity in the Pure $t$-$J$ Model at $1/8$ Doping on an Infinite Cylinder](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37191)
*Guangyu Yu,Zheng Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 采用变分均匀矩阵乘积态（VUMPS）方法在热力学极限下研究正方晶格 $t$-$J$ 模型 $1/8$ 空穴掺杂基态，发现在 $L_y=8$ 无限圆柱上为均匀电荷密度 $d$-波超导态，无条纹或磁序迹象，为条纹—超导之争提供方法论上独立的证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 正方晶格 $t$-$J$ 模型在 $1/8$ 空穴掺杂下的基态长期存在争议：数值研究对条纹序（stripe order）与超导（superconductivity）给出相互冲突的报道，且有限圆柱计算可能受边界钉扎效应（boundary pinning effects）干扰。

Method: 使用变分均匀矩阵乘积态（variational uniform matrix product state, VUMPS）方法，在热力学极限下沿圆柱轴直接优化波函数，从而消除有限圆柱计算的边界钉扎效应；在周长 $L_y = 8$ 的无限圆柱上进行计算，并辅以扭曲边界条件（twisted-boundary-condition）计算；同时以 $L_y=6$、$1/6$ 掺杂的圆柱作为对照。

Result: 在 $L_y = 8$ 圆柱上得到均匀电荷密度的 $d$-波超导基态，具有准长程配对关联（quasi-long-range pairing correlations）；主导配对出现在零质心动量处，未探测到可观的有限动量配对成分。扭曲边界条件计算显示有限超导相位刚度（phase stiffness），序参量相位随外加磁通平滑绕转。电荷与自旋关联均快速衰减，无长程条纹或磁序特征；相比之下，$L_y=6$、$1/6$ 掺杂时同一方法可轻易识别出条纹序。

Conclusion: 结果为纯 $t$-$J$ 模型中长期的条纹—超导之争提供了方法论上不同的证据，并揭示掺杂莫特绝缘体（doped Mott insulators）最小模型中存在均匀 $d$-波超导。

Abstract: The ground state of the square-lattice $t$-$J$ model at $1/8$ hole doping has been the subject of a long-standing controversy, with conflicting numerical reports of stripe order versus superconductivity. Here, we address this debate using the variational uniform matrix product state (VUMPS) method, which optimizes the wave function directly in the thermodynamic limit along the cylinder axis and eliminates the boundary pinning effects that can arise in finite-cylinder calculations. On an infinite cylinder of circumference $L_y = 8$, we find a uniform-charge-density $d$-wave superconducting ground state with quasi-long-range pairing correlations. The dominant pairing occurs at zero center-of-mass momentum, while no sizable finite-momentum pairing component is detected. Twisted-boundary-condition calculations reveal a finite superconducting phase stiffness, with the order-parameter phase winding smoothly to follow the applied flux, providing an independent probe of the robustness of the superconductivity. Moreover, both charge and spin correlations decay rapidly, with no signatures of long-range stripe or magnetic order, in contrast to an $L_y=6$ cylinder at $1/6$ doping where the same method readily identifies stripe order. Our results provide methodologically distinct evidence in the long-standing stripe--superconductivity debate in the pure $t$-$J$ model and reveal uniform $d$-wave superconductivity in a minimal model of doped Mott insulators.

</details>


### [281] [Correlation-assisted topological and metamagnetic transitions in Rashba-coupled superconductors: $t$-$J$-$U$ model study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37812)
*Tushar Dey,Maciej Fidrysiak,Józef Spałek*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作采用统计一致变分近似（SGA），在含 Rashba 型自旋轨道耦合（RSOC）的方格子 t-J-U 模型中构建相图，揭示电子关联与 RSOC 联合效应对配对与拓扑超导（TSC）态的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 铜氧化物（cuprates）等强电子关联体系是非常规超导（unconventional superconductivity）的典型平台，而 Bi 系铜氧化物中观测到的自旋-动量锁定（spin-momentum locking）暗示存在不可忽略的 Rashba 型自旋轨道耦合（RSOC）。因此有必要研究电子关联与 RSOC 对铜氧化物及相关超导体配对的联合效应。

Method: 采用统计一致变分近似（statistically-consistent variational approximation, SGA），在引入 RSOC 的方格子 t-J-U 模型中构建相图；同时引入 Zeeman 场考察时间反演对称性破缺效应，并对涌现的拓扑超导（topological superconducting, TSC）态进行表征，同时与弱耦合 Bogoliubov-de Gennes（BdG）方案作对比。

Result: 在接近半填充（half-filling）的较宽在位库仑排斥（on-site Coulomb repulsion）区间发现 Chern 数 C = ±4 的 TSC 态，该态不受关联支配，在弱耦合 BdG 框架下亦可出现。另识别出由电子关联驱动的、C = ±2 的独特 TSC 态，其拓扑转变不伴随体能隙闭合，并伴随不连续的变磁（metamagnetic）与 Lifshitz 转变；在金属-绝缘体转变之上，混合宇称（mixed-parity）超导序参量的 d 波与 p 波分量呈现定性不同的掺杂演化。

Conclusion: 结果指向超越 BdG 方案的关联与 RSOC 联合效应对于 RSOC 耦合超导体相图的重要性。

Abstract: Unconventional superconductivity commonly emerges in systems characterized by strong electronic correlations, with layered copper-oxides serving as a canonical example. Observation of spin-momentum locking within Bi-family of the cuprates, compatible with the presence of non-negligible Rashba-type spin-orbit coupling (RSOC), calls for an investigation of the joint effects of electronic correlations and RSOC on pairing in copper-oxide and related superconductors. Employing statistically-consistent variational approximation (SGA), we carry out such an analysis by constructing the phase diagram for the case of square-lattice \textit{t-J-U} model incorporating RSOC. We also investigate the effects of time-reversal-symmetry breaking by Zeeman field, as well as characterize emergent topological superconducting (TSC) states. Chern number $C = \pm 4$ TSC is found in a broad regime of on-site Coulomb repulsion close to half-filling. The latter is not governed by correlations and emerges also within the weak-coupling Bogoliubov-de Gennes (BdG) scheme. Yet, we identify a distinct $C = \pm 2$ TSC state that is driven specifically by electronic correlations via a topological transition occurring with no bulk quasiparticle gap closure, and is accompanied by discontinuous metamagnetic and Lifshitz transitions. Moreover, a qualitatively distinct doping evolution of the $d$- and $p$-wave components of the underlying mixed-parity SC order parameter above the metal-to-insulator transition is demonstrated. Our work points toward the relevance of joint correlation and RSOC effects beyond BdG scheme to phase diagrams of RSOC-coupled superconductors.

</details>


### [282] [Strong-to-Weak Spontaneous Symmetry Breaking of Dephased Fermions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37965)
*Abhijat Sarma*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究 $U(1)$ 对称费米子在无限密度退相干（infinite density dephasing）下的混合态，导出其强弱自发对称破缺（SWSSB）性质：无限退相干时各 Renyi 关联函数可精确映射为紧致 XY 模型的关联函数，并据此判定金属与绝缘体母态的 SWSSB 相图、稳定性与普适类。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强弱自发对称破缺（SWSSB）是混合态中与全局电荷信息丢失相关的新奇相变。本文旨在刻画费米子母态在无限（及有限）密度退相干下所生成混合态的 SWSSB 性质，填补该体系系统性理解的空白。

Method: 核心手段是无限退相干的精确映射：将 Renyi 关联函数对应为紧致 XY 模型的 Boltzmann 权重，其等于经典系综全计数统计生成函数的平方（Renyi-2 情形即母态本身的全计数统计）。辅以适用于任意对角混合态、可计算 Renyi-1 XY 模型耦合的新型图形展开（diagrammatic expansion），以及适用于偶数 Renyi 指标、有限退相干的 replica 场论（replica field theory）。

Result: 完全退相干的金属在 $d\geq 2$ 呈现长程 SWSSB（$d=2$ 已获数值与实验证实），在 $d=1$ 呈现准长程 SWSSB。绝缘母态在无限退相干下依紫外（UV）细节可出现 SWSSB 相或平庸相。长程 SWSSB（$d\geq 3$）或准长程 SWSSB（$d=2$）在非普适的、依赖 Renyi 指标的阈值之上存活，当母态深处于绝缘相时消失，例外是量子霍尔绝缘体通常具有准长程 SWSSB。对偶数 Renyi 指标还确定了远离无限退相干时 SWSSB 相的稳定性及其相变普适类。

Conclusion: 通过对费米子混合态的精确映射与场论分析，本文建立了退相干强度、母态导电性质（金属/绝缘/量子霍尔）与 SWSSB 长程性之间的对应，并给出 SWSSB 相稳定性和相变普适类的判据。

Abstract: Strong-to-weak spontaneous symmetry breaking (SWSSB) is a novel phase transition in mixed states associated with the loss of global charge information. In this work we study fermions with $U(1)$ symmetry under infinite density dephasing and derive the SWSSB properties of the resulting mixed states. Our central observation is that at infinite dephasing every Renyi correlator is exactly the spin-spin correlator of a compact XY model, whose Boltzmann weight is the squared full-counting-statistics generating function of a classical ensemble built from the parent state. For the Renyi-2 correlator it is the full counting statistics of the parent state itself. We complement the infinite dephasing mapping with a novel diagrammatic expansion for the Renyi-1 correlator, applicable to any diagonal mixed state, which computes the couplings of the Renyi-1 XY model. We also study a replica field theory for even Renyi index quantities, applicable at finite dephasing. Our analysis indicates that fully dephased metals display long-range SWSSB in $d\geq 2$, as confirmed numerically and experimentally in $d=2$, and quasi-long-range SWSSB in $d=1$. For insulating parent states both an SWSSB and a trivial phase are possible at infinite dephasing depending on UV details. Long-range SWSSB in $d\geq 3$, or quasi-long-range SWSSB in $d=2$, survives above a nonuniversal, Renyi-index-dependent threshold and is lost when the parent state is deep in the insulating phase, with the exception of quantum Hall insulators which generically have quasi-long-range SWSSB. For even Renyi indices we also determine the stability of the SWSSB phase away from infinite dephasing and derive the universality class of the SWSSB transition.

</details>


### [283] [Variational Monte Carlo for the Hubbard model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38015)
*Luca F. Tocchio*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本讲义介绍了用于哈伯德模型（Hubbard model）的变分蒙特卡洛（variational Monte Carlo, VMC）方法，涵盖单轨道与多轨道电子模型、变分波函数、蒙特卡洛计算与优化，以及关联函数结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 讲义旨在系统引入变分蒙特卡洛方法在哈伯德模型中的应用，并说明如何描述与探测单轨道及多轨道体系中的超导态与磁性态。

Method: 方法包括：先介绍单轨道哈伯德模型及其多轨道扩展（仅限电子模型）；随后给出变分蒙特卡洛的一般框架、可描述超导与磁性态的变分波函数、针对给定波函数的蒙特卡洛计算流程，以及用随机重构（stochastic reconfiguration）方法优化变分波函数。

Result: 文末给出若干可在变分蒙特卡洛中计算的关联函数（correlation functions）结果，用于判定电荷序、自旋序及超导性等。

Conclusion: 该讲义提供了从模型、波函数、蒙特卡洛实现与优化到关联函数测量的完整入门路径，可支撑对哈伯德模型中磁性与超导态的变分研究。

Abstract: In these lecture notes, we introduce the variational Monte Carlo method for the Hubbard model. First, we briefly introduce the single-orbital Hubbard model and its multi-orbital extension, focusing only on electronic models. Then, we introduce the general scheme under which variational Monte Carlo works and we present the variational wave functions that can properly describe superconductive and magnetic states in single-orbital and multi-orbital Hubbard models. We also detail how to perform Monte Carlo calculations for the given wave functions. We also describe how to optimize a variational wave function with the stochastic reconfiguration method. Finally, we present a few results for correlation functions that can be computed in variational Monte Carlo, to determine, for instance, the presence of charge or spin order and superconductivity.

</details>


### [284] [Neural-quantum-state based downfolding of the three-band Emery model for cuprates and nickelates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38150)
*Hannah Lange,Julius F. A. Tirpitz,Annabelle Bohrdt*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 该工作将隐费米子行列式态（Hidden Fermion Determinant States, HFDS）确立为求解铜氧化物与无限层镍氧化物三带 Emery 模型（Emery model）的可扩展变分方法，并在全二维体系及下折叠所得有效单带模型中发现显著偏离常规费米-哈伯德模型（Fermi-Hubbard model）的相互作用项。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解铜氧化物及近年无限层镍氧化物中高温超导电性（high-temperature superconductivity）的物理机制，仍是凝聚态物理的核心难题，而多带 Emery 模型的可扩展求解与有效低能描述是关键环节。

Method: 采用神经量子态（neural quantum states, NQS）中的隐费米子行列式态（HFDS）；先在宽度为二的几何构型上与矩阵乘积态（matrix product states, MPS）进行基准对比，随后求解最大至 10×10 晶胞（300 位点）的全二维基态，并通过构造相互作用 Wannier 函数（interacting Wannier functions）将三带模型下折叠为有效单带描述。

Result: 在动量空间掺杂分布中观察到类似铜氧化物实验的显著电子-空穴二分性（electron-hole dichotomy）；参数覆盖铜氧化物相关的电荷转移区（charge-transfer regime）、镍氧化物的哈伯德-莫特区（Hubbard-Mott regime）以及电荷转移能隙的系统扫描。所有区间的有效模型均显著偏离常规费米-哈伯德模型：U/t 比值增强、部分参数具有明显掺杂依赖性、存在超越常规哈伯德模型的显著项，其中最突出的是密度辅助跃迁（density-assisted hopping）t_n；且在各有效模型中 t_n 对粒子-空穴不对称性（particle-hole asymmetry）贡献最大，而非次近邻跃迁贡献。

Conclusion: HFDS 被确立为研究二维 Emery 模型的高效工具；有效单带描述可能需引入由底层多带模型产生的相互作用项，且有效参数敏感依赖于电荷转移能（charge-transfer energy）、掺杂与相互作用比值。

Abstract: Understanding the physics underlying high-temperature superconductivity in cuprates and, more recently, infinite-layer nickelates has remained a central challenge in condensed-matter physics. We establish neural quantum states (NQS), specifically Hidden Fermion Determinant States (HFDS), as a scalable variational approach to the three-band Emery model of the copper- and nickel-oxide layers in these materials. After benchmarking HFDS against matrix product states on width-two geometries, we study ground states of fully two-dimensional (2D) systems of up to $10\times10$ unit cells ($300$ sites). We characterize the momentum-space distribution of dopants and find a pronounced electron-hole dichotomy similar to cuprate experiments. We further downfold the three-band model to effective single-band descriptions by constructing interacting Wannier functions. We consider a wide range of parameters -- from the charge-transfer regime relevant to cuprates to the Hubbard-Mott regime of nickelates, as well as systematic scans of the charge-transfer gap that has been demonstrated to impact the critical superconducting temperatures. Across all regimes, the effective model significantly deviates from the usual Fermi-Hubbard model: The typical ratio $U/t$ is enhanced, some parameters experience a significant doping dependence, and sizable terms beyond the conventional Hubbard model are present, most notably a density-assisted hopping $t_n$. Notably, in all effective models, $t_n$ has the largest contribution to particle-hole asymmetry, rather than next-nearest-neighbor hopping contributions. The effective parameters sensitively depend on the charge-transfer energy, doping, and interaction ratios. Our results establish HFDS as an efficient tool for studying the 2D Emery model and demonstrate that single-band descriptions can require interaction terms generated by the underlying multi-band models.

</details>

### [285] [Input to the SCAC Facilities Subcommittee from the U.S. Muon Collider Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37117)
*S. Jindariani,T. Holmes,P. Meade,K. DiPetrillo,K. Kennedy,L. Lee,S. Pagan Griso,D. Stratakis*

Main category: physics.acc-ph

TL;DR: 本文档为美国缪子对撞机合作组（U.S. Muon Collider Collaboration）向SCAC设施小组委员会提交的意见书，阐述未来缪子对撞机（Muon Collider）的科学动机、可行性所需的研发与验证计划，及其对美国2026-2036年设施规划的关联意义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文档旨在为未来缪子对撞机（Muon Collider）提供科学动机，并说明其可行性的论证需求，同时服务于美国2026-2036年设施规划的决策背景。

Method: 以向SCAC设施小组委员会提交意见书的形式，梳理并呈现所需的研发与验证（R&D and demonstration）计划，以及相关能力利用与合作路径。

Result: 摘要未报告具体研究结果，仅说明文档涵盖：科学动机、建立可行性所需的研发与验证计划、与美国2026-2036年设施规划的关联，以及利用美国现有能力、培养加速器人才队伍、与DOE其他项目及国际努力形成协同的机会。

Conclusion: 该意见书主张通过系统性研发与验证确立缪子对撞机（Muon Collider）可行性，并将其纳入美国设施规划、人才培养及国内外协同的整体布局之中。

Abstract: This document presents input from the U.S. Muon Collider Collaboration to the SCAC Facilities Subcommittee. It outlines the scientific motivation for a future Muon Collider, the R&D and demonstration program needed to establish its feasibility, and its relevance to U.S. facilities planning for 2026-2036. It also discusses opportunities for leveraging existing U.S. capabilities, developing the accelerator workforce, and building synergies with other DOE programs and international efforts.

</details>

### [286] [A robust single-sensing-element tactile sensor for concurrent pressure and tackiness detection with real-time signal decoupling capability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36558)
*Ying Yang,Mingwei Gu,Jia-Sen Xie,Xingyu Ma,Yan-Na Lu,Lin Zheng,Jinhui Gu,Junshuai Chen,Yunjie Lu,Denys Makarov,Jin Ge*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种表面柔性触觉传感器（surface-soft tactile sensor），将霍尔效应传感器（Hall effect sensor）与软磁复合材料（soft magnetic composite）集成于稳固弹性框架中，通过磁-机耦合机制实现压力与脱附力的双模态同步感知。该设计可全程追踪、基线分离地输出信号，兼具高精度、高灵敏度与良好稳定性，为机器人粘附性评估等能力提供支撑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将粘性/黏附感（tackiness sensation）集成至人形机器人人工皮肤可显著提升其认知与操作能力，但现有触觉传感器在多模态信号解耦（decoupling）与稳定性方面存在挑战。因此需要一种能在同一接触点同时感知压力与脱附力且无串扰的方案。

Method: 传感器在稳固弹性框架（robust elastic framework）内集成霍尔效应传感器与软磁复合材料；受压时表面内凹、从粘性表面脱离时明显凸起，从而动态改变霍尔传感器与磁体间距。该单敏感单元（single-sensing-element）设计在同一接触点实现双模态感知，并消除应力串扰（stress cross-talk）。

Result: 该机制产生全程可追踪（whole-process-traceable）且基线分离（baseline-separated）的信号，可实时区分压力与脱附力（pull-off force）；单敏感单元设计消除应力串扰，同时提升精度与灵敏度。稳固框架与磁-机传感机制的融合赋予传感器优异的可靠性与出色的信号基线稳定性。

Conclusion: 该触觉传感器在推进机器人能力方面具有显著潜力，包括评估粘附特性（adhesive properties）、监测橡胶老化、精确操作轻质物体以及认知自然物体表面特征等应用。

Abstract: Integrating tackiness sensation into the artificial skin of humanoid robots significantly enhances their cognitive and operational capabilities. However existing tactile sensors face challenges in decoupling of the multimodal signal and stability. Here we present a surface-soft tactile sensor that incorporates a Hall effect sensor and a soft magnetic composite within a robust elastic framework. The sensor surface indents under pressure and bulges prominently when retracted from sticky surfaces dynamically altering the Hall sensor-magnet distance. This generates whole-process-traceable and baseline-separated signals enabling real-time differentiation between pressure and pull-off force. This single-sensing-element design facilitates bimodal sensing at the same contact spot while eliminate stress cross-talk enhancing both accuracy and sensitivity. The fusion of a robust framework and magneto-mechanical sensing mechanism equips the sensor with exceptional reliability and excellent signal baseline stability. This tactile sensor holds substantial potential for advancing robotic capabilities in evaluating adhesive properties monitoring rubber aging precisely handling lightweight objects and cognizing natural objects surface characteristics.

</details>
