### [287] [Measurements of the inclusive Branching fractions of the $D_s^+ \to η(η^\prime) X$ decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36028)
*BESIII Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于 BESIII 探测器在质心能量 4.128–4.226 GeV 的正负电子对撞数据（积分亮度 7.33 fb⁻¹），测量了 D_s⁺→ηX 与 D_s⁺→η′X 的包容分支比，精度较此前 CLEO 测量分别提升 2.8 倍与 1.9 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: D_s⁺ 的包容衰变分支比是检验与η、η′相关衰变机制及理论计算的重要输入，而此前测量精度有限，需要更高统计量的实验数据进行精确测定与交叉验证。

Method: 利用 BESIII 探测器在质心能量（center-of-mass energy）4.128 至 4.226 GeV 区间采集的 e⁺e⁻ 对撞数据，对应积分亮度（integrated luminosity）7.33 fb⁻¹，测量 D_s⁺→ηX 与 D_s⁺→η′X（X 表示任意可能粒子）的包容分支比（inclusive branching fraction）。

Result: 测得 D_s⁺→ηX 的包容分支比为 (30.72 ± 0.90 ± 0.46)%，D_s⁺→η′X 为 (12.30 ± 0.94 ± 0.37)%，其中第一项误差为统计误差（statistical uncertainty），第二项为系统误差（systematic uncertainty）。结果与此前 CLEO 测量一致，且对 D_s⁺→ηX 与 D_s⁺→η′X 的精度分别提升 2.8 倍与 1.9 倍。

Conclusion: 该测量以更高精度确认了先前 CLEO 的包容分支比结果，为 D_s⁺ 衰变中 η 与 η′ 产生的理论描述提供了更精确的实验约束。

Abstract: Using $e^+ e^-$ collision data collected with the BESIII detector at center-of-mass energies between $4.128$ and $4.226$ GeV, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $7.33~{\rm fb}^{-1}$, the inclusive branching fractions of the decays $D_s^+ \to ηX$ and $D_s^+ \to η^\prime X$ ($X$ denotes any possible particles) are measured to be $(30.72 \pm 0.90 \pm 0.46)\%$ and $(12.30 \pm 0.94 \pm 0.37)\%$, where the first uncertainties are statistical and the second systematic. The results are consistent with the previous CLEO measurements, with precision improved by factors of 2.8 and 1.9 for $D_s^+ \to ηX$ and $D_s^+ \to η^\prime X$, respectively.

</details>


### [288] [Trace isotopes set the neutron horizon of liquid argon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37087)
*Yasser Maghrbi*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 在约57 keV处，氩-40因破坏性干涉（destructive interference）对中子近乎透明，天然氩中仅占0.4%的氩-36与氩-38贡献了超过90%的不透明度，且该效应被放大230倍。已发表的透射数据（transmission data）否定了FLUKA默认使用的氩-36评价，并限定其在该能窗内的截面为2-11 b。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 天然氩在中子输运中的不透明度通常被归因于主要同位素氩-40，但57 keV附近的干涉效应可能使微量同位素主导不透明度，从而影响探测器模拟所依赖的核评价（nuclear evaluation）的准确性。该问题对DUNE量级探测器的标定刻画具有实际意义。

Method: 依据已发表的透射数据，检验FLUKA默认氩-36评价的合理性，并在假定其他同位素全部透明的情形下对氩-36截面给出能窗内的上下界；随后在DUNE量级探测器中对标定俘获（calibration captures）进行模拟比较。

Result: 已发表数据否定FLUKA默认的氩-36评价，并将该能窗内氩-36截面限定为2-11 b。改用与数据一致的评价后，DUNE量级探测器中氩-41标定俘获的基准体积份额（fiducial fraction）变动9-13%，而总产额仅变化约2%。

Conclusion: 该效应影响俘获发生的位置而非俘获数量；微量同位素在特定能窗内的截面评价对探测器空间响应刻画具有决定性作用。

Abstract: Near 57 keV, destructive interference makes argon-40 almost transparent to neutrons. The 0.4% of argon-36 and argon-38 in natural argon then supply over 90% of its opacity, amplified 230-fold. Published transmission data rule out the argon-36 evaluation FLUKA uses by default, even with every other isotope made transparent, and bound its cross section in the window to 2-11 b. In a DUNE-scale detector, switching to the data-consistent evaluations moves the fiducial fraction of argon-41 calibration captures by 9-13% while the total yield changes by only 2%: the effect is on where captures occur, not how many.

</details>


### [289] [Measurements of electroweak penguin $B$ decays with missing energy at Belle and Belle II](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37146)
*Lorenz Gärtner*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 利用 Belle 与 Belle II 数据，首次搜索电弱企鹅（electroweak penguin）B 衰变中末态含 τ 轻子或中微子的过程，给出分支比（branching fraction）上限，并在弱有效理论（Weak Effective Theory）中重新诠释 B⁺→K⁺νν̄ 结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 末态含中微子或 τ 轻子的电弱企鹅 B 衰变对超出标准模型（Standard Model）的新物理敏感，是探测新物理的探针。

Method: 使用完整的 Belle 与 Run 1 Belle II 数据集，对 B⁰→K⁰_Sτ⁺τ⁻、B⁺→K⁺τ⁺τ⁻ 及 B→X_sνν̄ 进行首次搜索；并基于已发表的似然（likelihood）在弱有效理论中重新诠释 Belle II 的 B⁺→K⁺νν̄ 结果。

Result: 在 90% 置信水平（CL_s）下，B⁰→K⁰_Sτ⁺τ⁻ 分支比上限为 8.3×10⁻⁴，B⁺→K⁺τ⁺τ⁻ 为 5.6×10⁻⁴，其同位旋组合（isospin combination）B→Kτ⁺τ⁻ 为 5.4×10⁻⁴；B→X_sνν̄ 上限为 3.3×10⁻⁴。弱有效理论拟合中，数据偏好增强的矢量（vector）贡献，并轻微倾向非零张量（tensor）贡献。

Conclusion: 上述测量为相关电弱企鹅 B 衰变提供了首批分支比上限，并在弱有效理论框架下对 Wilson 系数（矢量与张量）给出初步倾向。

Abstract: Electroweak penguin $B$ decays with neutrinos or $τ$ leptons in the final state are sensitive probes of physics beyond the Standard Model. Using the full Belle and Run 1 Belle II datasets, the first search for $B^0 \to K^0_Sτ^+τ^-$ sets the upper limit on the branching fraction to $\mathcal{B} < 8.3 \times 10^{-4}$, the search for $B^+ \to K^+τ^+τ^-$ sets $\mathcal{B} < 5.6 \times 10^{-4}$, and their isospin combination gives $\mathcal{B}(B \to Kτ^+τ^-) < 5.4 \times 10^{-4}$, all at 90% confidence level (CL$_s$). The first search for inclusive $B \to X_sν\barν$ decays at Belle II sets $\mathcal{B} < 3.3 \times 10^{-4}$. Finally, the Belle II $B^+ \to K^+ν\barν$ result is reinterpreted in the Weak Effective Theory using its published likelihood. The data prefer an enhanced vector contribution and mildly favour a nonzero tensor contribution.

</details>


### [290] [Search for compressed electroweak SUSY production in final states with two $τ$-leptons in $\sqrt{s} = 13$ and $13.6$ TeV $pp$ collisions with the ATLAS detector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37215)
*ATLAS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 该研究利用 ATLAS 探测器在压缩质量谱（compressed mass spectra）情景下搜寻 τ-超子（τ-slepton, $\tilde\tau$）、带电微子（chargino, $\tilde\chi^{\pm}_1$）与中性微子（neutralino, $\tilde\chi^0_2$）的产生，采用 13 TeV（140 fb⁻¹）与 13.6 TeV（53 fb⁻¹）质子-质子对撞数据，结果总体与标准模型（Standard Model）一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究对象针对压缩质量谱情景中质量接近的超对称粒子（supersymmetric particles），此类情形下传统搜寻灵敏度较低。为提升对弱衰变产物的探测能力，工作引入初始态辐射（initial-state radiation, ISR）喷流以增强信号特征。

Method: 分析聚焦至少一个强子化衰变 τ-轻子（hadronically decaying τ-lepton）的末态，并采用两类末态选择：一个强子化衰变 τ-轻子加另一带电轻子，或两个及以上强子化衰变 τ-轻子。对带电微子与中性微子，考虑产生 τ-超子或 τ-超中微子（τ-sneutrino）的级联衰变（cascade decays）。

Result: 结果总体与标准模型一致，但观察到若干局部显著性为 2.1–2.4σ 的偏差，这些偏差削弱了观测到的排除上限（exclusion limits）。在仅 ISR 通道中，对 $\Delta m(\tilde\tau, \tilde\chi^0_1)=30$ GeV 情景，$τ$-超子质量上限设为 115 GeV（95% 置信水平）；综合所有通道后，未对直接 $τ$-超子产生设定排除上限。

Conclusion: 对电弱微子（electroweakino）产生，在 $\Delta m(\tilde\chi^0_2, \tilde\chi^0_1)=30$ GeV 下中性微子质量上限设为 200 GeV；对纯带电微子对产生，相同质量劈裂下质量上限设为 175 GeV。该搜寻为轻 τ-超子压缩质量谱情景提供了灵敏度。

Abstract: Searches for the production of $τ$-sleptons ($\tildeτ$), charginos ($\tildeχ^{\pm}_1$), and neutralinos ($\tildeχ^0_2$) in events with at least one hadronically decaying $τ$-lepton are presented, targeting compressed mass spectra scenarios. For charginos and neutralinos, the cascade decays producing either a $τ$-slepton or a $τ$-sneutrino are considered. Two final selections are studied, requiring either one hadronically decaying $τ$-lepton and one other charged lepton, or two or more hadronically decaying $τ$-leptons. To enhance sensitivity to the compressed mass spectra, production modes with initial-state radiation (ISR) jets are considered. The searches use 140 $\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$ and 53 $\mathrm{fb}^{-1}$ of proton-proton collision data recorded by the ATLAS detector at the Large Hadron Collider during 2015-2018 at a centre-of-mass energy of $\sqrt{s} = 13$ TeV and 2022-2023 at a centre-of-mass energy of $\sqrt{s} = 13.6$ TeV, respectively. The searches provide sensitivity to the compressed mass spectra scenarios with light $τ$-sleptons. The results are generally consistent with the Standard Model, although several deviations with local significances of $2.1-2.4 σ$ are observed. These deviations weaken the observed exclusion limits. For direct $τ$-slepton production, in the ISR channel alone the limits are set for $Δm(\tildeτ, \tildeχ^0_1) = 30$ GeV for $τ$-slepton masses up to $115$ GeV at 95% confidence level. Given the observed data, no exclusion limits are set for direct $τ$-sleptons production when statistically combining all considered channels. For electroweakino production, limits are set for $Δm(\tildeχ^0_2, \tildeχ^0_1) = 30$ GeV for $\tildeχ^0_2$ masses up to $200$ GeV. For pure chargino pair-production, limits are set for chargino masses up to $175$ GeV for the same mass splitting.

</details>


### [291] [First Measurement of the Superscaling Variable as a Function of Visible Kinematics in Charged-Current Quasi-Elastic Neutrino--Nucleus Scattering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37335)
*The MINERvA Collaboration,L. Giannessi,S. Akhter,Z. Ahmad Dar,N. S. Alex,M. Sajjad Athar,J. Felix,L. Fields,R. Gran,E. Granados,D. A. Harris,A. Klustová,M. Kordosky,D. Last,and A. Lozano,S. Manly,W. A. Mann,K. S. McFarland,M. Mehmood,O. Moreno,J. G. Morfín,J. K. Nelson,G. N. Perdue,C. Pernas,M. A. Ramírez,R. D. Ransome,N. Roy,D. Ruterbories,F. Sánchez,H. Schellman,C. J. Solano Salinas,D. S. Correia,V. S. Syrotenko,N. H. Vaughan,A. V. Waldron,L. Zazueta*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本研究首次在中微子-核散射（neutrino-nucleus scattering）中测量超标度变量（superscaling variable）$\\psi^{\\prime}_{\\rm vis}$，利用MINERvA实验的$\\nu_\\mu$-碳荷电流准弹性类事例，通过前向折叠（forward-folding）技术与五种模型比较，发现相对论平均场（Relativistic Mean Field）方法对峰位的预测更准确。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: $\\psi^{\\prime}$原用于描述电子-核准弹性散射中标度行为随原子序数与动量转移的变化，本研究将其引入中微子-核散射以探测不可见的核缺失能量（missing energy）效应，弥补相关实验测量的空白。

Method: 利用MINERvA实验中能NuMI束流下收集的$\\nu_\\mu$-碳荷电流准弹性类相互作用，由可见末态运动学重建$\\psi^{\\prime}_{\\rm vis}$，并将其作为外部标定变量；以强子反冲$\\Sigma T_p$与缪子横动量$p_{\\text{T}\\mu}$为函数进行测量，采用前向折叠技术将扣除本底的数据与五种中微子-核相互作用模型进行对比。

Result: 模型间预测的$\\psi^{\\prime}_{\\rm vis}$在低$\\Sigma T_p$与低$p_{\\text{T}\\mu}$区域差异最大，且该区域与数据符合度也最差，表明部分模型未能刻画缺失能量（进而影响由可见运动学外推的中微子能量）的相空间依赖性；在被检验的模型中，相对论平均场（Relativistic Mean Field）方法对$\\psi^{\\prime}_{\\rm vis}$峰位的预测更准确。

Conclusion: 该测量首次实现中微子-核散射中$\\psi^{\\prime}_{\\rm vis}$的测定，揭示低$\\Sigma T_p$与$p_{\\text{T}\\mu}$区域是模型区分的关键相空间，并支持相对论平均场方法在描述缺失能量相关峰位方面的优势。

Abstract: We report the first measurement of the superscaling variable $ψ^{\prime}_{\rm vis}$ in neutrino--nucleus scattering, using charged-current quasielastic-like $ν_μ$-Carbon interactions collected by the MINERvA experiment in the medium-energy NuMI beam. Originally developed to characterize phenomena of scaling with respect to atomic number and momentum transfer in quasielastic electron-nucleus scattering, $ψ^{\prime}$ is reconstructed here from visible final-state kinematics ($ψ^{\prime}_{\rm vis}$). $ψ^{\prime}_{\rm vis}$ is used here as an externally calibrated variable whose peak position is sensitive to the invisible nuclear missing energy. We measure $ψ^{\prime}_{\rm vis}$ as functions of the hadronic recoil $ΣT_p$ and muon transverse momentum $p_{\text{T}μ}$, comparing background-subtracted data to five neutrino--nucleus interaction models via a forward-folding technique. The predicted $ψ^{\prime}_{\rm vis}$ diverges most strongly among model predictions at low $ΣT_p$ and $p_{\text{T}μ}$, where agreement with data is also poorest, exposing which models fail to capture the phase-space dependence of the missing energy and therefore the neutrino energy as extrapolated from visible kinematics. Among tested models, we find that the Relativistic Mean Field approach predicts more accurately the $ψ^{\prime}_{\rm vis}$ peak position.

</details>


### [292] [Measurement of Neutrino Oscillation Parameters at JUNO and Indication of the Neutrino Mass Ordering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37895)
*The JUNO Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 基于江门中微子实验（JUNO）207.2天数据与8294个逆β衰变候选事件，实现了对大气中微子质量平方差约1%的世界领先精度测量，并结合长期基线加速器中微子约束，给出支持正质量排序（normal ordering）的中微子质量排序（neutrino mass ordering）迹象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中微子振荡参数（neutrino oscillation parameters）的高精度测定及中微子质量排序（neutrino mass ordering）的判定是粒子物理的核心问题。该工作旨在利用江门地下中微子天文台（JUNO）的数据，提升质量平方差与太阳振荡参数的测量精度，并检验质量排序假设。

Method: 分析基于207.2天有效数据，包含8294个逆β衰变（inverse beta-decay）候选事件，平均反应堆基线为52.5 km。拟合中采用大亚湾（Daya Bay）约束以在两种质量排序假设下确定正确的物理极小值，并引入长期基线加速器中微子实验对 Δm²₃₁ 的约束，进行频率学派（单侧显著性）与贝叶斯（Bayes factor）分析。

Result: 大气中微子质量平方差 Δm²₃₁ 的精度约为1%：正序下为 +2.509(+0.027/−0.025)×10⁻³ eV²，倒序下为 −2.482(+0.025/−0.026)×10⁻³ eV²。太阳振荡参数精度进一步提升：sin²θ₁₂=0.3036±0.0064（2.1%），Δm²₂₁=(7.388±0.078)×10⁻⁵ eV²（1.1%）。结合长期基线加速器约束后，在所有可能的 CP 破坏相位（CP-violating phases）下，正序优于倒序的单侧显著性至少为 2.1σ；贝叶斯分析给出支持正序的 Bayes 因子为 10.18。

Conclusion: JUNO 以约1%精度改进了 Δm²₃₁ 的测量并提升了太阳振荡参数的最精确结果，且结果倾向于正质量排序（normal ordering）而非倒质量排序（inverted ordering），但所给出的显著性水平仍属迹象级别。

Abstract: We report an improved measurement of neutrino oscillation parameters at the Jiangmen Underground Neutrino Observatory (JUNO) and the consequent indication of neutrino mass ordering. The analysis is based on 207.2 live days of data, comprising 8,294 inverse beta-decay candidates collected at an average reactor baseline of 52.5 km. A world-leading precision of approximately 1% was achieved for the atmospheric neutrino mass splitting $Δm^2_{31}$, with $Δm^2_{31}=+2.509^{+0.027}_{-0.025}\times10^{-3}\,\mathrm{eV}^2$ for normal ordering and $Δm^2_{31}=-2.482^{+0.025}_{-0.026}\times10^{-3}\,\mathrm{eV}^2$ for inverted ordering, using the Daya Bay constraint in the fit to resolve the correct physical minimum under each mass-ordering hypothesis. We further improved the world's most precise measurements of the solar oscillation parameters, obtaining $\sin^2θ_{12}=0.3036\pm0.0064$ and $Δm^2_{21}=(7.388\pm0.078)\times10^{-5}\,\mathrm{eV}^2$, corresponding to relative precisions of 2.1% and 1.1%, respectively. Incorporating constraints on $Δm^2_{31}$ from long-baseline accelerator neutrino experiments into our analysis favored normal ordering over inverted ordering, with a one-sided significance of at least $2.1σ$ across all possible CP-violating phases; a Bayesian analysis yielded a Bayes factor of 10.18 in favor of normal ordering.

</details>


### [293] [Irradiation Studies and Design Optimization of the ATLAS Tile Calorimeter for the High-Luminosity LHC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37908)
*Róbert Astaloš*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文介绍 ATLAS 实验 TileCal 量能器为应对高光度 LHC（HL-LHC）挑战所做的读出电子学与光电倍增管升级设计，以及配套辐照测试结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HL-LHC 计划于 2030 年运行，其瞬时光度（instantaneous luminosity）将显著超过基线 LHC 设计，对探测器读出与触发系统提出更严苛要求，TileCal 需在高辐射与高粒子通量下可靠运行并兼容升级后的 ATLAS 触发架构。

Method: 在 2026-2030 年长期停机（Long Shutdown）期间，将 TileCal 读出电子学整体更换为抗辐射（radiation-tolerant）系统，以处理约为基线配置一个数量级的数据率；同时更换高辐照区域的光电倍增管（photomultiplier tubes, PMTs）为稳定性与抗辐射能力更优的器件，并开展广泛的辐照测试（irradiation testing）计划。

Result: 系统设计经相应优化，以在高辐射环境下实现更优性能、效率与鲁棒性；并已获得一系列辐照测试结果。

Conclusion: 该报告呈现 TileCal 系统设计的升级进展及辐照测试所得结果。

Abstract: The Tile Calorimeter (TileCal) is a sampling hadronic calorimeter covering the central region of the ATLAS experiment at the CERN Large Hadron Collider (LHC). It employs steel as the absorber material and plastic scintillators as the active medium. The High-Luminosity LHC (HL-LHC), scheduled to start operation in 2030, will deliver instantaneous luminosities significantly exceeding the baseline LHC design, imposing more stringent requirements on detector readout and trigger systems. Consequently, TileCal has to be capable of reliable operation under increased radiation levels and very high particle flux, while maintaining full compatibility with the upgraded ATLAS trigger architecture. During the Long Shutdown period (2026-2030), the TileCal readout electronics will be entirely replaced with radiation-tolerant systems designed to handle data rates approximately an order of magnitude higher than those of the baseline LHC configuration. The photomultiplier tubes (PMTs) in the most highly irradiated regions will also be replaced with improved devices exhibiting enhanced stability and radiation tolerance. To meet these challenges, the system design has been correspondingly optimized to ensure improved performance, efficiency and robustness in high-radiation environments, and an extensive irradiation testing program has been carried out. This contribution presents the resulting design developments of the TileCal system, together with the results obtained from the irradiation tests.

</details>


### [294] [Search for $\boldsymbol{π_1(1600)}$ in a three-pion system at GlueX](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.37954)
*Ilia Belov,Farah Afzal*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: GlueX 实验利用线偏振光子束在 γp 相互作用中研究轻介子谱，重点搜寻具有奇异量子数（exotic quantum numbers）的杂化介子（hybrid mesons）。本文通过对 Δ++ 上产生的 π+π−π− 系统做分波分析（partial-wave analysis），搜寻 π1(1600) 介子，核心目标是确认 ρπ− P 波中 1−+ 共振贡献的存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: GlueX 的主要科学目标之一是寻找无法用常规强子（conventional hadrons）分类的杂化介子，其具有奇异量子数。π1(1600) 是候选的奇异态，需要通过分波分析确认其在 ρπ− P 波中的共振性。

Method: 在 Δ++ 重子上产生的 π+π−π− 末态数据被按 m3π 分 bin，用反射基（reflectivity basis）中定义的分波振幅的相干求和进行拟合。通过 ρπ− D 波中提取的信号研究 a2−(1320) 的产生性质，并对各模型贡献提取整体 m3π 线形。

Result: 通过 ρπ− D 波中提取的信号考察了 a2−(1320) 的产生性质；针对各模型贡献提取了整体 m3π 线形。摘要未给出具体的定量结果数值。

Conclusion: 分析的主要兴趣在于确立 ρπ− P 波构型中是否存在共振的 1−+ 贡献，从而为 π1(1600) 的杂化介子解释提供判据。

Abstract: The GlueX experiment in Hall D at Jefferson Lab enables studies of the light meson spectrum in $γp$ interactions with a linearly polarized photon beam. GlueX aims in particular to search for hybrid mesons that have exotic quantum numbers and therefore cannot be classified as conventional hadrons. We present the search for the $π_1(1600)$ meson by means of a partial-wave analysis of the $π^{+}π^{-}π^{-}$ system produced off the $Δ^{++}$-baryon. Data with the selected three-pion final state are fitted in bins of $m_{3π}$ as coherent sums of partial-wave amplitudes defined in the reflectivity basis. The properties of $a_{2}^{-}(1320)$ production are investigated through the extracted signal in the $ρ\,π^{-}$ $D$-waves. In this analysis, the overall $m_{3π}$ lineshape will be extracted for each of the model contributions. The main interest lies in establishing the existence of a resonant $1^{-+}$ contribution in the $ρ\,π^{-}$ $P$-wave configuration.

</details>


### [295] [Overview of the HL-LHC CMS outer tracker upgrade and lessons learned from 2S module production](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38026)
*F. Zhang*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: CMS 合作组为高亮度大型强子对撞机（HL-LHC）研制升级版硅探测器系统，其外层径迹探测器（Outer Tracker）采用 2S 与 PS 两类模块为 L1 触发提供径迹信息。本文概述 2S 模块的生产流程，并介绍组装与性能测试中的典型问题及经验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为满足高亮度大型强子对撞机（High-Luminosity LHC, HL-LHC）的运行需求，CMS 需要显著提升硅探测器系统的抗辐照能力、 granularity 与覆盖范围，并将径迹信息用于 L1 触发。

Method: 外层径迹探测器（Outer Tracker）配备 2S 与 PS 两类模块，通过排除横向动量（transverse momentum）低于特定阈值的粒子信号，向 HL-LHC CMS 探测器的 L1 触发提供径迹信息；模块须高精度组装并满足严格要求。

Result: 摘要未给出定量结果，仅指出模块必须通过严格的组装与性能要求，并总结了组装与性能测试中出现的典型问题与所获经验。

Conclusion: 本报告概述了 2S 模块生产情况，并汇总了模块组装和性能测试过程中的代表性问题和经验。

Abstract: For operation at the High-Luminosity LHC, the CMS collaboration is developing a significantly enhanced silicon detector system with improved radiation hardness, higher granularity, and extended coverage up to a pseudorapidity of |eta| < 4. The Outer Tracker will be equipped with two types of modules (2S and PS) that will provide tracking information to the L1 trigger of the HL-LHC CMS detector, by rejecting signals from particles below a certain transverse momentum threshold. Hence, these modules must be assembled with high precision and have to pass strict requirements. In this note, an overview of 2S module production is presented, as well as representative examples of problems and experience acquired during module assembly and performance tests.

</details>

### [296] [Enhanced Critical Currents and Irreversibility Fields in YBa$_2$Cu$_4$O$_8$ Films through Ca-Substitution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36402)
*Jiangteng Liu,Shuhei Funaki,Yuki Ogimoto,Bryan Zhang,Ryoya Nagaura,Ryuji Yoshida,Takeharu Kato,Masashi Miura,Serena Eley*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: Ca 掺杂可提升本征欠掺杂外延 (Y,Ca)Ba₂Cu₄O₈ 薄膜的空穴浓度，使其趋近最优掺杂，从而提高 T_c 与 J_c，且不改变主导钉扎机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导材料（superconducting materials）的本征载流子浓度（intrinsic carrier concentration）可能并非最优，且不同超导性质可能对应不同的最优掺杂水平，因此需通过掺杂调控载流子浓度以优化性能。

Method: 在 x ≤ 0.10 范围内制备本征欠掺杂（underdoped）外延 (Y₁₋ₓCaₓ)Ba₂Cu₄O₈（YCa124）薄膜，通过 Ca 替代引入空穴；测量其 T_c、J_c 及磁场依赖关系，并结合电子显微镜观察与热激活磁通运动（creep）分析。

Result: 10% Ca 掺杂在 40 K、0.03 T 与 6 T 下使 J_c 分别提升 2.4 倍与 10 倍；J_c 的磁场依赖遵循 J_c ∝ B⁻⁰·⁵，与 Ca 含量无关，符合平面状 Y-125 层错共生（stacking-fault intergrowths）钉扎；Ca 掺杂在 T ≳ 15 K 减缓蠕变，低温下蠕变率 S 随磁场增大而异常降低。

Conclusion: 掺杂可调控由去对电流密度（depairing current density）J_d 决定的 J_c 上限与由 Ginzburg 参数（Ginzburg parameter）G_i 决定的蠕变下限；J_c 与 J_d、S 与 G_i 之间呈正相关，并与多种超导体的趋势进行了比较。

Abstract: The intrinsic carrier concentration of superconducting materials may not be optimal, and different superconducting properties may have distinct optimal doping levels. Here, we show that Ca substitution increases the hole concentration in intrinsically underdoped epitaxial (Y$_{1-x}$Ca$_x$)Ba$_2$Cu$_4$O$_8$ (YCa124) films with $x \leq 0.10$, driving YCa124 toward optimal doping, increasing $T_c$ and $J_c$. At 40 K, 10% Ca doping increases $J_c$ by factors of 2.4 and 10 at 0.03 and 6 T, respectively, compared with the undoped film. The field dependence of $J_c$ follows $J_c \propto B^{-0.5}$, independent of Ca content, indicating that Ca substitution does not alter the dominant pinning mechanism. This field dependence is consistent with pinning by planar Y-125 stacking-fault intergrowths observed by electron microscopy. In addition, Ca substitution slows thermally activated vortex motion (creep) at $T \gtrsim 15$ K, while at lower temperatures the creep rate $S$ shows an unusual decrease with increasing field. Finally, we consider how doping tunes the upper bound on $J_c$, set by the depairing current density $J_d$, and the lower bound on creep, set by the Ginzburg parameter $G_i$. We find positive correlations between $J_c$ and $J_d$, and between $S$ and $G_i$, and compare them with trends across a broad range of superconductors.

</details>


### [297] [Evanescent-wave Johnson Noise from Superconductors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.36497)
*Hruday Mallubhotla,Gustav Romare,Ilya Esterlis,Maxim Vavilov,Robert Joynt,Alex Levchenko*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作计算了超导体上方真空半空间中的倏逝波约翰逊噪声（evanescent-wave Johnson noise, EWJN），以及置于距表面纳米量级处自旋与电荷量子比特的弛豫时间（$T_1$）。基于BCS超导体的微观横向电流响应核（transverse current-response kernel）$Q(q,\omega)$，结合涨落-耗散定理（fluctuation-dissipation theorem）与非局域表面阻抗，得到任意距离的磁场与电场噪声并进而给出$T_1$。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 纳米尺度量子比特与超导电极表面的接近会引入噪声与退相干，而超导态下EWJN的微观起源及杂质、温度、频率依赖尚需系统刻画。该文旨在建立统一框架，定量预测超导体上方自旋与电荷量子比特的噪声与弛豫，并厘清磁性与电场噪声在超导转变附近及低温区的不同行为。

Method: 以单一微观横向电流响应核$Q(q,\omega)$描述BCS超导体的电磁响应，在非磁杂质与磁杂质不同密度、任意温度与频率、且$q\ll k_F$条件下计算该核。将其与涨落-耗散定理及半空间非局域表面阻抗结合，得到距表面$z\gg k_F^{-1}$处任意距离的磁场与电场噪声，从而导出弛豫时间$T_1$。

Result: 恰在$T_c$下方，磁噪声因耗散电导率的相干（Hebel-Slichter型）峰相对正常态增强，并在更低温度指数下降；电噪声无相干峰。理论预测磁杂质诱导出零温噪声下限。在配对破坏产生的无能隙区（gapless regime），有限亚能隙态密度$\nu(0)$给出与温度无关的噪声谱密度，且当$T\ll T_c$时弛豫率受限于$T_1^{-1}(T)\le[\nu(0)/\nu_F]^{2}T_{1,N}^{-1}(T)$，在极端非局域区取等号。

Conclusion: 超导态EWJN的磁性成分受相干峰与磁杂质控制，其低温行为与电噪声显著不同；亚能隙态密度决定无能隙区弛豫率的温度无关性与上限，极端非局域极限下该上限饱和。结果为纳米尺度量子比特在超导表面附近的弛豫时间提供了可计算的微观预测。

Abstract: We compute the evanescent-wave Johnson noise (EWJN) in the vacuum half-space above a superconductor, and the resulting relaxation time ($T_1$) of spin and charge qubits placed at nanometer distances from the surface. The electromagnetic response is described by a single microscopic transverse current-response kernel $Q(q, ω)$ for a BCS superconductor. This is computed for varying densities of both non-magnetic impurities and magnetic impurities, for arbitrary frequency and temperature and for wave vectors $q \ll k_F$ (the Fermi wavevector). When combined with the fluctuation-dissipation theorem and the nonlocal surface impedances of the half-space, this yields the magnetic and electric field noise at any distance $z \gg k_F^{-1}$ from the surface, from which we obtain $T_1$. Just below $T_c$ the magnetic noise is enhanced relative to the normal state by the coherence (Hebel-Slichter-type) peak of the dissipative conductivity and drops exponentially at lower temperatures; the electric noise shows no coherence peak. The theory predicts that there is a zero-temperature noise floor induced by magnetic impurities. In the gapless regime produced by pair breaking, the finite subgap density of states $ν(0)$ yields a temperature-independent noise spectral density and a relaxation rate bounded by $T_1^{-1}(T)\le[ν(0)/ν_F]^{2}\,T_{1,N}^{-1}(T)$ for $T\ll T_c$, with equality in the extreme nonlocal regime. Here $ν(0)$ and $ν_F$ are the superconducting and normal-state densities of states at the Fermi energy, and $T_{1,N}(T)$ is the relaxation time the same electrode would produce in its normal state at the same temperature.

</details>


### [298] [Reversible field-free superconducting diode effect controlled by an antiferromagnet](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38003)
*Filip Krizek,Kamil Olejník,Tobias Edwinson,August Jacobi,Athira Suresh,Andrej Farkaš,Jan Kraus,Vít Novák,Christoph Müller,Vojtěch Pařízek,Niclas Heinsdorf,Peter Wadley,Oliver Amin,Kevin Edmonds,Tomas Jungwirth,Libor Šmejkal,Anna Birk Hellenes,Sumit Ghosh,Michal Mazur,Dominik Kriegner,Lucas Casparis,Saulius Vaitiekėnas*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作利用共线反铁磁体 CuMnAs 与常规超导体 Al 的邻近效应，在零外场下实现了超导二极管效应（superconducting diode effect），并证明其不依赖自旋劈裂或净磁化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超导二极管效应需要在破坏时间反演对称性（time-reversal symmetry）的前提下产生无耗散的非互易超电流，而零场下的实现此前通常依赖本征非常规超导电性或磁性邻近诱导的自旋劈裂电子态，机制受限。

Method: 采用常规超导体 Al 与共线反铁磁体 CuMnAs 构建邻近结构，其电子结构破坏时间反演对称性但不产生自旋劈裂；通过绝缘 AlAs 中间层调控邻近效应，并将二极管极性反转与剩余奈尔态（remament Néel state）反转进行关联分析。

Result: 在零外加磁场下观测到超导二极管效应；二极管极性的反转与剩余奈尔态的反转相对应，表明该效应由反铁磁体所控制。

Conclusion: 磁控零场超导二极管效应既不要求自旋劈裂能带，也不要求净磁化，从而将超导非互易性拓展至更广泛的共线补偿磁体（collinear compensated magnets）体系。

Abstract: The semiconductor diode, which allows current to flow preferentially in one direction, is a fundamental building block of numerous modern electronic circuits. Its superconducting analogue---the superconducting diode effect---enables directional dissipationless current flow and may provide similar functionality in future superconducting quantum circuits. Realization of such a nonreciprocal supercurrent requires broken time-reversal symmetry. At zero applied field, this has been typically associated either with intrinsic unconventional superconductivity or extrinsic spin-split electronic states induced by magnetic proximity. Here we demonstrate a field-free superconducting diode effect in conventional superconducting Al proximitized by collinear antiferromagnetic CuMnAs, whose electronic structure breaks time-reversal symmetry without generating spin splitting. By tuning the proximity effect through an insulating AlAs interlayer and correlating the reversal of the diode polarity with the reversal of the remanent Néel state, we establish that the antiferromagnet controls the superconducting diode effect. Our results show that neither spin-split bands nor net magnetization is required for a magnetically controlled field-free superconducting diode effect, extending superconducting nonreciprocity to a broader class of collinear compensated magnets.

</details>


### [299] [Anisotropic interface-confined superconductivity in FeTe-based heterostructures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38148)
*Bicky Singh Moirangthem,Kamal R. Joshi,Zi-Jie Yan,Pu Xiao,Lok-Kan Lai,Cui-Zu Chang,Ruslan Prozorov*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该工作通过测量六种 FeTe 基异质结（heterostructure）的全样品迈斯纳响应（Meissner response），建立了超薄极限下的磁化率标定方法并反演出伦敦穿透深度 λ(T)，表明界面超导具有强各向异性配对、λ(0) 约为 1 μm，且与覆盖层性质无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 界面限域超导（interface-confined superconductivity）可为实现磁性与拓扑共存的超导相提供途径，但其超导层仅数纳米厚并埋于多个正常层之下，难以确定其本征性质。传统标定下 χ(T→0)=-1 的超薄极限失效，因此需要新的测量与标定方案。

Method: 采用频域隧道二极管谐振器（frequency-domain tunnel-diode resonator），在均匀磁场中测量六种 FeTe 基异质结的全样品迈斯纳响应；建立合适的标定方法并对 χ(T) 进行反演以确定伦敦穿透深度（London penetration depth）λ(T)。

Result: χ(T) 中观测到的宽转变可由极端几何与较大 λ 自然解释，无需化学或结构不均匀性，且提取的 λ(T) 与电阻转变紧密对应；λ(T) 及其对应的超流密度（superfluid density）与全能隙各向同性 s 波不符，而指向具有线节点或深能隙极小值的强各向异性序参量；推断 λ(0) 约为 1 μm，与二维相位刚度（2D phase stiffness）的独立分析一致；六种异质结在低温下均呈相似幂律行为，且对覆盖层身份无系统依赖。

Conclusion: 尽管三层覆盖层在化学、磁性与拓扑性质上各不相同，六种 FeTe 异质结的超导响应表现相似，表明界面 FeTe 层是超导性的共同来源。

Abstract: Interface-confined superconductivity emerges from the interaction of electronic states across chemically distinct boundaries, providing a route to engineer superconducting phases where magnetism and topology coexist. Determining the intrinsic nature of such superconductivity, however, is challenging because the superconducting layer is only a few nanometers thick and buried beneath several normal layers. Here, we measure the whole-sample Meissner response of six $\mathrm{FeTe}$-based heterostructures in a uniform magnetic field using a frequency-domain tunnel-diode resonator. In the ultrathin limit, the conventional normalization of the measured susceptibility, $χ(T\to0)=-1$, fails by tens of percent. We establish the appropriate calibration and invert $χ(T)$ to determine the London penetration depth $λ(T)$. Two key results emerge. First, the broad transitions observed in $χ(T)$ arise naturally from the extreme geometry and large $λ$, without requiring chemical or structural inhomogeneity; the extracted $λ(T)$ closely tracks the resistive transition. Second, $λ(T)$ and the corresponding superfluid density are inconsistent with a fully gapped isotropic $s$-wave state and instead indicate a strongly anisotropic order parameter possibly with line nodes or deep gap minima. The inferred $λ(0)$ is of order $1\,μ$m, consistent with an independent analysis of the 2D phase stiffness. Despite the distinct chemical, magnetic, and topological character of the three overlayers, all six FeTe heterostructures exhibit similar low-temperature power-law behavior, with no systematic dependence of the superconducting response on overlayer identity. These results point to the interfacial $\mathrm{FeTe}$ layer as the common origin of superconductivity.

</details>


### [300] [Imaging phase winding in topological superconductors with a fork-tip Josephson STM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.38174)
*Vladislav Poliakov,Archisman Panigrahi*

Main category: cond-mat.supr-con

TL;DR: 该文提出一种利用叉形针尖约瑟夫森扫描隧道显微镜（fork-tip Josephson STM），在杂质附近探测拓扑超导体（topological superconductor）相位绕转（phase winding）的方案，并通过数值计算验证可重建序参量的幅值与相位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓扑超导体中的复杂相位绕转难以被常规实空间探针获取，直接探测相位绕转是长期悬而未决的实验难题。

Method: 在杂质附近使用叉形针尖约瑟夫森扫描隧道显微镜，由两个超导针尖构成干涉仪，对两个位置的相位差敏感；并对干涉图样进行数值计算。

Result: 数值计算的干涉图样表明，序参量（order parameter）的幅值与相位可作为角度的函数被重建；即使叉形针尖与样品之间的相对相位发生涨落，信号仍可获取。

Conclusion: 该方案为拓扑超导电性的局域相位敏感扫描隧道显微镜探测提供了途径，并有望解决直接探测拓扑超导体相位绕转这一长期实验难题。

Abstract: The complex phase winding in a topological superconductor remains challenging to access by conventional real-space probes. We propose a scheme to probe this winding with a fork-tip Josephson STM near an impurity, where two superconducting tips form an interferometer sensitive to phase differences between two positions. The signal remains accessible even if the relative phase between the fork-tip and sample fluctuates, establishing a route towards a local phase-sensitive STM probe of topological superconductivity. We numerically compute the interference pattern to demonstrate that the magnitude and phase of the order parameter can be reconstructed as a function of angle, providing a route towards resolving the longstanding open experimental problem of directly detecting phase winding in topological superconductors.

</details>
